Conclusion: 上下文感知的语义关系建模优于静态概念擦除，可在不重训主干的前提下实现精细的安全对齐，且统一适配T2I/T2V；但评估集中于安全指标与生成质量，未涉及3D/世界模型或具身场景。

Abstract: Recent advances in flow-matching Transformers have greatly improved Text-to-Image (T2I) and Text-to-Video (T2V) generation, but unconstrained generation risks producing illegal or unethical content. Safety alignment must therefore suppress unsafe content while preserving legitimate generation. A concept's safety depends on context: a knife used for cooking is safe, whereas its use in violence is harmful. Yet most concept erasure methods indiscriminately suppress static, isolated representations, overlooking evolving semantic interactions. Consequently, safe scenes containing potentially sensitive terms may be unintentionally suppressed, compromising generation quality. To address this, we propose Dynamic Graph Semantic Recomposition (DGSR), which dynamically constructs and updates a semantic interaction graph from evolving text visual states during sampling. Context aware graph reasoning captures semantic dependencies, while paired safe/unsafe supervision distinguishes harmful from legitimate interactions, enabling precise identification of and selective intervention on high risk semantic relationships while preserving benign semantics. DGSR freezes the pretrained backbone and supports both image and video generation. Experiments show that DGSR achieves the lowest Attack Success Rate (ASR) in 10 of 12 T2I evaluations and reduces the average T2V unsafe frame rate by 41.2\% relative to the strongest baseline, while maintaining high generation fidelity and temporal consistency.

</details>


### [35] [MatrixSplat: Matrix-Parallel and Feature-Scalable 3D Gaussian Splatting Rendering on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15153)
*Yangming Zhang,Siyi Wu,Jian Wang,Bingzhe Li,Wei Niu,Sheng Di,Ming Li,Miao Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MatrixSplat 将 3DGS 中依赖顺序的 alpha 合成改写为顺序无关的加权累积，从而把高维特征渲染映射到 GPU Tensor Core 的 warp 级矩阵乘加运算，实现特征可扩展的加速渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 渲染管线的深度排序 alpha 合成存在强逐像素顺序依赖，限制并行度，且以标量 FMA 为主，无法利用 Tensor Core 高吞吐矩阵单元；在多模态/语义增强 3DGS 中需累积数百万高斯的高维特征，计算与访存开销急剧上升，问题更严重。

Method: 采用顺序无关的加权累积公式，将特征渲染转化为矩阵计算；三项关键设计：(i) 基于 tile 的 splatting 管线，将特征累积映射为 warp 级 MMA 在 Tensor Core 上执行；(ii) warp 粒度的高斯打包策略，缓解可见性不规则性并提升特征复用；(iii) 惰性通道分批特征获取管线，降低共享内存压力、维持高 warp 占用率，并使访存与矩阵计算重叠。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360、LERF、3D-OVS 上，相比 3DGS 最高 3.8× 加速、相比 TC-GS 约 2× 加速，同时保持渲染质量与语义精度；高维特征下渲染核时间最多降低 36.7×；集成到现有快速渲染器可额外获得 1.69–2.84× 加速。

Conclusion: 通过消除 alpha 合成的顺序依赖并利用 Tensor Core 矩阵并行，MatrixSplat 为高维/语义 3DGS 提供了可扩展、高吞吐且即插即用的渲染加速框架。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a mainstream representation for real-time novel view synthesis and 3D reconstruction. However, existing 3DGS rendering pipelines are poorly aligned with modern GPU architectures: depth-sorted alpha compositing introduces strong per-pixel sequential dependencies, limiting parallelism and resulting in scalar FMA-dominated execution that underutilizes high-throughput matrix units such as Tensor Cores. This mismatch becomes more severe for multimodal and semantically enriched 3DGS, where high-dimensional features must be accumulated from millions of Gaussians, leading to rapidly increasing computation and memory costs. We propose MatrixSplat, a matrix-parallel rendering framework for feature-scalable 3DGS on modern GPUs. We first identify the sequential dependency of alpha compositing as a key obstacle to efficient high-dimensional feature rendering, and adopt an order-independent weighted accumulation formulation that transforms feature rendering into matrix-oriented computation. Our framework features three key designs: (i) a tile-based splatting pipeline that maps feature accumulation to warp-level matrix-multiply-accumulate execution on Tensor Cores; (ii) a warp-granularity Gaussian packing strategy that mitigates visibility irregularity and improves feature reuse; and (iii) a lazy channel-batched feature-fetching pipeline that reduces shared-memory pressure, maintains high warp occupancy, and overlaps memory access with matrix execution for high-dimensional features. On Mip-NeRF 360, LERF, and 3D-OVS, MatrixSplat achieves up to 3.8$\times$ speedup over 3DGS and 2$\times$ over TC-GS while maintaining rendering quality and semantic accuracy, and further reduces render-kernel time by up to 36.7$\times$ for high-dimensional features. Integrated into existing fast renderers, it provides an additional 1.69-2.84$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [36] [CineMME: From Dialogue Grounding to Narrative Reasoning in Multimodal Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15161)
*Jin Liu,Dawei Du,Yexiang Liu,Sijie Zhu,Fan Chen,Junxian Duan,Huaibo Huang,Ran He,Longyin Wen,Zhenfang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CineMME：约1600专家人时标注的叙事视频理解基准，含975短片、对话定位/说话人脸框（Grounding）与五维多选题+开放题（Reasoning）两条轨道；评测20个MLLM与9个全模态模型，揭示"谁-说什么-何时"的解耦现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基准（VideoQA、音视频推理、时序定位）各自评测孤立能力，很少检验同一片段内"对话→说话人绑定→情节推理"的证据链是否连贯，缺少诊断对话驱动情节理解的紧凑测试平台。

Method: 构建CineMME：975个精选叙事短片；Grounding轨道含13,554条带时间戳语音片段与33,083个说话人脸框，评测语音定位、转录、活跃说话人定位；Reasoning轨道含1,600道多选题与316道开放题，覆盖感知、音频、叙事、社会关系与镜头呈现。并做模态消融。

Result: Gemini-3 Pro时序定位tIoU 68.34%、WER 28.99%，但活跃说话人vIoU仅7.74%，同时有可提取答案题上MCQ准确率67.66%，呈显著who-what-when解耦；模态消融显示视觉并非普遍有益——对感知类问题提升，对对话中心推理反而可能有害。

Conclusion: CineMME提供紧凑诊断测试床，量化多模态视频模型在对话、说话人、视觉证据与叙事意义之间连接上的短板；属基准/诊断类工作，缺乏方法性创新。

Abstract: Narrative video understanding requires more than recognizing visible events: models must extract evidence from dialogue, bind it to speakers, and use it to infer narrative meaning. Existing video benchmarks evaluate aspects of this problem, including video question answering, audio-visual reasoning, and temporal grounding, but rarely test whether these abilities form a coherent dialogue-to-plot evidence chain within the same clips. We introduce \textbf{CineMME}, a human-verified benchmark designed to diagnose dialogue-grounded plot understanding, supported by approximately $1,600$ expert person-hours of annotation. CineMME contains $975$ curated short narrative video clips and two linked tracks: \textit{CineMME-Grounding}, with $13,554$ timestamped speech segments and $33,083$ speaker face boxes for temporal dialogue localization, transcription, and active-speaker grounding; and \textit{CineMME-Reasoning}, with $1,600$ multiple-choice and $316$ open-ended questions spanning perception, audio, narrative, social, and scene-presentation understanding. Our main evaluation compares $20$ MLLMs on reasoning and $9$ omni-modal models on grounding. The results reveal a pronounced who-what-when decoupling: Gemini-3 Pro localizes dialogue in time ($68.34\%$ tIoU) and transcribes it with a WER of $28.99\%$, yet achieves only $7.74\%$ vIoU for active-speaker grounding while achieving $67.66\%$ MCQ accuracy on questions with extractable answers. Modality ablations further show that vision is not uniformly beneficial: it improves performance on perception-heavy questions but can hurt dialogue-centric reasoning. CineMME provides a compact diagnostic testbed for evaluating how multimodal video models connect dialogue, speakers, visual evidence, and narrative meaning.

</details>


### [37] [Bridging Update Coordination and Flatness in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15165)
*Chongwei He,Shichen Huang,Jiayun Li,Deyuan Lin,Wangze Jiang,Shengjia Li,Xu Wang,Xiaofan Li,Zhi-Ri Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一联邦学习框架，将客户端更新协调（update coordination）与平坦性优化（flatness optimization）解耦后联合：客户端沿同一轨迹分别累积下降信号与平坦性修正，服务器以独立状态协调两组信号再融合为全局更新，从而支持多种协调策略与平坦性机制的灵活组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的数据异构会导致客户端更新冲突并损害泛化。更新协调可降低客户端间干扰，平坦性优化可降低对参数扰动的敏感性；但现有联合设计多为特定算法定制，难以灵活组合两类互补机制。

Method: 构造统一框架：在本地同一轨迹上分别累积下降信号与平坦性修正，显式定义"轨迹信号↔模型更新"的转换，使服务器能用两组独立状态分别协调后融合成全局更新；不同协调策略可与兼容的本地/全局引导平坦性机制搭配，且不改变推理架构。

Result: 实验中，所评估的多种组合在平均精度与最差客户端精度上总体优于单独使用更新协调策略，验证了两类互补优化机制联合的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为联邦学习提供了一种算法无关的模块化接口，使更新协调与平坦性优化可以自由替换与组合；未来可扩展到更多协调策略与平坦性机制的组合评估。

Abstract: Federated learning combines complementary knowledge across clients to alleviate local data limitations without sharing raw samples. However, data heterogeneity can cause conflicting updates and hinder generalization. Update coordination reduces interference among client updates, while flatness optimization reduces sensitivity to parameter perturbations to improve generalization. Existing joint designs largely target specific algorithms, limiting flexible combinations of these approaches. To address this problem, we propose a unified federated learning framework that bridges update coordination and flatness optimization. Clients separately accumulate descent signals and flatness corrections along the same local trajectory. The server coordinates both groups with independent states before fusing their outputs into a global update. Explicit conversion between trajectory signals and model updates enables different coordination strategies to work with compatible local or globally guided flatness mechanisms without changing the inference architecture. Experiments show that the evaluated combinations generally improve average and worst-client accuracy over update coordination alone, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework in combining these complementary optimization mechanisms.

</details>


### [38] [MAST: Medoid-Anchored Subspace Steering for Visual Grounding in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15168)
*Zhitao Chen,Zhenxin Cai,Jingyuan Li,Jin Sha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAST：以任务分组多中心聚类的medoid为锚点，提取低秩局部引导方向并在推理时多锚路由聚合，实现免训练、输入自适应的MLLM激活引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM激活引导以全局激活均值作引导向量，落在任务簇之间的空白区域，导致跨任务干扰与性能不稳定；作者将其归因于任务激活的多中心几何结构及局部簇内引导信号的低秩性。

Method: 按任务分组样本，组内多中心聚类并每簇选一个代表medoid；推理时查询路由到多个medoid，对每个匹配簇提取低秩局部引导方向并跨簇聚合得到最终引导向量；MAST-PR进一步用轻量任务感知probe对局部方向族重加权。全程免训练。

Result: 声称缓解跨任务干扰，MAST-PR相对MAST平均再提升+8.09点（摘要未给出基准、模型与绝对指标）。

Conclusion: 激活几何的多中心/低秩结构可解释全局均值引导的失效，基于medoid锚定与多锚路由的局部子空间聚合是更稳健的免训练引导范式。

Abstract: Existing activation steering methods for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) use the global activation mean as the steering vector, which tends to cause cross-task interference and unstable performance. We trace this failure to the multi-centered geometry of task activations. The global mean lands in the empty region between clusters and is faithful to no single task. Moreover, the steering signal in each task-pure local cluster is low-rank, and different query tasks benefit from emphasizing different families of local-basis directions under the shared routing protocol. To address this, we propose MAST (Medoid-Anchored Subspace Steering), a training-free, input-adaptive activation steering framework. MAST groups samples by task, performs multi-center clustering within each group, and selects one representative medoid per cluster. At inference time, each query is routed to multiple representative medoids. For each matched cluster, a low-rank local steering direction is extracted and aggregated across clusters to produce the final steering vector. Building on MAST, MAST-PR (Probe-Reweighted) uses a lightweight task-aware probe to reweight task-specialized local-direction families, yielding a further average improvement of +8.09 points over MAST.

</details>


### [39] [GingivitisSeg: A Public Dataset and Comparative Benchmark for Gingivitis Segmentation in Intraoral Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15184)
*Zeynep Ozdemir,Zehra Hasgul,Irem Ulku,Omer Ozgur Tanriover,Fatma Karacaoglu,Murat Akkaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出牙龈炎分割公开基准 GingivitisSeg（961 张口内照片、像素级标注、672/144/145 划分），系统对比 7 种分割模型-骨干配置及损失函数消融。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口内照片牙龈炎分割可辅助计算机辅助牙周评估，但面临前景-背景类极不平衡、低对比度边界、光照与解剖变异等困难，且缺乏标准化公开基准。

Method: 构建 961 张口内图像的像素级牙龈炎标注数据集与固定划分；在统一划分、输入分辨率和评价指标下比较 7 种语义分割模型-骨干组合；进行类别加权交叉熵、Dice 损失与辅助监督的受控消融实验。

Result: UPerNet+ResNet50 取得最高 mIoU 76.49% 和牙龈炎 IoU 54.91%，但与 DeepLabV3+/ResNet50、SegFormer/MiT-B2 的差异经 Holm 校正后不显著；优化组件组合后在 UPerNet 配置下有效提升；性能与炎症区域大小相关，且强模糊下下降。

Conclusion: 该工作提供标准化的牙周影像分割基准与分析框架，但方法论创新有限；模型差异不显著，任务性能受目标尺寸与成像质量制约，偏医疗器械类基准研究。

Abstract: Gingivitis segmentation from intraoral photographs can support computer-assisted periodontal assessment, but pixel-level delineation remains challenging due to severe foreground-background class imbalance, low-contrast boundaries, and variations in illumination and anatomy. We introduce GingivitisSeg, a public benchmark comprising 961 intraoral images with pixel-level annotations of gingival inflammation and predefined training (672), validation (144), and test (145) partitions. Seven semantic segmentation model-backbone configurations were compared using standardized partitions, input resolution, and evaluation metrics. Controlled ablation experiments examined class-weighted cross-entropy loss, Dice loss, and auxiliary supervision. UPerNet with ResNet50 achieved the highest mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 76.49% and gingivitis IoU of 54.91%. However, differences from DeepLabV3+ with ResNet50 and SegFormer with MiT-B2 were not statistically significant after Holm correction. The evaluated optimization components improved performance when combined in the UPerNet-ResNet50 configuration. Further analyses showed that performance was associated with gingivitis region size and decreased under stronger image blur. The dataset, benchmark splits, and source code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [40] [OT-BLS: Optimal-Transport-based Backbone Layer Selection for Frozen DINOv3 in Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15187)
*Natsuo Okamoto,Kazuki Kobayashi,Yoshio Kato,Shuhei Tarashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OT-BLS：利用查询与支持集patch token特征间的最优传输对应关系，为无训练CD-FSS在DINOv3各层中自动选择最优骨干层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无训练跨域小样本分割依赖DINOv3等视觉基础模型，但最优层随support-query episode变化；现有层选择准则未显式检验预测的查询前景/背景与支持集标签的一致性。

Method: 先用成熟最优传输算法在查询-支持patch token特征间估计传输方案；再按每个候选查询掩码定义的前景-背景划分聚合传输质量，得到层选择分数，反映查询预测分区与支持集分区的对应一致性。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上，OT-BLS在16个数据集/shot设置中有12个优于末层基线；平均mIoU由55.72提升至57.62，较最佳现有层选择准则提升2.99点，速度快3.7–14.6倍。

Conclusion: 以最优传输对应性作为层选择代理信号，可在不更新参数的低成本前提下显著提升无训练CD-FSS性能与效率。

Abstract: Cross-domain few-shot segmentation (CD-FSS) aims to segment a target class in images from a previously unseen domain using only a few annotated support examples. Training-free CD-FSS methods, which typically leverage vision foundation models such as DINOv3, achieve competitive performance without parameter updates. However, the optimal DINOv3 layer for mask prediction varies across support--query episodes. Although several layer-selection criteria have been proposed, they do not explicitly evaluate whether the predicted query foreground and background labels agree with the corresponding support labels. To address this limitation, we propose a novel layer-selection criterion, Optimal-Transport-Based Backbone Layer Selection (OT-BLS). Our key insight is that an effective layer-selection criterion can be derived from the optimal-transport correspondence between query and support patch-token features. Based on this insight, we first estimate the transport plan using a well-established optimal transport algorithm and then compute a layer-selection score by aggregating the transported mass according to the foreground--background partition defined by each candidate query mask. The resulting score reflects how well the predicted foreground and background of the query correspond to those of the support. Experiments on eight benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of OT-BLS. It outperforms the conventional last-layer baseline in 12 of 16 dataset/shot settings. Furthermore, OT-BLS improves the average mIoU from 55.72 to 57.62 and achieves a 2.99-point improvement over the best-performing existing layer-selection criterion while running 3.7--14.6$\times$ faster.

</details>


### [41] [The Neighbour's Label: Opposite Subgroup Effects of Same-Dialogue Exposure in Next-Act Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15194)
*Ali Amini,Masoumeh Chapariniya,Rytis Maskeliunas,Robertas Damaševičius*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究基于窗口的下一动作评估中的标注暴露问题：重叠窗口与相似度检索会使测试对话的已标注样本泄漏进训练/示例，其中最近样本往往是目标的标注前驱动作，模型复制它会在"延续"与"切换"两个子群中产生相反得失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窗口化 next-act 评估本意是衡量跨对话泛化，但官方划分"干净"不代表无泄漏：重叠窗口和检索式 few-shot 示例都可能把同一测试对话的标签带进来，而聚合指标会掩盖方向相反的子群效应。

Method: 在 SGD、STAR、AMI 三语料上做受控注入实验：随机把目标的前驱标注行放入训练，对比词法分类器的延续/切换得分；再清除相邻行验证增益消失。另在 TF-IDF 检索式示例设定下测试两个 7B 指令模型，并在不重训模型的情况下做两项诊断对比（含排除测试对话）。

Result: 三语料上加入前驱行均提高延续得分、降低切换得分，清除相邻行后大部分增益消失；检索示例下 8 个模型-语料-样本组合全部呈现相同反向模式（部分单格置信区间含零，一次复现不完全），而自然权重准确率仅变动 -0.05 至 +0.04；SGD 上排除测试对话未显著降低准确率。

Conclusion: 对话行为预测的窗口化评估对检索管线造成的标注暴露高度敏感，聚合准确率具有误导性；建议以子群（延续/切换）分解报告并控制检索来源。所有暴露均为人为诱导，未重评已发表系统。

Abstract: Window-based next-act evaluation often aims to measure generalisation across dialogues, yet overlapping windows and similarity retrieval can expose labelled examples from the test dialogue. The nearest such example is the target's labelled predecessor, whose act is correct when the target continues it and wrong when the target changes act; a model that copies it can gain and lose in two subgroups that one aggregate averages. We induce this exposure under controlled designs on SGD, STAR and AMI, whose official splits are clean. Randomly including the predecessor's row in training raises a lexical classifier's continuation scores and lowers its transition scores in all three corpora, and purging the adjacent rows removes most of the gain. With TF-IDF-retrieved demonstrations, two 7B instruction-tuned models show the same opposite pattern in all eight model-corpus-sample arms (some single-cell intervals include zero; one replication is partial), while natural-weight accuracy changes by only -0.05 to +0.04. Exposure frequency depends on the retrieval pipeline. We evaluate two diagnostic comparisons without model retraining and find that excluding the test dialogue does not detectably reduce accuracy on SGD. All exposure is induced; no published system was re-evaluated.

</details>


### [42] [A General Concept-based Decomposition for Vision-Language Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15195)
*Simone Alberto Peirone,Ortal Senouf,Francesca Pistilli,Giuseppe Averta,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRACE 在特征空间层面学习概念级结构，通过保持样本间几何关系的新训练目标，实现与人类感知一致且跨任务/域忠实的视觉概念分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通过实例级优化将视觉嵌入分解为概念贡献，本质上是局部的，无法刻画模型的全局语义结构，因而难以支撑跨输入、跨任务、跨域的组合泛化；同时 VLM 的多模态对齐是否与人类描述方式一致尚不明确。

Method: 提出 GRACE：在特征空间层面学习基于概念的结构，输出语义级与通用概念分解；设计新的训练目标，在概念空间中保持不同样本之间的几何关系，从而刻画概念如何组合与相互关联，避免逐实例的局部优化。

Result: 在多个图像与视频基准上验证：概念分解与人类感知对齐，并在不同任务与域上取得忠实的分解结果；在极小额外开销下提升现有 VLM 的可解释性，同时保留其大部分原始性能。

Conclusion: 概念分解应在特征空间全局建模而非实例局部优化；GRACE 以轻量方式为现有 VLM 提供可组合、可迁移的概念级可解释性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models are designed to capture the compositional structure of the world through multi-modal alignment; however, it remains unclear whether their representations truly align with the way a human would naturally describe the same visual input. Prior works attempt to decompose visual embeddings into their concept-level contributions, but their instance-level optimization is inherently local and does not capture the model's global semantic structure, which is essential for compositional generalization across different inputs, tasks, and domains. We introduce GRACE, a method that learns this concept-based structure at the feature space level, producing semantic and general concept decompositions. To achieve this, we propose a novel training objective that preserves the geometric relations of different samples in the concept space, enabling a better understanding of how concepts compose and relate to each other. We validate GRACE on several image and video benchmarks, showing that its concept decomposition is aligned with human perception and achieves faithful decompositions across different tasks and domains. With minimal overhead, GRACE makes existing Vision-Language Models more interpretable, while preserving most of their original performance.

</details>


### [43] [Where is My Mug? Training-Free Framework for Personalized Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15216)
*Quang Qui-Vinh Nguyen,Hung Nguyen,Rang Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 指出个性化图像分割(PIS)现有方法的高分源于单实例基准，真实多实例场景下失效是召回瓶颈而非掩码质量问题；提出免训练模块化管线（类无关检测器出高召回proposal + BLIP-2特征匹配 + top-K Sinkhorn局部匹配 + SAM细化），并发布多实例基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PIS方法在单实例基准上表现强劲，但在含多个同类相似实例的真实场景中，点提示类方法常无法把正确实例作为提示暴露出来，导致性能崩溃。作者定位这是召回瓶颈，而非分割掩码质量问题，因此需要高召回候选生成与鲁棒匹配。

Method: 免训练模块化系统：1) 用现成类无关检测器生成高召回对象proposal；2) 用基础模型特征（如BLIP-2）将每个proposal与参考图像匹配；3) 用SAM细化选中框。匹配上提出top-K Sinkhorn——一种patch级距离，仅用置信度最高的匹配patch给候选打分；通过裁剪Sinkhorn对偶势，以低于部分最优传输100倍以上的代价获得其鲁棒性，可嵌入逐proposal候选循环。另构建多实例真实基准数据集。

Result: 在新基准及PerSeg、PerMIS、DAVIS上，较PerSAM、P²SAM、PDM提升最高约48 mIoU，运行时间相当且参数量更少。

Conclusion: 多实例场景下的PIS瓶颈在于实例召回而非掩码质量；免训练的“检测—匹配—细化”管线配合top-K Sinkhorn可显著超越现有方法，成为可部署的强基线。

Abstract: \textbf{Personalized Image Segmentation (PIS)} aims to segment the specific object in a target image that matches a user-provided reference. Unlike category-based segmentation or few-shot segmentation, PIS must discriminate between multiple visually similar instances, a requirement central to deployed applications such as augmented reality, personalized content editing, robotics manipulation, inventory tracking, and fine-grained visual retrieval. We show that the strong numbers reported by existing PIS methods are largely an artifact of single-instance benchmarks: when evaluated on realistic scenes with several same-category look-alikes, prior point-prompt methods collapse, because the correct instance is frequently never surfaced as a prompt. We trace this to a recall bottleneck rather than a mask-quality one, and address it with a training-free, modular system. The pipeline first generates high-recall object proposals using an off-the-shelf class-agnostic detector, then matches each proposal to the reference using foundation-model features (e.g., BLIP-2), and finally refines the selected box with SAM. For robust matching we propose \textbf{top-$K$ Sinkhorn}, a patch-level distance that scores each candidate only on its most confidently matched patches; trimming the Sinkhorn dual potentials recovers the robustness of partial optimal transport at more than $100\times$ lower cost, keeping it inside the per-proposal candidate loop. To evaluate this regime, we introduce \textbf{\datasetname}, a realistic multi-instance benchmark. Across \datasetname, PerSeg, PerMIS, and DAVIS, our system surpasses PerSAM, $\mathrm{P}^2\mathrm{SAM}$, and PDM by up to $\sim\!48$ mIoU while matching their runtime and using fewer parameters, establishing a strong, deployable training-free baseline for real-world personalized segmentation.

</details>


### [44] [Driving World Models Move the Camera, Not the Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15260)
*Yuqiao Feng,Xiaoyu Ao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Plan-conditioned driving world models barely move the scene when plans change; the authors diagnose the plan channel and fix it training-free by feeding the plan as geometry.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing control metrics only check whether the generated camera follows the planned trajectory, not whether the scene responds as reality would. Since recorded logs implicitly contain counterfactual outcomes (retimed poses, stereo-shifted views), one can measure and fix the scene-response gap.

Method: Build pose-matched references by retiming logs (speed counterfactuals) and using stereo lateral shifts; measure "scene response" (fraction of true depth/distance change reproduced) of released world models. Intervene inside the generator to localize the failure to plan tokens. Propose ReTime-Compose: training-free, warp frames of a logged-speed rollout to planned cameras as geometry conditioning, letting the frozen model inpaint only uncovered regions.

Result: Scene response of released models reaches only ~1/3 of real video; trajectory scores cannot distinguish executed vs. ignored speed commands; forecast geometry is inherited from context frames. ReTime-Compose makes frozen generators follow a 0.54 m lateral shift nearly like the real scene on KITTI, nuScenes, Argoverse 2, and respond better than resampling to slow-down (3 generators) and speed-up (2 generators) plans.

Conclusion: Plan conditioning in driving world models is bottlenecked by geometry carried in context frames, not plan tokens; delivering the plan as explicit geometry yields faithful counterfactual rollouts without retraining, and motivates scene-response-based evaluation.

Abstract: Plan-conditioned driving world models are used to compare candidate plans, so their forecast should change with the plan as the real scene would, yet widely used control metrics check only whether the camera follows the command. Recorded drives already contain real outcomes of plans that were never driven: retiming a log gives pose-matched images and LiDAR for other speeds, and a stereo rig records a laterally shifted view. Against these references, released world models move their camera but barely move the scene: their scene response, the share of the real change in distance that the forecast reproduces, reaches at most about a third of real video's, and the trajectory score cannot tell an executed speed command from an ignored one. Interventions inside the generator trace the failure to the plan's channel. The forecast geometry follows the context frames; once they carry the planned geometry, plan tokens add little. ReTime-Compose therefore delivers the plan as geometry, without training. It transports the frames of one logged-speed rollout to the planned cameras, and the frozen model completes only what no frame covers. Frozen generators then follow a 0.54 m lateral shift nearly as the real scene does on KITTI, nuScenes and Argoverse 2, and respond more than re-sampling to slow-down plans in three generators and to speed-up plans in two.

</details>


### [45] [PCLSegNet: Anatomy-Aware Deep Learning Framework for MRI-based Pancreatic Cyst Segmentation and Size Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15278)
*Hanyi Zhang,Tongyu Wang,Manuel Debic,Markus Wennmann,Johannes Haubold,Tamara Schikowski,Wilfried Reichardt,Fabian Bamberg,Christopher L. Schlett,Steffen Ringhof,Thoralf Niendorf,Tobias Pischon,Michael Leitzmann,Carsten Oliver Schmidt,Mark Oliver Wielpütz,Thomas Kroencke,Josua A. Decker,Stefan N. Willich,Thomas Keil,Rafael Mikolajczyk,Miriam Klauß,Hans-Ulrich Kauczor,Philipp Mayer,Bettina K. Budai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解剖感知两阶段深度学习框架，用于腹部MRI上胰腺囊性病变(PCL)自动分割：先用公开胰腺分割模型定位胰腺，再用引入解剖先验的PCLSegNet分割胰腺与PCL，并用类别特定误差加权(CSEW)损失缓解类别不平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胰腺囊性病变（尤其MCN与IPMN）存在显著恶变风险，需早期发现与随访；现有AI研究多基于CT，而MRI软组织对比度更优、更适合PCL评估，但缺乏有效的自动化分割方案，且存在严重类别不平衡与小目标问题。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 复用公开胰腺分割模型做胰腺定位（先验裁剪）；2) 提出anatomy-aware PCLSegNet，将解剖先验作为引导信号同时分割胰腺与PCL；设计Class-Specific Error-Weighted (CSEW)损失以应对前景/背景及类别间严重不平衡；后处理阶段施加解剖约束以剔除假阳性。

Result: 胰腺分割Dice显著高于TotalSegmentator等多器官分割模型；PCL分割的归一化表面Dice(NSD)与召回率优于nnU-Net等SOTA模型，假阴性率明显降低，支撑PCL的无创影像学自动评估与早期筛查。

Conclusion: 解剖先验引导的两阶段级联+类别感知损失，是提升小病灶MRI分割召回的有效范式；但方法为医学影像特定领域工作，缺乏公开代码与外部多中心验证，通用方法学价值有限。

Abstract: Early detection and follow-up of pancreatic cystic lesions (PCLs) are crucial, as some of their subtypes, such as mucinous cystic neoplasms (MCNs) and intraductal papillary mucinous neoplasms, carry a significant risk of malignant transformation. Although most AI studies investigating PCLs rely on computed tomography (CT) scans, magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) offers superior soft tissue contrast, making it highly suitable for PCLs assessment. In this work, we propose an anatomy-aware two-stage deep learning framework for automated PCLs segmentation on abdominal MRI scans. First, a publicly available pancreas segmentation model is used to localize the pancreas. Then, we introduce the anatomyaware PCLSegNet, which incorporates anatomical priors as guidance for pancreas and PCLs segmentation. To address the class imbalance problem, we develop a Class-Specific ErrorWeighted (CSEW) loss function. Postprocessing is applied through anatomical this http URL proposed method outperforms multi-organ segmentation models such as TotalSegmentator, achieving substantially higher Dice scores for pancreas segmentation. Moreover, it also achieves a superior normalized surface Dice (NSD) and recall for PCLs segmentationcompared to other state-of-the-art models like nnU-Net, while also notably reducing the false negative rate, facilitating the automatic early detection and the noninvasive imaging-based assessment of PCLs.

</details>


### [46] [Track4D: Representing Dense 3D Tracking for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15287)
*Yushi Lan,Zeren Jiang,Kelvin Li,Xingang Pan,Chuanxia Zheng,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将预训练视频扩散模型(Wan+LoRA)适配为稠密3D点跟踪器，系统比较两种"视频化"3D轨迹表示：RTV(逐像素3D偏移，可直接解码轨迹)与NCMV(归一化坐标图，需2D跟踪器后处理)，并验证回归读出可进一步提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密3D点跟踪训练数据稀缺，而视频扩散模型蕴含丰富的时空先验；但3D轨迹作为"类视频信号"可能偏离自然视频分布，因此需要厘清何种视频表示更利于VDM学习与迁移。

Method: 以冻结的Wan视频扩散骨干加LoRA微调；输入点图(point map)来自现成3D重建器作为几何支架；对比两种表示——RTV(相对查询帧的逐像素3D偏移视频)与NCMV(时间不变的规范化坐标图)；仅用单一动态3D跟踪数据集训练；另设绕过视频VAE的回归读出并引入额外数据。

Result: RTV表示跟踪更精确，NCMV则证明VDM可生成稠密对应；Track4D在四个真实与合成基准上取得有竞争力的零样本3D跟踪性能；回归读出进一步带来改进。

Conclusion: VDM先验可有效迁移至稠密3D跟踪；表示设计是决定迁移效果的关键因素，回归式读出与几何条件可放大收益，为4D重建方向的后续研究提供分析性基线。

Abstract: We study how to repurpose video diffusion models (VDMs), pretrained on millions of videos, for dense 3D point tracking, where training data is scarce. Doing so is non-trivial, as it requires representing 3D tracks as video-like signals that may lie outside the natural-video distribution on which the VDM was trained. We thus systematically assess the effectiveness of two such video representations. The first, Residual 3D Tracking Video (RTV), encodes per-pixel 3D offsets relative to a query frame and allows recovery of 3D trajectories directly from the generated video. The second, Normalized Coordinate Map Video (NCMV), labels each pixel with a canonical coordinate invariant through time. This is closer to a natural video and easier for the VDM to learn, but requires additional post-processing with an off-the-shelf 2D tracker to recover 3D tracks. RTV tracks more accurately, while NCMV shows that a VDM can generate dense correspondence as a video. We instantiate both representations with LoRA adaptation of a frozen Wan backbone, conditioned on point maps from an off-the-shelf 3D reconstructor as a geometric scaffold. Trained with a single dynamic 3D tracking dataset, Track4D achieves competitive zero-shot tracking on four real and synthetic benchmarks. A regression readout that bypasses the video VAE and uses additional training data brings further improvement, demonstrating the potential of this direction for dense 3D tracking. We hope our analysis will inspire future work that leverages VDM priors for dense 3D tracking and 4D reconstruction. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [47] [Beyond the Explicit: Benchmarking and Detecting Implicit Harm in Short Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15306)
*Chenyang Li,Qifan Yu,Saihui Hou,Zhaofeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建7,110条细粒度标注的隐式有害短视频数据集DeepHarm-7K，并提出融合视觉、音频与跨模态推理的两轮推理检测框架DeepHarm-VL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频中隐式有害内容通过叙事语境、情绪框架与跨模态线索呈现，现有工作聚焦暴力、仇恨等显式类别且缺乏隐式有害基准，导致检测能力不足。

Method: 按细粒度有害内容体系标注，经多阶段质控构建DeepHarm-7K；提出DeepHarm-VL框架，整合视觉、音频与跨模态推理，采用两轮推理策略：先识别表层证据，再推断潜在有害含义。

Result: 在多个开源视频-语言模型上相对强基线与SOTA方法取得一致提升，兼顾显式与隐式有害短视频检测。

Conclusion: 提供了隐式有害短视频的专用基准与可渐进式推理的多模态检测范式，推进细粒度内容安全分析。

Abstract: Short videos have become a dominant online medium, where harmful content is increasingly conveyed in implicit and fragmented forms. Prior work largely focuses on explicit categories, such as violence and hate speech, and often fails to detect videos that subtly promote misleading values through narrative context, emotional framing, or cross-modal cues, partly due to the lack of dedicated benchmarks for implicit harm. To bridge this gap, we construct DeepHarm-7K, a large-scale harmful short-video dataset comprising 7,110 samples annotated according to a fine-grained harmful-content taxonomy. The dataset systematically covers implicitly harmful videos across diverse real-world scenarios and is constructed through a rigorous multi-stage quality-control process. Building on this dataset, we propose DeepHarm-VL, a multimodal detection framework that integrates visual, audio, and cross-modal reasoning. The framework employs a two-round reasoning strategy to progressively identify surface-level evidence and infer latent harmful implications. Experimental results show consistent improvements over strong baselines and state-of-the-art methods across multiple open-source video-language models, demonstrating its effectiveness in detecting both explicit and implicit harmful short videos.

</details>


### [48] [Arbor: When Labels Are Not Coarsened at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15311)
*Faisal Mohammad,Mohamed Younis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Arbor models the label-coarsening process: in each superclass containing a never-finely-labelled "novel" class, it adds a virtual novel class and a logistic gate splitting the superclass probability mass between novel and known classes, trained via coarsening likelihood; at population optimum the gate equals the Elkan-Noto PU correction, but training it jointly beats post-hoc correction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Merging datasets labelled at different taxonomy levels yields images with only coarse labels and classes with no fine labels at all. Standard coarse-label scoring (summing probabilities under the superclass) is exact only if all classes in a superclass are equally likely to receive a coarse label; a never-finely-labelled class violates this and its images get misassigned to a known sibling.

Method: Add a virtual novel class per superclass containing a novel class; a logistic gate divides the superclass probability between it and known classes; train the gate with the coarsening likelihood of observed labels. Theoretical proof links the gate's population optimum to the Elkan-Noto positive-unlabelled correction, and the correction is trained directly into the classifier rather than applied afterwards.

Result: On Cars, CUB, Aircraft with 90% coarse labels, 76-85% of novel-class images are assigned to the correct superclass, beating hierarchical classifiers, hierarchical novelty detection and post-hoc Elkan-Noto; top-1 leaf accuracy on known-class images is within 1.6 points of the best baseline. On iNaturalist-2019, novel-class accuracy rises from 46% (uniform target, strongest baseline) to 68%.

Conclusion: Explicitly modelling label coarsening and training the derived PU-style correction inside the classifier improves both hierarchical novelty placement and fine-grained accuracy, generalizing from small benchmarks to a large-scale one.

Abstract: Merging datasets labelled at different levels of a taxonomy leaves some images with only a coarse label, and some classes with no fine-grained label at all. Standard training scores a coarse label by the total probability of the classes under it. Coarse labels name superclasses, and the standard objective is exact only when every class within a superclass is equally likely to receive a coarse label. We call a class novel if it is never labelled finely, and known otherwise. A novel class violates the assumption, and the classifier assigns its images to a known sibling. We propose Arbor, which models how labels become coarse. In each superclass with a novel class, Arbor adds a virtual novel class, and a logistic gate divides the superclass's probability between it and the known classes. The gate is trained with the likelihood of the observed labels under this model, the coarsening likelihood. We prove that at its population optimum the gate equals the Elkan-Noto correction of positive-unlabelled learning, and show that training the correction into the classifier improves on applying it after training. Across Cars, CUB and Aircraft with 90% coarse labels, Arbor correctly assigns 76-85% of novel-class images to their superclass, more than hierarchical classifiers, hierarchical novelty detection and the Elkan-Noto correction. Its top-1 accuracy over the leaf classes on known-class images is within 1.6 points of the best baseline, although its final predictions return the superclass more often. On the more challenging iNaturalist-2019 benchmark, Arbor improves novel-class accuracy from 46% (uniform target, the strongest baseline) to 68%.

</details>


### [49] [AcrossPlace: Do Vision--Language Models Recognize Places Beyond Their Source Views? A Comprehensive Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15314)
*Zhenyu Li,Tianyi Shang,Shuhan Dong,Ruotong Xu,Qingyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AcrossPlace：基于 GSV-Cities 构建的溯源感知（provenance-aware）双向语言-视觉地点识别基准（56,726 地点 / 23 城市 / 15.7 万描述 / 11.3 万目标图像），量化了"观察迁移"与实际识别能力之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导地点识别的聚合检索分数混杂了观察迁移、图库竞争与语言证据，无法反映真实识别能力；且描述与图像常来自同一观察，缺乏跨观察的严格评测。

Method: 构建基准：每段描述保留源图像溯源信息，检索在不相交的目标观察上进行；T2I 与 I2T 共享统一的地点身份相关性准则（允许多个有效匹配）；设 P0（已见城市内未见地点）与 P1（留出城市泛化）两种地理协议；设计配对观察比较、受控图库评测、固定查询的规模缩放实验及盲审标注审计。

Result: 跨三个训练种子，地点监督 SigLIP2 的 T2I Recall@1 在 P0 下由源图像 19.69% 降至独立目标 12.31%；固定 P1 查询时，图库从 500 扩至 8,425 地点使 Recall@1 由 14.67% 降至 2.87%；240 条描述审计显示自动接受与事实证据存在偏差。

Conclusion: AcrossPlace 提供了可复现的评测标准，用于衡量跨视觉观察与跨地理环境的可迁移地点身份识别能力，并揭示了当前模型的显著退化。

Abstract: Language-guided place recognition requires recovering geographic identity across visual observations beyond the image from which a description originates. However, aggregate retrieval scores often entangle observation transfer, gallery competition, and linguistic evidence, obscuring the underlying recognition capability. We introduce AcrossPlace, a provenance-aware benchmark establishing a systematic evaluation standard for bidirectional language--vision place recognition. Built upon GSV-Cities, AcrossPlace comprises 56,726 places across 23 cities, 156,800 automatically generated, rule-filtered descriptions, and 113,404 target images. Each description retains its source-image provenance, while retrieval operates on disjoint target observations. Text-to-image (T2I) and image-to-text (I2T) retrieval share a unified place-identity relevance criterion supporting multiple valid matches. Two geographic protocols evaluate unseen places within represented cities (P0) and generalization to held-out cities (P1). Paired observation comparisons, gallery-controlled evaluations, fixed-query scaling, and blinded annotation auditing systematically characterize retrieval performance. Across three training seeds, place-supervised SigLIP2 T2I Recall@1 decreases from 19.69% on source images to 12.31% on independent targets under P0. For fixed P1 queries, expanding the gallery from 500 to 8,425 places reduces Recall@1 from 14.67% to 2.87%. A 240-description audit further reveals discrepancies between automated acceptance and factual evidence. These findings establish AcrossPlace as a reproducible evaluation standard for assessing transferable place identity across visual observations and geographic environments.

</details>


### [50] [CaseScope: A Multi-Field Benchmark for Case-Level Pathology Diagnosis with Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15325)
*Wenchuan Zhang,Chengyang Zhang,Fengling Li,Ting Yin,Chen Shu,Jie Chen,Shuwan Zhang,Xiaoyan Wu,Zhang Zhang,Yuhao Yi,Jiancheng Lv,Hong Bu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CaseScope是一个面向病理多模态大模型的多视野诊断基准，包含439个真实驻培病例（CaseScope-Teaching）与800个来自88本已发表图集的病例（CaseScope-Atlas），覆盖18个器官系统、843个实体、4,679道题、六类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理诊断本质上是对同一病灶多个视野、多个倍数的联合推理，而现有多模态基准大多只给单张图或依赖全切片流程，少数多视野基准又仅从单一公开队列的癌症切片中抽取嵌套区域，缺乏跨来源、去标识、本体对齐的评测。

Method: 构建双来源数据集：Teaching集从住院医师培训图库重建并经自动预审+病理学家复核，按WHO本体谐和处理；Atlas集从88本图集（387个为单一疾病的系列图）中由两个LLM按图注标注并要求一致。设置六类任务（开放式诊断、十选一诊断并在循环评估下用本体引导的困难负例、形态学征象验证与定位），评测28个开源与4个闭源MLLM。

Result: 诊断高度不稳定：最佳开源与闭源模型在Teaching集上十种选项轮换全部正确的比例仅17.9%与61.7%，错误答案偏好经典相似病变；开源模型对新增视野利用不足（病理部位文本更关键），而Atlas集≥3视野时视野数与部位贡献相当；多数开源模型征象验证接近随机（中位62.0%，最佳81.5%），图文增益仅+3.4分，闭源模型达83.2–93.2%；两个来源间的模型排序基本一致。

Conclusion: CaseScope揭示了现有多模态模型在跨视野证据整合、选项顺序鲁棒性与形态学征象验证上的系统性缺陷，为病理MLLM提供可复现的评测协议与开源评测代码。

Abstract: Pathologists rarely diagnose from a single image: they scan a section at low power for architecture, zoom into selected fields for cytology, and integrate the evidence across views and scales. Pathology benchmarks for multimodal large language models (MLLMs), however, mostly ask about one image or require whole-slide pipelines, and the few that show several fields draw nested regions from cancer slides of one public cohort. We introduce CaseScope, a benchmark of diagnosis from several curated fields of one lesion: CaseScope-Teaching, 436 cases from a residency-training image library, reconstructed, de-identified, harmonised against a WHO-based ontology and re-examined by a pathologist wherever an automated pre-review raised a doubt, and CaseScope-Atlas, 800 cases from 88 published atlases (387 of them figure series of one disease), labelled from the captions by two LLMs that must agree. They cover 843 entities across 18 organ systems and pose 4,679 items in six tasks, from open-ended and ten-way diagnosis with ontology-guided hard negatives under circular evaluation to the verification and location of morphological findings. Across twenty-eight open and four proprietary MLLMs, diagnoses are unstable -- the best open and proprietary models are correct under all ten option rotations on 17.9% and 61.7% of the teaching cases -- and wrong answers favour the classic look-alike; open models make little use of the additional fields on teaching cases, where the one-line specimen site matters more, although on atlas cases with three or more fields the fields count about as much as the site; and most open models verify findings barely above chance (median 62.0% vs. 50%, best 81.5%), gaining a median +3.4 points from images; proprietary models reach 83.2-93.2%. Rankings broadly replicate between the sources. The evaluation code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [51] [Vision-Grounded Semantic Learning and Alignment for Scalable Multilingual Text-Based Person Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15328)
*Xinyu Zhou,Fan Li,Shasha Chen,Min Cao,Ahtamjan Ahmat,Qiaoming Zhu,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 1000 万图文对四语种行人检索数据集 MultiPedes 与 ViSeLA 框架（语义路由 MoE + 语义探针直接跨模态对齐），消除英文枢轴偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言文本行人检索（TBPR）此前只关注英文；唯一的多语言尝试缺乏大规模多语言预训练数据，且采用共享编码器并依赖英文枢轴做跨模态对齐，存在语言同质化假设与英文中心偏置。

Method: 构建 MultiPedes：首个大规模行人中心多语言预训练数据集，1000万图文对、四语种，采用多语言质量感知数据构建流水线；提出 ViSeLA 框架，含（1）语义感知多语言学习模块，将 MoE 扩展为按语言不变语义路由到专用专家；（2）视觉接地的跨模态对齐模块，用语义探针捕获跨模态细粒度语义，实现无需枢轴语言的直接对齐。

Result: 在多语言及纯英文 TBPR 数据集上实验验证 ViSeLA 的有效性，数据集与代码已开源。

Conclusion: 多语言 TBPR 需要专用数据与去英文枢轴的对齐架构；MoE 式语言不变语义路由+探针式跨模态对齐是可复用的设计范式。

Abstract: Multilingual Text-Based Person Retrieval (TBPR) aims to retrieve individuals with texts in multiple languages. Despite its practical importance, it has been overlooked by existing research, which mainly focuses on English-only retrieval. The only prior attempt at multilingual TBPR suffers from (1) the absence of a large-scale, multilingual pretraining dataset and (2) a suboptimal model architecture for multilingual text encoding and cross-modal alignment, which employs a shared encoder that overlooks linguistic heterogeneity and relies on English as a pivot for cross-modal alignment, introducing English-centric bias. For this, we contribute MultiPedes, the first large-scale, pedestrian-centric multilingual pretraining dataset. It contains 10 million image-text pairs across four languages, constructed by a multilingual quality-aware data construction pipeline. We also propose ViSeLA (Vision-grounded Semantic Learning and Alignment), a novel framework featuring: (1) a semantic-aware multilingual learning module that extends the mixture-of-experts architecture by routing language-invariant semantics to specialized experts for improved multilingual feature learning; and (2) a vision-grounded cross-modal alignment module that employs a semantic probe to capture fine-grained semantics across modalities, enabling direct cross-modal alignment without relying on a pivot language. Extensive experiments on both multilingual and English-only TBPR datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of ViSeLA. The dataset and code are available at this https URL

</details>


### [52] [CARE-Drive: Coupled Alternating Refinement for Ego-trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15360)
*Xiao Ma,Yijun Chen,Zhiquan Hu,Jicheng Zhao,Yuming Li,Chuang Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARE-Drive 将端到端自动驾驶轨迹精修分解为「路点残差修正」与「以位置为条件的航向生成」两个物理专门化专家，冻结骨干后交替进行监督初始化与强化微调，在 NAVSIM v1 上取得 88.8 PDMS（较同骨干未精修基线提升 2.4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 自动驾驶工作（候选精修、残差去噪、token 校正）通常对整条轨迹做统一精修，忽视位置与航向等不同物理量的独立特性及其耦合关系，建模不够直观且难以针对性优化。

Method: 骨干通过模仿学习与未来 token 辅助任务输出预测表征与基础轨迹并保持冻结；位置专家预测有界度量空间的路点残差，航向专家在修正后路径条件下由相邻角度增量重建车辆朝向；两专家通过监督初始化与强化微调交替优化，实现「物理专门化 + 条件耦合」的精修框架。

Result: NAVSIM v1 上 PDMS 达 88.8，相较同骨干未精修基础轨迹提升 2.4 点；消融实验验证了专门化专家与分阶段训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 将轨迹精修按物理量拆分并辅以条件耦合与交替优化，是提升 VLA 端到端驾驶轨迹质量的有效且简洁途径；但评测限于 NAVSIM 开环基准，缺少闭环或实车验证。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models bring multimodal understanding and world knowledge to end-to-end autonomous driving. To further improve the quality of predicted trajectories, existing studies introduce candidate refinement, residual denoising, and token correction. However, these methods typically focus on unified refinement of the entire trajectory, paying limited attention to the distinct physical quantities and their interactions. We further investigate physical specialization and coordination in trajectory refinement, centering on position and heading for simpler and more intuitive correction modeling. To this end, we propose CARE-Drive (Coupled Alternating Refinement for Ego-trajectories), which decomposes trajectory refinement into waypoint residual correction and position-conditioned heading generation. Through imitation learning and a future-token auxiliary task, the backbone provides predictive representations and a base trajectory. The position expert predicts bounded metric waypoint residuals, while the heading expert reconstructs vehicle orientation from adjacent angular increments conditioned on the corrected path. With the backbone frozen, the two experts are optimized alternately through supervised initialization and reinforcement fine-tuning. This design simplifies refinement through clear physical specialization while preserving conditional coupling during prediction and optimization. On NAVSIM v1, CARE-Drive achieves 88.8 PDMS, improving by 2.4 points over the unrefined base trajectories generated by the same backbone. Ablation studies further support the effectiveness of specialized experts and staged training.

</details>


### [53] [ChartGalaxy++: A Richly Annotated Dataset for Chart Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15418)
*Duan Li,Zheng Wang,Jiangning Zhu,Yuhui Yuan,Shixia Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChartGalaxy++ 是一个含 217,195 张信息图图表的数据集，以场景图（节点=视觉元素/语义组，边=层级与空间关系）为核心标注，并通过元素检测+渐进分组（真实图）与生成过程推导（合成图）构建场景图，支撑图到场景图预测、场景图增强的图表问答、生成中的场景图保真度评测三项应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息图图表数据集仅做元素级框与类别标注，缺乏层级与空间关系，难以刻画元素组织方式，也无法支撑结构化生成。

Method: 构建大规模场景图标注数据集：真实信息图通过检测并渐进分组视觉元素得到场景图；合成信息图直接从生成流程导出场景图；节点含视觉元素与语义组，边编码层级与空间关系。

Result: 在三个应用上验证数据集价值：图像到场景图预测、场景图增强的信息图问答、图像生成中场景图保持度评估。

Conclusion: 以场景图为中枢的标注范式可提升信息图结构理解与结构化生成能力，为图表理解与生成研究提供新基础。

Abstract: Infographic charts communicate data through cohesive compositions of visual elements such as charts, text, and images. Existing infographic chart datasets primarily annotate individual elements with bounding boxes and category labels. Such element-level annotations do not capture hierarchical and spatial relationships, limiting their use for understanding how elements are organized and related within an infographic and for generating well-organized designs. To address this limitation, we introduce ChartGalaxy++, a richly annotated dataset of 217,195 infographic charts. At the core of this dataset is a scene graph representation, in which nodes represent visual elements and semantic groups, and edges encode their hierarchical and spatial relationships. For real infographics, we construct scene graphs by detecting and progressively grouping visual elements; for synthetic ones, we derive them directly from the generation process. We demonstrate the utility of ChartGalaxy++ through three applications: 1) image-to-scene-graph prediction, 2) scene-graph-augmented infographic question answering, and 3) evaluation of scene graph preservation in image generation. Together, these applications highlight the value of ChartGalaxy++ for advancing chart understanding and generation.

</details>


### [54] [Learning Motion-Appearance Coupling Priors for Solving Video Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15420)
*Anselm Krainovic,Reinhard Heckel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用条件扩散模型学习"运动-外观残差"分布作为生成先验，作为即插即用正则项用于扩散式视频逆问题重建，优于光度一致性/噪声warping基线，并可迁移到心脏MRI重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"图像扩散先验+运动引导"的视频重建方法多用光度损失或warping约束强制时间一致性，隐含假设运动能解释帧间/隐变量间的变化（仅允许小偏差），在遮挡、光照变化、非刚性形变下失效。

Method: 将运动引起的表观残差建模为随机变量，用条件扩散模型学习其分布（以运动为条件）；在视频逆问题反演中把该先验作为plug-in正则项，与既有外观正则（图像扩散先验）联合，保持图像扩散模型优势。

Result: 在动态视频逆问题重建上，学习到的运动-外观耦合先验优于光度一致性基线与noise-warping基线；方法可迁移到心脏MRI重建任务。

Conclusion: 显式建模运动-外观耦合的生成先验是比硬性光度/warping约束更鲁棒的视频时间一致性正则；模块化设计可与现有外观先验组合，具跨域适用性。

Abstract: Reconstructing video from degraded measurements requires priors that capture both frame appearance and temporal dynamics. Many recent approaches combine pretrained image diffusion priors with motion guidance, sidestepping the costs of training large video models. Temporal consistency is often enforced via photometric losses or warping-based constraints, which implicitly assume that motion explains frame-to-frame or latent-to-latent changes up to small deviations, an assumption that fails under occlusions, lighting changes, and non-rigid deformation. In this paper, we propose to learn the coupling between motion and frame appearance as a generative prior. We learn the distribution of motion-induced appearance residuals with a conditional diffusion model and use it to regularize reconstruction in video inverse problems. Our motion-appearance prior acts as a plug-in regularizer for diffusion-based video reconstruction, preserving the benefits of image diffusion models while remaining compatible with existing appearance regularization techniques. Experiments on dynamic video inverse problems show that reconstruction with a learnt motion-appearance coupling prior outperforms photometric-consistency and noise-warping baselines, and that the approach also transfers to cardiac MRI reconstruction.

</details>


### [55] [IncR-3DSGG: Incremental Posed RGB Image Based 3D Scene Graph Generation via Privileged Depth Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15429)
*Mrunmai Phatak,Daniel Kienzle,Roman Küble,Jörg Hähner,Rainer Lienhart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IncR-3DSGG从带位姿RGB图像增量构建3D场景图，用可学习参数化关系预测器（PRP）估计物体间距离与相对角度，经位姿图优化融合多视角测量得到度量一致3D物体位置，推理阶段无需深度传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图生成方法在推理时依赖深度传感（点云重叠做跨视角实例关联，或几何线索计算物体关系），限制部署到仅有RGB相机的平台；希望仅用位姿RGB图像获得度量与关系都成立的3D场景图。

Method: 输入为RGB图像加现成视觉SLAM给出的相机外参（无深度、无重建几何）。训练阶段用3D几何监督训练PRP，从2D视觉特征与2D几何线索回归成对距离和相对角度；推理阶段把各视角学到的成对测量送入PGO（grisetti2010tutorial）融合，逐步恢复度量一致的3D物体位置，增量构建全局一致3D场景图。

Result: 在真实3RScan数据集、按3DSSG评估协议下，平均物体召回52.2、平均谓词召回30.8，而MonoSSG仅为27.0与17.6，且推理不使用深度或重建几何。

Conclusion: 表明物体关系与度量落地的3D物体表示可仅由位姿RGB图像预测，为无深度传感器平台（如低成本移动机器人、单目具身智能体）提供可行的3D场景图构建路径。

Abstract: Scene graphs provide a compact, structured representation of environments by capturing objects and their spatial or semantic relationships in a form suitable for embodied agents. Existing 3D scene graph generation methods often rely on depth sensing at inference, either to associate object instances across views via point cloud overlap or to compute inter-object relations from geometric cues, limiting deployment to platforms with depth sensors. We propose IncR-3DSGG, which builds 3D scene graphs incrementally from posed\footnote{RGB images with camera extrinsics estimated by an off-the-shelf visual SLAM system.} RGB images, eliminating the use of depth sensors at inference. Our method leverages a Parametric Relation Predictor (PRP) that learns to estimate pairwise distances and relative angles between objects from 2D visual features and 2D geometric cues, using 3D geometric supervision available only during training. These learned pairwise measurements are fused across views through Pose Graph Optimization (PGO)~\cite{grisetti2010tutorial} to recover metrically grounded 3D object positions and build a globally consistent 3D scene graph incrementally. On the real-world 3RScan~\cite{wald2019rio} dataset, our method attains $52.2$ mean object recall and $30.8$ mean predicate recall under the 3DSSG~\cite{wald2020learning} evaluation protocol, against $27.0$ and $17.6$ for MonoSSG~\cite{wu2023incremental}, while using no depth or reconstructed geometry at inference. This shows that relations and metrically grounded 3D object representations can be predicted from posed RGB images alone.

</details>


### [56] [Verifier Choice is a Benchmark Design Variable: Auditing Structural Counting Evaluation in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15433)
*Shurun Li,Michael Hua Wang,Haibo Zeng,Long Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出文本到图像自动评测中的验证器不可视为实现细节，构建以验证器为中心的计数审计套件（7类计数对象、6240张生成图像），发现聚合分数匹配可与图像级不一致共存，验证器协议改变可靠性，且系统性压缩/逆转生成器间的人类评分差异，并提出因子化验证器可靠性画像报告方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动评测已成为文生图基准的核心，但把图像转成分数的验证器常被当作实现细节忽略；作者认为基准分数是生成器、提示分布、验证器、使用协议与评分规则的联合属性，因此需要审计验证器对结论的扭曲效应。

Method: 在结构化计数这一可人工核验的受控任务上，围绕7类计数对象（手机/椅子/红绿灯；三叶草小叶/抽屉前板/插排插孔/涡轮叶片）构建以验证器为中心的审计套件；用Qwen-Image-2512与FLUX.2-dev生成6240张图像，比较开放式MLLM验证器在count-only、reject-aware、zero-shot、few-shot协议下的表现，并加入TIFA式目标查询、检测器基线与两个难对象上的前沿闭源模型（GPT-5.5、Claude Opus 4.7）检查；在2280张平衡评测集上做图像级一致性与生成器排序分析，并引入第三个生成器Z-Image验证排序反转。

Result: 聚合分数匹配下仍存在显著图像级分歧；验证器协议与生成器分布改变测得的可靠性；所有验证器-协议组合都把两生成器间的人类评分差异缩小7.5–25.0个百分点，且在Z-Image与FLUX.2-dev上四种协议均逆转人类排序；闭源模型检查未能消除失效模式（GPT-5.5三叶草有效计数保真度仅59.92%，Claude Opus 4.7同切片一致性κ=0.432）。

Conclusion: 自动基准分数不应单点报告，应附带因子化验证器可靠性画像：聚合分差、图像级一致性、生成器比较扭曲度、有效图像计数保真度、拒答行为、输出合规率与方向性偏差。

Abstract: Automatic evaluation is now central to text-to-image benchmarking, but the verifier that converts generated images into scores is often treated as an implementation detail. We argue that this is unsafe: an automatic benchmark score is a property of the image generator, prompt distribution, verifier, verifier-use protocol, and scoring rule, not of the generator alone. We study this issue in structural counting, a controlled setting where human-visible counts can be audited. We construct a verifier-centric audit suite over seven counting objects: cell phones, chairs, and traffic lights for object counting, and clover leaflets, dresser drawer fronts, power-strip sockets, and turbine blades for substructure counting. Across 6,240 generated images from Qwen-Image-2512 and FLUX.2-dev, we compare open MLLM verifiers under count-only, reject-aware, zero-shot and few-shot protocols, plus TIFA-like target queries, a detector baseline, and frontier closed-model checks on two hard objects. On a balanced 2,280-image evaluation split, aggregate score matching can coexist with image-level disagreement, and verifier-use protocols and generator distributions change the measured reliability. Every verifier-protocol configuration also shrinks the human score difference between the two generators, by 7.5-25.0 pp, and with a third generator, Z-Image, all four tested protocols reverse the human ordering of Z-Image and FLUX.2-dev on our fixed prompt suite. GPT-5.5 and Claude Opus 4.7 checks do not remove the failure modes: GPT-5.5 still has only 59.92% clover valid-count fidelity, while Claude Opus 4.7 has lower same-slice agreement ($\kappa=0.432$). We propose reporting automatic benchmark scores with a factorized verifier reliability profile: aggregate score gap, image-level agreement, generator-comparison distortion, valid-image count fidelity, rejection behavior, output compliance, and directional bias.

</details>


### [57] [ThermalSplat: Generalizable Pose-Free Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View Thermal 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15435)
*Prasanna S,Gopi Raju Matta,Divya Madhuri Vemunuri,Kaushik Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ThermalSplat：首个可泛化的前馈式3D高斯泼溅框架，可从未标定位、无RGB引导的稀疏热红外图像直接重建3D场景，无需逐场景优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热红外成像在搜救与自主导航中重要，但热图像低纹理、视角相关发射率导致标准位姿估计器崩溃，稀疏视角下3DGS管线失效；现有热重建方法依赖稠密采集、逐场景优化，且需对齐的RGB-热数据来获取位姿与初始点云，无法用于纯热红外无位姿环境。

Method: 前馈式泛化高斯泼溅架构，直接回归高斯表示与位姿；引入soft census loss实现亮度不变的结构匹配，并设计边界结构损失显式监督温度不连续处；在多源聚合数据集上训练，单次前向即可重建未见场景。

Result: 在稀疏视角条件下严格优于场景特定热泼溅方法；即使基线在其理想稠密采集设置下运行，ThermalSplat仍取得更优感知保真度；单次前馈合成清晰重建。

Conclusion: 通过消除昂贵的逐场景优化，ThermalSplat提供了高效、可泛化的热红外3D重建路径，突破无位姿纯热输入瓶颈。

Abstract: Thermal infrared imaging is vital for applications like search-and-rescue and autonomous navigation, yet trans- lating it into 3D scene representations remains a critical bottleneck. The low-texture nature and view-dependent emissivity of thermal images cause standard pose estima- tors to fail catastrophically, collapsing standard 3D Gaus- sian Splatting pipelines under sparse-view constraints. Fur- thermore, existing thermal reconstruction methods rely heavily on dense capture and computationally expensive per-scene optimization. Critically, many of these ap- proaches depend on aligned RGB-thermal datasets to ob- tain reliable camera poses and initial point clouds, ren- dering them unusable in purely thermal, unposed envi- ronments. To break this bottleneck, we present Thermal- Splat, the first generalizable, feed-forward Gaussian splat- ting framework that reconstructs 3D scenes directly from sparse thermal images without requiring known camera poses, RGB guidance, or per-scene training. We over- come thermal-specific challenges using a soft census loss for brightness-invariant structural matching and a bound- ary structure loss to explicitly supervise temperature discon- tinuities. Trained on an aggregated multi-source dataset, ThermalSplat synthesizes sharp reconstructions of unseen scenes in a single feed-forward pass. Extensive experi- ments demonstrate that ThermalSplat holds a strict advan- tage over scene-specific thermal splatting methods in the sparse regime. Furthermore, even compared against these baselines operating under their ideal dense capture set- tings, ThermalSplat achieves superior perceptual fidelity. By eliminating the need for computationally expensive per scene optimization, ThermalSplat offers an efficient and generalizable approach to thermal 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [58] [SteinPnP: Non-Parametric Uncertainty Quantification in the Perspective-N-Point Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15437)
*Baptiste Lardinoit,Jongseok Lee,Andres Yubero,Seong Hun Lee,Javier Civera,Rudolph Triebel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SteinPnP：用姿态粒子非参数化建模完整6-DoF相机位姿后验的概率PnP求解器，基于Stein变分推断（SVI），并提出三项实用改进以发现新位姿假设、聚类区分假设并校准簇间概率权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PnP求解器只输出单一位姿解，无法表达多模态不确定性（如位姿模糊、对称性、重复纹理导致的歧义）；而粒子表示虽具表达力，却缺乏高效优化手段。作者希望兼得粒子表示的表达力与优化求解器的效率。

Method: 以一组姿态粒子非参数化近似6-DoF位姿后验，利用Stein Variational Inference 进行粒子更新。针对SVI在PnP中的失效（漏掉备选位姿假设、簇概率权重错误），提出：发现新潜在位姿、识别不同假设簇、平衡簇间相对概率权重三项补救措施。

Result: 在合成与真实基准上，SteinPnP能准确刻画复杂的多模态不确定性，同时保持有竞争力的位姿精度。

Conclusion: 概率化、多假设的PnP求解可行且实用，为下游几何管线提供带不确定性的位姿后验，而非单一点估计。

Abstract: We propose SteinPnP, a probabilistic Perspective- N-Point (PnP) solver that models the full 6-DoF camera pose posterior using a non-parametric set of pose particles. By lever- aging Stein Variational Inference (SVI), SteinPnP combines the expressivity of particle-based representations with the efficiency of optimization-based solvers. However, a naive application of SVI struggles in the PnP problem, often missing alternative pose hypotheses or assigning incorrect probability weights to them. To address these limitations, we propose practical remedies that discover new potential poses, identify distinct hypothesis clusters, and balance their relative probability weights to capture the true underlying distribution. Our experiments across synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that SteinPnP accurately captures complex multimodal uncertainties while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [59] [Semantic Consistency of Vision Tokens: A Vision-Centric Perspective on Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15448)
*Nikola Đukić,Tim Lebailly,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"语义一致性"视角分析MLLM视觉编码器，定义语义一致/不一致patch token（SCT/SIT），发现MLLM更依赖SCT，并提出Semantically-Guided Training (SGT)损失，在保持语言对齐的同时提升视觉token语义一致性，在视觉中心任务上超越专用编码器MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM多用语言对齐编码器（利于语言理解），但视觉中心/密集任务可能更需自监督编码器的高质量patch表征。作者想探究自监督模型能否助力MLLM视觉中心任务，并找到可量化诊断指标。

Method: 定义patch token的语义（不）一致性，划分SCTs与SITs；通过从输入中移除SCTs或SITs进行系统性probe，测得MLLM对SCT依赖更强。据此设计SGT：在传统语言建模目标上增加最小化语义不一致性的语义引导损失，联合训练视觉编码器与MLLM。

Result: 移除SCT导致更大性能下降，说明语义一致性关键。SGT在视觉中心基准上优于所有基线及使用专用视觉编码器的MLLM，同时在纯文本基准上保持强表现，所有任务平均取得SOTA。

Conclusion: 应在MLLM内部联合优化语言对齐与语义一致性，而非独立选择编码器范式；为设计更好的MLLM视觉编码器提供方向。

Abstract: In computer vision, vision-language and self-supervised encoders are two unique paradigms characterized by global language alignment and high-quality vision-only patch representations, respectively. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often use the former because of language alignment, despite the recent rise in vision-centric tasks resembling traditional dense vision tasks that would likely benefit from the high-quality patch representations, raising the question whether self-supervised models could aid MLLMs in vision-centric tasks. To investigate this, we define semantic (in)consistency of patch tokens from language-aligned encoders and identify two sets of tokens, semantically consistent (SCTs) and semantically inconsistent (SITs). Through systematic probing via the removal of SCTs or SITs from the input of modern MLLMs, we show higher reliance on SCTs than SITs, as removal of the former induces larger performance drops. This demonstrates that MLLMs benefit from semantic consistency of vision tokens. With this in mind, we devise a simple yet effective extension of the MLLM training objective that combines traditional language modeling with semantic guidance via minimization of semantic inconsistency. This strategy, named Semantically-Guided Training (SGT), combines the signals of both loss terms to push the vision encoder towards providing semantically consistent tokens, while preserving language alignment. Extensive evaluation shows that SGT outperforms all baselines and MLLMs with specialized vision encoders on vision-centric benchmarks while keeping strong scores on text-centric benchmarks, thus setting state of the art on average across all tasks. Our results highlight the importance of combining language alignment and semantic consistency through the lens of MLLMs rather than independently from them and pave the way towards the design of better MLLM vision encoders.

</details>


### [60] [Reading the Unreadable: Text-Aware Image Super-Resolution Needs Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15449)
*Jaeseong Lee,Jinwoo Kim,Jinho Jeong,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出 ReasonText 基准与 GTTCA 指标，诊断出文本感知超分在"需推理文本"上系统性失效，并提出免训练插件 RTC（用 MLLM 推理出文本并以 caption 形式注入文本条件 SR 模型），在六个模型上一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAISR 现有方法将任务视为纯视觉复原问题，但严重退化的文字在局部视觉证据不足时无法被读取；人类依靠上下文、逻辑模式与先验知识推理来读字。作者据此质疑：TAISR 是否需要推理能力？

Method: 1) ReasonText：人工构建的基准，将需推理文本与局部可恢复文本分离；2) GTTCA：基于识别的指标，用真值文本裁剪度量复原质量，规避检测误差；3) 评估 12 个近期 SR 模型定位盲点；4) RTC（Reasoning Transfer via Captioning）：免训练即插即用，用外部 MLLM 推断退化文本并生成 caption，输入文本条件 SR 模型。

Result: 12 个 SR 模型在需推理文本上性能一致且大幅下降；文本条件 SR 模型在给真值文本时显著变好，说明瓶颈在文本先验缺失；MLLM 能通过推理解读低分辨率退化文字。RTC 在评估的六个文本条件 SR 模型上均一致提升，在需推理文本上尤为明显；意外地，真实场景 SR 模型 + RTC 甚至优于 TAISR 模型 + RTC。

Conclusion: 推理应成为未来 TAISR 的核心设计轴；将"读不出来的字"交给具备推理能力的外部模块处理，而非依赖局部像素复原，是更有效、可即插即用的范式。

Abstract: Text-aware image super-resolution (TAISR) aims to recover high-resolution images from low-resolution inputs while preserving textual content. Existing methods treat this as a visual restoration problem, yet severely degraded text often cannot be read from local visual evidence alone. Humans instead read it by reasoning over image context, logical patterns, and prior knowledge. This raises a fundamental question: Does TAISR need reasoning? To answer this, we introduce two diagnostic tools. The first, ReasonText, is a human-curated benchmark that separates reasoning-required text from locally recoverable text. The second, Ground-Truth Text-Crop Accuracy (GTTCA), is a recognition-based metric that measures text restoration quality without text detection errors. Evaluating twelve recent super-resolution (SR) models, we find a consistent and substantial performance drop on reasoning-required text, revealing a systematic blind spot. Two observations point to a remedy. One is that text-conditioned SR models restore text substantially better when given ground-truth text. The other is that multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can read degraded text in low-resolution images through reasoning. We therefore propose Reasoning Transfer via Captioning (RTC), a training-free plug-in that uses an external MLLM to infer the degraded text and feeds the resulting caption to a text-conditioned SR model. RTC consistently improves all six text-conditioned SR models we evaluate, especially on reasoning-required text. Surprisingly, a real-world SR model with RTC can even outperform the TAISR model with RTC, suggesting that reasoning should be a core design axis for future TAISR.

</details>


### [61] [WaveFlow-UIE: A Single-Pipeline Wavelet-Domain Flow Model for Efficient Underwater Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15457)
*Abdulaziz Alfaraj,Mohammed Al Naser,Hassan Al Nasser,Muzammil Behzad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaveFlow-UIE：在小波域用flow matching单管线直接做水下图像复原，物理启发退化线索调制速度网络，单步推理即可保留几乎全部质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式水下复原依赖多阶段流水线和昂贵迭代细化；而色偏、散射、细节损失对高/低频分量影响不同，需频域解耦。

Method: DWT分解+轻量物理启发退化线索（透射率、环境光）条件化速度网络，在小波空间用flow matching训练；目标函数含flow-matching、频率加权重建、感知、色彩一致、物理一致损失。

Result: UIEB/LSUI/UFO-120/EUVP上取得较好质量-效率折中，感知指标突出；较强小波扩散基线显著降低推理开销；5步降至1步几乎无损且256×256下约4×加速。

Conclusion: 单步、单遍小波域flow模型可作为重型扩散式细化的有效替代，为水下复原提供高效单管线范式。

Abstract: Underwater image restoration remains challenging because color distortion, scattering, and detail loss affect low- and high-frequency image components differently. Recent diffusion-based methods have shown strong restoration quality, but often rely on multi-stage pipelines and costly iterative refinement. We present WaveFlow-UIE, a single-pipeline wavelet-domain flow model that directly learns to transform degraded underwater wavelet representations into clean ones. Our method applies a discrete wavelet transform, estimates lightweight physics-inspired degradation cues including transmission and ambient light, and conditions a velocity network trained with flow matching in wavelet space. The training objective combines flow-matching, frequency-weighted reconstruction, perceptual, color-consistency, and physics-consistency losses. Across UIEB, LSUI, UFO-120, and EUVP, WaveFlow-UIE achieves a strong quality-efficiency tradeoff, with especially competitive results on perceptual metrics. Relative to a strong wavelet-based diffusion baseline, the proposed model substantially reduces inference cost while maintaining or improving restoration quality on several paired benchmarks. We show that reducing the inference process from five flow steps to a single step preserves nearly all of the restoration quality while providing an additional speedup of about $4\times$ on UIEB at $256\times256$ resolution. This single-step result suggests that a single-pass wavelet-domain model is an effective alternative to heavier diffusion-style refinement.

</details>


### [62] [Fear of Memorizing Objects: End-to-End Zero-Shot Model-Based Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15459)
*Leon Greiner,Wout Boerdijk,Junwen Huang,Benjamin Busam,Rudolph Triebel,Maximilian Durner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FOMO: an end-to-end transformer for zero-shot model-based object detection that injects CAD template information early via bidirectional attention among latent tokens, multi-view templates, and image features, trained on a new large synthetic Clutter6D dataset.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Proposal-and-match pipelines for zero-shot model-based detection are loosely coupled, producing fragmented masks in clutter and fragile matching under occlusion; existing training data also lacks object diversity and realistic dense clutter.

Method: End-to-end trainable transformer that jointly attends over learnable latent tokens, multi-view CAD templates, and query image features, yielding object-aware features and template-conditioned queries decoded into boxes and confidence; trained on Clutter6D (500k+ images, 10k+ models, multi-instance clutter with varied occlusion).

Result: 54.2 mAP on BOP-Classic-Core at 0.23 s/image; second overall, 3.8 pp behind top method while >7x faster; ablations validate design choices.

Conclusion: Early, tightly coupled template fusion in a single-stage transformer is a strong, efficient alternative to proposal-and-match for zero-shot model-based detection, with dense-clutter synthetic data as a key enabler.

Abstract: This paper presents FOMO (Fear of Memorizing Objects), an end-to-end trainable transformer-based architecture for zero-shot model-based object detection. Existing approaches typically follow a proposal-and-match pipeline: target-agnostic mask proposals are generated and then matched to template features in a set of loosely coupled stages, making them susceptible to fragmented proposals in clutter and degraded matching under occlusions. FOMO elegantly replaces this paradigm by injecting object template information early in the detection pipeline: Bidirectional attention between learnable latent tokens, multi-view CAD templates, and query image features produces object-aware image features and conditioned queries which are subsequently decoded into bounding-box and confidence predictions. We train FOMO on Clutter6D, a novel large-scale synthetic dataset with over 500k images featuring more than 10k diverse object models rendered in highly cluttered scenes with multiple instances and varying occlusion levels, which addresses the limited object diversity and lack of realistic dense clutter in existing datasets for training zero-shot multi-instance algorithms. In true zero-shot fashion, FOMO achieves 54.2 mAP on BOP-Classic-Core at 0.23 s per image, ranking second overall with 3.8 pp difference to the top method while being more than 7x faster. Throughout comprehensive ablation studies we investigate and verify architectural design choices.

</details>


### [63] [Real Pairs Are Not Enough: Physically Anchored Supervision for Volumetric CT Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15514)
*Haoran Liu,Zipei Yan,Mingzhe Liu,Jizhou Li,Guibin Zan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理锚定隐式网络PIN，用实配对双锚学习把物理HR/LR配对解耦为结构指导与低通约束，避免逐体素回归导致的过平滑，在真实CT上显著提升保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体数据CT超分依赖LR-HR配对，但合成配对缺真实退化，配准的物理配对含噪声、伪影与微结构差异，直接体素回归或朴素混合合成/真实数据会过度平滑多孔细结构。

Method: PIN为共享的体数据隐式神经表示，配合Real-Pair Dual-Anchor学习：HR锚通过配对条件谱重放传递细结构；LR锚通过仿射校准低通投影约束输出；再加精确合成监督。推理仅需实测LR。

Result: 30组外部基线配置下全卷LPIPS最低；平均对称表面距离降低42.3–49.5%，骨体积分数误差降低52.2–53.4%（对比各指标最强三基线）；被抹除参考孔体积分数从37.3–72.9%降至10.4%。

Conclusion: 将物理配对监督拆分为结构引导与退化一致性两种互补角色，可在不要求实测LR逐体素逼近注册HR的前提下有效利用真实扫描，缓解过平滑。

Abstract: Volumetric CT super-resolution (SR) models are commonly trained on synthetic or physically acquired pairs of low-resolution (LR) and high-resolution (HR) volumes. However, synthetic pairs miss real acquisition effects, whereas registered physical pairs retain inter-scan noise, artifact, and microstructure differences---causing direct voxelwise regression, and even naive synthetic-real mixing, to oversmooth fine porous structures. We introduce the Physically-anchored Implicit Network (PIN) with Real-Pair Dual-Anchor learning, which decouples physical-pair supervision into two complementary roles. The HR anchor teaches fine structure from registered HR through pair-conditioned spectral replay, while the LR anchor constrains the prediction from acquired LR via affine-calibrated low-pass projection. Together with exact synthetic supervision in one shared volumetric implicit model, PIN learns from both physical scans without forcing the acquired-LR reconstruction to match registered HR voxel by voxel. Inference requires only acquired LR. Across 30 external baseline configurations on real CT, PIN achieves the lowest full-volume LPIPS, reduces average symmetric surface distance by 42.3-49.5% and bone-volume-fraction error by 52.2-53.4% relative to the three strongest baselines for each metric, and lowers the erased reference-pore volume fraction from 37.3-72.9% to 10.4%. Code, pretrained models, and evaluation scripts will be publicly available.

</details>


### [64] [SCR-Mamba: Skeleton-conditioned Mamba with cross-scale incremental refinement for efficient 3D human pose estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15526)
*Jie Wang,Xuejing Gu,Congzhe Zhang,Zibo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCR-Mamba，用骨架条件状态建模（SCSM）把骨骼连接与左右对称关系注入Mamba选择性参数，并用跨尺度增量细化（CSIR）在粗尺度建模后逐帧细化，实现高效视频3D人体姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba类方法虽能以线性复杂度建模长序列，但选择性状态更新未直接利用骨架结构关系；同时在全时间分辨率堆叠模块计算开销大，而压缩时间序列又损失逐帧细节。需要同时解决"结构关系未被显式利用"和"分辨率-效率矛盾"两个问题。

Method: SCSM：由骨连接与左右对称关系生成骨架上下文，以此条件化空间/时间分支的选择性参数（Δ、B、C等），使结构先验贯穿状态的写入、传播与读取。CSIR：通过内容自适应聚合构建多尺度运动表示，用粗尺度建模产生的增量逐步细化保留的逐帧特征，融合长程运动上下文同时减少全分辨率计算。给出L/X两个规模变体。

Result: Human3.6M（SH检测、243帧输入）上SCR-Mamba-L达36.8mm P1，7.72M参数、15.10G MACs，计算量不足StructMamPose-L的1/7且精度相当；SCR-Mamba-X达36.6mm P1、30.9mm P-MPJPE，SOTA。MPI-INF-3DHP上PCK 99.3%、AUC 88.1%、MPJPE 14.2mm。

Conclusion: 将骨骼结构先验注入Mamba选择性状态机制、并用跨尺度增量细化替代全分辨率堆叠，可在显著降低计算量的同时达到或超过现有SOTA，验证了结构条件化状态建模与层次化时间细化的有效性。

Abstract: Mamba-based methods for video-based 3D human pose estimation can model long sequences with linear complexity, but their selective state updates usually lack direct exploitation of skeletal relations. Meanwhile, stacking modeling blocks at the full temporal resolution leads to high computational overhead, while compressing the temporal sequence makes it difficult to retain per-frame details. To address these issues, we propose SCR-Mamba, whose core consists of Skeleton-Conditioned State Modeling (SCSM) and Cross-Scale Incremental Refinement (CSIR). SCSM generates a skeleton context from bone connections and left-right symmetry relations and generates the selective parameters of the spatial and temporal branches conditioned on it, so that structural relations are involved in the writing, propagation, and reading of state information. CSIR constructs multi-scale motion representations through content-adaptive aggregation and progressively refines the retained per-frame features with the increments produced by coarse-scale modeling, integrating long-range motion context while reducing full-resolution computation. On Human3.6M with Stacked Hourglass (SH) 2D detections and 243-frame inputs, SCR-Mamba-L achieves 36.8 mm MPJPE (P1) with 7.72 M parameters and 15.10 G MACs, less than 1/7 of the computational cost of StructMamPose-L with similar accuracy; SCR-Mamba-X further reaches 36.6 mm P1 and 30.9 mm P-MPJPE (P2), achieving state-of-the-art performance. On MPI-INF-3DHP, SCR-Mamba-X achieves a percentage of correct keypoints (PCK) of 99.3%, an area under the curve (AUC) of 88.1%, and an MPJPE of 14.2 mm.

</details>


### [65] [MAGIC: Modality-Aware Gini Inspection for Clinical Hallucination Detection in LMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15530)
*Sachin Acharya,Niyoj Oli,Shishir Adhikari,Aminu Lawal,Yash Raj Shrestha,Prashnna K Gyawali,Binod Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将医学LMM幻觉检测建模为答案生成时跨模态注意力权重分配失衡问题，用基尼系数量化各层/头的模态注意力集中度，训练逻辑回归分类器，在两个医学LMM与三种影像模态上取得与基线相当或更优的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学多模态大模型存在幻觉（自信但临床不可验证的输出），威胁高风险临床可靠性；现有幻觉检测多面向单模态，无法刻画医学影像中视觉-语言交互的动态特性。

Method: 以答案token生成过程中模态间注意力分布失衡为假设；用基尼系数在层与注意力头上量化视觉/文本token的注意力集中度，发现幻觉响应中注意力质量在视觉与文本token间发生统计显著的重分配；据此提取特征训练逻辑回归分类器。

Result: 在两个医学LMM、三种医学影像模态上评估，检测性能与现有幻觉检测基线相当或更优；验证了幻觉响应中存在统计显著的跨模态注意力重分配。

Conclusion: 跨模态注意力集中度（基尼系数）是轻量、有效且可解释的医学LMM幻觉检测信号，逻辑回归即可达到有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in medical large multimodal models (LMMs) have brought promising progress to clinical decision support, yet hallucination, the generation of confident but clinically unverifiable outputs, poses a critical threat to the reliability demanded in high-stakes medical settings. Existing hallucination detection methods, predominantly designed for unimodal settings, fail to capture the visual-linguistic dynamics inherent to medical imaging. We formulate the problem of hallucination detection in LMMs as disproportionate distribution of attention weights across the modalities during answer token generation. We quantify modality-specific attention concentration across layers and heads using the Gini coefficient and demonstrate statistically significant redistribution of attention mass between visual and textual tokens in hallucinated responses. We leverage these features to train a logistic regression-based classifier and evaluate it on two medical LMMs across three medical imaging modalities, demonstrating that our method achieves competitive or superior performance against contemporary hallucination detection baselines.

</details>


### [66] [TraceKV: Policy-Preserving Low-Rank KV Cache Compression for Reasoning Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15569)
*Ke Li,Zishan Shao,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TraceKV：免训练低秩KV缓存压缩框架，统一on-policy校准、敏感度感知SVD与全局秩分配，最小化推理时策略KL漂移，在VLM视觉推理上实现近无损精度下5.64×KV压缩（含量化）与最高1.83×解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型VLM（长多模态上下文+长推理链）KV缓存开销巨大；现有低秩KV压缩在压缩推理态KV时，相对稠密模型的预测策略漂移累积导致推理失败。作者归因于两点：校准状态与on-policy推理状态失配，以及各KV状态的误差敏感度不均。

Method: 免训练框架：以"保持策略"为单一准则，结合on-policy校准（用模型自身生成轨迹的KV分布做校准）、敏感度感知SVD（按状态误差敏感度加权分解）、全局秩分配（跨层/头统一预算分配），并可与量化叠加；配套优化低秩注意力kernel。

Result: 多个VLM与视觉推理基准上取得更优精度—内存折中；与量化结合时5.64×KV缓存压缩下推理精度近无损；优化kernel带来最高1.83×端到端解码加速。

Conclusion: 将KV压缩目标从重建误差转向策略分布保持，可显著抑制推理链上的策略KL漂移，是长推理VLM部署的实用效率方案（代码将开源）。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language models (VLMs) impose substantial KV cache demands from both large multimodal contexts and growing reasoning traces. Low-rank KV compression has emerged as a promising paradigm that stores low-dimensional latents of KV states and can be combined with quantization. However, existing low-rank methods often induce growing predictive policy drift from the dense model when compressing reasoning KV states, resulting in reasoning failures. We identify two key causes of this degradation: calibration-state mismatch between static contexts and on-policy reasoning states, and unequal error sensitivity across KV states.
