To address these challenges, we propose TraceKV, a training-free low-rank KV compression framework that unifies on-policy calibration, sensitivity-aware SVD, and global rank allocation under a single policy-preserving principle, substantially reducing policy-KL divergence during reasoning. Experiments across multiple VLMs and visual reasoning benchmarks demonstrate superior accuracy--memory trade-offs, achieving near-lossless reasoning accuracy with 5.64$\times$ KV cache compression when combined with quantization. Optimized low-rank attention kernels further deliver up to 1.83$\times$ end-to-end decoding speedup. Code will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [67] [RipBench: A Unified Benchmark for Multi-Level Rip Current Detection, Classification and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15574)
*Andrei Dumitriu,Aakash Ralhan,Florin Miron,Florin Tatui,Radu Tudor Ionescu,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RipBench 是一个统一的海滩离岸流（rip current）检测基准，在同一批数据、统一划分下覆盖分类、水平框/旋转框目标检测，以及实例/语义/全景分割六类任务，共304段视频、303,491帧（其中51,011帧六任务全标注）。结果显示粗略识别与精细空间定位之间存在显著性能落差：帧级分类 F2=0.93，最佳检测器0.72，最佳实例分割0.66，且任务间模型排序不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离岸流是全球沿海溺水的主要成因且极具安全意义，但其形态无定形、与背景高度相似、视角与环境变化大，导致自动检测困难。现有研究在数据集、指标、模型与实验协议上高度碎片化，无法横向比较不同方法，也难以判断模型在各层级视觉抽象上的真实能力边界。

Method: 构建统一基准：采集多海岸线304段视频（303,491帧，51,011帧完成六任务全标注），在标准化数据划分上同时评测分类、轴对齐与旋转目标检测、实例/语义/全景分割，从而在同一数据上对齐比较各抽象层级模型；提供逐任务多个基线，并辅以视频级置信区间、空间标签的盲法标注者间一致性研究、按视角与视觉特征的分层分析，以及时间基线（单种子短 schedule 参考跑）。指标以强调召回的安全关键导向 F2 为主，其余采用标准指标。

Result: 帧级分类达到 F2=0.93，而最佳检测器仅0.72、最佳实例分割器0.66；模型排名随任务变化，说明"能否识别"与"能否精确定位"的差距明显，粗粒度基准会高估实际可用性。分层分析与标注者间研究进一步刻画了视角与视觉特征对性能的影响。

Conclusion: RipBench 通过统一数据、统一划分与多任务评测，首次系统暴露离岸流检测中粗识别与精细空间理解之间的性能鸿沟，并提供基线、协议与分析工具，公开可复现，推动面向海滩安全的多任务视觉研究。

Abstract: Rip currents are a serious and often under-addressed threat to beach safety and the leading cause of coastal drownings worldwide. They are difficult to detect due to their amorphous structure, similarity to the background, and large variability in viewpoints and environments. Despite their dangers and growing interest in automated detection, existing research remains fragmented across datasets, metrics, models, and approaches, leading to an incomplete understanding of model performance. We introduce RipBench, a unified benchmark that enables controlled evaluation of rip current detection spanning multiple tasks, including classification, axis-aligned and oriented object detection, and instance, semantic, and panoptic segmentation, on the same data with standardized splits. This allows direct comparison of model performance on all levels of visual abstraction. Across $304$ videos ($303{,}491$ frames, $51{,}011$ of them annotated for all six tasks) collected from diverse coastlines, our results expose a clear performance gap between coarse recognition and precise spatial understanding: frame-level classification reaches $F_2 = 0.93$, while the best detector and the best instance segmenter reach $0.72$ and $0.66$, with model rankings that change from task to task. Video-level confidence intervals, a blind inter-annotator study of the spatial labels, stratified analyses by viewpoint and visual signature, and video views with temporal baselines (single-seed, short-schedule reference runs) accompany the benchmark. All tasks are supported with carefully curated annotations and evaluated using standard metrics, with a focus on the $F_2$ score to emphasize recall in this safety-critical setting. RipBench, along with multiple baselines per task, is publicly available at this https URL to support progress in real-world, multi-task vision for beach safety.

</details>


### [68] [FoveaDet: Software-Hardware Co-Design of Foveated CNN-Transformer for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15579)
*Deepak Mewada,Apporva Saraf,Ashwin Lele*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoveaDet：受生物中央凹机制启发，软硬件协同设计框架——用显著性驱动的注视点预测与ViT patch剪枝实现高效目标检测，并配套自定义RISC-V加速核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘平台算力受限，但处理全分辨率帧存在大量冗余计算；借鉴生物视觉按显著性分配可变分辨率的特性以降低开销。

Method: 软件侧动态识别显著区域并预测注视点，对ViT进行激进patch剪枝丢弃无关背景；优化插值与patch选择算法以避免顺序生成带来的延迟；硬件侧设计含专用指令的RISC-V核加速该流水线。

Result: 标准GPU上FLOPs降低65.4%–92.2%，推理加速4.76倍，mAP相对密集基线下降可忽略；尚无真实硬件实测数据。

Conclusion: 在边缘视觉感知效率上给出可扩展方案，但评测以GPU模拟为主，缺少RISC-V实片数据与机器人/真实部署验证。

Abstract: Deploying real-time object detection on power-constrained edge platforms is challenging as processing full-resolution frames is often computationally redundant. Inspired by biological foveation which assigns variable resolution based on visual saliency, we propose a novel software-hardware co-design framework called FoveaDet. On the software side, FoveaDet dynamically identifies salient regions to predict fixation points, followed by aggressive patch pruning in Vision Transformers (ViT) to discard irrelevant background data. To mitigate slowdowns from sequential patch generation, we implement optimized interpolation and patch selection algorithms. Evaluated on standard GPUs, this foveated approach reduces floating-point operations by 65.4% to 92.2% and accelerates inference by 4.76x, while suffering a negligible mAP drop compared to dense baselines. To eliminate hardware bottlenecks, we complement the algorithm with a custom RISC-V core featuring specialized instructions to accelerate the foveated processing pipeline. FoveaDet provides a scalable, efficient architecture capable of unlocking advanced vision processing on severely constrained mobile platforms.

</details>


### [69] [Cat Royale Comprehensive Dataset: A Dataset for Pose Detection, Segmentation, Animal Action Recognition and Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15601)
*Zane K. J. Hartley,Joseph Ho,Alan Chamberlain,Michael P. Pound,Steve Benford*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRCD：基于Cat Royale项目500+小时猫视频构建的多模态数据集，含2万张实例分割图、5千张多主体姿态图、800+动作片段与800+文本描述片段，并提供四类任务基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物行为与福利研究需要计算机视觉对复杂场景的理解能力，但缺乏同时覆盖分割、姿态、动作与文本描述的多模态猫科数据集。

Method: 从Cat Royale艺术与研究项目的500多小时视频中筛选并标注：实例分割掩码、多主体姿态坐标、时序动作类别、细粒度文本描述；同时发布自研标注工具以支持持续扩展，并搭建实例分割、姿态检测、时序动作定位、视频-文本检索四类基准。

Result: 首个将多种标注类型联动、支持多模态用途的猫科数据集，并给出四个任务的基准结果。

Conclusion: 该数据集可同时支撑单一视觉任务与依赖多标注组合的复杂多模态任务，代码与工具开放以促进后续扩展。

Abstract: Animal focused computer vision datasets are of a growing interest in the fields of both Computer Vision and Animal behaviour. The ability to make sense of complex scenes via a variety of Computer Vision tasks has substantial implications for behavioural understanding and welfare. The perfection of these tasks will help lead to better health and emotional outcomes for wild and domestic animals. In this work we present the Cat Royale Comprehensive Dataset (CRCD), a large, comprehensive, multi-modal dataset for feline understanding, based on a huge database of over 500 hours of video captured during the Cat Royale artwork and research project. Our dataset contains annotations for a range of computer vision problems including 20,000 images with instance segmentation masks, 5000 images with multi-subject pose coordinates, over 800 video clips with action classes, and over 800 clips with detailed text description. This is the first such dataset to link this wide range of annotation types together for multi-modal use. We release this entire dataset, and our custom annotation tools will also be made available, in order to allow for ongoing expansion. We also provide a series of benchmarks for the tasks of Instance Segmentation, Pose detection, Temporal Action Localisation, and finally video-text retrieval. The totality of this multi-modal dataset is the data needed to solve both these individual computer vision tasks as well as enabling more complex multi-modal tasks making use of a combination of annotations.

</details>


### [70] [CoCode-World: Shared 3D Proxy Representation for Multi-Player World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15602)
*Qirui Li,Xin Zhang,Yabo Chen,Jinkun Hao,Paul L Rosin,Yu-Kun Lai,Haibin Huang,Yin Chen,Ran Yi,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoCode-World 提出共享3D代理(proxy)框架，将结构控制与视觉合成解耦，用配对代理视频+RGB参考+目标视角拼接布局，单分支联合生成双人视角视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人生成式世界模型需在不同相机运动与遮挡下对共享实体保持一致。显式世界表示可提供空间控制但限制场景多样性；纯共享几何无法保证跨视角外观一致。

Method: 可编程3D代理指定场景布局、运动与遮挡，RGB参考引导角色与场景外观；将成对的代理视频、外观参考和目标视角按统一空间布局拼接，用预训练视频模型以原生注意力实现跨视角通信，并通过LoRA适配学习代理遵循与外观对应关系。构建双人GTA V同步数据集（RGB观测+由共享地图几何与游戏引擎状态导出的代理视频）。

Result: 可迁移外观到训练中未见过的角色与场景风格；视频质量与跨视角玩家身份一致性优于基线；消融分析了代理构建与跨视角通信的影响。

Conclusion: 拼接式单分支联合生成是协调多视角视觉细节的有效、简洁方案，但验证局限于游戏数据。

Abstract: Multiplayer generative world models must produce observations that agree on shared entities despite different camera motions and occlusions. Explicit world representations provide spatial control, but precise modeling of individual game maps limits scene diversity, while shared geometry alone cannot ensure consistent appearance across views. We present CoCode-World, a shared 3D proxy framework that separates structural control from visual synthesis for two-player video generation. Programmable proxies specify scene layout, motion, and occlusion, while RGB references guide character and scene appearance. To coordinate visual details across players, we concatenate paired proxy videos, appearance references, and target views in a consistent spatial layout and generate both views jointly with a pretrained video model. This formulation uses the model's native attention for cross-view communication within a single generation branch, with LoRA adaptation learning proxy adherence and appearance correspondence from paired observations. We construct a two-player GTA~V dataset that pairs synchronized RGB observations with proxy videos derived from shared map geometry and game-engine state. Experiments demonstrate appearance transfer to characters and scene styles unseen during training, with improvements in video quality and cross-view player identity over the evaluated baseline. Ablations further examine how proxy construction and cross-view communication affect generation. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [71] [Contrastive Bootstrap Representation Learning for Label-Efficient Agricultural Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15610)
*Hazem Ahmed,Ali Hamdi,Ammar Mohammed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CBRL，将SimCLR式NT-Xent对比学习与BYOL式双向EMA目标预测联合优化在同一投影空间，用于农业图像在极端标签稀缺下的自监督预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业图像分类中专家标注是主要瓶颈，而对比学习与bootstrap两类自监督信号各有优势，故探究在下游标签严重稀缺时能否把两种信号用于同一条表征路径。

Method: ViT-L/16骨干；在共享在线投影空间中同时优化NT-Xent对比损失与双向BYOL式EMA目标预测；仅用10%下游标签，单种子点估计。

Result: 四个主要农业基准上宏F1点估计最高：DeepWeeds 79.77%（较随机初始化仅标签基线高32.29点，较次优方法高15.78点）；PlantVillage 98.14%（分别高10.43与0.43点）；PlantDoc上所有方法均未达可用水平。

Conclusion: 该联合形式在标签受限迁移上表现有前景，但未证明目标协同、统计显著性或跨随机种子鲁棒性。

Abstract: Expert annotation remains a major bottleneck in agricultural image classification, where collecting images is often easier than obtaining reliable labels from domain specialists. Self-supervised learning can reduce this dependence by pretraining transferable representations on unlabelled imagery. Contrastive and bootstrap methods provide two established but distinct training signals, motivating an empirical study of a formulation that applies both signals within one representation path under severe downstream label scarcity. We present Contrastive Bootstrap Representation Learning (CBRL), which jointly optimises SimCLR-style NT-Xent contrastive learning and bidirectional BYOL-style exponential-moving-average target prediction within a shared online projection space. Using a ViT-L/16 backbone and 10% of the downstream labels, we report single-seed point estimates on four primary agricultural test benchmarks plus an exploratory 33-image New Plant Diseases evaluation. CBRL records the highest macro-F1 point estimate on the four primary benchmarks. On DeepWeeds, its 79.77% test macro-F1 is 32.29 points above the random-initialisation, label-only baseline and 15.78 points above the next-highest comparison point estimate; on PlantVillage, its 98.14% macro-F1 is 10.43 and 0.43 points higher, respectively. On PlantDoc, however, all methods fail to reach practically useful performance. These results identify the evaluated joint formulation as a promising candidate for label-limited transfer, but they do not establish objective synergy, statistical superiority, or robustness across stochastic runs.

</details>


### [72] [Learning-Based Interpolation of Detector Gaps in Photon-Counting X-ray Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15614)
*Johannes B. Thalhammer,Patrick Ilg,Tina Dorosti,Sebastian Peterhansl,Theresa Hiu,Daniel Frey,Zijin Huang,Florian Schaff,Daniela Pfeiffer,Franz Pfeiffer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用WGAN-GP与Pix2Pix对探测器拼接缝隙做“受限”图像修复，掩码限制改动只在缝隙像素，并约束填充区均值与直方图，避免重建环状伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拼接式探测器视场存在物理缝隙，需在下游相位恢复/层析重建前填补；与自然图像修复不同，除缝隙外像素必须不变，且填充区强度统计须保持（均值微小偏移即导致重建环伪影）。

Method: 适配WGAN-GP与条件Pix2Pix；输出掩码使仅缝隙像素被修改；增加约束填充区均值与强度分布的损失项；在模拟缝隙的nanoCT投影上训练，与线性插值、Navier–Stokes修复、MADF对比，量化评估模拟缝隙并在真实缝隙上做视觉评估，附消融。

Result: WGAN-GP与Pix2Pix取得最佳LPIPS与直方图距离，推理时间仅次于线性插值；Pix2Pix训练GPU小时约为WGAN-GP的1/5；消融显示输出掩码贡献最大；用于正弦图时Pix2Pix填充最自然，但重建提升相对线性插值有限。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的受限修复能生成更逼真且统计一致的缝隙填补，但下游重建收益有限；掩码机制是关键设计。属领域专用（X射线CT）修复方法。

Abstract: Image detectors assembled from tiled modules have physical gaps in the field of view that must be filled before downstream processing such as phase retrieval or tomographic reconstruction. Unlike natural-image inpainting, detector-gap interpolation must leave all other pixels unmodified and preserve the intensity statistics of the filled region, since even a small shift in mean intensity can cause ring artifacts after reconstruction. We adapted two generative models, a Wasserstein GAN with gradient penalty (WGAN-GP) and a conditional Pix2Pix model, to this task. We implemented a masking strategy that restricts all changes to the gap pixels and loss terms that constrain the mean intensity and the intensity distribution of the filled region. Both models were trained on nanoCT projections with simulated gaps and compared with linear interpolation, Navier--Stokes inpainting, and the deep image-inpainting network MADF, quantitatively on simulated gaps and visually on real detector gaps. WGAN-GP and Pix2Pix achieved the best perceptual similarity (LPIPS) and histogram distance and, apart from linear interpolation, the shortest inference time. Pix2Pix required about five times fewer training GPU-hours than WGAN-GP. Ablation studies showed that masking the network output contributes by far the most to plausible inpainting results. Applied to sinograms, Pix2Pix produced the most natural gap filling but improved the reconstruction only slightly compared with linear interpolation. The source code and the weights of the final Pix2Pix model are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [73] [WaveletLoRA: Frequency-Aware Content-Style Decomposition for Personalized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15617)
*Peiyao Wang,Jiahui Sun,Weining Wang,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用小波频带分解+分频 LoRA 分支显式解耦单图的内容与风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图内容-风格解耦是个性化图像生成的核心问题，需将主体身份与视觉风格分离以支持灵活重组。现有方法多在空间域操作，未显式利用内容与风格天然对应的频域差异特性。

Method: 提出 WaveletLoRA 频域感知框架：对参考图做多级离散小波变换（DWT）分解为低频与高频子带，为每个子带配置专用 LoRA 分支——低频分支建模全局外观以获取风格，高频分支捕捉结构细节以保持内容；并引入稠密 Mixture-of-Experts 聚合模块与频域正则化目标抑制风格泄漏、提升内容保真；同时构建 WaveBench 及基于 VLM 的评测协议，显式度量属性相似性与属性分离度。

Result: 大量实验表明 WaveletLoRA 一致优于现有方法，生成结果更符合人类偏好。

Conclusion: 通过在 LoRA 参数空间中按小波频带分离风格与结构，并辅以 MoE 聚合与频域正则，可实现更显式、更可控的单图内容-风格解耦；WaveBench 为解耦质量提供了可量化的评测手段。

Abstract: Content-style decomposition from a single image is a fundamental challenge in personalized image generation, aiming to separate subject identity from visual style for flexible recomposition. Existing approaches primarily operate in the spatial domain, without explicitly exploiting the distinct frequency characteristics naturally associated with content and style. In this paper, we propose WaveletLoRA, a novel frequency-aware framework for explicit content-style decomposition through wavelet-domain supervision. Specifically, we first apply a multi-level discrete wavelet transform (DWT) to decompose the reference image into low-frequency and high-frequency subbands. We then assign a dedicated LoRA branch to each subband, where the low-frequency branch models global appearance for style acquisition, while the high-frequency branches capture structural details for content preservation. To further improve disentanglement quality, we introduce a dense Mixture-of-Experts aggregation module together with a frequency-domain regularization objective, which reduces style leakage and enhance content fidelity. In addition, we establish WaveBench and develop a VLM-based evaluation protocol that explicitly measures both attribute similarity and attribute separation for disentanglement assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WaveletLoRA consistently outperforms existing methods and produces results that better align with human preference.

</details>


### [74] [MemInStream: Memorizing or Merely Recognizing? Rethinking Streaming Video Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15640)
*Zhenqi He,Dunyuan Xu,Yang Bo,Kien T. Pham,Yanghao Wang,Shuhan Li,Xintong Guo,Yuyang He,Fang Liu,Huankang Guan,Jingyan Chen,Rui Liu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemInStream：面向流式视频理解的内存能力专用基准，通过“证据受控验证协议”将记忆缺陷与视觉理解失败解耦，覆盖情节检索、状态跟踪与更新、多证据整合、上下文记忆四类能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解基准虽含记忆类问题，但仅凭QA准确率无法可靠度量记忆能力：错误预测可能源于视觉理解失败而非遗忘，正确预测也可能依赖语言先验或近期观测而无需历史记忆。因此需要专门衡量“记忆必要性”的评测协议。

Method: 构建MemInStream基准，每道题关联时间局部化的证据区间。采用证据受控验证协议：筛选同时满足两个条件的问题——(1) 证据可解性，即模型在仅给定标注证据区间时可作答；(2) 记忆必要性，即模型仅凭近期观测、证据后观测或纯文本输入均无法作答。以此剔除记忆无关的捷径，并按情节检索、状态跟踪与更新、多证据整合、上下文记忆四大类组织评测。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验数字；主要产出为经协议过滤后的高质量基准，声称可系统解耦记忆局限与理解失败，并减少依赖语言先验或近期观测的捷径。

Conclusion: MemInStream提供了较严谨的流式视频记忆评测框架，其“证据可解性+记忆必要性”双重验证思路对长视频/流式记忆方法的数据构建亦有参考价值，但属基准类工作，无新方法或物理AI相关实证。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding aims to continuously process incoming visual observations, making the retention and retrieval of relevant information increasingly important as the video history grows. Although existing benchmarks include memory-related questions, question-answering accuracy alone does not reliably measure memory capabilities. Incorrect predictions may arise from failures in visual understanding rather than forgetting, whereas correct predictions may rely on linguistic priors or recent observations without requiring historical memory. To address these ambiguities, we introduce MemInStream, a benchmark dedicated to evaluating memory capabilities in streaming video understanding. We develop an evidence-controlled validation protocol that associates each question with temporally localized evidence intervals. The protocol validates evidence solvability and memory necessity by retaining questions that models can answer from the annotated evidence but cannot resolve using recent observations, post-evidence observations, or textual input alone. This helps disentangle memory limitations from understanding failures while reducing memory-independent shortcuts. MemInStream provides a systematic evaluation of memory capabilities through four major categories, covering Episodic Retrieval, State Tracking \& Updating, Multi-Evidence Integration, and Contextual Memory across diverse video scenarios. Overall, \textsc{MemInStream} provides a comprehensive and reliable benchmark for systematically evaluating memory capabilities in streaming video understanding.

</details>


### [75] [Ultrasound Elastography: Excitation Methods and Low-Cost Alternative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15645)
*Corin F. Otesteanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用智能手机作为机械激励源，替代昂贵专用致动器，配合FEM求解弹性反问题，实现低成本超声弹性成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超声弹性成像需要额外的、昂贵的致动器/工具来诱导组织激励，且需额外软硬件集成到成像流程中，成本与控制复杂度高。

Method: 以现成智能手机产生的机械振动作为激励源，测量组织诱发的位移场，再用有限元方法（FEM）求解弹性反问题，重建组织力学参数。

Result: 对组织仿体（phantom）的初步弹性重建结果与使用专用致动器施加谐波位移时的结果相近，验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 智能手机激励可作为低成本、易集成的超声弹性成像方案，但仅停留在仿体层面的初步验证，尚未涉及活体或临床应用。

Abstract: Ultrasound elastography (or elasticity imaging) is a method that characterizes the tissue mechanical properties with the purpose of aiding the diagnosis of certain pathologies. Many novel clinical applications use the assumption that these tissue properties are changed in the presence of pathological processes. Characterization of tissue mechanical properties is achieved by imaging the response of the tissue to an induced stress or excitation. A plethora of approaches have been used in the scientific community to induce excitation in tissue, which usually require additional tools or devices than are costly. Furthermore these are usually integrated in the imaging pipeline using additional software or hardware for control. An alternative that is simpler, faster to integrate (and low-cost) is proposed in this work, by using readily available smartphones as a source of mechanical excitation. The induced displacements are used to solve the elasticity inverse problem (IP) using a finite element method (FEM) approach. Preliminary elasticity reconstructions of a phantom that mimics tissue are similar to when using a dedicated actuator as means of inducing harmonic displacements, illustrating the feasibility of our low-cost ultrasound elasography approach.

</details>


### [76] [Taming Neural Fields with a Frequency-Lipschitz Curriculum for Sparse-View CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15652)
*Songtao Hu,Jingrui Wu,Hong Ding,Fanhao Zhu,Yunhao Liu,Suyang Xi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FLIC——频率-Lipschitz课程的自先验INR框架用于稀疏视角CT：联合调度随机傅里叶特征编码与谱归一化MLP解码的Lipschitz预算，实现由粗到精重建，终端容量由FBP梯度统计自动标定，并加入先验引导的自适应射线采样以提升前向模型保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVCT因投影视图少导致欠定反问题，重建易拟合条纹伪影与幻觉结构；现有INR依赖隐式谱偏置或迭代外部去噪，对优化过程中高频容量何时释放缺乏显式控制。

Method: 将网络表示容量显式化并可调度：(1) 频率-Lipschitz联合课程，耦合随机傅里叶特征与谱归一化MLP，在非均匀Lipschitz预算下引导粗到精重建；(2) 由FBP梯度统计自动标定终端容量，实现扫描自适应正则，无需逐扫描调参；(3) 先验引导自适应射线采样，在高梯度解剖边界附近集中积分采样点。单阶段训练，无迭代去噪。

Result: 在四个公开CT数据集、无噪与低剂量设置下的实验表明，该方法取得最先进的无监督重建质量，并在多样解剖结构上保持稳定性能。

Conclusion: 以显式容量调度替代隐式谱偏置，是提升稀疏视角重建稳定性的有效途径；单阶段、免迭代去噪的流程兼顾了效率与自适应正则能力。

Abstract: Sparse-view computed tomography (SVCT) reduces radiation dose by acquiring fewer projection views, but the resulting under-determined inverse problem requires careful regularization to prevent reconstruction algorithms from fitting streak artifacts and hallucinated structures. Existing implicit neural representation (INR) methods rely on implicit spectral bias or iterative external denoising, yet they provide limited control over when high-frequency capacity is exposed during optimization. To address this limitation, we propose FLIC (Frequency-Lipschitz INR Curriculum), a capacity-controlled self-prior INR framework that makes the network's representational capacity explicit and schedulable. The core of our method is a joint frequency-Lipschitz curriculum that couples random Fourier feature encoding with spectrally normalized MLP decoding under non-uniform Lipschitz budgets, guiding reconstruction along a coarse-to-fine trajectory from stable global anatomy to fine details. The terminal capacity is automatically calibrated from FBP gradient statistics, enabling scan-adaptive regularization without per-scan manual tuning of the terminal Lipschitz budget. We further introduce a prior-guided adaptive ray sampling strategy that concentrates integration samples near high-gradient anatomical boundaries, improving forward-model fidelity. The entire framework operates in a single training stage without iterative denoising, enabling efficient per-image reconstruction. Extensive experiments on four public CT datasets under noise-free and low-dose settings show that our method achieves state-of-the-art unsupervised reconstruction quality with consistent performance across diverse anatomical structures.

</details>


### [77] [CGOcc: Learning Cross-Gaussian Dependencies for 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15692)
*Zirui Huang,Jiongle He,Yunfan Zeng,Yuanzhi Lu,Guangye Li,Boyan Tang,Weite SU,Pedro V. Sander,Amy Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CGOcc：显式建模跨高斯依赖关系的监督式多相机3D语义占据预测框架，用对角加低秩表示与类条件投影实现无需稠密矩阵的高斯混合读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于高斯的语义占据预测中，重叠高斯基元会产生类别歧义，其联合解释对体素分类很关键；但现有空间加权混合方法忽略了跨基元依赖的显式建模，限制了读出利用类别相关关系证据的能力。

Method: 提出CGOcc监督式多相机框架：融合外观与几何观测以精化高斯状态；以对角加低秩（diagonal-plus-low-rank）形式编码跨高斯依赖；通过类条件投影在不构建稠密依赖矩阵的前提下提取对角与带符号的跨高斯统计量；读出阶段用这些统计量精化占据类概率，同时保持学习参考的占据质量与空闲概率。

Result: 在nuScenes上达到SOTA；高斯重叠度高时增益更大，边界附近预测更优；消融验证协方差感知预测的有效性及其与几何交互的协同作用。

Conclusion: 显式跨高斯依赖建模能有效缓解重叠基元带来的类别歧义，配合协方差感知与几何交互可提升3D语义占据预测精度，尤其在高重叠与边界区域。

Abstract: In Gaussian-based semantic occupancy prediction, overlapping primitives can provide ambiguous class predictions, making their joint interpretation important for voxel classification. However, current methods using spatially weighted mixtures neglect explicit modeling of cross-primitive dependencies, limiting the readout's ability to utilize category-specific relational evidence. Hence, we introduce CGOcc, a supervised multi-camera framework that explicitly models Cross-Gaussian dependencies for 3D semantic Occupancy prediction. CGOcc integrates appearance and geometric observations to refine Gaussian states and encodes cross-Gaussian dependencies in a diagonal-plus-low-rank representation. To incorporate these dependencies into voxel classification, CGOcc uses a class-conditional projection to extract diagonal and signed cross-Gaussian statistics without constructing a dense dependency matrix. The readout then refines occupied-class probabilities using these statistics while preserving a learned reference's occupied mass and free probability. Extensive experiments on nuScenes demonstrate CGOcc achieves state-of-the-art performance, with larger gains at high Gaussian overlap and improved predictions near boundaries. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of covariance-aware prediction and support its synergy with geometric interaction.

</details>


### [78] [How Far Can Frozen Vision-Language Models Go on Event Data? A Training-Free Study on EventBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15756)
*Md Kamrujjaman Mobin,Md Salman Shamil,Md Adnan Arefeen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练系统参加ECCV 2026 EventBench通用理解赛道：将event bins渲染为红红/蓝OFF帧并拼接成网格图，用冻结的Qwen3.5 9B加类别提示完成四类任务，动作识别直接调用EventGPT-Plus-2B，测试集总体62.28%（9/27）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在衡量现成的RGB预训练视觉语言模型仅靠表示转换与提示设计，在事件相机数据上能走多远，完全不使用挑战赛训练数据。

Method: 四个类别（手势识别、细节理解、因果推理、事件OCR）将event bins渲染为红/ON、蓝/OFF帧，按类别挑选子集bin拼成单张网格图，统一用冻结的Qwen3.5 9B VLM配类别特定提示；动作识别每帧bin直接输入官方EventGPT-Plus-2B。另对比六种替代表示方式并做全测试集事后分析。

Result: 2,084题总体准确率62.28%，排名9/27；动作识别80.27%、因果推理77.22%较高，手势识别54.70%、事件OCR 34.57%较低。事后分析发现边界框裁剪几乎从未贴合内容，OCR文本仅以约1/5水平分辨率输入模型，细节理解预测偏向前两个选项。

Conclusion: RGB预训练VLM在事件数据上无需训练即可达到中等水平，但受限于表示/裁剪流水线缺陷与选项偏置；该方法本质为挑战赛工程方案，缺乏方法学创新。

Abstract: We present a training-free system for the EBMV @ ECCV 2026 challenge "Event Stream Understanding with EventBench" (General Understanding track), which evaluates vision-language models on five event-camera understanding tasks. We use none of the challenge's training data: our goal is to measure how far off-the-shelf, RGB-pretrained models can go on event data through representation and prompting alone. For four of the five categories (gesture recognition, detailed understanding, causal reasoning, and event OCR) we render event bins as red/ON, blue/OFF frames, select a category-specific subset of bins, stitch them into a single grid image, and query the same frozen Qwen3.5 9B vision-language model with a category-specific prompt for all four tasks. The fifth category, action recognition, feeds every bin directly into the organizers' EventGPT-Plus-2B. The system reaches 62.28% overall accuracy across 2,084 test questions (rank 9/27), led by action recognition and causal reasoning (80.27%, 77.22%) and limited most by gesture recognition and event OCR (54.70%, 34.57%). We visualize test samples from every category together with the exact images the model receives, score six alternative representations against the evaluation server, and analyze the full test set post hoc. That analysis shows that our bounding-box crop almost never tightened around content, so event OCR text reached the model at roughly one fifth of its horizontal resolution, and that detailed-understanding predictions are skewed toward the first two answer options.

</details>


### [79] [Image Deblending with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15760)
*Dan Botchan,Ohad Fried,Yacov Hel-Or*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的文本引导扩散后验采样框架，将图像unblending建模为联合逆问题，一次性重建两幅源图，并用两阶段（潜空间语义分离＋像素空间细化）实现盲解混。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像解混（deblending）本身是严重欠定问题：单一混合图可对应多种合理源层分解。已有扩散模型逆问题方法多针对单输出重建，无法同时分离两幅完整图像；同时人类可借助语义理解消除视觉歧义，因此希望引入文本提示约束解空间，并在无监督/无真值条件下获得多样且可排序的分解结果。

Method: 将deblending表述为联合逆问题，使用单一扩散先验的扩散后验采样（DPS类）同时重建两个源图像；提出免训练两阶段架构：第一阶段用文本提示引导的潜扩散模型做高层语义分离，第二阶段在像素空间细化以恢复细节；通过随机采样生成多个可行分解，并提出无需ground truth的排序准则；方法可扩展到空间变化的alpha matte（盲解混）。

Result: 在未见域上取得state-of-the-art的无训练解混效果，可处理空间变化的alpha map，并在标准标量混合基准上与有监督基线相当；采样得到的多个分解可在无真值情况下排序。

Conclusion: 语义级文本先验足以在单次联合采样中分离两幅完整图像，免训练框架在跨域泛化与盲解混上优于既有方法，为多输出逆问题的扩散求解提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Image deblending is the task of decomposing a composite image back into its source images, which is an ill-posed problem, as a single blended image can be decomposed into multiple plausible source layers. While diffusion models are often used to solve inverse problems, they typically address single-output reconstruction problems. In this work, we formulate deblending as a joint inverse problem, employing diffusion posterior sampling to simultaneously reconstruct two source images, demonstrating that a single diffusion prior is expressive enough to disentangle two full images at once rather than just one. Drawing inspiration from human perception, where semantic understanding helps disentangle visual inconsistencies, we propose a training-free framework that leverages text prompts to constrain this solution space. We address the blind deblending task through a two-stage architecture: text-guided latent diffusion performs semantic separation using prompts as high-level cues, followed by a pixel-space refinement to recover fine details. Stochastic sampling produces multiple plausible decompositions that can be ranked without ground truth. Extensive experiments show that our training-free method achieves state-of-the-art results on unseen domains and extends to spatially-varying alpha maps, while remaining competitive with supervised baselines on standard scalar blending benchmarks.

</details>


### [80] [AIFR: Adaptive Identity Feature Recovery for Diffusion-Based Makeup and Anime Portraits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15764)
*Linyan Dai,Xinwei Zhang,Yaxin Xiao,Mengyu Sun,Xinwei Liu,Renyang Liu,Qingqing Ye,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在冻结的FR模型中间层插入1.22M参数的自适应恢复模块，预测样本级修正强度与空间自适应残差，单次前向即可将化妆/动漫人脸的embedding拉回原空间，免去gallery重编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成的化妆与人像动漫化改变外观，导致人脸识别在身份筛查中失效；现有方案或需重编码gallery（改编码器），或依赖图像复原而损失/扭曲身份细节。

Method: 提出AIFR：在冻结FR模型单一中间层插入轻量恢复模块，输出样本级修正强度与空间自适应残差更新；训练用融合身份目标监督原空间embedding恢复，并用身份偏移分数约束修正强度；推理无需参考图，单次前向输出可直接与已有模板比对的embedding。

Result: 仅1.22M可训练参数，FAR=1e-4下平均TAR由ArcFace的66.1%提升至90.2%；在未见过的化妆风格、风格化强度和生成器上均有一致提升；恢复后的embedding可直接驱动Arc2Face生成并改善身份保持。

Conclusion: 将"改模型/复原图像"转为"冻结模型内部特征空间纠正"，是一种低成本、可插拔且兼容下游embedding条件生成的方案；但验证集中于人脸识别表征层，与具身智能核心方向关联较弱。

Abstract: Diffusion-based makeup and anime portraits are increasingly popular in online content creation (e.g., virtual makeup editing and anime avatar generation), but their altered appearances can hinder face recognition (FR) in identity-based content screening. Existing methods mainly adapt FR encoders or restore input images. The former require costly gallery re-encoding, while the latter may be incomplete or distort identity-related facial details. To address these limitations, we aim to correct identity features within a frozen FR model to recover its output embeddings in its original space. Appearance changes vary in strength and affect facial regions differently. To this end, we propose Adaptive Identity Feature Recovery (AIFR), a framework that inserts a lightweight recovery module at a single intermediate layer to predict sample-level correction strength and spatially adaptive residual updates. Module training uses fused identity targets to guide embedding recovery in the original FR space and an identity-shift score to regulate correction strength and limit unnecessary changes. At inference, AIFR processes a single portrait in one forward pass without reference images, producing recovered embeddings that can be directly matched with existing gallery templates without re-encoding. With only 1.22M trainable parameters, AIFR improves the average TAR at FAR=$10^{-4}$ from 66.1\% to 90.2\% over the original ArcFace, and further experiments show improvements across unseen makeup styles, stylization strengths, and generators. Beyond recognition, the recovered embeddings can directly condition a pretrained Arc2Face model to generate faces with improved identity preservation, showing compatibility with embedding-conditioned downstream generation.

</details>


### [81] [Prior-as-Skill: Remember Outside and Read Inside with On-Policy Self-Distillation for Self-Evolving VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15766)
*Yan Lin,Jingyu Sun,Yifei Qian,Zhengtao Yao,Yuxia Wang,Daizong Liu,Chun Pong Lau,Zhongliang Guo,Stephen McGough*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PriorSplit将步骤顺序先验放在模型外部，通过统计相邻步骤先后频次，在缺失帧时用计数补全顺序；帧可见时仍由帧作答，仅需OPSD训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉智能体的技能内化方法（on-policy自蒸馏）把顺序编码进读帧权重：全帧训练时缺帧会靠猜，抽帧训练时又让记忆压过视觉观测。部署中帧缺失或顺序被重排很常见，需要解耦"顺序记忆"与"视觉感知"。

Method: 外部化顺序先验：统计训练回合中相邻两步谁先出现的计数；推理时模型先判断两个被问步骤是否同时在视野中——在则依帧作答，不在则由外部计数回答。除OPSD外无需额外训练。

Result: 在合成技能上跨5个模型、以及在已见web、烹饪与自我中心任务的新回合步骤顺序提问上，缺帧条件下PriorSplit以OPSD同级训练成本超过所有对比方法；Qwen3-VL-4B在异常顺序上仍接近OPSD，而最受益于顺序存储的方法明显落后。

Conclusion: 把顺序知识放在模型之外、只在必要时查询，可避免记忆覆盖感知，是一种低成本的视觉-语言智能体技能内化鲁棒性改进。

Abstract: Self-evolving visual agents keep what they learn as skills: key frames with step notes on a task's steps and their order. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is now a popular way to train such skills into a vision-language model that matches, on its own answers, a teacher that also reads the written order. At deployment, frames often go missing or show an unusual order, as when a person or a website reorders the steps. Existing internalization methods keep any order they store in the frame-reading weights: trained on full frames, the model guesses when they are missing, and trained with frames withheld, it stores the order, so memory can override what it sees. We propose PriorSplit, which fills in missing frames from an order kept outside the model, with no training beyond OPSD. It counts which of two neighboring steps comes first in training episodes, and the model checks if both asked steps are in view: if so, it answers from the frames, and otherwise the counts answer. Extensive experiments on synthetic skills with five models and on step-tagged order questions in new episodes of seen web, cooking and egocentric tasks demonstrate that, with frames missing, PriorSplit outperforms every compared method at OPSD's training cost. With Qwen3-VL-4B on synthetic skills, it also stays close to OPSD on unusual orders, where methods that gain most by storing the order fall behind.

</details>


### [82] [VibePrevisBench: Evaluating Agentic Coding for 4D Scene Generation in Blender](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15789)
*Yuzhe Chen,Muxi Diao,Lele Yang,Wenbin Xu,Xuyang Liu,Qile Su,Changyi Liu,Jiankang Chen,Kaiyu Jiang,Haoyu Zhang,Hongyi Fu,Jianxiong Wang,Tianke Zhang,Haonan Fan,Yingxin Li,Bingyi Li,Xu Wang,Xuanyu Zheng,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao,Wentao Zhang,Jiale Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VibePrevisBench：面向编码智能体从简报到可执行4D场景重建的基准，242任务、四类输入，用规则化Scene Check对比隐藏真值与VLM为主的Screen Check评估代理视频，评测11个前沿智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控制的真实世界视频生成需要渲染的graybox代理视频来固定布局、运动与相机，而编码智能体可通过在Blender中重建4D场景来生成此类视频；但目前缺乏同时检验代理视频质量及其背后长时长、多镜头场景的基准。

Method: 构建VibePrevisBench：智能体将简短需求转为可执行4D场景并渲染代理视频。242个任务覆盖脚本、照片、续写、参考视频四类输入。双重评估：规则化Scene Check重跑代码并在九个维度上与隐藏真值事实比对；VLM Screen Check判断通过的事实能否在代理视频中被识别。分析11个前沿编码智能体的结果与轨迹。

Result: 三点观察：(1) 多模态理解是区分智能体的关键，尤其在空间与相机运动理解上；(2) 所有智能体已掌握基础运镜与调度，但在高难度请求上失败；(3) 智能体会检查自己的渲染结果，却未能纠正错误。

Conclusion: 智能体需要更强的空间与相机的视觉理解、对高难度请求的显式检查，以及逐项将渲染与简报核对的验证机制。

Abstract: Controllable real-world video generation models can condition on a rendered graybox proxy video, which fixes layout, motion and camera. Coding agents can build such videos by reconstructing a 4D scene in Blender, yet no benchmark checks both these videos and the long, multi-shot scenes behind them. We introduce VibePrevisBench, a benchmark for 4D scene reconstruction: an agent turns a brief into an executable 4D scene and renders its proxy video. Its 242 tasks span four input kinds, including scripts, photos, continuations, and reference videos. Each delivery is scored along two evaluation dimensions. Rule-based Scene Check re-runs the code and compares the exported 4D scene with hidden ground truth facts in nine dimensions. VLM-based Screen Check asks a VLM judge whether the facts that pass can be recognized in the proxy video. We analyze the evaluation results and trajectories of 11 frontier coding agents and identify three key observations. First, multimodal understanding separates the agents, especially in understanding space and camera motion. Second, all agents seem to already master basic camera work and staging, and fail at challenging requests. Third, agents inspect their own renders but fail to correct their errors. Agents thus need better visual understanding of space and camera, explicit checks for challenging requests, and item-by-item verification of renders against the brief.

</details>


### [83] [Fine-Grained Generative Hallucination Detection and Correction for Aerial Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15809)
*Debolena Basak,Somsubhra De,Maunendra Sankar Desarkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量、无参考的无人机航拍图像描述幻觉检测与纠正框架，基于InternVL3与Qwen2.5-VL检测并修正生成描述中的幻觉内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在航拍图像描述中易产生幻觉，且航拍存在极端视角变化、小目标、类别不平衡与实体歧义等问题，加剧幻觉；现有方法依赖GPT-4等昂贵外部模型（在航拍域表现差）或将问题简化为二分类，忽略歧义情形的不确定性。

Method: 利用InternVL3、Qwen2.5-VL作为检测器，以极小额外算力在自由形式描述中定位幻觉片段；再由专门的refinement模块纠正，提升与视觉输入的事实一致性；整体为无参考、轻量框架。

Result: 在Labelled Illusion Dataset (LID)上实验显示显著降低幻觉率，并保持资源受限环境下实时部署的实用性。

Conclusion: 该无参考检测-纠正框架可作为航拍图像描述幻觉抑制的实用方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Aerial imagery captured by unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) plays a vital role in applications such as surveillance, disaster response, and large-scale environmental monitoring. Automatic generation of textual descriptions for such imagery is essential for reducing manual inspection overhead and enabling better downstream decision-making. However, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), despite their impressive captioning capabilities, are prone to hallucination, producing descriptions that are inconsistent with the visual input. This problem is significantly amplified in aerial imagery due to its unique challenges such as extreme viewpoint variation, small objects, class imbalance, and ambiguous visual entities. Existing hallucination detection approaches either rely on computationally expensive large external models such as GPT-4, which perform poorly on aerial domains, or treat the problem as a rigid binary classification task, ignoring the inherent uncertainty in ambiguous cases. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight, reference-free framework for hallucination detection and correction in free-form aerial image captioning. Our method leverages state-of-the-art MLLMs, specifically InternVL3 and Qwen2.5-VL, to detect hallucinated content in generated captions with minimal computational overhead. Detected hallucinations are corrected through a dedicated refinement module that improves the factual consistency of the original captions. Extensive experiments on the Labelled Illusion Dataset (LID) demonstrate that our framework significantly reduces hallucination rates while remaining practical for real-time deployment in resource-constrained environments. Code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [84] [Where to Generate Touch: Do Image Latents Preserve Material Recognition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15834)
*Wanghao Ye,Aarosh Das,Sihan Chen,Yiting Wang,Bowei Tian,Guoheng Sun,Shwai He,Zheyu Shen,Ziyao Wang,Yexiao He,Zhaoyi Liu,Meng Liu,Yuning Zhang,Meng Feng,Ziyi Wang,Yilong Dai,Yifei Dong,Siyuan Peng,Zhenle Duan,Joshua Liu,Lang Xiong,Daeun Jung,Aditya Sarkar,Parastoo Pilevar,Xiaochuan Gong,Yancheng Zhu,Yingying Fan,Qiong Wang,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过冻结核验（用真实GelSight训练的材质分类器与物体识别器检验编码-解码重建的可分性）来选择触觉生成器的潜空间，最终选定SD-VAE而非16x离散tokenizer，并据此训练图文到触觉的生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式触觉可突破真实接触数据的覆盖限制，但触觉生成器必须依赖某个潜空间；现有图像/视频离散tokenizer是否保留触觉判别信息此前未被系统评估。

Method: 在ObjectFolder Real、Touch and Go、DIGIT YCB-Slide上用真实GelSight按压训练材质/物体分类器，测其在三种16x离散tokenizer与SD-VAE重建图上的精度损失；再用无新标签的重建数据重训分类器以量化弥合程度；最后在SD-VAE潜空间训练照片到触觉的生成器。

Result: ObjectFolder Real上离散tokenizer重建使分类器下降6.5–9.7点，SD-VAE仅降0.3点；离散模型在Touch and Go与YCB-Slide上损失更大；重建数据重训可缩小离散差距；SD-VAE重建物体匹配率76.7%（真实触觉78.9%，MAGVIT-v2 67.0%）。

Conclusion: 以"判别性保持"作为潜空间选择的工程准则有效，SD-VAE比重建型离散tokenizer更适合触觉潜空间；但论文仅为潜空间选择与初步生成器验证，代码尚未发布，缺少真机闭环任务证据。

Abstract: Generated touch could extend robot learning beyond recorded contacts. A tactile generator needs a latent space, and we choose one by testing whether a material classifier trained on real GelSight presses still works on encode-decode reconstructions from pretrained image models. On ObjectFolder Real, the classifier loses 6.5 to 9.7 points on reconstructions from three 16x discrete tokenizers and 0.3 points on those from the Stable Diffusion VAE (SD-VAE). The 16x tokenizers again lose far more on Touch and Go and on DIGIT object recognition (YCB-Slide). Retraining the classifier on reconstructions, with no new labels, narrows the discrete gap. SD-VAE reconstructions also keep object identity, with 76.7% object-matching accuracy against 78.9% for real touch and 67.0% for MAGVIT-v2. We therefore select SD-VAE latents and show a photo-to-touch generator trained in them. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [85] [Open Vision Banana: A Fully Open Recipe for Generative Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15844)
*Arpit Jadon,Valentinos Pariza,Jona Ruthardt,Ryousuke Yamada,A. Sophia Koepke,Andrei Bursuc,Yuki M. Asano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OVB 给出将预训练图像生成器改造为统一多任务感知系统的完全开源配方，分析色彩编码、提示设计、参数适配、任务平衡等关键设计，并在 Cityscapes 零样本上超过 Vision Banana（73.9 vs 69.9 mIoU），提出 legend ensembling 测试时缩放方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Banana 把视觉任务建模为条件图像编辑，但数据、权重、代码与实现细节均未公开，无法复现与推进；作者希望提供一个完全开放、可复现的生成器式统一感知方案，并厘清影响泛化的关键设计选择。

Method: 基于 4B 与 9B 开源权重生成模型，使用公开数据集的 2.1M–3.8M 训练对，将分割/深度/指代与推理分割等任务统一为条件图像编辑；系统分析颜色编码、prompt 设计、参数适配方式与任务平衡的交互效应；提出 legend ensembling：在多种类-颜色图例下分别分割并按多数投票融合，属于面向生成式感知的测试时缩放方法。

Result: Cityscapes 零样本 mIoU 73.9 超过 Vision Banana 的 69.9，并接近 SOTA 仿射不变深度估计器；非零样本设置在指代与推理分割上差距 5 点以内，实例分割仍落后较多；保留基础模型的图像编辑能力；legend ensembling 无需重训即可提升 mIoU 最多 8.7（Cityscapes）与 15.0（COCO-Object）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明开放权重生成器可复现并超越闭源生成式感知系统，设计选择之间存在耦合、需联合权衡；测试时多图例集成是一种低成本、可泛化的增益手段；全部数据构建管线、训练评测代码与各阶段权重开源。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that pretrained image generators encode real-world understanding beyond image synthesis. Vision Banana exploits this by framing vision tasks as conditional image editing. However, its data, weights, code, and implementation details are not public. We present Open Vision Banana (OVB), a fully open and reproducible recipe for converting pretrained generators into unified multi-task perception systems. Our analyses identify the design choices that matter most for generalization, including color encoding, prompt design, parameter adaptation, and task balancing. These choices interact, so individually helpful choices can hurt when combined with others. Starting from 4B and 9B parameter open-weight base models and using 2.1M-3.8M training pairs from public datasets, OVB surpasses Vision Banana's reported Cityscapes zero-shot performance (73.9 vs. 69.9 mIoU) and rivals state-of-the-art affine-invariant depth estimators. Its non-zero-shot variants come within five points of Vision Banana on referring and reasoning segmentation but remain further behind on instance segmentation. All models largely retain the base model's image-editing abilities. With legend ensembling, we introduce a test-time scaling method for generator-based perception. It segments an image under several class-color legends and fuses the results by majority vote, correcting unstable color-class bindings and improving mIoU by up to 8.7 points on Cityscapes and 15.0 points on COCO-Object without retraining. We release the dataset construction and processing pipeline, the training and evaluation code, as well as the final, intermediate, and ablation checkpoints. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [86] [Lang-SVG: Hierarchical Image Vectorization with Language Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15941)
*Xi Liu,Chaoyi Zhou,Run Wang,Jiaang Li,Feng Luo,Junxiang Huang,Siyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lang-SVG：以显式树结构组织带语义标签与父子关系的SVG图元，结合可控粒度开放词汇分割先验、树上下文驱动的MLLM标注、深度自适应简化与树引导剪枝合并，实现兼顾渲染保真与对象-部件层级可编辑性的矢量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像矢量化方法以像素级渲染保真为优化目标，生成的SVG缺乏与人类感知的对象-部件层级对齐，图元无语义、难编辑。作者希望让矢量化输出成为结构清晰、可语义操控的代码表征。

Method: 1) 显式SVG树结构（语义标签+父子关系）；2) 粒度可控、开放词汇分割先验初始化对象-部件层级；3) 利用带树上下文的多模态大模型为未标注节点赋语义标签；4) 深度自适应简化降低形状复杂度；5) Tree-Guided Pruning and Merging 消除冗余节点；6) L1-SAM-Prompting 恢复缺失区域以提升保真与结构完整性；7) 提出衡量结构质量与语义部件对齐的新评测协议。

Result: 实验表明在渲染保真度与结构质量两项目标上均达到SOTA；并给出结构质量与语义部件对齐的可量化评测协议。

Conclusion: 将矢量化从"像素拟合"推进到"语义结构化"，使SVG成为可分层编辑的图像代码表征；语义分割先验+MLLM标注+树引导简化的组合是有效路径。

Abstract: Image vectorization reconstructs raster images as compact scalable vector graph- ics (SVG), providing a structured and editable code representation of images. However, most existing vectorization methods optimize for pixel-level render- ing fidelity, often producing SVGs that lack alignment with human-perceived object-part hierarchies, making them difficult to manipulate. To address this, we propose Lang-SVG, which introduces an explicit tree structure to organize SVG primitives with semantic labels and parent-child relationships. Lang-SVG incorporates granularity-controllable and open-vocabulary segmentation priors to initialize object-part hierarchies and leverages multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with SVG tree context to assign semantic labels to unlabeled nodes. To reduce redundancy, we introduce depth-adaptive simplification to reduce shape complexity and Tree-Guided Pruning and Merging to eliminate redundant SVG nodes. L1-SAM-Prompting further recovers missing regions to improve recon- struction fidelity and structural completeness. Beyond reconstruction fidelity, we introduce a new evaluation protocol that measures structural quality and semantic part alignment. Experiments demonstrate that Lang-SVG achieves state-of-the-art performance in both rendering fidelity and structural quality. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [87] [DuoJEPA: Cross-Modal Translation Between Unaligned Self-Supervised Embedding Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15968)
*Mahmut S. Gokmen,Mitchell A. Klusty,Evan W. Damron,Caroline N. Leach,Samuel E. Armstrong,V.K. Cody Bumgardner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuoJEPA 让每个模态用自监督 LeJEPA 各自训练编码器，再各加一层 transformer 作为"翻译器"把嵌入映射到对方空间，对比损失只在目标空间内计算，两模态自身嵌入空间不直接对齐。实验发现残余连接决定跨模态压力落在何处：带残差时两编码器最终直接对齐，去掉残差(skipless)时配对余弦检验检测不到对齐却仍有相当跨模态性能，且翻译近似为源空间的单一缩放旋转。在 CheXpert Plus 胸片-报告上零样本 AUC 86.6、检索 78.5（对比 LeVLJEPA 重实现 60.0/64.2）；Sentinel-2/1 上冻结编码器达公开 Sentinel 基座模型水平，4000 候选跨传感器检索命中 77-79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流跨模态学习默认"对齐即目标"——训练两个编码器使配对样本嵌入重合。作者质疑这一等价性，想检验跨模态检索与分类是否可以在两编码器自身嵌入空间保持不直接对齐的前提下获得，并厘清"对齐"究竟来自何处。

Method: 每模态一个编码器，各自用自监督 LeJEPA 目标训练；每个编码器后接单层 transformer 翻译器，将本模态嵌入映射到对方编码器空间，对比损失完全在目标空间内计算；两模态自身空间之间无任何直接比较项，仅通过翻译目标联合优化。对比 residual 与 skipless 两种翻译器，并用配对余弦检验度量对齐程度；分析类 token 翻译的结构（近似单一缩放旋转）。

Result: 带残差翻译器时两编码器直接对齐；skipless 时配对余弦检验在最终模型中未检出对齐，消融中至多弱对齐，而跨模态性能相当。CheXpert Plus 胸片-报告：零样本 AUC 86.6、检索 78.5，显著高于同编码器同数据的 LeVLJEPA 重实现（60.0/64.2）。Sentinel-2/Sentinel-1：冻结编码器在 Copernicus-Bench 线性探针复现下处于公开 Sentinel 基座模型区间，跨传感器检索在 4000 候选中命中 77-79%，patch 级扩展可迁移图内相似度结构。

Conclusion: 跨模态能力与嵌入空间"直接对齐"可以解耦：对齐并非跨模态性能的必要条件，而是由翻译器残差结构诱导的吸收路径所决定。单层翻译近似为源空间的缩放旋转，提示跨模态桥梁可极简。该结论对多模态表征学习的设计原则（尤其医学与遥感双传感器场景）有方法学价值，但缺少物理智能/具身方向证据。

Abstract: Learning from paired modalities is commonly equated with aligning them: two encoders are trained until the embeddings of a pair coincide in a shared space. We study whether cross-modal retrieval and classification can instead be obtained while the two encoders' own embedding spaces remain directly unaligned. DuoJEPA trains one encoder per modality with the self-supervised LeJEPA objective and appends to each encoder a single transformer layer that translates its embedding into the other encoder's space, supervised by a contrastive objective computed entirely within that target space. No loss term compares the two own spaces directly, although the encoders are optimised jointly through the translation objective. In our experiments, the translator's residual connections determine where the cross-modal pressure is absorbed: with a residual translator the two encoders become directly aligned, whereas with a skipless (residual-free) translator a paired-cosine test detects no alignment in the final models, and at most a weak one in the ablations, at comparable cross-modal performance. The learned class-token translation is well approximated by a single scaled rotation of the source space. We evaluate DuoJEPA on two modality pairs that differ in nearly every respect. On chest radiographs and reports from CheXpert Plus, it attains a zero-shot AUC of 86.6 and a cross-modal retrieval score of 78.5, compared with 60.0 and 64.2 for our reimplementation of LeVLJEPA with the same encoders and data. On Sentinel-2 optical and Sentinel-1 radar imagery, the frozen encoders fall within the range of published Sentinel foundation models under our reproduction of the Copernicus-Bench linear-probe protocol, the translator retrieves the paired image of the other sensor among 4,000 candidates in 77-79% of cases, and a patch-level extension transfers the within-image similarity structure of the scene.

</details>


### [88] [TRACE: Trajectory Synchronization for Training-Free Resolution Extension in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15983)
*Libo Zhu,Fan Li,Zhixin Wang,Binglu Wang,Weisheng Dong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE：免训练的视频超分辨率生成方法，通过同步低分辨率与高分辨率去噪轨迹实现高分辨率视频生成，无需高分辨率模型推理，配合局部窗口注意力与重复主体校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频模型多局限于低分辨率；高分辨率重训成本高昂，而现有免训练方法在细节、场景结构和效率上仍不足。

Method: 免训练框架：早期去噪阶段在对应噪声水平上对齐低/高分辨率的预测场景内容；由低分辨率预测与当前高分辨率状态直接计算高分辨率更新（省去高分辨率模型推理）；保留高分辨率轨迹中的细粒度变化以生成新细节；用局部窗口注意力在共享高分辨率隐状态上精修细节；对疑似重复主体与低分辨率布局比对，仅校正选定区域及其求解器历史，保持其余区域细节。

Result: 在多种视频生成骨干与分辨率上，感知质量和文-视频对齐优于 SOTA 免训练方法 FreeSwim，端到端加速 2.0–2.9 倍。

Conclusion: 轨迹同步式免训练高分辨率视频生成可在不重训、无高分辨率推理开销的前提下同时提升质量与效率，代码与模型将开源。

Abstract: More text-to-video models are available, but many remain limited to low resolutions. Training them for higher resolutions is costly, while existing training-free methods still struggle with fine detail, scene structure, and efficiency. We propose TRACE, a training-free method that synchronizes low- and high-resolution generation trajectories. During early denoising, TRACE aligns predicted scene content at corresponding noise levels. It computes high-resolution updates from low-resolution predictions and the current high-resolution state, without high-resolution model inference. The high-resolution trajectory retains fine-grained variation for generating new details. Local window attention then refines fine details in a shared high-resolution latent state. TRACE checks suspected duplicate subjects against the corresponding low-resolution layout and corrects selected regions and their solver history while preserving detail elsewhere. Experiments across all kinds of video generation backbones and resolutions show improved perceptual quality and text-video alignment over FreeSwim, a state-of-the-art training-free method, with 2.0-2.9x end-to-end speedups. Our code and models will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [89] [Adaptive Distance Detection and Adaptive Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15991)
*Kai Yi Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在FPS游戏CS: Condition Zero中对比YOLOv5s目标检测与定制语义分割模型检测敌人的推理速度与mask精度，并提出"自适应距离检测"融合两种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPS游戏自动瞄准/敌人检测场景中，目标检测推理快但只给边界框，语义分割更准但慢；作者希望量化这一权衡并给出融合方案。

Method: 用YOLOv5s训练目标检测模型；自定制语义分割架构（逐像素）进行敌人分割；对比两者推理时间与形状检测精度；提出"Adaptive distance detection"按距离自适应融合检测与分割，并提出"Adaptive instance segmentation"融合语义分割与全景分割。

Result: 目标检测推理略快，语义分割较慢但对目标精确形状的识别更好；作者认为随着模型规模增大，这一速度差距会指数性扩大。

Conclusion: 在精度与速度的权衡下，单一模型不足，需采用自适应融合策略；文中仅提出思路，融合方法的定量结果与实现细节未在摘要中体现。

Abstract: This paper is about object detection model and semantic segmentation model in an FPS game Counter Strike: Condition Zero to detect enemies. Semantic segmentation is pixel-wise detection algorithm. We customized the architecture for semantic segmentation. We used YOLOv5s to train object detection model. After training, the inference time of semantic segmentation was compared to object detection. It was observed that object detection was slightly faster and semantic segmentation is slower but better at detecting the exact shape of the object. However, we found out that this slight difference of inference can grow exponentially if the both models get more complex. To solve this, we propose a novel approach called Adaptive distance detection which combines both algorithms. We will also introduce Adaptive instance segmentation to combine semantic segmentation and panoptic segmentation.

</details>


### [90] [Predicting the Needle in a Petabyte Scale Haystack: Open-Vocabulary Event Anticipation in Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16000)
*Shreelekha Revankar,Mikhail Klassen,Creon Levit,Ash Hoover,Kavita Bala,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将时间变化的语义表示为视觉语言模型embedding的向量差，在潜空间中搜索语义变换，从而自动挖掘全球事件数据，并训练出可预测“何处/将变成什么/何时完成”的全球尺度卫星事件预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星可日频成像，但现有系统无法在开放词表下同时回答变化位置、变化结果与完成时间；而事件样本在全球尺度上极其稀疏，人工标注不可行。因此需要一个可自动发现事件数据并支持开放词表事件预测的框架。

Method: 用不同时刻VLM embedding的向量差表征变化，在潜空间做语义变换检索/搜索；据此构建无需人工标注的自动数据引擎，挖掘全球事件；构建21,000+地点、64+事件类型、约2M张PlanetScope与Sentinel-2图像、覆盖六大洲的卫星事件数据集；在該数据上训练全球尺度事件预测模型，联合输出变化检测、事件类型检索与完成日期回归。

Result: 变化检测最大F1为93.5%；预测事件检索优于基线3.6倍；完成日期预测中位误差3天，且可提前九个月预测。

Conclusion: embedding差分的“算术”表述是有效的开放词表事件发现与预测归纳偏置，可把标注成本转化为潜空间检索，并支撑跨大洲、多事件类型的长期事件预测。

Abstract: Satellite constellations now image the Earth daily, capturing the lifecycle of events from deforestation to urban construction. Anticipating these events before completion enables timely intervention, yet existing systems cannot jointly identify where a change occurs, what it will become, and when it will finish across an open vocabulary. Building such a model requires diverse event data, but events are like needles in a haystack, making manual annotation at global scale infeasible. We address this problem by representing change as the vector difference between vision-language model embeddings at distinct time points. This arithmetic approach allows us to search for semantic transformations directly in the latent space, enabling (1) an automated data engine for global event discovery without manual annotation and (2) the largest global satellite event dataset to our knowledge, comprising 21,000+ locations across 64+ event types with ~2M images from PlanetScope and Sentinel-2 spanning six continents, to train (3) a novel global-scale event anticipation model. Our model detects change with a 93.5% max F1 score, outperforms baselines 3.6x on predicted event retrieval, and forecasts completion dates with a 3-day median error, even nine months before completion. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [91] [Iris-Z: Scaling One Generative Model Past Specialist Geometry Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16010)
*Alejandro Cañada,Chema Garabito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Iris-Z：单一通用图像生成骨干同时生成外观与几何，统一单目深度估计、图像+深度联合生成与深度条件图像生成，规模化后超越专用SOTA深度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受"苦涩的教训"启发，主张视觉与3D/空间理解应放弃任务专用架构，转而投入统一生成式通用架构并扩大规模；以长期被专用架构占据的单目几何为验证任务。

Method: 单一backbone统一去噪、推理并理解图像与深度；一套权重支持三种模式：(1) 同时生成图像及其深度，(2) 从照片估计深度，(3) 在给定深度上绘制图像；不引入任何专用层。

Result: 在足够规模下，于成熟深度基准上超过SOTA专用方法，同时保留原骨干的生成能力；向社区发布两个模型。

Conclusion: 通用可扩展的生成式架构足以覆盖几何任务，规模与通用性优于任务专用设计，支持视觉智能的统一路线。

Abstract: Language moved from many task-specific models to one unified model: a single generative model with a single generative training task that handles all of them within the same backbone, and keeps improving with data, parameters and compute. We believe the bitter lesson applies just as much to computer vision, 3D and spatial understanding, in the end to visual intelligence: the effort should go into general architectures that solve all these tasks in a unified way, and into scaling them, rather than into task-specific designs. We test this on monocular geometry, a task that has belonged to specialised architectures for years. Iris-Z is a general image generator that produces appearance and geometry together. A single backbone denoises, reasons about and understands the image and its depth at the same time, and one set of weights generates images with their depth, estimates depth from photographs and paints images onto a given depth. At enough scale it surpasses state-of-the-art specialists on established depth benchmarks while keeping the generative abilities of the backbone it started from without specialized layers. We release two models for the community.

</details>


### [92] [Topology-Aware 4D Gaussian Splatting for Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16012)
*Leonardo Vanni,Stefano Gasperini,Federico Tombari,Chiara Plizzari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoEgo benchmark + Topo-4DGS: part-level rigid motion with dynamic Gaussian activation/deactivation to reconstruct topological changes (split/merge/open/close) in monocular egocentric 4D reconstruction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing 4DGS deforms a fixed point set, so cutting, assembly, opening/closing (topology changes) produce severe artifacts; egocentric interaction reconstruction is key for embodied AI.

Method: Assign independent rigid transformations to interacting parts for part-level motion; dynamically activate/deactivate Gaussians to model time-varying surface support as surfaces appear, disappear, or separate. Release TopoEgo benchmark covering fission, fusion, open, close.

Result: Topo-4DGS significantly outperforms SOTA 4DGS baselines on topological changes, especially on post-event frames.

Conclusion: Topology-aware representation is necessary for egocentric dynamic interaction reconstruction; benchmark and method to be open-sourced.

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic scenes and objects from monocular egocentric video is crucial for embodied AI, yet existing 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) approaches struggle with everyday interactions involving topological changes, such as cutting, assembly, opening, or closing. Since 4DGS methods rely on deforming a fixed representation, events that split, merge, or reveal surfaces cause severe visual artifacts. To address this challenge, we introduce TopoEgo, the first benchmark dedicated to topological changes in egocentric video (covering fission, fusion, open, and close), alongside Topo-4DGS, a topology-aware reconstruction method. Topo4DGS jointly models part-level motion and time-varying surface support by assigning independent rigid transformations to interacting parts and dynamically activating or deactivating Gaussians as surfaces appear, disappear, or separate. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Topo-4DGS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art 4DGS approaches across topological changes, especially on post-event frames. Code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [93] [Sub-actions in Action: Text-Guided Hand-Role Alignment for Sub-Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16056)
*Sadegh Rahmaniboldaji,Filip Rybansky,Quoc Vuong,Frank Guerin,Andrew Gilbert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BiScopeNet：不改动视觉编码器与语言模型，用门控文本引导注意力模块 FocusAlign 与掩码、物体加权损失 MetaLoss 增强视频 LLM 对第一人称动作中左右手角色及被操作物体的辨识，并构建 BiScopeCap-100K 稠密字幕数据集进行监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 LLM 在第一人称视频中难以区分左右手各自扮演的角色，缺乏对该细粒度能力的显式建模与监督数据。

Method: 提出子动作检测任务（显式识别左右手角色与被操作物体）；设计 BiScopeNet 侧路增强框架，含 FocusAlign（门控文本引导注意力，选择性对齐视频特征与文本上下文）与 MetaLoss（掩码的物体加权损失，强化视觉可落地词项）；构建 BiScopeCap-100K 视频级稠密字幕数据集，联合描述高层动作、手角色、操作物体并附空间元数据。

Result: 在多个 Video-LLM 骨干上，动作、手角色与物体识别均优于对应有监督基线，在 BiScopeCap 上取得所评估配置中最强的综合表现。

Conclusion: 无需修改骨干结构即可通过轻量适配模块与针对性数据提升第一人称手-物交互理解，可作为手-物交互重建/机器人数据标注的上游感知组件；但缺少真实机器人验证与三维接触重建。

Abstract: Our objective is to enhance VLMs ability to distinguish the roles of the left and right hands in egocentric actions. We introduce sub-action detection, a task that requires models to explicitly recognise the left- and right-hand roles and their manipulated objects within an ongoing high-level action. To address this challenge, we propose BiScopeNet, a framework that augments existing video-LLMs without modifying their vision encoders or language models. BiScopeNet incorporates two components: FocusAlign, a gated text-guided attention-based module that selectively aligns video features with textual context to emphasise action-relevant cues, and MetaLoss, a masked, object-weighted loss that prioritises visually grounded terms. To supervise sub-action detection, we introduce BiScopeCap-100K, a large-scale video-level dataset curated from existing egocentric datasets, providing dense captions that jointly describe high-level actions, left/right-hand roles, and manipulated objects, enriched with spatial metadata for grounding. Across the evaluated Video-LLM backbones, BiScopeNet improves action, hand-role, and object recognition over the corresponding supervised baselines, achieving the strongest aggregate performance among the evaluated configurations on BiScopeCap.

</details>


### [94] [Pothole Risk Assessment with Geometrically Grounded Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16061)
*Abdalmalek Aburaddaha,Zaid A. El Shair,Ali Al-Bustami,Samir A. Rawashdeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出五阶段模块化框架，对已检测的坑洞做离线严重度评估：透视变换+零样本SAM分割+几何特征+Gemini 2.5 Pro分级与复合风险评分+自然语言预警生成；严重度分级与人工/真值一致性弱，但几何接地显著改变预警等级且人工偏好更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有坑洞检测器仅输出"位置"，不提供严重度与处置建议；单张RGB判断严重度本身病态，而无几何接地约束的VLM评估往往过于保守。

Method: 五阶段流水线：透视变换校正、零样本SAM分割、几何特征提取、Gemini 2.5 Pro严重度分类与复合风险打分、自然语言预警生成。评估：3,092个坑洞/1,119个行车记录场景，对照三名标注员独立面板与独立RGB-D数据集实测深度；六臂prompt消融；盲测评比。

Result: 与三标注员面板一致性弱（κ=0.139，95%CI [0.00,0.28]，n=101），远低于标注员彼此一致性（κ=0.605）；与RGB-D实测深度一致性亦弱（κ=0.098），但预测类别保持了深度序关系。几何接地使26.7%场景退出高紧急层级，紧急层级判定增加109%（Stuart-Maxwell p<10^-15）；盲评中三位评分者以3.5:1偏好接地的预警。六臂消融显示规则遵循来自逐坑洞测量而非评分标准或示例。

Conclusion: 该分级应视为"感知视觉类别"而非深度测量；几何接地主要提升预警可操作性与规则遵循，成本约USD 0.105/场景，适合大规模离线基础设施巡检；机动建议仅为研究性输出，非经验证的车辆指引。

Abstract: Potholes impose billions of dollars in annual vehicle damage, yet detectors report only where potholes are, not how severe they are or what to do. Judging severity from a single RGB image is ill-posed, and ungrounded vision-language model (VLM) assessments tend to be over-conservative. We present a five-stage modular framework for the offline assessment of already-detected potholes that combines geometric measurements with VLM reasoning: perspective transformation, zero-shot SAM segmentation, geometric feature extraction, Gemini 2.5 Pro severity classification with composite risk scoring, and natural-language warning generation. We evaluate it on 3,092 potholes in 1,119 dashcam scenes against independent references. Severity agreement with a prediction-free three-annotator panel is weak (Cohen's $\kappa = 0.139$, 95% CI [0.00, 0.28]; $n = 101$) and far below the annotators' agreement with one another ($\kappa = 0.605$); against physically measured depth on an independent RGB-D dataset, it is also weak ($\kappa = 0.098$), although the predicted classes preserve the order of measured depths. The severity classes are therefore perceived visual categories, not depth measurements. Geometric grounding does change the warnings: relative to an image-only baseline, it moves 26.7% of scenes out of the high-urgency tier and raises critical-tier assignments by 109% (Stuart-Maxwell $p < 10^{-15}$), and in a blind study, three raters preferred the grounded warnings 3.5:1. A six-arm prompt ablation attributes the hybrid prompt's rule adherence to the per-pothole measurements rather than to the rubric or worked examples. At USD 0.105 per scene, it suits large-scale offline infrastructure assessment; its maneuver suggestions are advisory research outputs, not validated vehicle guidance.

</details>


### [95] [Split-and-scale Latent 3D Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16126)
*Dorian Chan,Muhammed Kocabas,Xiaoming Zhao,Oncel Tuzel,Jen-Hao Rick Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 split-and-scale：利用预训练3D tokenizer将场景表为连续函数，推理时对任意小区域重复应用同一tokenizer以扩展隐表示容量，在受限内存下获得高细节重建与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D隐表示容量受限，难以刻画复杂物体细节；训练时扩大容量代价高且受显存限制，推理时朴素增加latent数量又无法有效利用。

Method: 把3D场景表示为3D坐标的连续函数，推理时把场景切分为小sub-region，用同一预训练tokenizer对每个子区域编码，从而在更小空间范围上分配同等latent容量；并进行关于算力、内存、latent容量与重建质量的消融研究。

Result: 推理时缩放的重建质量可媲美超过单卡显存极限的更大模型；进一步训练自回归3D生成器，性能与SOTA基线相当。

Conclusion: 隐容量可通过推理时切分-缩放高效扩展，兼容训练成本约束，并支持高质量3D生成建模。

Abstract: Latent 3D representations have significantly expanded the capabilities of modern shape modeling, enabling compact encoding of 3D scenes and supporting powerful 3D generative models. Despite this progress, current representations struggle to capture the fine details of complex objects, fundamentally limited by the capacity of their latents. However, scaling latent capacity at training time is computationally expensive and often infeasible under memory limits, and naively increasing the number of latents at inference fails to make effective use of the added capacity. In this paper, we address both challenges with \textit{split-and-scale}, a method that effectively expands latent capacity at inference while keeping training cost tractable. Our key observation is that by representing 3D scenes as continuous functions of 3D coordinates, a single pretrained tokenizer can be applied at inference to arbitrarily small sub-regions of a scene, allocating the same latent capacity to a smaller spatial extent and thus capturing far higher detail. We validate this insight by conducting extensive ablations to study the trade-offs between compute, memory, latent capacity, and reconstruction quality, demonstrating our inference-time scaling method achieves quality competitive with much larger models that exceed single-GPU memory limits. We further demonstrate that split-and-scale supports high-quality generative modeling, training an autoregressive 3D generator that performs competitively with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [96] [GOOSELoc: A Multimodal Benchmark for Ground-to-Aerial Localization in Unstructured Off-Road Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16162)
*Fabian Deuser,Peter Mortimer,Robin-Nico Kampa,Anna Bößendörfer,Mirko Mählisch,Martin Werner,Norbert Oswald*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GOOSELoc：面向森林、田野、乡村道路等非结构化环境的跨视角地面-航空定位多模态基准（9,356 场景、四季、正射影像+环视RGB+3D LiDAR），并给出融合环视影像与LiDAR几何的多模态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有度量级跨视角定位基准（VIGOR、cross-view KITTI）以城市与道路场景为主，富含人造结构，缺乏越野/非结构化地形；而GNSS在树冠遮挡、城市峡谷下不可靠，需要评估方法在自然场景中的泛化能力。

Method: 在慕尼黑周边采集9,356个场景，配准航空正射影像与环视RGB、3D LiDAR点云；提供铺装/非铺装标注与四季重复采集以分析地形结构与外观差异（正射影像多为夏季）。将四种SOTA跨视角定位基线适配并微调至该基准，进而提出融合环视影像与LiDAR几何特征的多模态基线。

Result: 即使经过任务特定微调，现有方法在越野非结构化环境中性能显著下降；所提多模态影像+LiDAR基线取得更优定位性能，为后续工作提供更强参照。数据与基线已开源。

Conclusion: 跨视角定位在自然非结构化场景存在明显域差距，单模态RGB方案不足；多模态几何融合是提升越野定位鲁棒性的有效方向，该基准为后续研究提供了评测平台。

Abstract: Cross-view pose estimation estimates a ground camera's geographic pose by matching onboard sensor data with geo-referenced overhead imagery. This is particularly relevant when GNSS is unreliable, for example under forest canopy or in urban canyons. Existing metric cross-view benchmarks, such as VIGOR and cross-view KITTI, are dominated by urban and road scenes with strong human-made structure without off-road terrain. We introduce GOOSELoc, a multimodal benchmark for ground-to-aerial localization in unstructured environments, including forests, fields, and rural tracks. GOOSELoc comprises 9,356 scenes recorded across all four seasons around Munich, Germany, pairing geo-referenced aerial orthophotos with surround-view RGB imagery and 3D LiDAR point clouds. Paved/unpaved labels enable analyzing the impact of terrain structure, while the seasonal recordings allow studying the appearance gap to mostly summer-captured orthophotos. To assess the transferability of existing methods, we adapt and fine-tune four state-of-the-art cross-view localization baselines on GOOSELoc. Despite task-specific fine-tuning, the performance degrades substantially in off-road environments. We therefore propose a multimodal baseline combining surround-view imagery and LiDAR geometry, improving localization performance and providing a stronger reference for future work. Data and baselines are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [97] [SPEM: Scan-Prior Entropy Modeling for LiDAR Point Cloud Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16212)
*Bojun Liu,Yangzhi Ma,Jie Li,Dong Liu,Li Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPEM 提出"扫描先验只用于熵模型、不改变几何表示"的 LiDAR 几何压缩框架：用多频球面旋转位置编码在自注意力与解码上下文检索中注入扫描规律，并配合基于可复用 KV 缓存的解码记忆自回归预测，在无损/有损压缩上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 scan-aware 方法把几何转换到球面/beam 对齐坐标系来利用扫描规律，导致采集先验与几何表示耦合，改变了空间划分与量化，可能无法精确恢复原始笛卡尔几何。作者主张采集先验应指导概率建模，而非决定编码表示。

Method: 1) 无需坐标变换的 Scan-Prior Entropy Modeling：直接对笛卡尔占用做熵编码，仅用扫描先验调制概率分布；2) 多频球面旋转位置编码（multi-frequency spherical rotary positional encoding），在 backbone 自注意力与解码上下文检索中刻画扫描对齐的空间关系；3) 解码记忆自回归预测器，通过可复用 key/value 缓存高效利用已解码占用信息。

Result: 在有损与无损 LiDAR 几何压缩任务上均达到 state-of-the-art，表明扫描先验建模可跨坐标表示生效，且无需坐标变换即可获得收益。

Conclusion: 扫描先验可作为概率建模的软引导而非硬性表示约束，从而在保持原始笛卡尔几何可精确恢复的同时获得压缩增益；该思路可推广到其他依赖采集先验的点云编码任务。

Abstract: LiDAR point clouds exhibit scan-induced spatial regularities that are informative for geometry compression. Existing scan-aware approaches often exploit these regularities by transforming coded geometry into spherical or beam-aligned coordinate systems. However, this couples acquisition priors with geometry representation, altering spatial partitioning and quantization, and potentially preventing exact recovery of the original Cartesian geometry. We argue that acquisition priors should guide probability modeling rather than dictate the coding representation. We propose SPEM, a Scan-Prior Entropy Modeling framework that exploits LiDAR scanning regularities within the entropy model without requiring coordinate transformation. We introduce a multi-frequency spherical rotary positional encoding that captures scan-aligned spatial relationships in both backbone self-attention and decoded-context retrieval. Building upon this encoding, we develop a decoded-memory autoregressive predictor that efficiently exploits previously decoded occupancy information through reusable key/value caches. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both lossy and lossless LiDAR geometry compression, highlighting the effectiveness of scan-prior modeling across different coordinate representations.

</details>


### [98] [WorldDraft: If Code Builds the World, Can Video Models Just Render It?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16217)
*Zhiyu Hou,Wen-Jie Shu,Harold Haodong Chen,Weilin Ruan,Chenfei Liao,Liuzhou Zhang,Disen Lan,Xiaogang Xu,Qi Chen,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WorldDraft基准（432个严格配对用例、48个可执行4D世界族、6类动态挑战，3×3因子设计交叉草稿粒度与外观对齐），发现视频渲染器在世界保持与视觉实现上存在根本性张力，并提出即插即用的视觉提示策略WorldDraft-Primer缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态编码智能体将4D世界构建与视觉生成解耦，视频模型从"世界生成器"转为"生成式渲染器"。该分工引出核心问题：若代码构建世界，视频模型能否仅负责渲染？需可靠评价其"世界保持"与"视觉实现"两项能力，并诊断现有模型的失效模式。

Method: 构建WorldDraft基准，按3×3析因设计在同一动力学条件下正交交叉草稿粒度（抽象代理→previs）与外观对齐（中性→冲突）；在12个代表性模型上开展大规模评测，并据此提出WorldDraft-Primer——面向冻结视频渲染器的即插即用视觉提示策略。

Result: 评测揭示三点结论：(I) 世界保持与视觉实现存在显著操作张力；(II) 更丰富的草稿脚手架能锚定结构，却悖论性地损害外观保真度；(III) 冲突源线索引发严重source leakage（最高达64%），暴露跨模态解耦脆弱性。WorldDraft-Primer在冻结渲染器上稳定提升两项指标。

Conclusion: WorldDraft与WorldDraft-Primer为打通程序化4D仿真与视频生成奠定基础，推动物理落地的生成式渲染；但工作重心在基准与提示层，缺乏对4D动力学生成的深层方法学突破。

Abstract: Multimodal coding agents decouple 4D world construction from visual generation, repurposing video models from world generators toward generative renderers. This division of labor raises a fundamental question: if code builds the world, can video models just render it? A reliable generative renderer hinges on two complementary criteria: (i) world preservation, retaining prescribed 4D kinematics and camera paths; and (ii) visual realization, persistently binding text-specified target attributes over time. To evaluate this capability, we introduce WorldDraft, a benchmark of $432$ strictly paired test cases across $48$ executable 4D world families spanning six dynamic challenge categories. Under a $3 \times 3$ factorial design, WorldDraft orthogonally crosses draft granularity (abstract proxy to previs) and appearance alignment (neutral to conflicting) under identical dynamics. Extensive evaluations across $12$ representative models reveal that: (I) world preservation and visual realization remain in stark operational tension; (II) richer draft scaffolding anchors structure but paradoxically impairs appearance fidelity; and (III) conflicting source cues trigger severe source leakage (up to $64\%$), exposing fragile cross-modal disentanglement. To mitigate these bottlenecks, we propose WorldDraft-Primer, a plug-and-play visual prompting strategy that consistently improves both objectives across frozen video renderers. Together, WorldDraft and WorldDraft-Primer lay a foundation for bridging programmatic 4D simulation with video generation, advancing physically grounded generative rendering.

</details>


### [99] [Disentangled Embedding Optimization for Diffusion-Based Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16224)
*Hongcheng Li,Yucan Zhou,Yibin Wang,Xiaoyan Gu,Bo Li,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解耦嵌入优化（DEO）框架，用可训练的类别嵌入替代扩散式数据集蒸馏中的类别聚类，并加多样性正则，生成更多样、更具代表性的合成数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的数据集蒸馏方法用类内聚类原型作为扩散模型输入，簇边界样本纠缠使原型趋于接近、簇间可分性下降，导致潜在输入过于相似、合成数据多样性不足。

Method: 用可学习嵌入替代静态聚类原型；将嵌入优化为匹配原始数据分布的高斯统计量（均值与协方差），并设计多样性正则项促使各嵌入相互分离，以提升类内变异性。

Result: 多数据集实验显示，DEO 生成的蒸馏数据比基于聚类的扩散方法更多样、更具代表性，取得 SOTA 性能与更好的泛化性。

Conclusion: 以可训练嵌入+统计量匹配替代聚类，是提升扩散式数据集蒸馏样本多样性的简单有效方案；但摘要未给出具体指标，且与具身智能核心任务（VLA、世界模型、3D 重建等）关联较弱。

Abstract: Dataset distillation aims to compress large-scale datasets into compact synthetic subsets that enable efficient model training with comparable performance to the original data. Recent advances show that diffusion models are highly effective for this task due to their strong generative capability. Existing diffusion-based approaches typically perform class-wise clustering and use the obtained cluster prototypes as diffusion inputs to generate distilled samples. However, the uniformly distributed original samples in a category inevitably lead to entanglement near cluster boundaries, pulling the cluster prototypes closer and reducing the inter-cluster separation. Consequently, the diffusion model receives overly similar latent inputs, resulting in synthetic data with limited diversity. To address this issue, we propose Disentangled Embedding Optimization (DEO), a simple yet effective framework that replaces static clustering with trainable embeddings. These embeddings are optimized to match the Gaussian statistics (i.e., mean and covariance) of the original data distribution, while a diversity regularizer is designed to encourage separation among these embeddings to improve the intra-class variability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed DEO can generate more diversified and representative distilled data than existing cluster-based diffusion methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and superior generalization across multiple datasets.

</details>


### [100] [ATune: Unsupervised Photo Retouching Through Image-Editor Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16236)
*Divyanshu Goyal,Gabriel Ruiz,Amir Meisami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATune 用 1000 万张无标注商业图库照片，通过"模拟劣化"自动构造差/好配对，学习 13 个可测量图像属性（亮度、对比度、锐度等）的目标值，再以声明式方式由简单搜索驱动现成编辑器滑块（RawTherapee、darktable）完成修图，全程不使用任何专家修图数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家修图配对数据昂贵，FiveK、PPR10K 合计仅 1.6 万张原图；而高质量照片本身（Pixabay、Unsplash、商业图库）数以百万计且易得，因此希望摆脱对专家编辑标签的依赖。

Method: 对每张图按相机或粗心编辑的方式做劣化得到 (差, 好) 配对；在此类自监督配对上训练 54M 参数的 ATune，输出 13 个属性的目标值；再用简单搜索调节现成编辑器的滑块直到逼近目标值，编辑结果为可读可调的滑块列表，且同一模型可迁移到不同编辑器。

Result: 在 FiveK、PPR10K、SPAQ 上测试；9 个质量评判器打分，157 名被试完成 9,253 次二选一：相对 GPT-6 Astra 偏好率 54.3%，与 Claude Opus 5 打平（50.5%）；与各自在本数据集专家标注上训练的监督修图模型（FiveK 上 NeurOP、PPR10K 上 AdaInt）对比，偏好率 46.3%。

Conclusion: 一个无任何专家编辑监督的 54M 小模型即可在"设置编辑器滑块"这一任务上追平前沿大模型，说明声明式属性目标 + 搜索式滑块求解是可行且可解释的修图范式。

Abstract: Photo retouching models are trained on pairs of original and retouched photographs, usually edited by experts. Expert edits are costly, so the two main public sets, FiveK and PPR10K, hold only 16,161 originals. Good photographs on their own are far easier to find: commercial stock collections and free ones such as Pixabay and Unsplash hold millions. We learn retouching from them alone: we damage each photograph the way a camera or a careless edit would, which gives a worse and a better version of the same photograph. From these pairs, our model, ATune, learns how 13 measured attributes, such as brightness, contrast and sharpness, should change to raise a photograph's quality. The edit is declarative: ATune sets target values for the attributes, and a simple search adjusts the sliders of an out-of-the-box photo editor until the photograph reaches them. Every edit is a list of slider values a person can read and adjust. The same model can drive any editor that makes those changes; we test RawTherapee and darktable. We train on 10M photographs from a commercial stock collection and test on FiveK, PPR10K and SPAQ. Nine quality judges score every edit, and in a user study 157 human raters made 9,253 choices between our edit and another method's edit of the same photograph. When Claude Opus 5 and GPT-6 Astra edit the same photographs in the same editor, raters prefer our edits to GPT-6 Astra's (54.3%) and rate them even with Claude's (50.5%). We also compare with supervised retouchers on their home ground: NeurOP on FiveK and AdaInt on PPR10K, each trained on that set's own expert edits. Raters chose our edits in 46.3% of those comparisons. ATune, a 54M-parameter model trained without a single expert edit, now matches frontier models at setting an editor's sliders.

</details>


### [101] [Reconstruct What You Can: Distractor-free 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16259)
*Joel Julin,Liuyue Xie,Ananya Bal,Mose Sakashita,Koichiro Niinuma,Laszlo A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.CV
