Our approach outperforms state-of-the-art driving VLMs, rivals domain-specific expert models in 3D perception, and successfully retains its original general reasoning capabilities. This demonstrates that Triplet Incremental Training offers a unified, deployment-ready paradigm for transforming general foundation models into robust driving intelligences.

</details>


### [135] [Fog-FWL: A Large-Scale Full-Waveform LiDAR Dataset for Recovering Fog-Hidden Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16742)
*Kazuma Ikeda,Kotaro Oishi,Ryosei Hara,Ryo Yoshida,Takahiro Kado,Ibuki Fujioka,Taro Beppu,Mariko Isogawa,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fog-FWL：首个面向雾天全波形LiDAR的目标恢复数据集与物理仿真器，含7,527帧真实数据与12,000帧合成数据，统一标注协议支持严格sim-to-real评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雾散射导致近端强后向散射并衰减远端目标回波，传统峰值检测丢弃弱回波，使雾中目标点云稀疏；全波形LiDAR保留弱回波，但缺乏数据阻碍了相关研究。

Method: 构建Fog-FWL-Real（8场景58序列共7,527帧真实雾天FWL，含直方图级Object/Fog/Noise标签、10,719个3D框及按目标反射衰减划分的三级难度）；构建Fog-FWL-Simulator：空间非均匀雾密度场+含多次散射的时间分辨光传输+SPAD光子计数接收模型，生成12,000帧合成数据；两子集格式与标注协议一致，实现零真实波形的sim-to-real评估。

Result: 在最难条件下，仅34.9%的目标射线的前三强峰包含目标回波，量化了波形级恢复的必要性；数据集规模为现有唯一真实雾天FWL数据的579倍。

Conclusion: 真实+合成双子集与仿真器将公开，为雾鲁棒LiDAR感知与波形级目标恢复提供基准与训练基础。

Abstract: LiDAR is a core sensor for autonomous driving, yet it is highly vulnerable to fog. Fog scattering produces strong backscatter near the sensor and attenuates reflections from objects behind it, so weak object returns are often discarded by the peak detection that converts received signals into point clouds. Full-waveform LiDAR (FWL), which records the received optical signal before peak detection, retains these weak returns, but the lack of suitable data has prevented research on recovering fog-hidden objects from FWL. We present Fog-FWL, a dataset foundation for this task that consists of a real-world benchmark, Fog-FWL-Real, and a large-scale synthetic training set, Fog-FWL-Sim. Fog-FWL-Real contains 7,527 real foggy FWL frames from 8 scenes and 58 sequences, which is 579x larger than the only existing real foggy FWL data, together with histogram-level Object/Fog/Noise labels, 10,719 3D bounding boxes, and three difficulty levels defined by the attenuation of object reflections. Fog-FWL-Sim contains 12,000 frames generated by our physics-based simulator, Fog-FWL-Simulator, which integrates spatially heterogeneous fog density fields, time-resolved light transport including multiple scattering, and a photon-counting SPAD receiver model. The two subsets share the same sensor format and annotation protocol, enabling a strict sim-to-real evaluation in which no real waveforms are used for training. Our analysis shows that, under the hardest condition, object returns are included in the three strongest peaks for only 34.9% of object rays, underscoring the need for waveform-level recovery. Fog-FWL-Real, Fog-FWL-Sim, and the Fog-FWL-Simulator will be publicly released to facilitate research on fog-robust LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [136] [PhysLoop: Harnessing LLMs' Physics Awareness for Self-Improving Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16748)
*Yuxuan Chen,Xiaojie Li,Hao Chen,Juncheng Ma,Kewei Zhang,Zhongjing Du,Guoqiang Wei,Fangfang Wang,Zhen Dong,Zhiyang Dou,Liming Zhao,Yan Zeng,Jianwei Zhang,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysLoop 提出统一 harness，用 LLM 在视频生成/筛选/修正全流程中提供物理监督与约束，实现物理一致的可控数据构建与推理时视频修复，并开源 10 万条视频与 PhysLoop-Gen、PhysLoop-Repair 两个评测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型视觉逼真但物理动力学与因果一致性差；从生成视频中学习需要细粒度物理监督，而高质量判别、修正对与受控变体难以获取；LLM 具备物理知识却无法可靠地指导视频生成模型产出训练数据。

Method: 构建 PhysLoop 统一框架：在生成、选择、修正三阶段引导并约束 LLM，将其物理知识蒸馏为物理一致的视频；支持可控训练数据构造与推理时局部化视频修复；采用可控制生成与局部修复两项评估，并在 Physics-IQ Verified 外部基准上评测物理行为。

Result: 发布含多样真实室内外场景的 100K 视频数据集、生成工具及 PhysLoop-Gen/PhysLoop-Repair 评测集；在可控生成与局部修复上给出定量评估，并在 Physics-IQ Verified 上测度物理一致性表现。

Conclusion: 以 LLM 驱动的生成–选择–修正闭环，将物理先验注入视频生成并支持失败定位与修复，是通向视频生成模型递归自我改进（RSI）的关键一步，同时提供了可复用的数据与评测基础设施。

Abstract: Video generation models produce visually realistic videos but still struggle with physical dynamics and causal consistency. Learning from generated videos requires fine-grained physical supervision, yet reliable quality judgments, correction pairs, and controlled variations are difficult to obtain. Large language models (LLMs) possess physical knowledge, but struggle to reliably guide video generation models in producing their training videos. We present PhysLoop, a unified harness designed to support physically grounded self-improvement in video generation models. The harness guides and constrains LLMs throughout video generation, selection, and correction, distilling their physical knowledge into physically consistent videos. The framework supports controllable training-data construction and inference-time video repair. By identifying and correcting failures, PhysLoop marks a key milestone toward recursive self-improvement (RSI) in video generation models with limited human intervention. We release 100K videos spanning diverse, realistic indoor and outdoor scenes, along with generation tools and two evaluation datasets: PhysLoop-Gen and PhysLoop-Repair. We evaluate controllable generation and localized repair on these datasets, respectively, and assess physical behavior on the external Physics-IQ Verified benchmark.

</details>


### [137] [ORCA:Orthogonal Residual Consensus Alignment for Multi-View Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16749)
*Xiaojian Ding,Xian Li,Xiaoying Zhu,Lin Zhao,Kaixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORCA 用跨视图正交残差循环融合 + 共识校准层次对比对齐做多视图聚类，5 个基准上最优，100leaves 提升 5.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度多视图聚类（DMVC）在融合过程中冗余信息累积、以及跨表示层次无差别对齐的问题。

Method: 提出 ORCA：(1) 语义增量循环融合模块，提取跨视图正交残差作为非冗余增量，并用分歧引导门控自适应加权循环状态；(2) 共识校准的层次对比对齐模块，以全局共识为视图级、成对级、循环级表示加权进行对比对齐，平衡视图特异结构与跨视图一致性。

Result: 在 5 个基准数据集上与 15 种竞争方法对比，验证有效性；在 100leaves 上较最强基线提升 5.25% 准确率；消融实验验证两个模块均有贡献；代码开源。

Conclusion: 正交残差增量融合与共识校准层次对比对齐可有效抑制融合冗余并实现层次化对齐，是一种通用的多视图表示学习框架；但属于多视图聚类方向，与具身智能/物理 AI 主线关联有限。

Abstract: Deep multi-view clustering often suffers from redundant information accumulation during fusion and indiscriminate alignment across representation levels. We propose Orthogonal Residual Consensus Alignment (ORCA) to address these challenges. Its semantic incremental recurrent fusion module extracts cross-view orthogonal residuals as non-redundant increments and adaptively weights recurrent states through divergence-guided gating. Its consensus-calibrated hierarchical contrastive alignment module uses the global consensus to weight contrastive alignment of view-level, pairwise, and recurrent representations. Together, these modules balance view-specific structure with cross-view consistency. Experiments on five benchmarks against fifteen competing methods demonstrate ORCA's effectiveness, including a 5.25 percent accuracy improvement over the strongest baseline on 100leaves. Ablation studies validate both modules. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [138] [Efficient Periocular Recognition through Model Scaling and Cross Spectral VIS NIR Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16764)
*Fabian Stockhard,Juan E. Tapia,Christoph Busch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaEye is a systematic empirical study of input and backbone scaling for periocular (eye-region) verification in VIS and cross-spectral VIS/NIR settings, comparing full-face, masked-face, and dedicated 80x40 periocular crops with varying backbone width and embedding dimensionality under an AdaFace-style framework.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Full-face verification degrades when faces are occluded or image resolution is low; periocular crops promise cheaper, more robust verification, but the cost/accuracy trade-offs of input region, backbone width, and embedding size are unquantified.

Method: AdaFace-style recognition framework; three input regimes (full-face, masked-face, dedicated 80x40 periocular) crossed with backbone width scaling and embedding-dimension scaling; evaluated on four VIS/VIS and VIS/NIR protocols (UFPR periocular, CASIA, ND-NIVL); metrics cover verification accuracy, GMACs, throughput, training memory, template size, and multi-seed training stability.

Result: Periocular input cuts cost from 6.30 to 1.64 GMACs per image with ~3x measured throughput; periocular models win on native UFPR periocular data while face-based protocols favor richer input; moderate width reduction preserves mean accuracy on CASIA and ND-NIVL; embedding compression mainly reduces template storage; some combined reductions require numerical stabilization.

Conclusion: Compact periocular architectures should be chosen per target protocol and deployment constraints (accuracy, compute, storage, robustness); direct periocular processing is efficient but not universally superior, and aggressive joint compression of embedding and backbone hurts training stability.

Abstract: Periocular recognition offers an efficient alternative to full-face verification when facial regions are occluded or image resolution is limited. We present AdaEye, a systematic study of input and backbone scaling for periocular verification in visible-spectrum (VIS) and cross-spectral (VIS/NIR) settings. Using an AdaFace-style framework, we compare full-face, masked-face, and dedicated 80x40 periocular inputs while varying backbone width and embedding dimensionality. Across four VIS/VIS and VIS/NIR protocols, we assess verification accuracy, computational cost, inference throughput, training memory, template size, and training stability. Direct periocular processing reduces recognition cost from 6.30 to 1.64 GMACs per image and increases measured throughput by approximately threefold. Its effectiveness depends on the evaluation protocol: periocular models perform better on native UFPR periocular images, whereas face-based protocols favor retaining more input information. Moderate backbone width reductions maintain comparable mean verification performance on CASIA and ND-NIVL while reducing computation. Embedding compression decreases template-storage requirements, primarily benefiting deployment. Multi-seed experiments reveal that some configurations require numerical stabilization, particularly when embedding and backbone reductions are combined. These findings support selecting compact periocular architectures according to the target protocol and deployment constraints, balancing accuracy, efficiency, storage, and robustness.

</details>


### [139] [Evaluating Video LLMs' Understanding of Corner Cases in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16767)
*Gabriel Manalu,Minh Son Hoang,Hyeontaek Hwang,Jaesung Lee,Hyojin Choi,Tran Tuan Phong,Seon Kwon Kim,Saad Wazir,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DashBench：面向自动驾驶"长尾极端场景"（碰撞与非碰撞罕见事件）的 Video LLM 评测基准，覆盖25个模型、1709段精选行车视频，通过开放式问答揭示模型在安全关键场景解释能力上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶场景 Video LLM 基准多基于常规交通条件与通用理解任务，聚焦"corner case"（最罕见、最安全关键的驾驶事件）的评测缺失，难以判断模型能否用于安全关键的自动驾驶解释与决策。

Method: 构建 DashBench 基准：人工精选 1709 段驾驶视频，涵盖碰撞相关与罕见的非碰撞 corner case；设计多样化提示词与开放式问答协议，系统评测 25 个 Video LLM，并对错误类型（漏检危险事件、误判、幻觉不存在的交通参与者或动作）进行诊断分析，配套评测工具包。

Result: 所有被评测模型均显著低于安全关键驾驶应用所需阈值，普遍存在漏检/误判前方危险事件，以及在解释中虚构不存在的 agent 或动作（幻觉）等问题，暴露出关键能力差距。

Conclusion: DashBench 以大规模精选数据、严格评测工具与细致的模型局限诊断，为视频大模型在自动驾驶安全关键场景下的评估建立基线，推动更安全、更可解释的自动驾驶系统研究；代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: Natural language explanations promised by modern Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) offer autonomous driving systems a path toward detailed scene description and interpretable decision making on the road. While recent benchmarks have begun assessing these models on driving scenes, they mainly target routine traffic conditions and general comprehension tasks based on existing driving datasets and therefore fail to evaluate model performance on the rarest and most safety-critical driving events, also known as "corner cases". To provide a comprehensive evaluation of Video LLMs on precisely these scenarios, we introduce DashBench, a benchmark testing 25 Video LLMs on 1,709 carefully curated driving videos spanning not only collision-related corner cases but also rare non-collision ones. Through extensive prompts and open-ended question answering, we expose a critical capability gap: all evaluated models perform substantially below the threshold required for safety-critical driving applications, frequently misjudging or missing dangerous events on the road ahead and hallucinating non-existent agents or actions in their explanations. We believe DashBench, combining an extensive dataset with a rigorous evaluation toolkit and a detailed diagnosis of current model limitations, drives progress toward safer, more explainable autonomous driving systems. The code and data will be shared at: this http URL

</details>


### [140] [EG360: Efficient and Generalizable $360^\circ$ Depth Estimation via Adaptive Panorama-Perspective Distillation and Spherical Cross-Level Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16793)
*Laiyan Ding,Chongxi Meng,Rui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EG360：轻量全景深度估计模型，用几何引导的自适应全景-透视蒸馏(APPD)与球面跨层特征融合(SCFF)，重参数化后仅6.593M参数，实现强零样本泛化并在Jetson Orin NX 16GB上达35 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全景深度估计呈两极分化：大预训练模型零样本泛化强但算力开销大，难以在嵌入式平台实时推理；而既有轻量全景模型多为单域评测、跨域泛化差。作者希望弥合二者差距，兼顾高效性与泛化性。

Method: 以轻量深度模型为骨干，设计场景几何引导的自适应全景-透视蒸馏(APPD)策略，将大模型(透视/全景)知识迁移至轻量学生；并引入球面跨层特征融合(SCFF)模块实现全局语义感知，最后经结构重参数化压缩推理开销。

Result: 重参数化后仅6.593M参数，在真实世界基准上取得强零样本性能；Jetson Orin NX 16GB（MAXN）上约35 FPS，而此前ViT-small模型不足2 FPS，速度提升约18倍。代码将开源。

Conclusion: EG360 表明通过几何引导蒸馏与球面特征融合，可在极小参数下获得可泛化的全景深度估计，适合嵌入式实时部署，为轻量全景感知提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Generalized panorama depth estimation has witnessed great advances in recent years. These methods typically achieve robust zero-shot generalization performance using large pretrained models, but their high computational cost makes real-time inference on embedded platforms challenging. In contrast, previous lightweight panorama models have mostly been developed for single-domain evaluations and cannot generalize well due to domain shifts. To reduce the gap between these two types of models, we introduce EG360, an efficient and generalizable panorama depth estimation model. Specifically, building on a lightweight depth model, we design an Adaptive Panorama-Perspective Distillation (APPD) strategy guided by scene geometry, along with a Spherical Cross-Level Feature Fusion (SCFF) module for global semantic perception. As a result, with only 6.593M parameters after reparameterization, EG360 achieves strong zero-shot performance on real-world benchmarks and runs at 35 FPS on a Jetson Orin NX 16GB in MAXN mode, whereas a previous ViT-small model runs at less than 2 FPS. Our code will also be made public for future research.

</details>


### [141] [Kosmo-GFM: A Production-Ready Geometry Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16840)
*Sheng Tang,Shengzhe Wei,Tiancheng Chen,Wenhua Jin,Xinyang Lu,Tinghao Jiang,Shuoxuan Feng,Yupeng Liu,Yu Zhang,Xingshuo Chang,Keren Zhou,Yuhan Xiong,Jiahao Li,Haoming Liu,Jituo Tian,Hui Liang,Chaoqun Tang,Feng Tian,Wentao Ouyang,Kaojin Zhu,Xiaolin Chai,Peihuang Fu,Miao Zheng,Yunbo Hu,Feixue Cai,Zizhen Zou,Weigang Wang,Yexuan Yang,Haoyu Feng,Weiqi Zhang,Juntong Fang,Zesong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Kosmo-GFM：几何基础模型+位姿优化引擎+合成监督闭环的场景重建系统，深度/位姿/动态点全面领先，且运行时间约为GLUEMAP的20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角场景级重建中，相机位姿与几何的鲁棒恢复仍是瓶颈；作者希望将学习到的几何先验（GFM）与几何优化结合，并用重建场景反向生成数据来增强先验。

Method: 系统由三部分组成：GFM从图像/视频预测相机参数、深度、跨视角对应与动态3D点；PoseEngine将预测与几何优化融合，恢复场景级位姿与几何供3D/4D Gaussian Splatting使用；KosmoWorld用重建的真实场景生成图像与几何监督回训GFM，形成闭环。另有Flash版本采用混合精度加速。

Result: SpatialBench Sparse/Medium子集上绝对相对深度误差最低、5°相对位姿AUC最高；五个数据集的动态点与场景流平均端点误差最低；34个共同场景上1°/3°/5°场景平均位姿AUC最高，累计运行14.07小时（约为GLUEMAP 69.67小时的20%），Flash版2.62小时（约4%）。缩放实验显示数据、算力、训练预算增加带来正向趋势。

Conclusion: 学习到的几何先验可有效嵌入场景级重建流程，兼顾精度与效率；后续可探索更大规模数据与更强监督信号。

Abstract: We present Kosmo-GFM, a scene reconstruction system that integrates a geometry foundation model (GFM), a pose optimization engine (PoseEngine), and reconstruction-derived supervision from KosmoWorld. GFM predicts camera parameters, depth, cross-view correspondences, and dynamic 3D points from images and videos. PoseEngine combines these predictions with geometric optimization to recover scene-level camera poses and geometry for downstream 3D and 4D Gaussian Splatting. KosmoWorld generates images and geometric supervision from reconstructed real-world scenes to improve GFM's geometric predictions. On the Sparse and Medium subsets of SpatialBench, GFM achieves the lowest absolute relative depth error and the highest relative-pose AUC at 5 degrees among the compared methods. It also achieves the lowest mean endpoint errors for dynamic points and scene flow across the five evaluated datasets. Across 34 scenes completed by all compared reconstruction systems, PoseEngine achieves the highest scene-averaged pose AUC at thresholds of 1, 3, and 5 degrees, with a cumulative runtime of 14.07 hours, approximately 20% of GLUEMAP's 69.67 hours. PoseEngine Flash provides a further accuracy-efficiency trade-off, with a measured mixed-precision runtime of 2.62 hours, approximately 4% of GLUEMAP's runtime. Separate scaling experiments show generally favorable trends with increased data, model computation, and training budgets. These results support the use of learned geometric priors within a scene-level reconstruction pipeline.

</details>


### [142] [Visual Cipher Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16861)
*Deyao Hong,Jiayi Jiang,Yuchun Chen,Yixuan Shi,Wenyi Li,Zheng Jiang,Chengxing Xie,Mingyu Su,Qingle Liu,Mingju Gao,Yizhe Chi,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Kaisen Yang,Huan-ang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Visual Cipher Bench：40个原创视觉谜题（空间操作、逻辑约束、规则归纳、知识解码），仅给1-3张黑白线稿和最简提交指令，考察智能体能否自主从图像中发现解题机制。8个前沿模型29种推理配置下，最强GPT-6 Astra(max)仅26.7%，5/8模型低于1%；在10题子集上给一句不含解题细节的机制提示，准确率从7.8%升至55.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体的长程能力依赖"显式文本规格"和"即时执行反馈"两个条件，而真实问题中关键信息常只存在于图像中，且缺乏测试用例反馈。现有视觉推理基准多退化为单轮短链问答，智能体基准又集中于文本环境或允许试错的交互状态，无法衡量"长程视觉推理"这一能力。

Method: 构建Visual Cipher Bench：40个原创视觉谜题，分四类（空间操作与模拟、逻辑约束与规划、规则归纳、知识解码）；每题仅提供1-3张黑白线稿与最少提交指令，要求智能体反复查看图像、模拟空间变换、自主交叉验证线索以发现底层机制。在8个前沿模型、29种推理强度配置上评测；并做对照实验：10题子集给出单条非剧透机制提示。

Result: 即使最强模型GPT-6 Astra(max)准确率仅26.7%，8个模型中5个低于1%。10题子集上，加入单条非剧透机制提示后准确率从7.8%跃升至55.6%，说明瓶颈在"从视觉证据中自主发现规则"，而非执行多步计划。

Conclusion: 当前智能体的核心短板是从视觉证据中自主归纳规则（规则发现），而非多步规划执行；该基准为衡量智能体长程视觉推理提供了严格评测工具。属于基准/评测类工作，未见具身或3D重建方法创新。

Abstract: While language-model agents excel at long-horizon coding and terminal tasks, this success relies on two conditions: explicit text specifications and immediate execution feedback. However, neither condition holds in real-world problems where critical information exists only in images, leaving it unclear whether long-horizon capability transfers from text to visual reasoning. In such visual settings, because rules and clues resist text transcription and lack test-case feedback, agents must repeatedly inspect images, simulate spatial transformations, and autonomously cross-check clues. Despite this challenge, existing evaluations fail to capture such capability: visual reasoning benchmarks largely reduce tasks to single-turn, short-chain question answering, whereas agent benchmarks focus on text-based environments or interactive states that permit trial-and-error. To bridge this gap, we introduce Visual Cipher Bench, a collection of 40 original visual puzzles across four categories: spatial manipulation and simulation, logical constraints and planning, rule induction, and knowledge-based decoding. Each puzzle provides only one to three black-and-white line drawings and a minimal submission instruction, requiring agents to discover the underlying solving mechanism entirely from the images. Across 29 reasoning-effort configurations of eight frontier models, even the strongest model, GPT-6 Astra (max), achieves only 26.7% accuracy, while five of the eight models score below 1%. Yet on a ten-task subset, providing a single non-spoiler mechanism hint boosts accuracy from 7.8% to 55.6%. This sharp contrast shows that the primary bottleneck of current agents lies not in executing multi-step plans, but in autonomously discovering rules from visual evidence. Visual Cipher Bench thus provides a rigorous benchmark for measuring long-horizon visual reasoning in agents.

</details>


### [143] [Raysemble: Metric 3D Bone Reconstruction from a Single X-ray](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16869)
*Xiaoyang Li,Xinghao Yan,Yuan Chai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Raysemble 从单张 X 光片重建腰椎与骨盆共 8 块骨的三维形状：通过在每个 3D query 的图像投影处采样区域+全局特征，共享解码器输出归一化骨形状，再由预测深度与相机几何确定各骨中心与尺度，将其重采样到统一毫米网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张正位 X 光片缺乏深度信息，多骨结构相互重叠；直接回归绝对坐标难以泛化，需要将"形状"与"摆放"解耦，以在统一度量空间中对多块骨进行可比较的重建与评估。

Method: 形状-位姿分离建模：(1) 每个 3D 查询点投影到图像上采样局部区域特征与全局特征；(2) 共享解码器预测归一化骨形状；(3) 由网络预测的深度与相机几何推导各骨 3D 中心与尺度，将归一化形状重采样至统一毫米网格；评估采用 23 位留出患者、43 个 CT 生成的模拟正位片，且不做 CT 刚性配准。

Result: 在无刚性对齐条件下领先 15 个对比方法，三次随机种子平均 65.24% Dice、9.09 mm HD95、3.32 mm ASSD；相同输入下 Dice 较 VolumeNeRF 提升 5.86 个百分点。

Conclusion: 解耦形状与摆放的多骨单视图重建在临床指标上有效，但验证依赖模拟 X 光片而非真实影像，属医学影像专用方法，对通用 3D 重建/具身智能任务的迁移价值有限。

Abstract: Raysemble reconstructs eight lumbar and pelvic bones from a single X-ray by separating shape and placement. Each 3D query samples regional and global features at its image projection. A shared decoder predicts normalized bone shapes. Predicted depth and camera geometry determine 3D centers and scales for resampling these shapes onto a common grid in millimeters. Evaluation uses 23 held-out patients with 43 CT scans, each generating one simulated anteroposterior radiograph. Without rigid alignment to CT,Raysemble leads 15 evaluated methods, averaging 65.24% Dice, 9.09 mm HD95, and 3.32 mm ASSD over three random seeds. With identical inputs, it improves Dice over VolumeNeRF by 5.86 percentage points.

</details>


### [144] [TokenStream: Multi-Subject Personalized Video Generation with Token-wise LoRA and Guided Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16897)
*Hung Nguyen,Quang Qui-Vinh Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TokenStream: 基于优化的多主体个性化文本到视频生成框架，用 token-wise LoRA (ToRA) 为每个主体限制低秩更新至其关联文本 token 的 cross-attention value 投影，并用 T2V-guided stream 复用冻结基座模型的查询/键布局与运动先验；同时提出 Multi-DB 基准（30 主体、122 提示）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张参考图学习难以将主体外观与静态图像先验解耦，而独立训练的多个 adapter 合并时会发生身份混淆；同时缺乏多主体组合的评测基准。

Method: Token-wise LoRA (ToRA)：把低秩更新限制在主体相关文本 token 的 cross-attention value 投影上，形成互不干扰的独立适配路径；T2V-guided stream：在早期去噪阶段从冻结基座复制 query/key，以复用其布局与运动先验，仅保留个性化 value 注入；免参考视频训练集、免逐组合联合优化。

Result: 在 1.3B 模型上，Multi-DB 基准中身份保持从 DreamBooth 的 0.422 提升至 0.467，文本对齐从 0.652 提升至 0.688。

Conclusion: 通过 token 级适配隔离实现可组合的多主体个性化视频生成，验证了结构化解耦优于 adapter 合并的朴素做法。

Abstract: We introduce TokenStream, an optimization-based framework that composes independently learned subject identities in text-to-video generation without a reference-video training dataset or joint optimization of each new subject combination. Learning from a single reference image makes subject appearance difficult to separate from static-image bias, while merging independently trained adapters can mix identities. Token-wise LoRA (ToRA) restricts low-rank updates to the cross-attention value projections of subject-associated text tokens, providing a separate adaptation path for each subject. A T2V-guided stream copies queries and keys from the frozen base model during early denoising to reuse its layout and motion priors while retaining personalized values. We also introduce Multi-DB, a 30-subject, 122-prompt benchmark with appearance-free prompts and varied interactions. On Multi-DB at 1.3B, TokenStream improves identity preservation from 0.422 for DreamBooth to 0.467 and textual alignment from 0.652 to 0.688.

</details>


### [145] [Automated PD-L1 Tumor Proportion Scoring with Uncertainty-Guided Triage in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16907)
*Kesi Xu,Ayesha Azam,Tanvier Kaur,Monika Kondratowicz,Kinza Asim,Brinder Chohan,Paapa Appiah-Odame,Zhao Chen,David Snead,Nasir Rajpoot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段PD-L1 TPS评分框架：肿瘤区域识别+巨噬细胞排除、双分支细胞计数网络D2Count-Net，以及不确定性引导分诊机制TPS-Guard，对评分接近1%/50%阈值且模型分歧的切片给出复核理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PD-L1 TPS决定NSCLC免疫治疗选择，1%和50%阈值附近评分误差影响临床决策；纯自动化评分缺乏可信度提示，需要机制指出哪些切片/评分应交由病理医生复核。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 定位肿瘤区域并剔除富巨噬细胞斑块；(2) D2Count-Net双分支网络分别计数PD-L1阳性/阴性肿瘤细胞并计算TPS；(3) TPS-Guard以多个独立训练模型间的预测分歧作为不确定性，转诊最高不确定切片，并在估计跨越1%/50%阈值时触发告警。回溯性单中心154张WSI，三位病理医生互评基线；另在公开外部数据集上零重训验证。

Result: 114张未见切片上模型MAE为6.96个百分点，处于三位病理医生范围(4.24–8.29)内；模型不确定性与TPS绝对误差相关(Spearman ρ=0.77)，与病理医生间分歧相关(ρ=0.71)；转诊最不确定的15%切片后模型与医生MAE均下降；外部数据集上仍能定向高误差病例，增加转诊提升50%阈值处敏感度。

Conclusion: 不确定性引导分诊可为个体化自动评分提供复核依据，并识别出同样易致病理医生分歧的切片；下一步需前瞻性阅片研究验证其对TPS报告的实际影响。

Abstract: Programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) tumor proportion score (TPS) informs immunotherapy selection in non-small cell lung cancer. Near the 1% and 50% cutoffs, the score should also indicate when closer review is warranted. We designed a three-stage framework to follow the sequence of TPS assessment. Stage 1 identifies tumor regions and excludes macrophage-rich patches. Stage 2, a dual-branch cell counting network (D2Count-Net), counts PD-L1-positive and PD-L1-negative tumor cells in these regions to calculate TPS. Stage 3, TPS-Guard, an uncertainty-guided triage mechanism, reports model uncertainty as the disagreement among independently trained models, refers the most uncertain slides, and flags scores near the 1% or 50% cutoff when model estimates disagree across that cutoff. Flags state the reason for referral or alert the pathologist to possible scoring errors on auto-reported slides. In a retrospective, single-center study, 154 whole-slide images were scored by three pathologists, and the model and each pathologist were compared in turn with the mean of the other two. On the 114 slides unseen in training, model mean absolute error (MAE) was 6.96 percentage points, within the range of the three pathologists (4.24 to 8.29), and model uncertainty correlated with absolute TPS error (Spearman rho = 0.77) and inter-pathologist disagreement (rho = 0.71). After referral of the 15% most uncertain slides, model and pathologist MAE both decreased. Applied without retraining to an external, publicly available dataset, TPS-Guard still directed review to higher-error cases, and referring more uncertain cases raised sensitivity at the 50% cutoff. TPS-Guard provides reasons for reviewing individual automated scores and identifies slides that also produce greater disagreement among pathologists. Prospective reader studies should assess TPS-Guard's effect on TPS reporting.

</details>


### [146] [Report of the 8th LSVOS Challenge: Mobile Unified Multimodal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16925)
*Chang Liu,Henghui Ding,Tong Yang,Dayoung Kil,Seong-heum Kim,Qilin Xiang,Ronghao Zheng,Ziyang Zhang,Shanlin Jiang,Ruohan Shi,Qilin Fan,Chengfeng Qiu,Kaifeng Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ECCV 2026 LSVOS 挑战赛 MUMU 赛道报告：在 0.5B 参数、8GB 峰值显存约束下用单一统一模型同时完成多概念图像打标、开放词表目标检测与简短图像描述；三支顶尖队伍均以 Florence-2-base 为基座，分别采用轻量打标头+跨任务推理精化、共享低秩适配器+任务相关解码、免训练流水线（描述→标签映射+检测 TTA），最终开放词表检测新类别仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端/边缘设备需要以极小参数量和显存预算运行能同时处理多任务的多模态理解模型，而现有统一多模态模型往往参数庞大、任务间相互干扰，缺少在严格效率约束下统一多任务的标准化评测与方案参考，故设立 MUMU 赛道并汇总可行做法。

Method: 设置统一评测协议：多概念图像打标（质量/场景/事件词表）、开放词表检测、简洁描述三项任务，0.5B 参数与 8GB 推理显存预算，按加权任务分数乘以效率因子排名。汇总前三名方案：(1) Florence-2-base + 轻量打标头 + 跨任务推理精化；(2) 共享低秩适配器，在覆盖感知的多源数据上训练，配任务相关解码；(3) 免训练流水线，用描述生成标签并施加检测测试时增强。

Result: 紧凑的提示式视觉语言模型（Florence-2-base）经针对性设计监督信号、解码策略与跨任务线索后，可同时支撑三类输出；开放词表检测在新颖类别上的性能是限制总分的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 任务-数据-解码的协同设计比单纯扩大模型规模更关键，共享适配+任务专属解码是高效率统一多模态的可行范式；报告属挑战赛综述性质，侧重方案归纳而非提出新方法，对具身/3D 核心方向参考价值有限。

Abstract: This report summarizes the Mobile Unified Multimodal Understanding (MUMU) track of the 8th Large-scale Video Object Segmentation (LSVOS) Challenge, held in conjunction with ECCV 2026. MUMU asks for a single unified model that jointly performs multi-concept image tagging over quality, scene, and event vocabularies, open-vocabulary object detection, and concise image captioning, within a budget of 0.5B parameters and 8 GB of peak inference memory. Submissions are ranked by a weighted task score scaled by an efficiency factor. We describe the tasks, rules, and evaluation protocol, report the final results, and review the methods of the top three teams. All three leading solutions build on the compact Florence-2-base model, but extend it in different ways: lightweight tagging heads with cross-task inference refinement, a shared low-rank adapter trained on coverage-aware multi-source data with task-dependent decoding, and a training-free pipeline that maps captions to tags and applies detection test-time augmentation. The results show that a compact prompt-based vision-language model can serve all three outputs when supervision, decoding, and cross-task cues are designed for each task, while open-vocabulary detection on novel categories remains the main bottleneck.

</details>


### [147] [A Disease-specific Retinal Aging Biomarker for Predicting Progression to Late Age-related Macular Degeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16955)
*Hexin Dong,Benjamin Hou,Puzhen Wu,Mingquan Lin,Emily Y. Chew,Zhiyong Lu,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于眼底图像的"AMD年龄"(FAA)，在AREDS/AREDS2上训练与验证，量化AMD特异性视网膜老化，将其纳入Cox模型后C-index由0.836升至0.888（AREDS），AREDS2由0.715升至0.756。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际年龄无法反映个体视网膜老化差异，限制AMD风险分层与及时干预；需要一种疾病特异的"视网膜年龄"以提升预后精度。

Method: 在AREDS数据集（2,192名受试者、36,782张眼底图像）上训练深度学习模型预测FAA，并在AREDS与AREDS2队列中验证；将FAA作为协变量引入Cox比例风险模型，评估C-index与Brier分数，并比较阅读中心判读与专科医师判读两种表型定义下的稳健性。

Result: C-index在AREDS由0.836提升至0.888，5年Brier由0.050降至0.046；AREDS2中C-index由0.715提升至0.756，2年Brier由0.078降至0.076；跨数据集、跨表型定义均表现一致增益。

Conclusion: FAA能够捕捉AMD相关加速视网膜老化，将其纳入风险预测框架可显著改善AMD风险分层，值得进一步评估。

Abstract: Age-related macular degeneration (AMD) is a leading cause of irreversible vision loss in older adults. While chronological age is a primary risk factor, it inadequately reflects individual variations in retinal aging, limiting effective AMD risk stratification and timely intervention. Here, we propose and validate fundus-based AMD age (FAA), which quantifies disease-specific retinal aging in AMD and captures accelerated AMD-related retinal aging, thereby improving prognostic precision over chronological age. Trained on 36,782 images from 2,192 participants in the Age-Related Eye Disease Study (AREDS) dataset and validated across AREDS and AREDS2 cohorts, our model demonstrates significant improvements over traditional chronological age-based approaches. Specifically, integrating the proposed FAA into Cox proportional-hazards models significantly enhances prediction performance. FAA improved the C-index from 0.836 to 0.888 and reduced the 5-year Brier score from 0.050 to 0.046 in AREDS, with consistent gains observed in AREDS2 (C-index 0.715 to 0.756; 2-year Brier 0.078 to 0.076). FAA showed improved prognostic performance across the two datasets and under different phenotype definitions (reading center-defined vs. specialist-defined grading), supporting its potential utility for AMD risk stratification. These findings highlight the advantage of incorporating FAA into AMD risk prediction frameworks, supporting further evaluation of FAA for AMD risk stratification.

</details>


### [148] [ElasticDino: Dense Semantic Features Using Elastic Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16984)
*Ulysse Mizrahi,Sean Zaretzky,Dov Danon,Amit H. Bermano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ElasticDino：一个基于空间形变的轻量适配器，把ViT特征上采样为像素对齐的高分辨率特征图，并利用"伪影跨通道一致"的特性同步消除伪影，用线性头做分割/深度监督，无需真值即可训练，在密集任务上精度更高且更省算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT的自注意力二次复杂度限制了特征分辨率，导致密集预测任务（分割、深度）空间伪影严重、细节丢失。现有上采样/解码方案（如插值、卷积解码器、隐式表示）要么计算昂贵，要么引入过度平滑偏差、丢失锐利语义。

Method: 核心观察：ViT低分辨率特征的伪影在通道间具有一致性。据此设计薄型适配器架构，以空间形变（spatial deformation，类似可变形采样）对特征做像素对齐的高分辨率重采样，而非插值或生成式补全，从而严格保持语义锐度。用线性头接像素级语义分割与深度估计作为代理监督，因无真值，引导形变学习但避免把任务特定语义泄漏进特征。

Result: 在多个主流密集预测基准上精度优于现有方法，且计算性能更优（轻量、高效）；高分辨率特征与输入图像结构对齐、伪影被同步消除，无平滑偏置。

Conclusion: 以"形变而非上采样/生成"的思路解决ViT密集特征分辨率瓶颈，是一种通用、轻量、即插即用的ViT主干增强，适合高分辨率密集感知，可用于下游3D/机器人感知管线，但论文未涉及机器人本体实验。

Abstract: Vision transformers (ViTs) serve as the backbone for countless vision tasks, including dense prediction tasks, such as segmentation and depth estimation, even though the quadratic complexity of self-attention severely limits feature resolution and induces spatial artifacts. In this work, we introduce ElasticDino, a lightweight adapter which produces high-resolution feature maps, adhering to the fine details of the input image. As a key insight, we observe the consistency of artifacts across channels, and simultaneously resolve them to match the input image structure. Using a slim architecture based on spatial deformations, ElasticDino produces pixel-aligned, high-resolution feature maps efficiently. Leveraging deformations allows strictly preserving sharp semantics, and thereby avoiding the smoothness biases inherent to alternative approaches. Due to the absence of ground truth, we employ pixel-level semantic segmentation and depth estimation with linear heads to predict a set of downstream dense tasks, guiding the deformation process without leaking task-specific semantics into the model. We demonstrate superior accuracy over current methods on a range of popular dense task benchmarks, with better computational performance.

</details>


### [149] [AURA 2.0: From User-Driven Responses to Model-Initiated Conversations in Streaming Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17048)
*Shuhan Li,Huankang Guan,Xintong Guo,Yang Bo,Yuyang He,Dunyuan Xu,Jingyan Chen,Fang Liu,Tsz Kin Wan,Jinpeng Chen,Zhenqi He,Yuxuan Hu,Wenbo Li,Han Gao,Qijun Li,Long Chen,Hongsheng Li,Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AURA 2.0 是流式视频大语言模型，新增模型主动发起对话（MIC）、流感知工具调用（SATC）、交互式视觉定位（IVG）与长时程交互记忆，并开源权重与推理代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 助手仅被动应答，即使视频流中出现值得讨论的事件也保持沉默；需突破纯用户驱动交互，让模型能自主发现话题并主动发起对话，同时支持工具调用与视觉定位以增强多轮流式交互。

Method: 在 AURA 基础上引入三项能力：MIC（识别值得讨论的话题并在恰当时机主动发起对话）、SATC（结合当前视觉与对话上下文调用外部工具并融合返回信息）、IVG（对用户输入与模型输出做视觉定位，识别用户标注对象并输出帧对齐边界框）。构建专门的流式交互数据集，采用三阶段训练整合新行为与原有流式交互能力，并加入 Long-Horizon Interaction Memory（LHIM）以在长视频流中维持上下文连贯。

Result: 在公开流式视频基准上，AURA 2.0 在开源流式模型中取得领先性能；针对性评测进一步验证了其新增的主动对话、工具调用与视觉定位能力。模型权重与推理代码已开源。

Conclusion: AURA 2.0 将流式视频 LLM 从被动应答推进到主动交互，通过 MIC/SATC/IVG 与长时程记忆扩展了流式多模态助手的能力边界，并开源以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Most AI assistants respond only when prompted, remaining silent even when events in the video stream offer meaningful opportunities for conversation. To move beyond purely user-driven interaction, we present AURA 2.0, a streaming video large language model that introduces three core upgrades to AURA. The key advance is Model-Initiated Conversation (MIC), which enables the model to identify topics worth discussing in the evolving visual stream and initiate conversations at appropriate moments without a user query. Stream-Aware Tool Calling (SATC) enables the model to invoke external tools using the current visual and conversational context and incorporate returned information into the ongoing interaction. Interactive Visual Grounding (IVG) supports visual grounding in both user inputs and model outputs. The model can identify objects marked by users on video frames and produce frame-aligned bounding boxes to link its responses to specific objects and regions. To develop these capabilities, we construct a dedicated streaming interaction dataset and adopt a three-stage training scheme to integrate the new behaviors with AURA's existing streaming interaction abilities. We further incorporate Long-Horizon Interaction Memory (LHIM) to maintain contextual continuity over extended video streams. Evaluations on public streaming benchmarks show that AURA 2.0 achieves leading performance among open-source streaming models, while targeted evaluations further validate its new interaction capabilities. We open-source the model weights and inference code.

</details>


### [150] [Ropedia Xperience 2: A Gold Standard for Scaling Physical AI Environments and Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17060)
*Hao Li,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Hui En Pang,Kairui Hu,Mingze Sun,Runmao Yao,Xinhao Ji,Xiyuan Zheng,Xuanli Chen,Yalun Dai,Yap Kim Hui,Dingwen Zhang,Yuanyuan Gao,Ziang Cao,Cheng Huang Yean,Jackson Peng,Rui Duan,Yaobo Liu,Zetong Wang,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Ropedia Xperience 2 数据集：1,589 个度量尺度 3D 扫描 + 4,230 段第一人称人类演示录像（74 项任务，131 小时），并将每次采集转化为机器人学习资源——场景转为物理可交互仿真环境、人体运动重定向为全身机器人轨迹、再通过虚拟遥操作与自动化长程轨迹生成扩充机器人行为数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型（如文中举例的 GPT-6 Astra、Claude Fable 5.1）可从视觉观测直接控制机器人，但在长程、接触密集的真实任务上仍然脆弱。物理 AI 的瓶颈在于弄清"能力如何随环境与行为数据规模扩展"，因此首先需要确定该扩展哪类数据、如何持续生产与复用数据；而人类演示与可交互环境是两类互补的关键资源。

Method: 构建"环境+行为"联合采集数据集：度量尺度 3D 扫描用于重建场景并转换为物理可交互仿真环境；第一人称人类录像用于提取行为，经运动重定向得到全身机器人轨迹；进一步用虚拟遥操作与自动化长程轨迹生成产生额外机器人行为，使同一份采集可反复复用于机器人学习。

Result: 公开 1,589 个度量尺度 3D 扫描与 4,230 段第一人称录制（74 任务，131 小时）；论证了每次采集均可复用为仿真环境、机器人轨迹与合成行为数据，为跨"环境×行为"的规模化研究提供一体化数据基础。

Conclusion: 提出以"环境与行为联合采集"作为物理 AI 数据采集的黄金标准，为建立环境与行为维度上的 scaling law 奠定数据基础；本质上是数据/基准型工作，未给出端到端策略性能或真机部署结果。

Abstract: Large foundation models such as GPT-6 Astra and Claude Fable 5.1 have recently been shown to control robots directly from visual observations. They nevertheless remain brittle on long-horizon, contact-rich tasks in real-world environments. Further progress depends on two complementary resources: demonstrations of human behavior that supervise how tasks are performed, and interactive environments in which policies can be rolled out, evaluated, and improved in closed loop. Language models have advanced along well-characterized scaling laws; the corresponding question for physical AI is how capability scales with environments and behaviors, which first requires identifying which data to scale and how to produce and reuse such data continuously. To address this question, we introduce Ropedia Xperience 2, comprising 1,589 released metric-scale 3D scans and a collection of 4,230 egocentric recordings of 74 tasks (131 hours). We further show that each capture can be repurposed for robot learning: scenes are converted into physically interactive simulation environments, human motion is retargeted to whole-body robot trajectories, and additional robot behaviors are obtained through virtual teleoperation and automated long-horizon trajectory generation. We propose this joint capture of environments and behaviors as a gold standard for physical AI data collection, laying a data foundation for establishing scaling laws over environments and behaviors.

</details>


### [151] [SALT: State-Aligned Low-Rank Transfer for Single-Temporal Cross-Dataset Structural Localization in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17077)
*Aggelos Psiris,Georgios Angelidis,Giorgos Sfikas,Maria Vakalopoulou,Thomas Lagkas,Panagiotis Sarigiannidis,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SALT（状态对齐低秩迁移）框架：冻结 DINOv3 主干，仅用当前单时相影像，通过可学习正/负结构状态对每个 patch 打分，并用状态对齐低秩适配（SALA）在秩-2 子空间修正特征，配合方向性状态对齐与几何保持约束，实现跨数据集变化的单时相建筑物变化检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥感变化检测依赖空间配准的双时相影像，实际中常不可得；现有单时相方法靠从有标注源数据集学习结构证据并迁移到目标影像，但特征适配易过度特化、削弱跨数据集泛化，且当适配参数与预测所用状态解耦时风险更高。

Method: 冻结 DINOv3 主干；学习负/正结构状态，二者既对 patch 打分又通过 SALA 张成秩-2 子空间用于特征校正；方向性状态对齐监督校正方向（提升正样本证据、压低负样本证据）；几何保持约束限制预训练特征间成对关系的变化。仅 0.068M 可训练参数。

Result: 在 LEVIR-CD 与 WHU 航空建筑物数据集上、两种迁移方向、统一的 source-only 协议下均优于 4 个复现基线：LEVIR-CD→WHU 像素级 AP +9.45、IoU +27.69；WHU→LEVIR-CD AP +5.79、IoU +14.25；推理 40.2 ms/图，参数 0.068M，已开源。

Conclusion: 将低秩适配的参数子空间与预测状态显式对齐，可在极少参数下显著提升单时相变化检测的跨数据集迁移能力，为无需双时相配准的遥感变化检测提供高效方案。

Abstract: Change detection in remote sensing conventionally relies on spatially aligned bi-temporal imagery, which may be unavailable or unreliable in practical scenarios. Recent single-temporal approaches address this limitation by learning structural evidence, such as building regions, from a labeled source dataset and transferring it to unseen target imagery using only the current image. However, adaptation to the source dataset can over-specialize pretrained features and reduce their cross-dataset generalization, a risk that this paper argues is higher when the feature adaptation is parameterized independently of the states used for prediction. To address this issue, the current study introduces SALT, a State-Aligned Low-Rank Transfer framework. SALT keeps a DINOv3 backbone frozen and learns negative and positive structural states that both score each patch and, through State-Aligned Low-Rank Adaptation (SALA), span the rank-2 subspace in which patch features are corrected. Directional State Alignment supervises these corrections to raise the evidence of positive patches and lower that of negative ones, while Geometry Preservation limits changes to the pairwise relations among pretrained patch features. Experiments on LEVIR-CD and the WHU aerial building dataset show that, at the pixel level and in both transfer directions, SALT outperforms four baselines reproduced under a common source-only protocol, improving over the strongest reproduced baselines by 9.45 and 5.79 points in pixel-level AP and by 27.69 and 14.25 points in IoU (LEVIR-CD to WHU and WHU to LEVIR-CD, respectively), with only 0.068M trainable parameters and 40.2 ms per image. In order to facilitate reproducibility and further research, the source code of SALT is available at this https URL

</details>


### [152] [Joint Null-Space Projection for Data-Free Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17103)
*Tian Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JNSP：将多任务模型合并视为子空间干扰问题，对每个权重张量取各任务 delta 的前-r右奇异子空间，从每个任务向量中移除落入其他任务子空间并集中的分量，闭式、无序依赖、无需前向传播，CLIP ViT-B/16 八任务免数据合并达 89.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立微调的 checkpoint 合并可免数据、免标签、免梯度，但不同任务的 task vector 相互干扰；现有补救手段（平均、幅度剪枝、符号选举）逐元素操作，忽略了每个 delta 对隐特征施加的是低维更新。作者希望以子空间视角刻画并消除干扰。

Method: 对每个二维权重张量，对每个任务的 delta 做奇异值分解取前-r右奇异子空间（该任务移动表征最强的输入方向），然后将每个任务向量中位于其他任务子空间并集内的分量投影剔除，得到联合零空间投影（JNSP）。该投影闭式求解、与任务顺序无关、无需前向传播，完全由 checkpoint 集合定义。

Result: CLIP 八图像分类任务上，ViT-B/16 专家合并平均准确率 89.45%（未用任何图像），较最强免训练参数级基线 TIES-Merging（77.57%）高 11.88 点，较 Task Arithmetic 高 14.22 点，较 WUDI 免数据共识合并（88.47%）高 0.98 点。在 TALL 上三种骨干均成立（ViT-B/16、B/32、L/14 较 TIES 分别 +10.8、+12.6、+9.1），复现 89.20%。消融：用单一共享子空间替代逐任务子空间掉 2.9 点；顺序变体崩溃至 69.6%。RoBERTa-large 上该方法弱于朴素 task arithmetic，作者如实报告。

Conclusion: JNSP 提供了免数据、闭式、可解释的子空间干扰消除式模型合并，在视觉 CLIP 骨干上显著优于逐参数方法，且消融证实收益来自逐任务子空间设计；但方法不具跨模态普适性（NLP 骨干失效），亦未涉及机器人/具身场景。

Abstract: Merging independently fine-tuned checkpoints into a single multi-task model requires no data, no labels, and no gradient steps. In practice the task vectors of different tasks interfere, and the standard remedies--averaging, magnitude pruning, sign election--operate entry by entry, ignoring that each delta induces a low-dimensional update on the hidden features it acts upon. We recast merging as a subspace interference problem. For every two-dimensional weight tensor we take the top-r right-singular subspace of each task's delta--the input directions along which that task moves the representation most--and remove from each task vector the component lying in the union of the other tasks' subspaces. The resulting Joint Null-Space Projection (JNSP) is closed-form and order-independent, needs no forward pass, and is defined entirely by the checkpoint set. On eight CLIP image-classification tasks JNSP merges ViT-B/16 experts to 89.45% average accuracy without using a single image, 11.88 points above the strongest parameter-wise training-free baseline (TIES-Merging, 77.57%), 14.22 above Task Arithmetic, and 0.98 above WUDI's data-free consensus merge (88.47%). The gain holds across three backbones on the independently trained TALL release (+10.8, +12.6, +9.1 over TIES for ViT-B/16, ViT-B/32, ViT-L/14) and replicates there at 89.20%; controls confirm the source--a single shared subspace instead of per-task ones loses 2.9 points, and a sequential variant collapses to 69.6%. On RoBERTa-large the same projection underperforms plain task arithmetic, which we report rather than hide. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [153] [TuMAP: A Dual-View, Morphotype-Aware Pathomics Pipeline for Quantifying Renal Tubular Remodeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17104)
*Ruijie Wu,Yuechen Yang,Yanfan Zhu,Yu Wang,Shilin Zhao,Ming-Tsun Tsai,Szu-Yuan Li,Shuo-Ming Ou,Tz-Heng Chen,Haichun Yang,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TuMAP 提出一个"肾小管形态型感知"的计算病理流水线：融合双视图基础模型表征与临时形态型标注，做组成性与形态型内部的双层 pathomics 分析，并进行患者级统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肾小管形态高度异质，量化疾病相关的肾小管重塑缺乏统一框架；现有方法难以区分"形态型组成比例变化"与"同一形态型内部表型差异"这两类生物学信号。

Method: 基于 358 张 PAS 染色肾活检切片中 456,158 个肾小管的实例分割，构建三阶段流程：(1) 双视图基础模型特征 + 临时形态型伪标注，聚类识别出 8 种图像学形态型（7 种在子采样中稳定）；(2) 组成性 pathomics（形态型比例）与形态型内部 pathomics（如核实体度 solidity、偏心率、面积归一化核密度）；(3) 患者级统计检验，跨 219 例患者、6 个诊断组与微小病变（MCD）对照比较。

Result: 糖尿病肾病（DN）与肾小管间质性肾炎/急性肾小管损伤（TIN/ATI）各在 7 个形态型比例上与 MCD 显著不同，IgA 肾病为 6 个；DN 在全部 8 个形态型中均表现为核实体度下降、偏心率升高；面积归一化核密度进一步揭示形态型内部的额外差异。

Conclusion: TuMAP 提供了一个可分离"形态型组成改变"与"形态型内部表型改变"的分析框架，提升了肾小管重塑定量的可解释性与统计严谨性；但其为单中心/单染色模态的医学影像工作，尚未在其他队列、染色方法或基础模型上验证泛化性。

Abstract: Quantifying disease-associated renal tubular remodeling is challenging because tubular appearances are heterogeneous. We introduce TuMAP (Tubular Morphotype-Aware Pathomics), a pipeline combining (1) dual-view foundation-model representations and provisional morphotype annotation, (2) compositional and within-morphotype pathomics, and (3) patient-level statistical inference. From 456,158 tubules in 358 PAS-stained kidney biopsy slides, TuMAP identified eight image-based morphotypes, seven of which showed stable subsampling assignments. Across 219 patients in six diagnostic groups, diabetic nephropathy (DN) and tubulointerstitial nephritis/acute tubular injury (TIN/ATI) each differed from minimal change disease in seven morphotype proportions, while IgA nephropathy differed in six. DN showed reduced nuclear solidity and increased eccentricity across all eight morphotypes. Area-normalized nuclear density exposed additional within-morphotype differences. TuMAP provides a framework for separating changes in tubular morphotype composition from phenotypic differences within morphotypes.

</details>


### [154] [Predicting Ethanol Drinking Status from Nonhuman Primate Liver Histology: A Proof of Concept for Parameter-efficient Fine-tuning in Few-Shot Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17105)
*Amirmohammad Mohammadi,Rupak Khadka,Kathleen A. Grant,Mary Lauren Benton,Joshua Peeples*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在非人灵长类肝脏组织学WSI少样本分类任务上，系统评测VLM+PEFT（adapter）相较非PEFT基线（ABMIL等）的增益，并做注意力可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理中WSI标注昂贵、样本稀缺，希望借助VLM的参数高效微调实现少样本分类，并验证其在特定生物学终点（乙醇饮酒状态）预测中的有效性。

Method: 基于MATRR数据子集，将adapter（文本通路/视觉通路）接入VLM+ABMIL框架，与纯ABMIL等非PEFT配置在少样本（如4-shot）设置下比较平衡准确率；辅以注意力热图定性与病理学家阅片评估。

Result: 所有PEFT配置的平均平衡准确率均高于非PEFT基线；4-shot下ABMIL为43.5%，加adapter后升至78.1%。文本通路适配产生更聚焦的注意力热图，视觉通路适配更弥散；病理学家认为各配置识别的组织学特征相似。

Conclusion: PEFT+adapter对医学WSI少样本分类显著有效，且不同适配通路在注意力定位上存在差异但语义特征一致；属领域应用型实证研究，缺乏通用方法创新。

Abstract: Combining vision-language models (VLMs) with parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) offers a promising approach to few-shot whole-slide image (WSI) classification in computational pathology. This study evaluates this approach for predicting ethanol drinking status from nonhuman primate liver histology. Adapters are evaluated against non-PEFT configurations on a subset of the Monkey Alcohol Tissue Research Resource (MATRR). All evaluated PEFT configurations achieve higher mean balanced accuracy than the non-PEFT baselines. For example, in the four-shot setting, attention-based multiple instance learning (ABMIL) alone achieves 43.5% mean balanced accuracy, compared with 78.1% after adding an adapter. Qualitative attention analysis shows that textual pathway adaptations produce localized attention heatmaps, while visual pathway adaptations produce more diffuse ones. A pathologist's review identified similar histological features across adaptations.

</details>


### [155] [STeP-C: Real-Time Neural Compression for Distributed Fiber-Optic Sensing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17126)
*Tingfeng Li,Ming-Fang Huang,Shaobo Han,Yue Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STeP-C：面向DAS流式数据的神经压缩框架，解耦空间编码、时间预测与熵优化残差编码，并在实时GPU流水线上实现98.85%体积缩减与535 MB/s吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFOS/DAS产生每秒数百MB的高时空分辨率流式数据，存储与传输压力大；已有神经压缩方法要么需逐段重训练不兼容流式，要么仅离线验证，缺少在连续采集系统上端到端、大规模的实时演示。

Method: STeP-C将空间编码、时间预测、熵优化残差编码解耦，以条件熵削减为动机的率失真目标端到端训练；识别熵编码为流式部署瓶颈，采用GPU端符号计算、缓存概率表、无GIL的并行算术编码，配合FP16推理与批量熵编码，并以GPU环形缓冲区构建全实时流水线。

Result: 实测DAS试验台上文件体积缩减98.85%，全频带PSNR 31.04 dB，平均吞吐535 MB/s（超过1 kHz、81 km DAS所需的324 MB/s）；在实用流式码率下显著优于常规编解码器；对同一采集系统的未见通道范围无需重训练即可泛化；下游检测器一致性保持良好（高置信类一致率96.62%，事件类型Jaccard相似度100%）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了神经压缩在真实流式DAS系统上首个端到端实时部署证据，兼顾高压缩率、实时吞吐与下游任务一致性，工程侧揭示了熵编码在流式推理中的主导瓶颈并给出系统性优化方案。

Abstract: Distributed fiber-optic sensing (DFOS) technologies such as Distributed Acoustic Sensing (DAS) generate continuous, high-resolution spatio-temporal data at rates exceeding hundreds of megabytes per second, creating severe challenges for real-time storage and transmission. While neural compression methods can achieve strong rate-distortion performance on sensing data, prior approaches either require per-segment retraining incompatible with streaming, or have only been validated offline. None have been demonstrated end-to-end on a continuously streaming DAS acquisition system at scale. We present STeP-C (Spatio-Temporal Predictive Compression), a neural compression framework that decouples spatial encoding, temporal prediction, and entropy-optimized residual coding, trained end-to-end with a rate-distortion objective motivated by conditional entropy reduction. Critically, we identify entropy coding as the dominant bottleneck for streaming deployment. We address it through GPU-side symbol computation, cached probability tables, and parallel GIL-free arithmetic coding, together with FP16 inference and batched entropy coding, enabling a full real-time pipeline built around a GPU ring buffer. We validate this pipeline on a live DAS testbed, achieving a 98.85% file size reduction at 31.04 dB full-band PSNR and an average throughput of 535 MB/s, exceeding the 324 MB/s requirement for 1 kHz, 81 km DAS streams. We further show that STeP-C substantially outperforms conventional codecs at practical streaming bitrates, can be applied to unseen channel ranges within the same acquisition system without retraining, and largely maintains downstream detector consistency, with 96.62% high-confidence class agreement and 100% Jaccard similarity on detected event types.

</details>


### [156] [HiVE-VQA: Hierarchical Interaction via Volumetric Embedding for Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17146)
*Robbie Hamilton,Fan Zhang,David Bull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiVE-VQA 用单个冻结的 V-JEPA 2.1 视频编码器提取多层 token 网格并堆叠为分层时空体，由轻量 3D 卷积头融合层/空间/时间维，仅训练 5.2M 参数（5.7%）即刷新 NR-VQA SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NR-VQA 依赖复杂的多分支流水线或对单分支主干做昂贵微调；作者想验证单个冻结视频基础模型能否作为有效且高效的 NR-VQA 主干。

Method: 首个将 JEPA 系视频编码器用于 NR-VQA；从预训练 V-JEPA 2.1 多个深度层抽取 token 网格，堆叠成分层时空特征体，用一个紧凑 3D 卷积头在层、空间、时间三轴融合，结合浅层失真线索与深层语义/时序上下文。编码器全程冻结，无辅助多模态模型，推理开销与分辨率无关。

Result: 仅在 LSVQ 上训练、不做任何微调，在 LSVQ-test、LSVQ-1080p（同域）与 KoNViD-1k、LIVE-VQC（跨域）上，SROCC 与 PLCC 均超越 17 个 SOTA 方法。

Conclusion: 单一冻结视频基础模型的分层深度融合，比任务定制的多网络融合更适合作为 NR-VQA 的基础，且更高效；代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: The proliferation of user-generated video content has made accurate and robust no-reference video quality assessment (NR-VQA) a highly important challenge. Existing research increasingly relies on architecturally complex multi-branch pipelines or computationally intensive fine-tuning of single-branch backbones. In this paper, we investigate whether a single frozen video foundation model can serve as an effective NR-VQA backbone, and propose HiVE-VQA, the first framework (to our knowledge) to apply a JEPA-based video encoder to this task. HiVE-VQA extracts token grids from multiple depths of a pre-trained V-JEPA 2.1 encoder and stacks them into a hierarchical spatio-temporal volume, which a compact 3D convolutional head fuses across its layer, spatial, and temporal axes, combining low-level distortion cues from shallow layers with the semantic and temporal context captured at greater depths. The encoder remains frozen throughout, so only the head is trained: 5.2M parameters (5.7\% of the total), with no auxiliary multi-modal models and an inference cost that is invariant to video resolution. Trained solely on LSVQ and evaluated without any fine-tuning, HiVE-VQA outperforms 17 state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in SROCC and PLCC on both intra-dataset (LSVQ-test, LSVQ-1080p) and cross-dataset (KoNViD-1k, LIVE-VQC) benchmarks. These results indicate that hierarchical, depth-wise fusion of features from a single frozen video foundation model provides a more effective and efficient basis for NR-VQA than task-specific multi-network fusion. Code and model checkpoints are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [157] [SpotDiff: A Plug-and-Play Framework for Zero-Shot Change Detection in Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17172)
*Anit Upadhyaya,Ata Akbari Asanjan,Chaitanya Jalluri,Subash Yeggina,Jeremy Kravitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpotDiff：无需训练的零样本变化检测框架，串联 DINOv3 语义特征、AnyUp 通用特征上采样与 SAM 3 文本引导区域提议，配合推理时多尺度策略 SWIMS，直接比较重建后双时相特征定位变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变化检测依赖大量时相配准的像素级标注，难以跨区域、跨平台、跨领域扩展；现有零样本方法因特征粗糙或跨时不一致，细粒度变化定位差。

Method: 组合视觉基础模型：DINOv3 提供语义稳健特征，AnyUp 将其重建至像素分辨率，SAM 3 做文本引导 ROI 提议，并用推理时多尺度策略（SWIMS）提升小目标可靠性；在提议区域内比较两期重建特征度量变化，无任何可学习参数、微调或变化标签。

Result: 通用特征上采样较双线性上采样单独贡献 +13.8 F1；LEVIR-CD 达 82.46 F1，超最强先验免训练方法 2.06 点；SECOND 多类基准上建筑变化 +10.3 IoU、六类平均 +2.2 IoU；PlanetScope（3 m）与 Sentinel-2（10 m）定性结果表明同一流程无需修改即可跨分辨率迁移。

Conclusion: 组合现代基础模型是通往可扩展、免标注变化检测的有效路径；但属遥感变化检测，与 Physical AI/VLA/世界模型/3D 重建等方向关联较弱，主要价值在于特征上采样与基础模型组合范式的可迁移性。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection usually relies on large collections of temporally aligned, pixel-level annotations, which limits its scalability across regions, imaging platforms and application domains. Recent zero-shot approaches reduce the need for supervision, but they often localize fine-grained changes poorly because their features are coarse or inconsistent across time. We present SpotDiff, a training-free, plug-and-play framework for zero-shot change detection built by composing complementary vision foundation models. SpotDiff uses DINOv3 for semantically robust features, AnyUp to reconstruct them at pixel resolution, and SAM 3 for text-guided region-of-interest proposals, which an inference-time multi-scale strategy (SWIMS) makes reliable on small objects. Change is then measured by comparing the reconstructed features of the two dates inside the proposed regions, with no learnable components, fine-tuning or change labels. To our knowledge, SpotDiff is the first change detection method to use a universal feature upsampler, which alone adds 13.8 F1 over bilinear upsampling of the same features. On LEVIR-CD, SpotDiff reaches 82.46 F1, 2.06 points above the strongest prior training-free method, and on the multi-class SECOND benchmark it improves building change detection by 10.3 IoU and the six-class average by 2.2 IoU. Qualitative results on PlanetScope (3 m) and Sentinel-2 (10 m) imagery show that the same pipeline transfers across resolutions without modification. Our results indicate that composing modern foundation models is an effective route to scalable, label-free change detection.

</details>


### [158] [Validation-Guided Prompt Selection for Differential Morphing Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17176)
*Raghavendra Ramachandra,Hailin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用冻结参数的视觉语言评估器做差分变形攻击检测（D-MAD），通过"50候选提示+面向安全的损失选优"实现提示工程，V1在160对主体不相交测试集上取得19.17% MACER与0.00% BSCER，显著优于手工提示（70.00%）；递归修订版V2未能超越Generation-0最优验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D-MAD需比对可信活体采集与可能含面部变形的证件照。微调大模型成本高且有数据风险，作者希望验证：冻结参数VLM能否仅靠带标签验证集上的提示选择（而非参数微调）获得实质性能提升。

Method: V1：生成50条候选提示，用安全导向复合损失（以MACER为主，兼顾BSCER、响应失败、解析失败、决策不一致）在验证集上排序选优；V2：基于聚合验证反馈递归改写提示。测试集160对、主体不相交，覆盖LMA、MIPGAN-2、PIPE三类攻击；采用配对主体bootstrap置信区间评估。

Result: V1选优提示测试MACER 19.17%、BSCER 0.00%，对照手工提示MACER 70.00%；配对差50.83个百分点，95%主体bootstrap区间[43.33,58.33]。递归修订后代仅得31.67% MACER，未超越Gen-0最优验证损失。V1漏检的23例攻击全部为LMA（关键点类）变形。

Conclusion: 提示表述对冻结VLM的D-MAD性能影响显著，验证集驱动的提示选择是可行的免微调路线；但在仅10名受试者的紧凑验证集下，递归提示精炼易过拟合，难以超越单轮选择，且关键点类攻击仍是薄弱环节。

Abstract: Differential morphing attack detection (D-MAD) compares a trusted live capture with a document image that may contain a facial morph. We investigate whether labelled validation data can improve a frozen-parameter vision-language evaluator through prompt selection rather than model fine-tuning. Version~1 generates 50 candidate prompts and selects the top-ranked instruction using a security-oriented loss that prioritises missed morph attacks (MACER) while accounting for bona fide classification errors (BSCER), response failures, parse failures, and decision inconsistency. Version~2 recursively revises prompts using aggregate validation feedback. On a subject-disjoint test set of 160 pairs spanning LMA, MIPGAN-2, and PIPE, the selected Version~1 prompt achieves a (19.17\%) morph attack classification error rate (MACER), with (0.00\%) observed bona fide sample classification error rate (BSCER), compared with (70.00\%) MACER for a controlled manual prompt. The paired (50.83) percentage-point difference has a (95\%) subject-bootstrap interval of ([43.33,58.33]). Recursive revision does not surpass the best Generation~0 validation loss; the reported revised descendant obtains (31.67\%) test MACER. All 23 attacks missed by Version~1 are landmark-based (LMA) morphs. Overall, the results show that prompt formulation can substantially influence frozen-parameter VLM-based D-MAD performance, while also highlighting the challenge of recursive prompt refinement under a compact 10-subject validation setting.

</details>


### [159] [Consensus Capture in Reference-Based UAV Video Stabilization: A Benchmark on Dynamic Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17190)
*Robert Fonod,Nikolas Geroliminis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文针对悬停无人机视频参考帧稳定化中的"共识捕获"失效模式（运动车辆的一致对应把鲁棒单应估计拉向自身运动），构建静态/动态双协议基准，在4K航拍帧上评测10条配准流程，并量化掩码的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有配准流程均在"仅相机运动"的特征匹配基准上选型，而真实路口场景中同向运动车辆会提供相互一致的对应，使RANSAC类鲁棒估计被前景运动"捕获"，且内点统计无法察觉，缺乏可量化的评测手段。

Method: 静态协议：已知精确单应，站点不相交划分调参、匹配分辨率与特征预算对齐；动态协议：仅从视频本身选取覆盖不同前景运动量与一致性的悬停片段，分离线与实时两档预算并设静止对照；以人工标注地面控制点审计的伪真值为参考，度量背景对齐、目标位置误差，以及估计跟随运动组位移的比例。

Result: 静态场景中调优后的AKAZE、RootSIFT、SIFT、BRISK领先；重调匹配阈值至多提升mAA 0.0062，掩码代价小于0.005。未掩码ORB在304个实时动态对上28例被捕获（离线6例），被捕获估计中位角点误差18.8 px但内点数量与比例仍属常态。掩码消除所有流程的捕获且不改变领先流程排序。

Conclusion: 内点统计不足以诊断运动前景导致的估计偏移，需将前景掩码作为标准环节；公开代码、数据与结果以支持可复现的鲁棒性评测。

Abstract: Reference-frame stabilization registers every frame of a hovering-drone video onto one reference frame, keeping object positions comparable. The registration pipelines it relies on are selected on feature-matching benchmarks in which only the camera moves. Over a busy intersection, vehicles moving together supply mutually consistent correspondences that can draw the robust homography estimate toward their motion: consensus capture, which inlier statistics cannot flag. A two-part benchmark measures it and the object masking meant to prevent it. A static protocol scores ten pipelines under exactly known homographies on 4K aerial frames, tuned on a site-disjoint split at a matched resolution and feature budget. A dynamic protocol scores them at an offline and a real-time budget on hovers selected from the video alone to span the amount and coherence of moving foreground, plus a stationary control. It measures background alignment, object position error and the share of a moving group's displacement an estimate follows, against a pseudo-ground-truth reference audited with manually annotated ground control points, which also score every candidate. On static scenes, tuned AKAZE, RootSIFT, SIFT and BRISK lead, retuning the matcher thresholds raises the mean average accuracy (mAA) by at most 0.0062, and masking costs less than 0.005. Unmasked ORB is captured on 28 of 304 real-time dynamic pairs and on 6 offline; its real-time captured estimates have a median corner error of 18.8 px yet typical inlier counts and ratios. Masking removes capture for every pipeline and leaves the leading pipelines unchanged. Code, data and results are released.

</details>


### [160] [eDMD: Endpoint Distribution Matching for Few-Step Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17194)
*Tong Zhao,Xianfang Zeng,Yucheng Han,Ruoyu Wang,Mingkun Lei,Gang Yu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: eDMD 仅在学生模型的最终输出上训练单个共享 fake 模型，并用其校正每一个去噪步，使分布匹配项在学生最终分布等于教师分布时恰为零；配合多步教师监督实现 4 步高质量生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多步 DMD 蒸馏中，fake 打分模型在中间状态或所有去噪步上训练，使学生更新信号与中间分布纠缠，导致匹配方向失真，且即便最终输出已匹配教师，匹配项仍非零，存在目标不一致问题。

Method: 提出 Endpoint DMD：在学生的最终输出（endpoint）上训练单一共享 fake 模型作为参考分布，并应用于所有去噪步的校正；理论上证明该端点参考使分布匹配项当且仅当最终输出分布匹配教师时为零。此外引入 precise teacher（PT），在最终噪声区间用短多步轨迹替代单步引导预测，并为中间步配置校准的查询密度。

Result: 在 SD3-Medium、FLUX.2-klein（4B/9B）、Qwen-Image-2.1 上，4 步生成下 eDMD 一致优于现有分布匹配蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 将 fake 参考锚定于学生终点分布可消除中间分布污染，带来理论上干净、实践中稳定的少步扩散蒸馏；该思路可推广至其他分布匹配式蒸馏框架。

Abstract: Distribution matching distillation (DMD) has emerged as a leading paradigm for distilling diffusion models into few-step students, where an auxiliary fake score model tracks the student's distribution and the discrepancy between the teacher and fake predictions drives the student toward the teacher. In multi-step distillation, although only the student's final output is delivered, existing methods train the fake model on intermediate states or across all denoising steps. Consequently, the student's update signal becomes entangled with intermediate distributions, which can distort the matching direction and remain nonzero even after the student's final outputs already match the teacher distribution. To resolve this mismatch, we propose Endpoint Distribution Matching Distillation (eDMD), which trains a single shared fake model exclusively on the student's final outputs and uses it to correct every denoising step. Our theoretical analysis proves that this endpoint reference guarantees that the distribution matching term vanishes if and only if the student's final output distribution matches the teacher's, regardless of intermediate distributions. Beyond the fake reference, we further strengthen teacher supervision through a precise teacher (PT) that replaces single-step guided predictions with short multi-step trajectories on the final noise interval, paired with a calibrated query density for intermediate steps. Experiments on SD3-Medium, FLUX.2-klein (4B and 9B), and Qwen-Image-2.1 demonstrate that eDMD consistently outperforms existing distribution matching methods for 4-step generation.

</details>


### [161] [MALNISI-Infused Graph Construction and Weighted Contrastive Loss based GAT for Unsupervised Representation Learning in HSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17199)
*Soumi Sarkar,Subhadip Boral,Sarbani Palit,Ashish Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出基于MALNISI邻域谱相似度构建HSI图结构，结合改进图注意力网络（多头注意力+残差/跳跃连接/归一化）与邻域感知加权对比损失，得到判别性低维嵌入后做谱聚类，实现无监督高光谱地物聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像高维且标注稀缺，需利用像素间复杂谱关系进行无监督表示学习；现有图神经网络方法在构建图权重、注意力表达与过平滑控制上仍有不足。

Method: 三项贡献：(1) MALNISI指数——基于邻域信息度量两像素谱相似度，构造加权邻接矩阵得到图结构；(2) 升级版图注意力网络——将MALNISI权重作为辅助特征融入增强多头注意力机制，并引入残差连接、跳跃投影与特征归一化以缓解过平滑、保留细粒度谱细节；(3) 邻域感知加权对比损失——促使强连接像素在潜空间靠近、弱连接像素分离，最终对低维嵌入做谱聚类。

Result: 在六个基准HSI数据集上验证三项贡献的有效性与必要性，聚类性能优于14种先进方法。

Conclusion: 谱-图联合建模加加权对比学习可有效提升无监督HSI表示质量，但未涉及真实部署、开放代码或跨域泛化证据。

Abstract: Hyperspectral images (HSI) possess discriminating spectral characteristics with respect to land utilization due to high-dimensional information. Recently, unsupervised representation learning using graph neural networks has unveiled these utilization-specific complex spectral relations. This article has three main contributions. First, it proposes an index, the Mean Absolute Local Neighborhood Inverse Spectral Index (MALNISI), which measures the similarity between two pixels based on their neighborhood information. The index is used to construct a weighted adjacency matrix, giving the HSI a graph structure. Second, it proposes an upgraded Graph Attention Network that learns compact latent representations of hyperspectral data from the constructed graph. In this network, an enhanced multi-head attention mechanism incorporates the MALNISI weights as an auxiliary feature to emphasize learned attention between neighboring pixels. To alleviate over-smoothing and preserve fine-grained spectral details, the architecture integrates residual connections, skip projections, and feature normalization, which together enhance representation capability, stabilize learning, and accelerate convergence. Third, it proposes a weighted contrastive loss function that incorporates neighborhood-aware MALNISI between connected pixels, encouraging strongly connected pixels in the high-dimensional space to remain close in the latent space, and weakly connected pixels to stay apart. The resulting discriminative low-dimensional embeddings are then clustered using spectral clustering. Experimental analysis on six benchmark HSI datasets demonstrates the efficiency and necessity of the three aforementioned contributions, and the comparative study highlights superior performance over fourteen state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [162] [Objects as Addresses: Instance Binding in Vision Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17240)
*Khalil Sabri,Guillaume-Alexandre Bilodeau,Nicolas Saunier,Wassim Bouachir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究发现预训练ViT的实例分离能力主要体现在注意力路由（query-key关系）而非token内容上，同一物体的patch共享一个可编辑的“注意力地址”（common readout region），并据此提出按query分量聚类可显著提升同类别实例的分割聚合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学中的object binding表明人类将视觉场景解析为独立物体，近期工作显示预训练ViT内在具备这一能力，但自注意力机制如何在机理层面保持实例分离尚不清楚。作者希望揭示该能力的实现机制并加以利用。

Method: 在8个预训练ViT上做机制分析：对比注意力路由（query-key关系）、value相似度与残差表示的实例区分度；提出head内patch共享"注意力地址"的概念，分析共享成因（query与key空间结构的交互）；在DINOv2等骨干的选定head上编辑query分量以观察注意力地址重定向；最后基于query分量对patch聚类做无监督实例分割。

Result: 实例分离在注意力路由中表达最强，明显优于value相似度和残差表示；同一人物视觉上差异很大的部位共享同一注意力地址，而重复出现的相同实例获得独立且可迁移的地址；编辑选定head的query分量可将其注意力地址重定向至另一实例；按query分量聚类达0.615 FG-ARI，远高于末层特征聚类的0.289（后者倾向于合并同类实例）。

Conclusion: ViT的实例绑定是注意力路由层面的可解释、可干预机制，注意力地址是承载实例身份的紧凑表征；该机制可用于改进无监督实例分割及注意力层面的编辑控制。

Abstract: Humans naturally parse visual scenes into coherent individual objects rather than scattered features, a capability cognitive science calls object binding. While recent work shows that pretrained Vision Transformers (ViTs) intrinsically acquire this ability, how native self-attention computations mechanistically keep object instances separate remains unresolved. By studying this across eight pretrained ViTs, we found that instance separation is expressed more strongly in attention routing than in token content: selected query-key relations distinguish individual objects more reliably than value similarity or residual representations. Inside heads, patches belonging to one individual tend to read from a common image region, which we term their attention address. Visually distinct parts of one person share an address, whereas identical duplicated instances receive separate, movable addresses. These shared destinations arise in part because query interactions with the spatial structure of keys direct attention toward consistent regions for one object and elsewhere for another. In DINOv2 and most other backbones we tested, editing this query component at the selected head redirects its attention address toward another individual. Grouping patches by this query component separates same-category instances, reaching .615 FG-ARI versus .289 for the same clustering of final-layer features, which tend to merge them.

</details>


### [163] [Consistent Video-to-Video Translation via Explicit Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17298)
*Gaurav Parmar,Zhengqi Li,Richard Zhang,Jun-Yan Zhu,Srinivasa Narasimhan,Eli Shechtman,Yotam Nitzan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于输入视频 patch 级对应关系检索历史生成结果，并以 correspondence cross-attention 条件化，使实时 vid2vid 突破固定长度因果窗口，提升长程时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式 vid2vid 需实时生成且长程时序一致；现有方法靠固定长度因果窗口换取速度，当相关历史落在窗口外时一致性退化。

Method: 利用 vid2vid 的固有监督信号——输入中匹配的区域在输出中也应匹配：在输入视频计算 patch 级对应，据此检索先前已生成输出中对应区域的 patch，并通过 correspondence cross-attention 将这些 patch 作为条件注入模型。

Result: 在多个近期视频模型上一致提升长程时序一致性，同时保持生成质量与实时性能。

Conclusion: 以输入-输出对应作为显式跨帧参考，可在不扩大注意力窗口的前提下实现长程一致性的高效检索式条件化，为实时交互式 vid2vid 提供可插拔方案。

Abstract: Interactive video-to-video applications require real-time generation while maintaining long-range temporal consistency. However, recent methods achieve speed by restricting attention to a fixed-length causal window. This limits long-range consistency whenever relevant history falls outside the context window. To address this limitation, we introduce vid2vid-long, a correspondence-based approach grounded in a simple observation. Video-to-video translation provides an explicit cross-frame reference: the regions that match in the input video should match in the output video too. Concretely, we compute patch-level correspondences in the input, guiding retrieval of previously generated outputs in the corresponding regions, and condition the model on these patches through correspondence cross-attention. Our experiments demonstrate that vid2vid-long consistently improves long-range temporal consistency across recent video models while preserving generation quality and real-time performance.

</details>


### [164] [FLEX-LBP: Learning Local Binary Patterns with Exact Binary Inference for Texture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17302)
*Mohamed Alimoussa,Amal El Kaid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLEX-LBP通过直通估计器反向学习LBP的半径、采样角、阈值与颜色变换，前向仍计算精确二值码与硬直方图，得可解释的90参数硬二值描述子，六数据集NN平均提升3.55点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LBP是最常用的纹理描述子之一，但其半径、采样角、颜色变换与阈值均需针对每个数据集手工设定，缺乏可学习性。

Method: FLEX-LBP：对半径、采样角、阈值（及可选颜色变换）参数化，前向完全计算精确二值码与硬直方图计数，反向仅用直通估计器（STE）传播梯度；主流配置冻结颜色变换、只学几何与阈值��另给全学习版本；配LDA读出头评估。

Result: 固定配置下最近邻精度相对同一固定描述子平均提升3.55点（5种子，6基准中5个显著更优、无显著变差）；LDA读出+0.9点，全学习版+2.7点且均值最高；优于5种经典LBP、PCANet及神经LBP（6个中5个）；NN精度超过冻结ImageNet ResNet-50（2350万参数）；Kather结直肠组织学达93.8%（最优经典CLBP为92.8%）；学到半径在多种子间呈一致方向；中值采样或灰度世界归一化可恢复96–97%。

Conclusion: 将手工LBP超参转为可微学习，在保持硬二值可解释性与推理成本不变的前提下获得稳定精度增益，可作为PyTorch即插即用层；但应用面为通用纹理描述，非具身/3D核心方向。

Abstract: Local Binary Patterns (LBP) remain among the most widely used texture descriptors, from industrial inspection to medical imaging. Yet their radius, sampling angles, color transform and thresholds are fixed by hand for each dataset. We present FLEX-LBP, which learns these parameters by backpropagation while every forward pass computes exact binary codes and hard histogram counts. A straight-through estimator acts only in the backward pass, so the deployed descriptor is an interpretable hard-binary LBP with 90 learnable parameters and unchanged inference cost. In a controlled ablation on six color texture benchmarks, the component worth learning is dataset-dependent. One configuration, applied unchanged everywhere, learns the geometry and thresholds with the color transform frozen. It improves the nearest-neighbor accuracy of the identical fixed descriptor by 3.55 points on average over five seeds, significantly on five of six benchmarks and never significantly worse. With a linear discriminant readout, it gains 0.9 points; the fully learned descriptor gains 2.7 and has the highest mean. FLEX-LBP is more accurate than five classical LBP-family descriptors, PCANet and a neural LBP on five of six benchmarks. Its mean nearest-neighbor accuracy exceeds that of frozen ImageNet ResNet-50 features (23.5 million parameters). On the Kather colorectal cancer histology benchmark, the fully learned descriptor reaches 93.8%, significantly above the best classical descriptor (Completed LBP, 92.8%). The learned radii adapt in directions that recur across seeds. Noise drowns single-pixel comparisons and illuminant change flips inter-channel ones; median sampling or gray-world normalization restores 96-97%. The code is available as a drop-in PyTorch layer at this https URL .

</details>


### [165] [Spatial Foundry: A Multimodal Dataset for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17318)
*Ethan Lin,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spatial Foundry: 5,280 个可编辑住宅3D场景数据集，含平面图、透视/全景RGB、度量深度、点云、网格、实例级3D包围盒、相机轨迹+视频，共享场景坐标系支持外观/相机/场景变化的统一研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一图像难以显式表达几何、物体与持久状态的关系，而割裂的标注又无法在同一参考系下同时研究外观、相机运动与场景变化，因此需要一个多模态对齐、可编辑的3D场景配置集合。

Method: 构建5,280个带家具住宅3D场景配置：以统一场景坐标系对齐平面图、透视与全景RGB、度量深度、点云、网格、实例级3D框、相机轨迹与视频；开源可编辑源场景以生成额外相机路径和受控物体改动；给出数据表示与构建流程描述。

Result: 提供可编辑场景与受控相机/物体变化，支持平面图条件3D生成、几何重建、实例级感知、相机条件视频生成、共享世界建模与场景编辑等应用的评测与训练；公开项目页含视频与交互式彩色点云预览。

Conclusion: 该数据集为空间感知与世界模型研究提供多模态对齐、可编辑的规模化3D场景资源，但目前以资源与工作流描述为主，尚缺具体方法与量化实验证据。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence requires connecting visual observations to the geometry, objects, and persistent state of an environment. Images alone leave these relationships implicit, while disconnected annotations make it difficult to study appearance, camera motion, and scene changes under a common reference. We introduce Spatial Foundry, a collection of 5,280 furnished residential 3D scene configurations with editable assets and aligned visual, geometric, and temporal data. Its modalities include floor plans, perspective and panoramic RGB, metric depth, point clouds, meshes, instance-level 3D bounding boxes, and camera trajectories paired with video. Shared scene coordinates link observations across views and time; editable source scenes enable additional camera paths and controlled object changes. We describe the data representation and construction workflow, and discuss applications in floor-plan-conditioned 3D generation, geometric reconstruction, instance-level perception, camera-conditioned video generation, shared world modeling, and scene editing. Controlled camera and scene variations support spatial perception and world-model research. The public project page provides videos and an interactive colored point-cloud preview.

</details>


### [166] [BrickFlow: Connectivity-Guided Brick Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17321)
*Peter Kulits,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 训练集合条件 SE(3) flow 模型学习 LEGO 拼装先验，仅用 1% 数据微调注入图像条件，测试时加入解析连通性场引导，从图像推断各零件的位姿以拼装物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从图像重建 LEGO 拼装体不同于传统 3D 重建：需要满足离散物理约束（零件间可连接性），使问题呈组合爆炸且对微小位姿误差高度敏感，需要学习"零件如何相互契合"的先验。

Method: 1) 在程序化生成的装配体上训练集合条件（set-conditioned）SE(3) flow 模型作为位姿先验；2) 用约 1% 的数据微调注入图像条件；3) 发现学到的 flow 只捕捉全局结构、不保证连通性，故引入解析连通性场（connectivity-field）引导，在推理时将预测拉向连通装配。

Result: 在预训练、模型规模扩大以及测试时引导的共同作用下，拼装质量（build quality）得到提升；作者依据引导强度/模型规模报告了改进趋势。

Conclusion: 将生成式流模型先验与解析物理约束引导结合，是处理离散、组合式 3D 装配任务的有效范式，且图像条件下微调数据需求极低。

Abstract: Given a set of LEGO parts and an image of an assembled object, our task is to infer the pose of each part to assemble the object. In contrast with traditional 3D reconstruction, doing so requires satisfying discrete physical constraints, which provide structure but render the problem combinatorial and highly sensitive to small pose errors. To learn a prior over how parts fit together, we train a set-conditioned SE(3) flow model on procedurally generated assemblies. We then finetune this model to inject image conditioning, using 1% as much data. However, while the learned flow captures global structure, it does not enforce connectivity. To address this, we introduce an analytic connectivity-field guidance to pull predictions toward connected assemblies at test time. Through this, we demonstrate improved build quality with model size, pretraining, and guidance. We make our models and data available for research purposes at this https URL

</details>


### [167] [K2V: Generative Keyframing For Physically Plausible Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17324)
*Felix O'Mahony,Daniel Olmeda Reino,Roberto Cipolla,Ayush Tewari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将物理推理任务从视频扩散模型重新分配到语言模型驱动的关键帧生成模块,再以关键帧条件化扩散模型做中间状态插值,在 Physics-IQ Verified 与 PhyGenBench 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型外观质量高但存在显著物理错误,原因在于传统设置中所有物理推理必须同时且只能在扩散过程中完成,缺乏显式推理能力。

Method: 提出任务重分配框架:由 LLM 支持的模块进行显式语言推理并生成关键帧,精确控制关键时刻外观;随后以这些物理正确的关键帧作为条件,使扩散模型仅承担关键帧之间中间状态插值的简单任务。

Result: 在 Physics-IQ Verified 基准与 PhyGenBench 上显著优于现有方法,取得 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 将物理推理外置到语言模型关键帧生成、并用扩散模型做结构化插值,是一种有效提升生成视频物理一致性的范式。

Abstract: Video diffusion models are able to generate samples with high-quality appearance and textural qualities. However, content produced by these methods is known to exhibit significant physical errors. This is because, in a classical video generation setting, all physical reasoning must take place simultaneously and exclusively within the diffusion this http URL propose to resolve these issues by redistributing the task of physical reasoning onto a language model-backed keyframe generation module, which allows for explicit reasoning in language, and fine-grained control over the appearance of the video at important moments in the video. We then condition a video generation model with these keyframes, reducing the role of the diffusion model to the simpler task of interpolating between physically-correct intermediate states, rather than inferring an entire sequence with diffusion. Results demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms existing approaches, achieving state-of-the-art performance on the Physics-IQ Verified benchmark and PhyGenBench.

</details>


### [168] [TTB: Test-time Baking for Efficient Rendering of Decoder-only View Synthesis Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17331)
*Chin-Yang Lin,Ryo Hachiuma,Jaesung Choe,Min-Hung Chen,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Yen-Yu Lin,Wei-Chen Chiu,Yu-Lun Liu,Cheng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Test-Time Baking (TTB)，在 decoder-only 大视角合成模型中将跨视角注意力映射"烘焙"进轻量 MLP，实现最高 23x 提速且质量与 SOTA 持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 decoder-only 大视角合成模型（LVSMs）依赖 KV-cache 做跨视角注意力回归新视角，质量高但推理开销大；而 Test-Time Training (TTT) 虽计算高效，却因完全替换注意力而损失质量。

Method: 两个关键设计：(1) 复用 TTT 的 fast-weight MLP，以输入-输出对显式学习跨视角注意力映射本身（而非替代注意力），从而在不引入注意力逐 token 开销的前提下达到 TTT 级效率；(2) 解决 context prefilling 与新视角渲染的模态错位：将 context token 解耦为仅含相机的 context rendering token（作为 TTB 输入）与含图像内容的 context ground-truth token（作为 TTB 映射目标），避免烘焙后的 MLP 承担跨模态泛化负担。

Result: 在多个基准上实现最高 23x 渲染加速，画质与当前 SOTA 相当。

Conclusion: 通过学习式"烘焙"跨视角注意力映射，可在保持前馈视角合成质量的同时大幅降低推理成本，为实时/高效 novel view synthesis 提供可行路径；与 3D 几何无关，属前馈渲染加速类工作。

Abstract: Decoder-only Large View Synthesis Models (LVSMs) with KV-cache have recently achieved state-of-the-art quality by regressing novel views from neural networks without reconstructing 3D geometry, while Test-Time Training (TTT) layers save computation but sacrifice quality. We propose Test-Time Baking (TTB), a strategy that learns to bake global scene information from cross-view attention into a lightweight MLP, enabling fast and high-fidelity novel-view decoding. The first key is to repurpose the fast-weight MLP of TTT to learn the cross-view attention mapping explicitly from its input-output pairs, rather than replacing attention entirely, achieving the same rendering efficiency as TTT while delivering substantially higher quality.
