The second key addresses a modality misalignment between context prefilling and novel-view rendering: we disentangle context tokens into camera-only context rendering tokens to serve as TTB input, and context ground-truth tokens with image content to serve as the TTB mapping target, ensuring the baked MLPs are relieved of the modality generalization challenge between context and novel-view processing. Our method achieves up to **23x** rendering speedup with quality on par with the state-of-the-art across several benchmarks. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [169] [Unbundling Multimodal Vision Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17334)
*Ellis Brown,Chris Hoang,Shengyi Qian,Shang-Wen Li,Mike Lewis,Marjan Ghazvininejad,Luke Zettlemoyer,Lucas Beyer,Mengye Ren,Jakob Verbeek,Saining Xie,Hu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文用受控实验拆分视觉编码器的三个设计要素（预训练视觉先验、专用视觉容量、融合前放置），在同等总计算量下发现：视觉先验非必需，关键是专用视觉容量，且该容量可置于融合内部早期层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型是否应使用预训练视觉编码器、从零联合训练还是完全去掉编码器存在争议；而已发表模型在数据、架构、算力上均不同，无法隔离各设计选择的影响。

Method: 构建受控框架：所有模型在共同数据上从零预训练，在生命周期计算量匹配条件下比较VQA与语言基准；分别操纵视觉先验（预训练vs联合从零训练）、专用视觉容量（有编码器vs无编码器但主干参数等量放大）、以及容量放置位置（融合前 vs 融合内早期层视觉专用模块）。

Result: 联合从零训练的编码器与预训练编码器表现相当，无需独立训练阶段；无编码器模型即使主干参数补足仍落后于视觉理解任务；融合内早期层的视觉专用模块与融合前编码器性能相当，并可降低首token时延；结论在多种主干规模、编码器预训练视为免费、以及从预训练语言模型初始化等设定下均成立。

Conclusion: 建议采用与主干联合训练的专用视觉容量，并将其放置在主干早期层（融合内），而非依赖上游预训练编码器或完全无编码器架构。

Abstract: How multimodal language models should encode visual input remains up for debate. Most models use a pretrained vision encoder, while some recent recipes train the encoder from scratch with the language model or remove it entirely. Vision encoders bundle together three design choices: a visual prior from upstream pretraining, dedicated vision capacity, and pre-fusion placement of that capacity (i.e., before the multimodal backbone). Because published models also differ in data, architecture, and compute, comparing them cannot isolate the effect of each choice. We disentangle these choices via a controlled framework where we pretrain all models from scratch on common data and compare them on visual question answering and language benchmarks at matched lifetime compute. We find that the visual prior is not necessary; an encoder trained jointly from scratch is on par with a pretrained one, without a separate training stage. What matters instead is dedicated vision capacity: an encoder-free model trails on visual understanding even when its backbone is enlarged by the encoder's parameter count. Yet that capacity need not precede multimodal fusion, as vision-only modules added in-fusion to early backbone layers perform comparably to a pre-fusion encoder and can lower time to first token. These results also hold across backbone sizes, when encoder pretraining is treated as free, and when starting from a pretrained language model. Overall, our findings favor dedicated vision capacity trained jointly with the backbone and placed early within it.

</details>


### [170] [Back to Pixels: Lifting Latent Video Diffusion into Pixel Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17336)
*Yanke Song,Shuo Chen,Chris Rockwell,Guande He,Nan Liu,Desai Xie,Tianpei Gu,Yong-Hyun Park,Aryan V S,Dennis Yuren Menn,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Xun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixVid 将预训练潜空间视频扩散模型（Wan2.1-14B）改造为逐帧像素空间生成：保留 DiT，用卷积头替换 VAE 解码器，训练时预测干净视频而非速度并采用更大噪声偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE 时空压缩虽使训练可行，但带来三大瓶颈：分辨率加倍使 token 数翻四倍；条件只能作用于帧组而非单帧；交互响应需等待整个多帧块。作者希望直接在像素空间逐帧生成以解除这些限制。

Method: 保留扩散 Transformer，将 VAE 解码器替换为卷积渲染头，使模型直接输出像素；发现预测 clean video（x0）而非 velocity、配合更大的 noise shift 是质量关键；从 Wan2.1-14B 微调。

Result: 约 130 万训练视频片段内即与潜空间基线相当；每帧 token 数相同时分辨率提升 2 倍；深度条件视频编辑在快速运动下保持对齐；每个动作可在下一帧即时响应。

Conclusion: 像素空间的逐帧生成是突破潜空间压缩瓶颈的可行路径，兼顾质量、分辨率、可控性与交互性，且微调成本可控。

Abstract: Frontier video generation models work in a VAE latent space that compresses video in both space and time. This compression made training tractable, but now limits high-resolution, controllable, and interactive generation -- doubling resolution quadruples token count, conditions apply to groups of frames rather than individual ones, and each response must wait for the rest of its multi-frame block. We present PixVid, a simple yet effective method that lifts a pretrained latent video model to directly generate every frame in pixel space. PixVid keeps the diffusion transformer and replaces the VAE decoder with a convolutional head that renders its outputs into pixels. We find that predicting clean video rather than velocity, together with a larger noise shift, is key to quality. Finetuned from Wan2.1-14B, PixVid is competitive with the latent baseline within $\sim 1.3$M training clips. Working framewise in pixels, it generates at $2\times$ higher resolution with the same per-frame token count, keeps depth-conditioned video editing aligned under fast motion, and responds to each action at the next frame. Project page: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Emergent Vision-Shaped Thinking in Text-Only Reasoning through Multimodal Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14942)
*Qi Zhang,Bohan Wang,Qianru Sun,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过跨10种多模态配置、6个模型家族的因果消融实验发现：多模态训练在隐空间中形成"视觉-语言轴"，推理时消融该轴会定向性地损害文本空间想象任务（如BBH navigate下降27.6pp）而不影响符号推理（+5.6pp），提出"涌现的视觉化思考"（EVT）概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者追问一个机制性问题：多模态训练是否不仅改变视觉输入处理方式，还重塑了纯文本推理的内部表征与计算几何？即模型在纯文本下是否"借用"了由视觉-语言训练引入的空间表征。

Method: 在6个模型家族的10种多模态配置上进行针对性因果消融，配对纯文本backbone并做跨家族迁移对照。任务分为"空间意象类"（需构建心理场景，如navigate、mental rotation、paper folding、perspective taking）与"符号规则类"（boolean expressions）。先在隐状态空间中识别视觉-语言轴，推理时消融其投影；检验方向特异性、层局部性，并排除任务难度、推理深度、顺序更新负担等混淆。在双路径统一模型（BAGEL、Janus-Pro）中定位因果载体的通路与维度，并用集中度、对齐性、语义位置等诊断区分"可解码"与"因果使用"。

Result: Chameleon-7B中消融视觉-语言轴使BBH navigate下降27.6pp，而boolean expressions上升5.6pp，形成33.2pp的双重分离；效应具方向特异性和层局部性，且可推广到心理旋转、折纸、透视采择等心理模拟任务。效应在7-8B规模上空间集中，在32B配置中扩展到非空间的视觉属性绑定。双路径模型（BAGEL、Janus-Pro）中，因果把手定位于生成通路，表现为紧凑的一维载体；理解通路虽以更高秩、可探针解码的形式编码空间信息，但并非因果把手。行为、探针与干预结果可因此系统性分离。

Conclusion: 多模态训练重塑的是纯文本推理的表征几何，而不仅是视觉输入的处理方式；文本推理会因果性地调用由视觉-语言训练引入或强化的表征（EVT），但不必然采用图像式内部格式。研究同时提示：可解码性不等同于因果使用，行为、探针与干预三者可能分歧，需用集中度、对齐、语义位置等诊断加以甄别。

Abstract: Does multimodal training shape how a language model thinks, even when the input is text only? We test this with targeted causal ablations across 10 multimodal configurations from 6 model families, with matched text-only backbones and cross-family transfer controls. We contrast spatial-imagery tasks that invite mental scene construction with symbol-manipulation tasks solvable by symbolic rules alone. We identify a vision-language axis in hidden-state space and ablate its projection at inference. In Chameleon-7B, this ablation drops accuracy on BIG-Bench Hard (BBH) navigate by 27.6 pp while leaving boolean expressions intact (+5.6 pp), a 33.2 pp double dissociation. The effect is direction-specific and layer-localized, extends to mental-simulation tasks such as mental rotation, paper folding, and perspective taking, and is not explained by task difficulty, reasoning depth, or sequential update load. Together, these results reveal emergent vision-shaped thinking (EVT): text-only reasoning that causally recruits representations introduced or strengthened by vision-language training, without assuming an image-like internal format. The measured effect is spatially concentrated at 7-8B and broadens to non-spatial visual attribute binding in the tested 32B configuration. In dual-path unified models (BAGEL, Janus-Pro), the causal handle localizes to the generation pathway as a compact one-dimensional carrier. The understanding pathway also encodes spatial information in a higher-rank, probe-decodable form, but is not the causal handle. To separate decodability from causal use, we analyze concentration, alignment, and semantic location. These diagnostics explain why behavior, probing, and intervention can diverge. Multimodal training thus reshapes the geometry of text-only reasoning, not just how visual input is processed.

</details>


### [172] [AFTER: A World Model with Adaptive Fast--Slow Timescales for Error-Driven Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15215)
*Haoyi Zhang,Qi Zhang,Yunfan Ren,Yi Bin,Lei Zhu,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 借鉴大脑预测编码的多时间尺度适应机制，提出AFTER-WM世界模型：预测误差驱动快速（衰减）与慢速（持久）两种修正，通过动态调节预训练专家组合权重而不改变专家参数，同时兼顾动力学适应的响应性、稳定性与持久性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在交互中常遇突发、持久但未知的动力学变化（如抓取未知负载），现有世界模型难以同时满足三重要求：响应性（快速反应抑制误差累积）、稳定性（区分持久变化与瞬时扰动避免错误更新）、持久性（无新观测时仍保留已适应的动力学）。

Method: 受人类大脑预测编码多时间尺度适应启发，构建双时间尺度误差驱动修正机制：快修正随时间衰减、慢修正持久保留；并通过动态调整预训练专家模型组合权重（专家参数冻结）实现适应。

Result: 在5个仿真机器人数据集与真实无人机数据上，多步平均误差低于全部10个对比模型，并在联合失效控制任务中取得最高平均回报；仿真中多步平均误差比最强基线低16%–34%，在变化后第1–4步、纯瞬态扰动下及第40步误差均为最低。

Conclusion: AFTER-WM以快慢双时间尺度误差驱动修正加专家权重动态组合，有效缓解响应性、稳定性与持久性之间的权衡，并在仿真与真实UAV数据上验证了其多步预测与控制效果；代码与数据集将公开。

Abstract: In robotics and embodied AI, a core challenge for world models is adapting to sudden, persistent, yet initially unknown changes in robot dynamics during interaction. For instance, when a robotic arm grasps an object with an unknown payload, the robot dynamics change suddenly. If the world model fails to adapt, its prediction errors accumulate over the open-loop rollout and ultimately mislead the robot's actions. Existing world models, however, struggle to simultaneously satisfy three critical requirements for dynamics adaptation: responsiveness, reacting promptly to sudden changes to curb prediction error accumulation; stability, distinguishing persistent changes from transient disturbances to prevent incorrect updates; and persistence, retaining the adapted dynamics without new observations so that later predictions do not revert to the old dynamics. Inspired by predictive coding in the human brain with multi-timescale adaptation, we propose AFTER-WM, a world model with Adaptive Fast-slow Timescales for Error-driven Retention, whose prediction errors drive a fast correction that fades and a slow correction that persists, while dynamically adjusting the combination weights of pre-trained experts without altering them, to overcome the trade-offs among responsiveness, stability and persistence. On five simulated robot datasets and real UAV data, AFTER-WM has a lower multi-step mean error than all ten compared models and the highest mean return in a joint-failure control task. In simulation, its multistep mean error is 16%-34% below the strongest compared model's and is also the lowest 1-4 steps after a change and under transient disturbances alone, and its step-40 error is the lowest as well, reflecting responsiveness, stability and persistence. All code (method, baselines, experiments) and datasets will be released online.

</details>


### [173] [A physics-constrained deep reinforcement learning method for autonomous mission planning of Titan deep space probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15389)
*Hanmin Li,Tao Wang,Shuangliang Liu,Zhe Zhang,Maodeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出物理约束可掩码近端策略优化（PCM-PPO）用于土卫六探测器自主任务规划，结合含中心引力、J2摄动、第三体引力与下行遮蔽的动力学模型，用固定容量目标池与物理约束动作掩码引导策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土卫六距离地球极远、环境复杂，地面在环规划不可行；现有规划方法多面向地球观测场景，难以泛化到不确定深空条件与时变任务状态。

Method: 建立土卫六专用离散时间马尔可夫决策过程刻画任务可行性与资源约束；引入系统动力学与几何模型（中心引力、J2摄动、第三体引力、下行遮蔽）增强物理表征；以固定容量目标池组织预定义与机会目标，用目标级状态矩阵与全局状态向量表示任务状态；构建物理约束动作掩码划定可行动作域，配合一体化训练策略在耦合约束下优化策略。

Result: 对比实验、消融实验与不确定性实验验证了PCM-PPO的有效性。

Conclusion: PCM-PPO为土卫六深空探测器自主任务规划提供了可行且可解释的方案。

Abstract: Titan is a compelling target for future deep space exploration, while its extreme Earth-Titan distance and complex environment make continuous ground-in-the-loop planning impractical. Existing planning methods, primarily developed for Earth-observation scenarios, have limited generalizability to uncertain deep space conditions and adaptability to time-varying mission states. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a physics-constrained maskable proximal policy optimization (PCM-PPO) method for Titan autonomous mission planning. A Titan-specific discrete-time Markov decision process is established to characterize mission feasibility and resource constraints. A system dynamics and geometric model is incorporated to enhance physical representation, including central gravity, J2 perturbation, third-body attraction, and downlink occultation. To accommodate opportunity targets under a bounded action dimension, PCM-PPO organizes predefined and opportunity targets through a fixed-capacity target pool and represents the mission state with a target-wise state matrix and a global state vector. A physics-constrained action mask constructs an admissible decision domain from current mission states, guiding policy learning toward physically executable decisions and avoiding infeasible exploration. An integrated training strategy facilitates policy optimization under coupled constraints. Comparative, ablation, and uncertainty experiments validate the effectiveness of PCM-PPO. PCM-PPO provides a feasible and interpretable scheme for autonomous mission planning of Titan deep space probes.

</details>


### [174] [SkillEarth: Teaching General-Purpose Agents to Act on Earth via Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15481)
*Haojun Tang,Wenda Zhao,Xiang Zhou,Qiwei Lin,Lida Huang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillEarth：面向遥感（RS）通用智能体的自演化技能抽取方法，将工具调用知识外化为可复用、可检索、可演化的技能文件，并基于真实工具链执行观测进行组件级精修。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感智能体依赖预设专家知识或参数更新，新工具/新任务/新执行模式出现时需重新引入专家或重训模型，难以在动态地表环境中持续适应。作者希望无需专家干预与参数更新，仅从演示轨迹中自动抽取RS工具调用知识。

Method: 提出SkillEarth：自演化技能抽取框架。将RS工具调用知识外部化为可复用、可检索、可演化的技能文件；与仅依赖结果级反思的整体式技能精修不同，SkillEarth以真实工具链执行为依据，结合双评委（dual-judge）反馈与基于观测的路由图（grounded observation routing graph），实现组件级精准精修。

Result: 在3个前沿LLM与4个开源LLM上均提升整体RS工具调用性能；技能经真实工具执行反馈演化；支持无需重训的跨模型复用。在OpenEarth-Bench与Earth-Bench上，工具选择、参数正确性、执行顺序一致性与任务完成度均优于现有RS智能体与技能抽取方法。

Conclusion: 以“技能文件+观测接地演化”替代参数更新，可为通用智能体注入可迁移的遥感工具调用能力；主要证据为基准评测结果，尚缺乏真实业务部署层面的验证。

Abstract: Recently developed remote sensing (RS) agents execute real-world tasks with complex workflows through multi-step tool invocation. These agents, however, typically rely on predefined expertise or parameter updates. They thus require updating expert knowledge or retraining models whenever new tools, tasks, or execution patterns emerge, limiting their continual adaptability in dynamic Earth environments. In this work, we seek to equip general-purpose agents with RS tool-call knowledge that can be automatically extracted from demonstrated trajectories without expert intervention or parameter updates. Specifically, we propose SkillEarth, a self-evolving skill extraction method that externalizes RS tool-call knowledge as reusable, retrievable, and evolvable skill files and refines them through observation-grounded evolution. Unlike existing automatic skill extraction methods that rely on outcome-based reflection to refine skills holistically, SkillEarth grounds skill evolution in real tool-chain execution and combines dual-judge feedback with a grounded observation routing graph for precise component-level refinement. The resulting skills are generalizable, reliable, and transferable: they improve overall RS tool-call performance across three frontier LLMs and four open-source LLMs, are refined through real tool execution feedback, and support cross-model reuse without retraining. Experiments on OpenEarth-Bench and Earth-Bench demonstrate that SkillEarth achieves better tool selection, argument correctness, execution-order consistency, and task completion than existing RS agents and skill extraction methods.

</details>


### [175] [AstroMemBench: A Benchmark for Memory Systems in Embodied Spacecraft Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16590)
*You Wu,Guhong Zhang,Hongwei Li,Xinyue Hong,Yuan Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AstroMemBench：面向航天器具身智能体的长期记忆基准，包含 16 个记忆子挑战、140 个模板、2240 个实例、9 项任务，采用 Scenario-Challenge-Capability 设计法，并在确定性环境中按任务完成度、状态变化、安全性与资源消耗打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间航天器自主运行要求具身智能体在通信延迟、时间紧迫、环境不确定、复杂物理交互、功耗与算力受限、以及安全可靠性约束下利用运行历史，但缺乏可执行、可量化的记忆评测手段。

Method: 提出 Scenario-Challenge-Capability 设计方法，把运行需求映射为可执行记忆测试；构建覆盖行星表面作业、在轨服务、深空探测三类场景、9 项任务、16 个记忆子挑战的基准（140 模板 / 2240 实例）；使用确定性环境评分动作（任务完成、状态变化、安全性、资源使用）；进行 9 种条件的消融实验与 6 种配置的主实验对比（原生 Pi Agent、RAG、Mem0、Chain-of-Memory、LightMem）。

Result: 消融实验表明基准对历史信息恢复与使用精度、以及记忆系统设计差异敏感；同一会话内 Mem0、Chain-of-Memory、LightMem、RAG 均优于原生 Pi Agent，但三种记忆系统相对 RAG 无显著优势；会话重启后 Mem0 比 RAG 高 12.25 分；收益集中在指令保持与结果复用，而非持续状态跟踪与能量预留。

Conclusion: AstroMemBench 为具身航天器智能体的记忆系统设计、开发与选型提供可复现评测平台，1.0 版本与评测代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-term spacecraft autonomy requires embodied agents to use operational history under communication delays, time-critical operations, environmental uncertainty, complex physical interactions, power and computational constraints, and safety and reliability requirements. We introduce AstroMemBench and a Scenario-Challenge-Capability design method that maps these requirements to executable memory tests. The benchmark covers 16 memory subchallenges through 140 templates and 2240 instances across nine tasks in planetary surface operations, on-orbit servicing, and deep-space exploration. A deterministic environment scores actions for task completion, state changes, safety, and resource use. Ablations under nine conditions show that the benchmark is sensitive to how accurately agents recover and use historical information and to differences in memory-system design. Main experiments compare six configurations. Mem0, Chain-of-Memory, LightMem, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improve same-session mean scores over native Pi Agent, with no clear advantage of the three memory systems over RAG. After a session restart, Mem0 outperforms RAG by 12.25 points. Benefits favor instruction retention and result reuse over ongoing state tracking and energy reservation. AstroMemBench supports the design, development, and selection of memory systems for embodied spacecraft agents. AstroMemBench 1.0 and its evaluation code are publicly available: this https URL .

</details>


### [176] [Safety as a Budget: Calibrating the Control Channel of Constrained GRPO for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16628)
*Bo Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为VLM安全拒绝行为引入双通道约束GRPO：分离原始奖励与惩罚/约束估计通道，修复均值守恒器偏置与组内标准化抵消乘子两个失效点，显著降低有害帮助率且不增加过度拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF式方法只能通过试错调整奖励权重间接设置VLM拒绝有害图文请求的安全水平；将安全表述为有害率与过度拒答率的预算约束、用拉格朗日乘子在GRPO内部强制，是自然方案，但存在两个结构性失效。

Method: 指出并分析两种失效：(1) 乘子比较打分器均值与预算，而面向排序训练的scorer均值有系统偏置，不动点分析表明均值偏置而非预算决定乘子的收敛/饱和/关断状态；(2) 标准GRPO在组内标准化惩罚奖励，乘子在进入梯度前被抵消。提出双通道约束GRPO：基础奖励保留原始分数用于组内比较，惩罚与约束估计经在judge标注训练响应上拟合的单调映射传递，优势函数保留乘子。

Result: Qwen3-VL-2B策略（以开源4B模型免训练读出作打分器）：1,037条对抗提示的留出有害率由标准GRPO的8.9%降至4.8%，各随机种子均更低；未校准预算为7.6%。400条良性查询上过度拒答≤2%，MMBench不变。意图在图像中时增益最大（10.1%→5.1%），未见过的HADES基准28.9%→12.8%。保序分数扰动可在每个种子复现预测的控制器状态，结果在他人judge标签、读图judge与人工审核下均成立。

Conclusion: 约束化安全对齐的失败源于打分器均值偏差与GRPO组内标准化对乘子的抵消，而非预算设置本身；双通道设计使乘子真正作用于梯度，实现可解释的安全预算控制，并对不同judge与分布外基准稳健。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can teach vision-language models (VLMs) to decline harmful image-text requests, but how safe the result becomes is set indirectly, through reward weights found by trial runs. Stating safety as budgets on the rate of harmful assistance and the rate of over-refusal, enforced by Lagrange multipliers inside GRPO with an off-the-shelf scorer as the cost, is the natural fix, and it fails twice. The multiplier compares the scorer's mean with the budget, and scorers built for ranking get the mean wrong: a fixed-point analysis shows that the mean bias, not the budget, then decides whether the multiplier settles, saturates or switches off. And standard GRPO standardizes the penalized reward within each group, which cancels the multiplier before it reaches the gradient. Two-channel constrained GRPO repairs both: the base reward keeps the raw score for the within-group comparison, the penalty and the constraint estimate pass through a monotone map fitted on judge-labeled training responses, and the advantage keeps the multiplier. On a Qwen3-VL-2B policy with a training-free readout of an open 4B model as the scorer, held-out harm on 1,037 adversarial prompts falls from 8.9% under standard GRPO to 4.8%, lower in every seed, where the uncalibrated budget gives 7.6%; over-refusal stays at most 2% on 400 benign queries and MMBench is unchanged. The gain is largest where the intent sits in the image (10.1% -> 5.1%) and grows on the unseen HADES benchmark (28.9% -> 12.8%). Order-preserving distortions of the scores reproduce each predicted controller state in every seed, and the results hold under a map fitted on another judge's labels, an image-reading judge and a human audit.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Terrain-Aware Thompson Sampling for Multi-Drone Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14685)
*Riana Gagnon Souleiman,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出去中心化、地形感知的多无人机Thompson Sampling搜索救援算法：以失联者行为模型(位移环、高程、流域、扩散角、轨迹偏移)构建定位概率分布，逐栅格高斯后验经贝叶斯更新实现探索-利用平衡，并用"森林覆盖率"建模遮挡导致的观测不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR场景中失联者位置高度不确定，且地形与植被遮挡显著降低探测性能；需要多机协同的主动搜索策略在巨大搜索空间内高效分配采样，同时兼顾不确定区域探索与高概率区域利用。

Method: 多无人机去中心化Thompson Sampling；每单元高斯后验(均值/方差)由贝叶斯推断更新，候选动作通过对后验采样选出；先验由YOSAR历史案例导出的失联者行为模型融合地形数据生成；观测模型引入分式森林覆盖率刻画树冠遮挡造成的检测率衰减。仿真对比覆盖式规划器与概率主动搜索基线，网格为1.5与3.8 km²，5/10/15/20架无人机，50 km/h。

Result: 1.5 km²区域内成功率约87%，耗时60–247 s；3.8 km²区域内成功率约75%–91%，耗时161–466 s，覆盖多种无人机数量配置。

Conclusion: 将地形先验与遮挡感知观测模型嵌入去中心化Thompson Sampling，可显著提升多无人机SAR搜索的效率与成功率，且随搜索区域扩大仍保持稳健；后续可考虑真实飞行验证与通信受限下的去中心化一致性。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a decentralized terrain-aware multi-drone Thompson Sampling algorithm tailored for search and rescue (SAR) applications. A probability distribution of locating a lost person is informed by terrain data, using established SAR models (displacement ring, elevation, watershed, dispersion angle, and track offset) derived from historical SAR case data on lost person behavior. Exploration is conducted by a team of drones using Thompson Sampling, where candidate actions are selected by drawing samples from this probability distribution, represented by a per-cell Gaussian posterior whose mean and variance are updated through Bayesian inference. This approach balances exploration of uncertain regions with exploitation of high-probability areas to locate a lost person. Observation uncertainty is incorporated through fractional forest coverage, which measures the percentage of an observation occluded by tree coverage and accounts for reduced detection performance in densely vegetated areas. Simulations using real SAR case data (YOSAR) evaluate the proposed method against a coverage planner and a probabilistic active search method in search regions of 1.5 and 3.8 square kilometers, with 5, 10, 15, and 20 agents flying at 50 km/hr. We demonstrate that our method achieves a success rate of approximately 87% within 60 to 247 seconds in the 1.5 square kilometer region, and success rates of approximately 75% to 91% within 161 to 466 seconds in the 3.8 square kilometer region, across different agent configurations.

</details>


### [178] [SunLightBots: Turning sunlight into self-sustained, high-power motion in soft robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14729)
*Yoo Jin Lee,Melanie Torres Crespo,Zhongxiao Deng,Andrew Tejada-Vega,Rebecca Kramer-Bottiglio,Wenzhong Yan,Yimin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SunLightBots：亚克级、太阳能驱动的无缆软体机器人，靠"自复位突跳（SRS）"机制将低功率太阳能在液晶弹性体（LCE）中积累后瞬时释放，实现持续自主游泳、爬行与脉冲跳跃，峰值质量比机械功率达89 W/kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人虽可把环境刺激直接转为运动，但环境能量采集功率低，而快速、高负载动作需要高机械输出功率，二者存在功率失配；亚克级尺度下载荷能力有限，难以集成常规电源与控制硬件。

Method: 将LCE驱动与突跳（snap-through）不稳定性耦合，构建自复位突跳机制：缓慢积累太阳能→失稳瞬间释放为高速高功率动作→通过光热-机械反馈实现复位；联合优化LCE驱动温度、光热转换效率、驱动应力与结构能垒，使系统在约1 Sun（100 mW/cm²）下工作。优化后LCE在约60 °C下产生0.1 MPa驱动应力。

Result: 在近1 Sun下实现持续自主游泳、爬行与重复脉冲跳跃；峰值质量比机械功率89 W/kg；1.5 Sun下游泳机器人单周期前进超三个体长。

Conclusion: 提出一条材料-力学协同路径，实现完全由环境能量驱动的自主软体机器人，为亚克级无缆系统的能量自主与"具身控制"提供范式。

Abstract: Soft robots built from active materials can directly convert environmental stimuli into motion, offering a route to energy autonomy and embodied control. However, environmental energy is generally harvested at low power, whereas rapid, mechanically demanding actions require high mechanical output power. Reconciling this mismatch while sustaining self-regulated cyclic actuation is challenging, particularly at the subgram scale, where limited payload capacity constrains the integration of conventional power and control hardware. Here, we present SunLightBots, a class of subgram, sunlight-powered, untethered soft robots that achieve autonomous locomotion under continuous illumination near 1 Sun (100 mW/cm2). Their operation is enabled by a self-resetting snapping (SRS) mechanism that integrates liquid crystal elastomers (LCEs) with snap-through instability to accumulate solar energy supplied at low power, release it as rapid, high-power motion, and reset through photothermal-mechanical feedback. Through coordinated optimization of the LCE actuation temperature, photothermal conversion, actuation stress, and structural energy barrier, the system operates near 1 Sun and achieves a peak mass-specific mechanical power of 89 W/kg. At this irradiance, the optimized LCE generates an actuation stress of 0.1 MPa at approximately 60 deg C. SunLightBots demonstrate sustained swimming, crawling, and repeated impulsive jumping. Under 1.5 Suns, the swimmer advances more than three body lengths per cycle. This work provides a materials-mechanics route toward autonomous soft robots powered entirely by the environment.

</details>


### [179] [Open-Source Transtibial Prosthetic Bypass Preserving Joint Alignment: Gait Learning and Kinematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14762)
*Apolline de Vaulchier,Pablo Nocito,Yann Corbel,Nathanaël Jarrassé*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: An open-source transtibial prosthetic bypass that preserves partial sagittal knee mobility without contralateral shoe lift or lateral offset; six able-bodied participants achieved consistent walking after ~10 min familiarization, with kinematics validated against prosthesis-user data.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Able-bodied bypasses enable safe early-stage testing of new transtibial prosthetic devices before clinical trials, but existing bypasses impose gait-altering constraints (contralateral shoe lift, lateral prosthesis offset) whose effects are poorly documented.

Method: Open-source mechanical bypass design preserving part of sagittal knee mobility with no shoe lift or lateral offset. Six able-bodied participants familiarized ~10 min, then walked on a treadmill with/without the bypass; joint kinematics captured by motion capture and extracted with OpenSim, compared against literature values for transtibial prosthesis users.

Result: All participants walked without handrail support; bypass-side single stance was shorter (27% vs 45% of gait cycle). Bypass-side ankle kinematics matched transtibial prosthesis users, while swing-phase peak knee flexion dropped 61°→36° (p<0.001) and sound-leg hip range rose 35°→51° (p<0.01).

Conclusion: Gait did not fully replicate that of prosthesis users but was consistent after only 10 min of learning; the open-source bypass is a practical tool for preliminary lower-limb prosthetic experiments with able-bodied participants.

Abstract: Prosthetic bypasses allow able-bodied individuals to walk with a prosthesis, enabling safe early-stage testing of new prosthetic devices before clinical trials. However, existing transtibial bypasses impose constraints, such as a contralateral shoe lift or a lateral offset of the prosthesis, and their effects on gait remain poorly documented. We present an open-source transtibial prosthetic bypass that preserves part of the sagittal knee mobility without these constraints. Six able-bodied participants familiarized themselves with the bypass for approximately 10~min, then walked on a treadmill with and without it, and joint kinematics were recorded with motion capture and then extracted with OpenSim. All participants were able to walk without handrail support, although single stance remained shorter on the bypass side (27% vs. 45% of the gait cycle). Bypass-side ankle kinematics were consistent with those reported for transtibial prosthesis users, whereas peak knee flexion during swing decreased from 61° to 36° (p<0.001) and hip range of the sound leg increased from 35° to 51° (p<0.01). Although gait with the bypass does not fully replicate that of prosthesis users, it yielded consistent gait patterns after only 10~min of learning. This bypass therefore provides a useful tool for the lower-limb prosthetics research community, facilitating preliminary experiments with able-bodied participants.

</details>


### [180] [Learning Low-Latency Action Verification for Frozen Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14834)
*Wen-Han Hsieh,Ken Jiankun Zheng,Chia-Chien Chen,David M. Chan,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HDH：利用冻结VLA自身部署回放数据训练轻量动作验证器，在推理时对候选动作块打分择优，提升机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA采样的动作序列常无效，而用重型验证器评估多个候选会显著增加每次策略查询的延迟，需在性能与效率间取得平衡。

Method: 三阶段：1) 部署冻结策略采集自主rollout；2) 用其 hindsight 数据训练紧凑动作验证器，为动作块预测任务成功分数；3) 冻结VLA生成候选动作，由验证器选取得分最高者执行。

Result: LIBERO-10四个任务平均成功率82.3%；三个真实操作任务上相对基线策略平均成功率提升22.6%。

Conclusion: 无需修改VLA本体、复用自身部署数据即可低成本实现动作择优，兼顾性能提升与推理效率，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have made significant progress in general-purpose robotic manipulation. However, sampled action sequences can be ineffective, while evaluating alternatives with computationally expensive verifiers adds latency at each policy query. In this work, we propose HDH, a lightweight framework that uses hindsight from a frozen VLA's own deployments to improve action verification. The framework has three stages. First, we deploy the policy and collect its autonomous rollouts. We then train a compact action verifier to assign each action chunk a predicted task-success score. Finally, the frozen VLA generates action candidates, and the verifier selects the candidate with the highest score for execution. Across four LIBERO-10 tasks, HDH achieves an average success rate of 82.3\% and on three real-world manipulation tasks, HDH improves average success over the baseline policy by 22.6\%.

</details>


### [181] [Can I join you? Extended Social Force Based Model with Predictive Control and Barrier Functions for Humanoid Group Repositioning Amidst Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14853)
*Chuxuan Zhang,Niloofar Jahanshahi,Yasaman Etesam,Mo Chen,Angelica Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出由 MPC 前瞻规划、CBF 约束保证安全的改进社会力（Social Force）框架，用于人形机器人群体在动态干扰下的社会性重定位行为，并通过消融实验与在线用户实验验证其更平滑、更安全且保持社会规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共空间中服务/社交机器人会成组行动，仅完成初始群体编队不足以应对动态场景：行人或其他智能体加入、离开或穿越会破坏队形。群体动力学与重定位在群体机器人和虚拟智能体领域已有研究，但人形机器人群体中"社会可接受的重定位行为"关注不足。现有社会力方法虽有良好基础，但障碍处理能力有限，环境复杂度上升时会产生不安全行为。

Method: 将传统 Social Force 模型作为底层行为生成器，用 Model Predictive Control 进行前瞻性重规划以调制社会力参数/目标，并以 Control Barrier Functions 作为安全约束（安全集/可行性约束）嵌入优化，形成 SF + MPC + CBF 的三层耦合框架，处理加入、离开、穿越等多类群体编队扰动；评测采用仿真消融实验与在线用户研究。

Result: 消融实验与在线用户研究显示，所提方法在复杂环境中产生更平滑、更安全的重定位轨迹，同时保持社会适当性（用户主观评价），各组件（MPC、CBF、SF 调制）对性能均有贡献。

Conclusion: 通过 MPC 前瞻与 CBF 安全约束增强社会力模型，可在动态干扰下为人形机器人群体提供兼顾安全性与社会规范的重定位行为；但证据仍以仿真与主观评测为主，缺乏真机部署验证与与学习式人群/群体模型的对比。

Abstract: As social and service robots increasingly share interaction spaces in public, they may form groups while coordinating tasks or interacting with humans. Initial group formation alone is insufficient in dynamic settings, because it can be disrupted when a person or another agent joins, leaves, or passes through the group. Although group dynamics and repositioning have been widely studied in swarm robotics and virtual-agent settings, little attention has been given to socially appropriate repositioning behaviors in groups of humanoid robots. Existing social-force-based approaches provide a useful foundation for group behavior, but they have limited obstacle handling, and can produce unsafe behavior as environmental complexity increases. We propose a novel framework that modulates traditional Social Force, guided by Model Predictive Control (MPC) to plan ahead, bounded by Control Barrier Functions (CBF) to increase safety for robot groups repositioning behaviors. This approach aims to produce safer and socially aware group repositioning behaviors under various group formation disturbance. We evaluate the method in simulation through an ablation study and an online user study. The results show that our proposed method can produce smoother and safer repositioning behaviors in complex environments while preserving social appropriateness.

</details>


### [182] [DIVAL: Diffeomorphic Vector-Field Alignment for Nonholonomic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14918)
*Yike Qiao,Xiaodong He,An Zhuo,Zhiyong Sun,Weimin Bao,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出微分同胚矢量场对齐(DIVAL)方法,通过局部微分同胚变形重塑标称矢量场,使其在初始位置与机器人初始航向对齐,消除非完整机器人的初始航向失配,从而保证全程避障收敛与平滑控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于矢量场的非完整机器人运动规划分两步:先设计标称矢量场,再控制机器人跟随。但非完整机器人无法瞬时跟随矢量场方向,初始航向与场方向失配会导致避障失败与碰撞。

Method: 对工作空间施加局部微分同胚变形以重塑标称矢量场:使重塑场在初始位置与机器人航向同向,且在变形区域外与原标称场完全重合。由此机器人从起步即保持航向与场方向对齐,并继承原场的避障、收敛、平滑控制等性质。

Result: 数值仿真中与原始矢量场跟踪基线对比,DIVAL 在所有试验中实现无碰撞收敛并避免控制不连续;地面车辆硬件实验在不同目标和初始条件下、真实扰动环境中均实现无碰撞收敛。

Conclusion: DIVAL 以保结构的方式解决了非完整机器人初航向失配问题,是一种兼具理论优雅性与实机可验证性的矢量场运动规划改进。

Abstract: Conventional vector-field-based motion planners for nonholonomic robots consist of two steps: first designing a nominal vector field and then controlling the robot to follow it to approach the target. However, a nonholonomic robot cannot follow the vector field instantaneously and may collide with obstacles, due to the inevitable mismatch between the robot's initial heading and the vector field's direction. This paper proposes a diffeomorphic vector-field alignment (DIVAL) method, which aligns the vector field's direction with the robot's initial heading so the robot can follow the vector field from the start. Specifically, the nominal vector field is locally reshaped by applying a diffeomorphic deformation to the workspace, such that the reshaped vector field has the same direction as the robot's heading at the initial position and coincides with the nominal vector field outside the deformed region. Since DIVAL eliminates the initial heading mismatch, the nonholonomic robot can maintain heading alignment with the reshaped vector field throughout the motion. This heading alignment further ensures that the robot preserves the nominal vector field's properties, including obstacle avoidance, convergence to the target, and smooth control signals. Numerical simulations provide vector-field-tracking baselines where the nominal vector fields are reshaped with DIVAL. Compared to the original baselines, the proposed method achieves collision-free convergence in all trials and avoids control discontinuities. Hardware experiments on a ground vehicle further demonstrate collision-free convergence across different targets and initial conditions under real-world disturbances.

</details>


### [183] [The Posterior-Prior Epistemic Gap: When World Model Imagination Fails to Carry Task-Relevant Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14955)
*Jaerock Kwon,Elahe Delavari,Donghyun Kim,Haewoon Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文在CARLA中用RSSM世界模型+主动推理规划，通过同一监督探针量化出"后验-先验差距"：想象rollout中障碍信息单调衰减，且规划器自身集成不确定性无法察觉该失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的规划器默认转移模型能忠实传播任务相关信号，但这一性质很少被直接测量；作者希望直接从模型内部状态诊断想象rollout中的信息损失，并检验规划器能否自我察觉。

Method: 单种子案例研究：CARLA驾驶场景下的RSSM世界模型配合主动推理规划。训练一个监督式障碍物探针，分别作用于后验（观测滤波）状态与想象出的先验状态，比较AUC与logit分离度的逐步变化；并用集成分歧度作为认知不确定性指标检验其是否能标记信息丢失。附加贡献：对同一世界模型的Effective Free Energy各分量做任务条件加权。

Result: 探针在后验状态AUC=0.85，作用于想象先验状态时15步内从0.85单调降至0.71，logit分离度下降57%；因探针相同，衰减可归因于转移模型而非表征。无特权运行时坐标时避障成功率降为0%；集成分歧度的障碍分类AUC=0.52（随机水平），未能标记失效。任务条件加权实现车道保持81.2±1.8%路线完成、28/28避障（使用特权坐标）。

Conclusion: "后验-先验差距"提供了低成本、可操作的转移模型诊断手段，并揭示"未被标记的想象失效"这一规划时的未知未知风险；方法为单种子案例研究，结论的统计泛化性有限，且避障依赖特权坐标，实用性待验证。

Abstract: Model-based planners that roll out imagined futures assume the transition model faithfully propagates task-relevant signals---a property rarely measured directly. In a single-seed case study of a Recurrent State Space Model world model with active-inference planning in the CARLA driving simulator, we identify a \textbf{posterior-prior gap}---posterior (observation-filtered) states retain obstacle information that imagined rollout states progressively lose: a supervised obstacle probe achieves AUC 0.85 on posterior states, but the same probe applied to imagined prior states shows monotonic decay (AUC 0.85$\rightarrow$0.71 over 15 steps; logit separation drops 57\%). Because the probe is identical, the decay isolates the transition model---not the representation---as the information bottleneck. Obstacle avoidance drops to 0\% without privileged runtime coordinates, and the planner's own ensemble-based epistemic term does not flag the loss---ensemble disagreement yields AUC 0.52 as an obstacle classifier (chance level), making the failure an unflagged imagination failure---an unknown unknown at planning time. As a secondary contribution, we show that \textbf{task-conditioned weighting of Effective Free Energy components} over the same world model yields lane keeping ($81.2 \pm 1.8$\% route completion) and obstacle avoidance (28/28 avoided, \emph{using privileged coordinates}) in the CARLA simulator, with runtime weight modulation enabling post-evasion centering.

</details>


### [184] [Adapting Monocular Depth Estimation in Novel Scenes through Disparity Refinement and Robot Ego-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14958)
*Dimitrios Chatziparaschis,Georgios Georgakis,Adnan I. Ansar,Shehryar Khattak,Konstantinos Karydis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EM-ViT：以单目深度基础模型的相对深度为先验，在线学习一个轻量ViT将相对深度适配为度量深度；用稠密视差细化+基线标量预测分解深度，借助稀疏LiDAR与自运动（Ego-Motion）快速在线训练，并用梯度匹配损失保持输入视差的细粒度语义结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在新颖非结构化环境中作业需要几何感知能力。单目深度估计（MDE）基础模型可提供稠密长程深度以替代LiDAR/双目等昂贵硬件，但这些离线模型多在仿射不变深度表征上训练，跨场景泛化性不明；同时部署时缺乏可靠的度量尺度。

Method: 受双目相机参数化启发，将深度预测分解为：(1) 稠密视差细化，恢复场景相对结构；(2) 有效基线标量预测，确定缺失的度量尺度。以基础模型输出的相对深度为输入先验，在线利用稀疏LiDAR点（可选结合机器人自运动）快速训练轻量Vision Transformer；并引入内部梯度匹配损失，实时保持输入视差中的细粒度语义结构。

Result: 在KITTI等公开序列上验证，在线学习流程相比现有MDE基础模型能更快适配新场景，且精度更高（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 将仿射不变相对深度在线适配为度量深度，是一种低成本、可快速部署的机器人感知方案，可部分替代LiDAR/双目硬件；优势在于快速在线自适应与实时结构保持，局限在于仍依赖稀疏LiDAR监督用于尺度标定。

Abstract: The geometric understanding of a scene is a crucial component for safe and efficient robotic operations in novel and unstructured environments. The recent emergence of foundation models for Monocular Depth Estimation (MDE) has provided an opportunity to enrich robotic perception capabilities with dense long-range depth predictions, replacing expensive onboard hardware (e.g., LiDARs and stereo cameras). However, these offline models are primarily trained on affine-invariant depth representations, and their generalization to novel scenes remains unclear. In this work we present EM-ViT, a monocular depth estimation model that learns to adapt online relative depth inputs from a foundation model prior to the metric scale with observations collected during robotic operations. Motivated by the stereo camera parameterization, our method decomposes depth prediction via dense disparity refinement to recover the scene's relative structure and an effective baseline scalar prediction to determine the missing metric scale. The learning process leverages sparse LiDAR points and, optionally, the robot's Ego-Motion (EM) to rapidly train a lightweight Vision Transformer (ViT), while an internal gradient matching loss preserves the fine-grained semantic structure of the input disparity in real-time. We demonstrate the efficacy of our method on publicly available sequences, such as KITTI, and show that our online learning pipeline can adapt to novel scenes faster and with higher accuracy than existing MDE foundation models. The code and supplementary materials will be publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [185] [Scaling VLA Serving Under Robotic Execution Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14969)
*Yue Zhang,Zicong Ye,Lufang Chen,Dance Yang,Mingyang Li,Zhenwei Duan,Michael Zhao,Ziye Deng,Starrick Liu,Ruizhi Chengze,YongHua Chen,SongAo Han,Yu Sun,Ran Yu,Xiaofan Li,Ruyi Gan,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XInfer：生产部署的VLA机器人推理服务系统，联合调度异构VLA计算与机器人执行的时变响应需求，在管理干扰的同时利用并行性提升GPU效率，保护时延关键的反馈回路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数百台VLA驱动的机器人已在真实环境运行，大规模机队服务的推理基础设施成本成为迫切问题；现有系统未协调VLA异构计算与机器人执行的动态时延要求。

Method: 系统级协同调度：利用VLA计算中的并行机会，同时控制并发带来的干扰，将对时延敏感的机器人控制反馈置于优先，提高GPU利用率与有效机器人容量。

Result: 在三种VLA模型与产线衍生负载上，物理执行约束下有效机器人容量（ERC）为SOTA系统的1.26–3.75倍；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 面向VLA机队推理的服务化系统设计可显著降低单位机器人推理成本，是具身智能规模化落地的关键工程支撑。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose robotic manipulation. In our production setting, hundreds of VLA-powered robots are already operating in real-world environments, creating an immediate need for efficient fleet serving. We present XInfer, a production-deployed VLA serving system designed to reduce the inference infrastructure cost of large-scale robotic deployments. XInfer jointly coordinates heterogeneous VLA computation with the evolving response requirements of robotic execution. By exploiting computational parallelism while managing interference, XInfer improves GPU efficiency while protecting time-critical robotic feedback. We evaluate XInfer across three VLA models using production-derived workloads, achieving 1.26-3.75 times the effective robot capacity (ERC) of state-of-the-art systems under physical-execution constraints. Our implementation is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [186] [From a Single RGB Image to Robot Execution: Metric Scene Alignment and Contact-Guided Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14981)
*Zhenxuan Ding,Yu Huang,Xinmiao Wang,Chenghao Sun,Ziyang Dong,Wenhao Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Single RGB image → reconstructed scene → human interaction generation → refinement (JMIR, CGRR) → humanoid motion reference; 89% sim, 70% real on Unitree G1.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Humanoid interaction motion generation is bottlenecked by requiring prebuilt 3D scene assets; reconstructing scenes from a single RGB image avoids asset authoring, but introduces geometric inconsistency across generation and embodiment retargeting.

Method: Unified pipeline: monocular scene reconstruction from one RGB image; scene-conditioned human interaction motion generation; Joint Metric Interaction Refinement (JMIR) before retargeting to align motion to metrically registered scene while preserving interaction structure; Contact Guided Residual Retargeting (CGRR) after retargeting to correct robot-specific support errors; produces trackable reference for frozen pretrained humanoid trackers without per-scene policy fine-tuning.

Result: Refinement time 480s→87s (5.5x speedup vs GRAIL); 89% success in simulation, 70% over 50 physical trials on Unitree G1.

Conclusion: Image-to-humanoid-motion pipeline with pre/post-retargeting refinement yields executable, scene-consistent motion references from a single RGB image, deployable across trackers without scene-specific finetuning.

Abstract: Extending humanoid interaction generation to new environments remains limited by the need for prebuilt 3D scene assets. Reconstructing scenes directly from single RGB images offers a way to overcome this limitation, but introduces a key challenge in maintaining geometric consistency throughout motion generation and transfer. Visually plausible human motions may be misaligned with the reconstructed scene, while retargeting can introduce additional support errors due to differences between human and robot embodiments. We present a unified pipeline that transforms a single RGB image into a humanoid motion reference. It reconstructs scene geometry from the input image, generates a human interaction conditioned on the reconstructed scene, and retargets the motion to a humanoid robot. To maintain geometric consistency throughout this process, we incorporate two complementary refinement stages before and after embodiment transfer. Before retargeting, Joint Metric Interaction Refinement (JMIR) refines human motion alignment with the registered metric scene while preserving the generated interaction structure. Following retargeting, Contact Guided Residual Retargeting (CGRR) corrects robot specific support errors to restore the intended support relationships. Together, these stages produce motion references that are consistent with the reconstructed scene and can be followed by multiple pretrained humanoid trackers without scene specific policy fine tuning. Compared with GRAIL, our method reduces refinement time from 480 s to 87 s, yielding a speedup of 5.5 times. The complete pipeline achieves a success rate of 89% in simulation and 70% across 50 physical trials on the Unitree G1, demonstrating its effectiveness in generating executable humanoid motion references from single RGB images.

</details>


### [187] [ILA: A Transferable Interface-Level Adapter for Compliant Humanoid Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14985)
*ZhuoChang Cai,Zihao Yan,Jinxuan Zhu,Yanjie Li,Hao Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ILA：一种无需专用交互传感器的“接口级适配器”，依据机器人观测历史修正运动参考，使预训练人形运动跟踪器具备顺应性物理引导能力，仿真训练、即插即用、无需重训跟踪策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人直接跟踪参考运动在受限环境或人机物理交互中不安全，需要顺应性跟踪；但专用力/触觉传感器成本高、泛化差，且已有跟踪策略重训代价大。

Method: 在运动参考接口层面插入适配器：以机器人观测历史为输入，预测由物理交互引起的参考修正量；全仿真训练，不修改也不重训底层跟踪策略，可跨多个预训练跟踪器即插即用。

Result: 在三个人形运动跟踪器上引导目标误差降低63.6%–75.4%；匹配仿真与硬件实验显示引导响应性提升且标称跟踪性能基本保持。

Conclusion: 顺应性引导可作为可复用、免传感器的扩展模块嫁接到现有预训练人形运动跟踪器上，为遥操作与数据采集提供安全交互能力。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is fundamental to reproducing human motions on humanoid robots and serves as an important tool for teleoperation and motion data collection. However, directly tracking reference motions can be unsafe in constrained environments or during physical human--robot interaction, motivating the need for compliant motion tracking. We present ILA, an Interface-Level Adapter for compliant humanoid motion tracking without dedicated interaction sensors. ILA adapts motion references from the robot's observation history, allowing the robot to respond to physical guidance while preserving the capabilities of a pretrained motion tracker. As an interface-level module, ILA can be directly plugged into different pretrained motion trackers without retraining either the adapter or the tracking policies. ILA is trained entirely in simulation to predict reference corrections induced by physical interaction. We evaluate ILA with three humanoid motion trackers and achieve a 63.6--75.4% reduction in guidance-target error compared with their original policies. Matched simulation studies and hardware experiments further show that ILA improves responsiveness to human guidance while preserving nominal motion-tracking performance. These results demonstrate compliant guidance as a reusable, sensor-free extension to pretrained humanoid motion trackers.

</details>


### [188] [Fast Inference-Time Policy Refinement via Action-Affine World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15011)
*Run Wang,Yilin Mo,Liang Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A²WM：从动作扰动策略 rollout 中离线学习"局部动作仿射"世界模型，把策略提案视为名义轨迹，对修正量做线性响应；在线用一次模型评估+一个凸 QP 求解即可精化，策略与模型均冻结，4 个仿真操作任务成功率最高，精化开销约 3.8 ms/控制步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略能完成复杂操作，但小执行误差会累积导致失败。现有精化方法需要额外 on-policy 学习或昂贵的在线规划，代价高。作者的关键洞察：预训练策略已提供有用的名义行为，精化只需预测"小动作修正如何改变结果"这一局部响应，且应以易于优化的形式表达。

Method: 提出 action-affine world model（A²WM）。离线阶段采集动作扰动后的策略 rollout 数据进行训练；模型以交互历史和策略提案为输入，直接预测整个规划时域上任务相关几何，输出为名义轨迹 + 对动作修正的线性响应项（避免递归 rollout 带来的中间误差累积）。在线精化时，对预测任务误差施加二次惩罚、对线性修正施加约束，构成一个廉价的凸二次规划（QP），只需一次模型前向 + 一次 QP 求解，策略与模型权重全部冻结。

Result: 在四个仿真操作任务上，该框架在所比较的基线中取得最高成功率；除基础策略采样外，每控制步平均精化开销仅 3.8 ms。实验限于仿真环境，缺少真实机器人验证。

Conclusion: 以"名义轨迹 + 线性动作响应"的仿射世界模型替代递归预测，可将策略精化转化为小规模凸 QP，实现免在线学习、免重训的低开销闭环修正；路线简洁高效，但真实世界迁移与对强非线性偏差的适用性是主要待验证点。

Abstract: Imitation policies can perform complex manipulation, yet small execution errors can accumulate and cause failure. Refinement can correct these errors, but existing approaches often require additional on-policy learning or costly online planning. Our key insight is that a pretrained policy already supplies a useful nominal behavior: refinement can focus on predicting how small action corrections change its outcome. We therefore learn this local response in a form that is cheap to optimize. Our \textit{action-affine world model} (A\textsuperscript{2}WM) learns local action sensitivities offline from action-perturbed policy rollouts. Given interaction history and a policy proposal, it predicts task-relevant geometry as a nominal trajectory plus a linear response to corrections. It predicts the full planning horizon directly, avoiding the propagation of intermediate errors through recursive model evaluations. Quadratic penalties on predicted task errors and linear correction constraints then yield a cheap convex quadratic program (QP). Online refinement requires one model evaluation and one QP solve, while both policy and model weights remain frozen. Across four simulated manipulation tasks, our framework achieves the highest success rate among evaluated baselines, adding only 3.8 ms of mean refinement overhead per control step, excluding base-policy sampling.

</details>


### [189] [Genie Sim Benchmark: Real-to-Sim Fidelity and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15020)
*Di Yang,Da Huang,Yang Lv,Fan Yang,Yi Lu,Zheyuan Xing,Linting Guo,Jiaen Shen,Wenjun Sun,Maoqing Yao,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Genie Sim Benchmark：用22个机器人-任务配对评估仿真与真机的一致性，任务级分数相关系数0.9686，关节位置RMSE 0.006–0.035 rad、力矩RMSE 0.74–4.00 N·m，并验证原生PhysX交互任务的可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要判断机器人仿真基准能否可靠替代或补充真机评测，因此必须同时检验任务成功率层面的排序一致性与底层物理响应（位置、力矩、接触）的一致性。

Method: 配对真机-仿真任务评测（22组、16个任务名），以真机微调的pi0.5策略为任务策略；G2 crsB长时序闭环实验测位置与关节侧力矩一致性；5个代表性物体案例做系统辨识与跨引擎接触迁移；用未参与辨识与伺服增益搜索的32.65 s遥操作轨迹检验外部位置目标在仿真运动控制指令整形与标定位置伺服下的跟踪；并对PhysX做原生适配。

Result: 任务分数相关性0.9686、MAE 0.0405、RMSE 0.0520，仿真-真机差异-0.110~+0.080；双臂逐关节位置RMSE 0.00614–0.03522 rad，电流推得力矩RMSE 0.73545–4.00099 N·m；原生PhysX适配恢复3个记录的交互任务，对齐收益依赖具体案例。

Conclusion: 结论是任务级分数与物理响应一致性可联合用于任务特定仿真评测；但样本量偏小、以描述性相关为主、PhysX交互迁移增益不稳定，尚不足以支撑通用仿真替代真机的强主张。

Abstract: We evaluate Genie Sim Benchmark through paired real-simulation tasks and complementary system-identification studies. Across 22 robot-task pairs spanning 16 task names, task-specific pi0.5 policies fine-tuned on real-robot data achieve a descriptive Score correlation of 0.9686, MAE 0.0405, RMSE 0.0520, with simulation-real differences from -0.110 to +0.080. G2 crsB experiments assess long-horizon closed-loop position and joint-side torque agreement, while five representative object cases examine identification and cross-engine contact transfer. A separate 32.6503 s teleoperation trajectory, excluded from mechanical identification and servo-gain search, evaluates external position targets through simulated motion-controller command shaping and a calibrated position servo. Across both arms, per-joint position RMSE spans 0.00614-0.03522 rad and current-derived torque RMSE spans 0.73545-4.00099 N m. Native PhysX adaptations recover three recorded interaction tasks, with case-dependent alignment gains. These results support task-specific simulation evaluation through joint assessment of task outcomes and physical-response consistency.

</details>


### [190] [Does Your World Model Know Actions Have Consequences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15048)
*Ayush Gaggar,Jared Berry,Todd D. Murphey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出应以"敏感性分析/受迫响应"来评估具身世界模型（WM）对反事实动作后果的预测保真度，指出当前仅看重建清晰度或任务成功率的指标不足，并呼吁面向硬件部署建立"状态误差 vs 敏感性预测准确性"权衡的新评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的独特价值在于无需真实部署即可模拟环境对替代动作的响应，但现有指标只衡量输出质量（重建清晰度、任务成功率），无法反映其反事实动作后果建模的忠实性，且硬件部署需要新的评测取舍分析。

Method: 观点/立场型工作：以敏感性分析中的"受迫响应"（对动作施加扰动并观察系统响应）作为审计WM反事实预测能力的手段，主张建立状态误差与敏感性预测精度之间的评测权衡框架；摘要未给出具体算法或实验设计。

Result: 摘要未报告定量结果，主要为指标批判与评测框架提案。

Conclusion: 世界模型评测需从"输出质量"转向"动作后果保真度"，敏感性分析是有前景的审计途径，未来需发展兼顾状态误差与敏感性准确性的标准化评测体系，以支撑硬件部署。

Abstract: Embodied world models (WMs) are implicit learning systems that predict the next future latent state directly from observations and actions. Unique from other end-to-end learning systems, the dynamics predictor of WMs can simulate the environment's response to alternative actions without physical deployment nor pre-specifying states of the dynamical system. Current metrics focus on the quality of model outputs, e.g., clarity of reconstructions or task success, but fail to assess the fidelity of modeling consequences of counterfactual actions. Inspecting the forced response to action perturbations through sensitivity analysis is one way to audit how well WMs can predict the consequences of counterfactual actions. Deploying WMs on hardware will require new evaluation frameworks to analyze the tradeoffs between state error and accurate sensitivity prediction.

</details>


### [191] [MiniPose: Miniature-Based Real Data Collection for 6D Pose Estimation of Large Industrial Pipe Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15066)
*Hyunjun Kim,Youngjin Seo,Sungwon Seo,Jiyou Shin,Kunwoo Kim,Chanyong Park,Hyungpil Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiniPose：用缩尺复制品采集真实RGB-D数据（深度经反比例变换得到全尺度几何一致表示），与合成数据混合训练6-DOF位姿估计器（PVN3D、RDPN6D），缓解大型工业物体真实数据采集负担与sim-to-real差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型重工业物体（船厂大管道、石化结构件）6-DOF位姿估计研究不足；重复摆放大型物体采集多样位姿真实数据成本高；纯合成数据难以复现真实深度传感器的结构化测量误差，存在sim-to-real差距。

Method: 制作缩尺复制品，用真实RGB-D相机采集多姿态数据；RGB图像直接使用，深度图通过简单反比例缩放变换为全尺度物体的几何一致表示；与合成数据混合训练，在两种形式不同的RGB-D位姿估计器（PVN3D、RDPN6D）上验证。

Result: 每种管件仅混入≤1000张缩尺图像（<10%训练数据）配合10000张合成图像，即可在两个估计器上提升跨管件平均ADD-S AUC；与全尺度真实数据训练对比表明缩尺数据可作为替代代理。

Conclusion: 缩尺真实数据采集是可实践的sim-to-real数据增强管线，能在难以获取全尺度真实数据的场景下降低工业大物体位姿估计的数据采集负担。

Abstract: 6-DOF pose estimation of large and heavy industrial objects, such as large piping systems in shipyards and structural components in petrochemical plants, remains relatively underexplored in robotic perception. Repeatedly repositioning large objects is physically and spatially demanding, making it difficult to acquire real training data with sufficient pose diversity. Synthetic data offer a scalable alternative, but faithfully reproducing real RGB-D observations, particularly the structured measurement errors of real depth sensors, remains difficult. We propose MiniPose, a practical pipeline for miniature-based real RGB-D data collection that addresses both the data-acquisition burden and the sim-to-real gap. We fabricate a scaled-down replica and capture RGB-D data of it in diverse poses using a real RGB-D camera. RGB images are used directly, while depth maps are converted to a geometrically consistent representation of the full-scale object via a simple inverse scaling transformation. This allows training to incorporate real appearance and sensor measurement characteristics that are difficult to reproduce using synthetic data alone. To examine whether the benefit depends on a particular network, we evaluate two RGB-D pose estimators with different formulations, PVN3D and RDPN6D. Mixing at most 1,000 miniature images per pipe type, less than 10\% of the training data, with 10,000 synthetic images improves the mean ADD-S AUC across pipe types over synthetic-only training for both estimators. A comparison with training on full-scale real data further suggests that miniature data can serve as a proxy where full-scale collection is difficult, reducing the data-collection burden for large industrial objects.

</details>


### [192] [CrowdMAPS: Crowdsourcing Multiple Actions per State for Real-World Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15078)
*Yilong Song,Harshvardhan Chandirasekar,Karthik Desingh,Chen Zhu-Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CrowdMAPS 提出基于网页的众包范式，让非专家标注者在 3D 界面中为已访问的机器人状态提供多个替代动作，从而沿"宽度"维度扩充真实机器人数据集（每状态多动作，MAPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人模仿学习受限于物理示教采集成本高，且每条轨迹每个状态通常只有一个动作标签，监督信号稀疏。

Method: 在标准数据采集期间或之后，将已访问的机器人状态通过网页 3D 界面分发给远程标注者；标注者无需机器人硬件即可对关键帧位姿给出多个其他有效动作；用这些 MAPS 数据训练关键位姿策略与轨迹策略。

Result: 在五个真实操作任务上，将每状态动作数从 1 增加到 10，样本效率提升，平均成功率分别提升 35.6 个百分点（关键位姿策略）与 33.0 个百分点（轨迹策略）。

Conclusion: 软件介导的人工标注可有效扩充物理机器人数据、提供更密集监督，CrowdMAPS 可作为真实机器人数据采集的可扩展数字层。

Abstract: Real-world robot imitation learning is often limited by the cost of collecting physical demonstrations, which typically yield only one action label per visited state. We present CrowdMAPS, a web-based crowdsourcing paradigm for augmenting real-robot datasets with Multiple Actions per State (MAPS). After or during standard robot data collection, CrowdMAPS distributes visited robot states to remote annotators through a web-based 3D interface, where they specify alternative valid actions without requiring access to physical robot hardware. This expands robot datasets along a complementary "width" dimension--by increasing the number of actions per visited state--rather than only by collecting more trajectories. We evaluate CrowdMAPS using keyframe-centric goal-pose annotations supplied by non-expert annotators and train imitation policies on the resulting MAPS data. Across five real-world manipulation tasks, increasing MAPS width from one to ten actions per state improves sample efficiency and increases average success rates by 35.6 percentage points for keypose policies and 33.0 percentage points for trajectory policies. These results show that software-mediated human annotations can augment physical robot data to afford denser supervision, establishing CrowdMAPS as a scalable digital layer for real-robot data collection.

</details>


### [193] [Decide Before You Move: Diffusion-Generated Search Reference for Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation in Semi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15093)
*Yuxuan Feng,Fujing Xie,Sören Schwertfeger,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PreNav：半静态室内环境下，用LLM把"目标-家具共现"转成空间先验，再由条件扩散模型结合持久结构生成一条带带状搜索区域的全局有序中心线，机器人沿带前进并在在线检测到目标后趋近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开词汇ObjectNav在真实室内场景中面临"半静态"挑战：墙体/大件家具稳定而小物体常被移动。先验地图法依赖重建时的物体位姿，物体一移动即失效；在线方法则需每步反复调用VLM，开销大。作者希望在保留稳定结构用于路径规划的同时，用一个一次性生成的全局搜索参考来指导"去哪里找"，避免逐步VLM调用。

Method: 1) 假设墙体与大件家具不动，保留其地图用于导航规划；2) 用LLM将目标-家具共现知识转换为空间先验；3) 条件扩散模型以该先验和持久结构为条件，输出有序中心线及其周围带状搜索区域（全局搜索参考，机器人移动前一次性生成）；4) 机器人沿带状区域搜索，在线检测到目标后转为趋近目标。

Result: 在Gibson ObjectNav基准上，在结构先验匹配的条件下优于强在线基线；生成器仅在房间级ScanNet++场景上训练，零微调直接迁移到楼层级Gibson场景。真实环境实验在物体移位与开词汇查询下进一步验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 以"半静态"假设分割静态结构与可动目标，用LLM先验+扩散生成一次性全局搜索参考，可在显著降低VLM调用频率的同时提升开词汇ObjectNav的效率与鲁棒性；生成器的跨场景（ScanNet++房间级→Gibson楼层级）零微调泛化是该方法的突出特性。

Abstract: Open-world navigation requires a robot to locate a user-specified target in an indoor scene under open-vocabulary queries. Realistic indoor environments are semi-static: walls and furniture largely persist while smaller objects move, relocate, or vanish between visits. Prior-map systems answer queries from object poses recorded at reconstruction time, which expire as soon as objects move, whereas online methods re-derive where to look from current observations every few steps, often through expensive calls to a vision--language model. Assuming that walls and large furniture do not move, we keep their map to plan paths, but use a target--furniture co-occurrence prior to decide where to search. We present PreNav, an ObjectNav framework on semi-static environments. Rather than invoking a vision-language model at each step, PreNav generates a single global search reference before the robot moves: an LLM converts target--furniture co-occurrence into a spatial prior, a conditional diffusion model turns that prior and the persistent structure into an ordered centerline with a band around it, and the robot approaches the target once it is detected online. Experiments on a Gibson ObjectNav benchmark show that PreNav outperforms a strong online baseline under matched structural priors, using a generator trained only on room-level ScanNet++ scenes and applied to floor-level Gibson without fine-tuning. Real-world experiments under object relocation and open-vocabulary queries further validate the approach.

</details>


### [194] [Elastra: A Simulation Framework for Elastic Bodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15112)
*Youran Sun,Chugang Yi,Xingyu Ren,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Elastra：面向可变形弹性表面（床垫/蹦床）的机器人交互仿真框架，用可垂直运动、弹性连接并带阻尼的单元表示表面，通过接触力驱动形变、形变再反算接触力；并用强化学习训练残差策略增强HoST与ProtoMotions在床垫上站立的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有刚体/平地仿真无法刻画床垫、蹦床这类弹性表面在负载下凹陷与回弹导致的接触位置与接触力变化，从而难以训练和评估机器人在可变形表面上的控制策略。

Method: 以一维垂直自由度单元网格表示表面，单元间用弹性连接与阻尼建模形变与恢复；接触力驱动表面运动，更新几何后再计算后续接触力（双向耦合）。验证方面与独立实现和真实床垫载荷曲线对比。应用方面以HoST策略与ProtoMotions运动跟踪器为原始控制器，用RL训练残差策略对关节目标加修正，在Unitree G1人形上用床垫与蹦床任务评估。

Result: 仿真经交叉实现对比与真实载荷曲线验证；HoST成功率由44.1%提升至72.5%（床垫），蹦床上由55%提升至89%；ProtoMotions在训练未见的初始位姿上从19.0%提升至45.0%。

Conclusion: 轻量化的弹性表面仿真可支撑人形机器人在可变形地形上的策略训练与残差微调，显著提升站立成功率；开源代码可供复用。

Abstract: When robots interact with elastic surfaces such as mattresses and trampolines, changes in loading cause the surfaces to deform and rebound, altering subsequent contact locations and forces. We present Elastra, a simulation framework for modeling this interaction and supporting the training and evaluation of robot control policies. Elastra represents each surface using elements that move only vertically, with elastic connections and damping to model deformation and recovery. Contact forces drive surface motion, and the updated surface geometry is used to compute subsequent contact forces. We systematically validate our simulations against an independent implementation and a real mattress load curve. We further use Elastra to train the Unitree G1 humanoid to stand up on mattresses and trampolines. Using the pretrained HoST policy and ProtoMotions motion tracker as original controllers, we train residual policies through reinforcement learning to add corrections to their joint targets. Adding these residual policies increases the success rate with HoST from 44.1\% to 72.5\%, and from 55\% to 89\% on the trampoline. With ProtoMotions, the success rate increases from 19.0\% to 45.0\% for initial poses not encountered during training. The code and video are available on this https URL .

</details>


### [195] [PolyMorphWBC: Whole-Body Control from End-Effector Trajectories for Quadrupedal Mobile Manipulation across Arm Morphologies with Extended Reach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15117)
*Heng Li,Yang Li,Yifan Duan,Ren Fang,Matao Duan,Wenhao Yu,Lu Zhang,Jianmin Ji,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PolyMorphWBC：以EE短时轨迹为唯一任务命令的四足+机械臂分层全身控制器，通过URDF形态条件化的协调器适配四种6-DoF机械臂，支持超固定基座可达范围的伸展轨迹，并实现跨臂复用与真机语言条件抓放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足移动操作臂可通过臂-基座协同扩展操作空间，但执行短时EE轨迹时需决定基座何时、如何移动，且该决策强依赖所装机械臂形态；现有方法难以跨机械臂形态复用且不覆盖超固定基座可达的伸展轨迹。

Method: 分层控制：轨迹条件化的coordinator基于预览的未来EE目标决定基座运动；臂专属IK跟踪当前目标；冻结的运动后端执行。用URDF导出的形态条件化+在线残余力矩反馈实现跨臂复用；在程序化生成的机械臂与结构化轨迹（含固定基座可达范围内外）上训练。

Result: IsaacLab中held-out轨迹与形态下93.95%完成率、2.30cm位置误差、4.81°姿态误差；MuJoCo对比UMI-on-Legs位置误差降69–72%、姿态误差降91–97%；Go2–ARX5真机用pi_0生成的EE轨迹完成语言条件伸展抓放，无需VLA输出基座指令。

Conclusion: 短时EE轨迹可作为机械臂形态无关的任务级接口，配合形态条件化协调器实现跨臂复用与真机部署；但缺少物理后端联合优化/端到端学习，跨形态泛化依赖程序化数据。

Abstract: Quadrupedal mobile manipulators can extend their manipulation workspace by coordinating the mounted arm with the mobile base. At the task level, short-horizon end-effector (EE) trajectories provide an interface for specifying the desired manipulation motion without prescribing how it should be realized by the arm and base. However, executing such trajectories remains challenging because the controller must determine when and how the base should move, and this decision depends on the mounted-arm morphology. We therefore present PolyMorphWBC, a hierarchical controller that takes an EE trajectory as the sole task-level motion command and generates coordinated arm--base motions across four families of 6-DoF mounted arms, with extended-reach trajectories beyond the initial fixed-base reach. Within PolyMorphWBC, a trajectory-conditioned coordinator determines the base motion from previewed EE targets, while arm-specific Inverse Kinematics (IK) tracks the current target, and a frozen locomotion backend executes the resulting motion. Cross-arm reuse is enabled by a Unified Robot Description Format (URDF)-derived morphology conditioning, together with online residual-wrench feedback. We train PolyMorphWBC on procedurally generated arms and structured trajectories within and beyond fixed-base reach. In IsaacLab, it achieves 93.95% completion with 2.30cm position error and 4.81° orientation error on held-out trajectories and arm morphologies. In MuJoCo evaluation against UMI-on-Legs, PolyMorphWBC reduces EE position error by 69--72% and orientation error by 91--97% across four within- and extended-reach trajectories. On a physical Go2--ARX5 system, we demonstrate language-conditioned extended-reach pick-and-place using EE trajectories from pi_0 without vision-language-action (VLA)-generated base commands.

</details>


### [196] [ARTS: An Open-Source, Reproducible Three-Arm Platform for Autonomy Research in Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15135)
*Jiawei Ge,Chunwoo Kim,Antony Goldenberg,Douglas McDonough,Justin Liao,Bo Ning,Sylvia Ladstaetter,Katya Van Anderlecht,Hao Ding,Laura Connolly,Ethan Kilmer,Francis Fan,Shouyue Hu,Tyler Salvador,Brendan Burkhart,Pit Henrich,Richard Cha,Tito Porras,Mathias Unberath,Kevin Cleary,Axel Krieger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARTS：约13.7万美元全可购件搭建的三臂开源微创手术自主研究平台（双臂持da Vinci X/Xi器械+UR5持双臂立体内窥镜），含力反馈主手控制台与硬件急停；硬件实时指标、精度对标dVRK，并经FLS钉转移与缝合验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最普及的手术自主平台依赖捐赠的临床硬件，而基于工业臂的开源替代方案又缺少链条中的某些环节，因此需要一个任何实验室都能复现的开放平台。

Method: 用UR5e×2驱动腕式da Vinci X/Xi器械、UR5承载双FLIR立体内窥镜；外科医生控制台提供触觉手柄、脚踏与立体显示，并配独立于软件的硬件制动可冻结三臂；ROS 2软件锁定依赖版本，裸机与容器同一脚本安装，并以硬件测量时间戳记录立体图像。评估含控制器满速指令（UR5e 500 Hz）、双目曝露同步性、端到端延迟、ISO 9283位姿重复性、光学跟踪RCM误差，以及3名专家+3名新手的FLS钉转移和1例活体（模型）缝合。

Result: 双臂可满速控制；双相机曝露时差<12 µs；手部运动到机械臂延迟58 ms，内窥镜图像到显示器68 ms；ISO 9283位姿重复性0.14–0.25 mm（与dVRK Classic同级）；RCM平均误差0.34–0.37 mm；完成FLS钉转移与缝合任务示范。硬件、固件与软件全部开源。

Conclusion: ARTS提供了一个可购件复现、低延迟、高精度且带安全硬件互锁的开源三臂手术自主研究平台，为自主手术算法（如模仿学习、技能学习）提供了标准化硬件与数据采集基础；局限在于临床有效性未验证、成本仍偏高、任务验证规模有限。

Abstract: Research on surgical autonomy needs open platforms that any laboratory can build, yet the most widely shared platform depends on donated clinical hardware, and open alternatives on industrial arms each lack part of the surgical chain. We present the Autonomous Robotic Translational System (ARTS), an open-source three-arm platform for autonomy research in minimally invasive surgery, built entirely from purchasable parts for about USD 137,000. Two UR5e arms hold wristed da Vinci X/Xi instruments, a UR5 arm holds a stereo endoscope with two FLIR cameras, the surgeon console provides haptic handles, footswitches and a stereo viewer, and a hardware stop independent of the software lets a supervisor freeze all three arms in place. The ROS 2 software pins every dependency, installs from the same scripts on bare metal or in containers, and records stereo images with hardware-measured timing. On the real arms, ARTS commands each arm at its controller's full rate (e.g., 500 Hz on the UR5e), the two cameras expose within 12 microseconds of each other, hand motion reaches the arm in 58 ms, and the endoscope image reaches the monitor in 68 ms. The ISO 9283 pose repeatability is 0.14-0.25 mm, comparable to a da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) Classic arm on the same protocol, and an optical tracker measured a mean remote-center-of-motion error of 0.34-0.37 mm. Three expert and three novice operators performed the Fundamentals of Laparoscopic Surgery (FLS) peg transfer through a laparoscopic trainer box, and one of the experts sutured on a suture pad. The hardware designs, firmware and software are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [197] [Are We Ready for Snow-tonomous Driving? Challenges Toward Robust Automated Driving in Extreme Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15217)
*Eren Erdal Aksoy,Abu Mohammed Raisuddin,Thien-Thu Ngo,Eren Aydemir,Osman Tahir Ekşi,Paula Litkey,Petri Manninen,Heikki Hyyti,Jyri Maanpää,Tarmo Kivioja,Tuomas Herranen,Väisänen Sofie,Niemi Kalle,Isabelle Morvan,Pierre Duthon,Ian Marsh,Martin Sanfridson,Henrik Toss,Thanh Bui,Yuri Poledna,Maikol Funk Drechsler,Mikko Tarkiainen,Matti Kutila,Topi Miekkala,Stefan Parzefall,Valentina Donzella,Pak Hung Chan,Sogol Kharrazi,Mario Ceccarelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是欧盟Horizon Europe项目ROADVIEW的综述性总结，基于四年多平台真实示范，提出恶劣天气（雪、雨、雾）下自动驾驶感知与控制面临的十项关键挑战（数据稀缺、域偏移、多模态感知局限、控制鲁棒性、验证缺口等），并给出未来研究）与标准化方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶感知与控制研究集中于晴朗天气，雪、雨、雾等恶劣条件研究不足，导致下游感知与控制性能显著退化，成为自动驾驶落地的最大障碍之一。

Method: 项目级经验总结：依托ROADVIEW项目四年大规模开发与多车型、多地点真实场景示范，归纳挑战与教训，提出研究与标准化路线图。无新算法或模型提出。

Result: 定性归纳出十项阻碍全天候自主性的挑战：数据稀缺、域偏移、多模态感知局限、控制鲁棒性不足、验证体系缺失等，并总结相应经验教训。

Conclusion: 恶劣天气鲁棒感知与控制仍需数据、域适应、多模态融合、控制与验证闭环协同推进；文章为未来研究与标准化提供方向性指引，但不提供可直接复用的方法洞察。

Abstract: Automated driving has gained remarkable momentum over the past decade, with both industry and academia investing substantial resources in developing perception and control solutions to advance vehicle autonomy. However, these efforts have largely focused on clear-weather scenarios, leaving harsh conditions, such as snow, rain, and fog, comparatively understudied. As a result, inclement weather remains one of the most significant hurdles for autonomous vehicles, often leading to substantial performance degradation in downstream perception and control tasks. In this paper, we present insights from the EU-funded Horizon Europe project ROADVIEW, which aims to develop robust, weather-aware perception and control modules for connected and automated vehicles operating under severe conditions, including snow, rain, and fog. Drawing on four years of large-scale development and real-world demonstrations across multiple vehicle platforms in different locations, we identify ten key challenges that hinder the development of weather-resilient autonomy, including data scarcity, domain shifts, limitations in multimodal perception, control robustness, and validation gaps, among others. We summarize these challenges and lessons learned toward enabling reliable snow-autonomous driving, highlighting critical directions for future research and standardization.

</details>


### [198] [DroneDiffusion: Uncertainty-Guided Diffusion Path Planner for UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15277)
*Ivan Valuev,Iana Zhura,Valerii Serpiva,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DroneDiffusion: a DDPM-based local 3-D UAV trajectory planner conditioned on a VL-model perceptual reliability map (lens flare/overexposure/reflective degradation), plus training-free CHOMP guidance on a 3-D ESDF for collision avoidance; trained in simulation, deployed zero-shot on a real drone.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Geometric-only planners assume sensor reliability is uniform, so they route through regions with degraded sensing (glare, overexposure, specular reflection) and suffer mid-trajectory perception failures. The authors treat perceptual reliability as a first-class planning signal.

Method: Vision-language perceptual safety estimator producing a pixel-aligned reliability map; DDPM conditioned on that map + onboard RGB + goal + initial velocity to generate egocentric 3-D trajectories; inference-time training-free CHOMP guidance over a 3-D Euclidean signed distance field to enforce obstacle avoidance without retraining.

Result: Mean perceptual reliability along trajectories 0.71 vs 0.63 (geometric baseline); guidance halves collision rate of the learned prior from 14.7% to 7.8%. Real-drone zero-shot deployment after simulation-only training.

Conclusion: Perceptual-safety conditioning and geometric guidance are complementary; modeling sensor reliability explicitly reduces incursion into unreliable regions while keeping navigation performance competitive. Evidence is metric-light on real hardware (no success-rate/flight statistics reported).

Abstract: We present DroneDiffusion, a perception-aware diffusion planner for local 3-D UAV trajectory generation that explicitly models and avoids failure zones caused by lens flare, overexposure, and reflective surfaces. A vision-language perceptual safety estimator produces a pixel-aligned reliability map that highlights regions of degraded sensing. A denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) is conditioned on this map together with the onboard RGB image, goal point, and initial velocity to generate egocentric 3-D trajectories. To enforce obstacle avoidance at inference time without retraining, we apply training-free CHOMP guidance over a 3-D Euclidean signed distance field. The model is trained entirely in simulation and deployed zero-shot on a real drone. Reliability conditioning raises the mean perceptual reliability along generated trajectories to 0.71, compared with 0.63 for a geometric baseline, while guidance halves the collision rate of the learned prior from 14.7% to 7.8%. Perceptual safety conditioning and geometric guidance are therefore complementary: treating sensor reliability as a first-class signal reduces incursions into unreliable regions while maintaining competitive navigation performance.

</details>


### [199] [Autonomous Trail Racing with a Bicycle Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15280)
*Fabian Jenelten,Deepana Ishtaweera,Pablo Garcia,Liana Margolese,Igor Bogoslavskyi,Luca Buonocore,Jan Migliazza,Aleksandrs Ecins,Annika Wollschlaeger,Dario Bellicoso,Gabriel Nelson,Abel Gawel,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将越野小径编码为地形语义层，用统一强化学习框架联合优化导航与运动，并在线重建语义高程图，实现两轮自行车机器人高速自主越野骑行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 山地自行车越野需要空中动作、过弯、打滑恢复等多种运动技能与精细场景感知，将此类能力迁移到自主两轮机器人极具挑战；传统分层控制（高层给速度/姿态指令，低层执行）接口割裂感知、导航与运动，难以应对非结构化高速越野。

Method: 核心思想：把赛道编码为地形的语义层。仿真中该语义层直接嵌入训练环境，部署时由相机+LiDAR 在线重建。1) 统一学习框架将局部导航与运动控制作为联合优化问题，绕过经典分层控制的 twist/pose 指令接口；固定奖励权重下，仅通过地形语义与回合长度塑造，即可产生赛道竞速与敏捷跑酷两种行为。2) 感知管线：冻结的视觉基础模型 + 轻量双头解码器，预测逐像素地形语义与置信度，与 LiDAR 距离融合，在 NVIDIA AGX Orin 上以 30 Hz 生成自中心语义高程图。

Result: 在 Ultra Mobility Vehicle（UMV）两轮自行车机器人上验证：自主完成一公里非结构化山地车道的全部路段，速度超过 5 m/s，平均 3.26 m/s；仿真中相比分层基线失败率降低最多一个数量级，同时速度更快。

Conclusion: 用单一“地形语义层”概念统一训练与部署，证明扁平化联合优化可替代分层控制架构，并在真实两轮机器人上实现高速自主越野导航。

Abstract: Navigating off-road trails on a mountain bike requires diverse locomotion skills, including aerial maneuvers, cornering, and slip recovery, paired with sophisticated perception for scene understanding. Translating these capabilities to autonomous, two-wheeled robots remains a formidable challenge. We show that autonomous, high-speed trail navigation can be achieved through a single idea: encoding the trail as a semantic layer of the terrain. In simulation, this layer embeds the navigation task into the training environment; during deployment, it is reconstructed onboard from camera and LiDAR data. First, we introduce a unified learning framework that treats local navigation and locomotion as a joint optimization problem, bypassing the twist or pose command interface of classical control hierarchies. With fixed reward weights, the same framework produces both trail racing and agile parkour, shaped only through terrain semantics and episode length. Second, our perception pipeline combines a frozen vision foundation model with a lightweight dual-head decoder that predicts per-pixel terrain semantics and confidence scores, which are fused with LiDAR range measurements into an egocentric semantic elevation map at 30 Hz onboard an NVIDIA AGX Orin. We validate our framework on the Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV), a bicycle robot that autonomously raced across all sections of a kilometer-long, unstructured mountain bike trail at speeds exceeding 5 m/s and averaging 3.26 m/s. In simulation, our policy fails up to an order of magnitude less often than hierarchical baselines while driving faster.

</details>


### [200] [APG-Planner: Learning Vision-Based High-Speed Trajectory Planning via Analytic Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15296)
*Jinwoo Lee,Geunsik Bae,Changseung Kim,Jiwoo Kim,Woojae Shin,Hyondong Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出使用解析策略梯度（APG）训练视觉高速轨迹规划器 APG-Planner，借助微分平坦性实现轨迹级可微状态转移，单 GPU 约 30 分钟训练即可在仿真中媲美基线，实机四旋翼达 11 m/s，并迁移到滑移转向地面机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉高速导航需要能从机载视觉观测实时生成平滑、无碰撞轨迹的规划器。深度强化学习虽有前景，但无模型策略梯度方法依赖采样估计，梯度噪声大、训练低效。

Method: 提出基于解析策略梯度的轨迹规划策略学习框架：利用微分平坦性从规划轨迹解析重建飞行器状态，无需显式低层动力学模型即可获得可微的轨迹级状态转移；通过迭代执行轨迹规划与可微状态转移构造可微 episode rollout，由 episode 级损失解析计算策略梯度。

Result: 单 GPU 约 30 分钟训练后，APG-Planner 在仿真中性能与基线相当或更优；实机实验最高速度 11 m/s；在滑移转向地面机器人上验证了框架可推广到四旋翼以外的微分平坦机器人系统。

Conclusion: 解析策略梯度结合微分平坦建模可显著提升训练效率与策略性能，形成一种适用于微分平坦机器人系统的通用视觉轨迹规划学习范式。

Abstract: Vision-based high-speed navigation requires a trajectory planner that can reactively generate smooth, collision-free trajectories from onboard visual observations. Deep reinforcement learning offers a promising approach to vision-based trajectory planning, as it enables the optimization of sequential decision-making policies in complex environments. However, model-free policy gradient methods rely on sample-based policy gradient estimation, which yields noisy gradient estimates and inefficient training. To this end, we propose a framework for learning trajectory planning policies using the analytic policy gradient (APG) method and use it to train APG-Planner, a vision-based high-speed trajectory planner. The proposed framework leverages differential flatness to analytically reconstruct vehicle states from planned trajectories, thereby enabling differentiable trajectory-level state transitions without requiring an explicit low-level dynamics model. By iteratively performing trajectory planning and differentiable state transitions, we construct a differentiable episode rollout and analytically compute policy gradients from episode-level losses, improving both training efficiency and policy performance. After approximately 30 minutes of training on a single GPU, APG-Planner demonstrates comparable or superior performance to existing baseline methods in simulation and achieves speeds of up to 11\,m/s in real-world flight experiments. We further validate the proposed framework on a skid-steer ground robot, demonstrating its applicability to differentially flat robotic systems beyond quadrotors.

</details>


### [201] [BiPOP: Partial-Order Planning for Long-Horizon Bimanual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15317)
*Kun Chu,Xufeng Zhao,Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Cornelius Weber,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiPOP couples LLMs with Temporal Partial-Order Planning: an explicit spatio-temporal model (arm reachability workspace partition, inter-arm dependencies, arm assignment, synchronization, safe concurrency) guides LLM-based PDDL generation, and a temporal planner schedules long-horizon bimanual manipulation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bimanual tasks are largely coordinated compositions of reusable unimanual skills plus a few synchronous bimanual skills; the bottleneck is high-level spatio-temporal coordination, not per-hand skill acquisition. Prior work emphasizes skill learning and neglects the structure of bimanual behavior.
