Method: Explicit spatio-temporal model partitioning workspace by arm reachability and encoding inter-arm dependencies, arm assignment, synchronization and concurrency opportunities; LLM translates natural-language instructions into PDDL; temporal planner enforces constraints and schedules actions for parallel execution.

Result: On simulated long-horizon tasks of varying complexity, BiPOP outperforms open-loop planning with strong reasoning models and closed-loop ReAct planning in success rate, planning time and plan efficiency; full pipeline runs end-to-end on real bimanual robot NICOL. Failure analysis shows LLM-only planners mainly violate spatial/temporal coordination constraints.

Conclusion: Delegating spatio-temporal coordination to a symbolic temporal planner while using LLMs for grounding yields logically consistent, efficient bimanual plans; the structural decomposition of bimanual behavior is a viable and effective design principle.

Abstract: Bimanual robotic manipulation is versatile, yet its central challenge lies not only in executing dexterous motion with each hand, but also in coordinating the two arms for long-horizon manipulation under coupled spatial and temporal constraints. Prior work has largely focused on endowing robots with human-level manipulation skills, paying less attention to the structure of bimanual behaviour itself: many practical tasks are a coordinated composition of reusable unimanual skills and a small set of synchronous bimanual skills. This makes high-level spatio-temporal coordination, rather than skill acquisition alone, a key bottleneck for efficient bimanual execution. We propose BiPOP, a framework that couples Large Language Models (LLMs) with Temporal Partial-Order Planning for long-horizon bimanual manipulation. At its core is an explicit spatio-temporal model that partitions the workspace by arm reachability and captures inter-arm dependencies, arm assignment, synchronization requirements, and opportunities for safe concurrent execution. Building on this model, an LLM translates natural-language instructions into a formal PDDL specification, while a temporal planner enforces the constraints and schedules actions, yielding plans that are logically consistent at the symbolic level and efficient through parallel execution. On simulated long-horizon tasks of varying complexity, BiPOP outperforms open-loop planning with strong reasoning models and closed-loop ReAct-style planning in success rate, planning time, and plan efficiency, and the full pipeline runs end to end on the real bimanual robot NICOL. A failure analysis further shows that LLM-only planners mainly violate spatial and temporal coordination constraints, which motivates delegating these constraints to a symbolic planner. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [202] [Interactive Perception of Granular Materials via Robotic Scooping and Force-Torque Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15339)
*Samuli Hynninen,Mohammad Amman,Meri Mäkelä,Anirvan Dutta,Ville Kyrki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Filter++ 交互式感知框架，从舀取过程中的力-力矩时序直接推断颗粒材料属性（粒径、堆密度），并支持少样本适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒材料交互动力学复杂多变，缺乏其物理属性先验时难以可靠操控；现有方法依赖手工信号预处理或需要大量数据，难以泛化到未见材料。

Method: 将类生物启发的频域-时域滤波器组表示与因果隐动力学模型结合，构成可随交互观测在线演化的隐表示，避免手工特征工程；在 26 种颗粒材料上用舀取力-扭矩信号训练，学习可解释的隐空间并回归粒径与堆密度，支持少样本的属性映射适配。

Result: 在 26 种颗粒材料上评估，可泛化到训练集外材料；少量额外交互样本即可完成少样本适配；推断属性与搬运时不洒落的最大加速度显著相关。

Conclusion: 力-扭矩信号的因果隐滤波表示是无监督、可泛化的颗粒材料属性感知有效手段，且对下游操控参数（如运输加速度上限）具有实用价值；但验证局限于固定勺具与桌面场景，真实机器人闭环操控与更复杂工具/容器泛化仍待验证。

Abstract: Granular materials exhibit complex and highly variable interaction dynamics, making their reliable manipulation challenging without knowledge of their physical properties. We present an interactive perception framework that infers granular material properties directly from force-torque measurements acquired during scooping. The proposed Latent Filter++ combines a bio-inspired frequency-temporal filter-bank representation with a causal latent dynamical model, allowing the representation to evolve as interaction observations become available while avoiding hand-crafted signal preprocessing. The learned latent representation captures physically meaningful variation in granular materials and supports estimation of properties including grain size and bulk density. Evaluated on 26 diverse granular materials, the approach generalizes to materials excluded from training and enables few-shot adaptation of property-specific mappings using a small number of additional interaction samples. Finally, we demonstrate the relevance of the inferred properties to downstream manipulation by examining their correlation with the maximum acceleration the materials can withstand without spilling from the spoon during transport.

</details>


### [203] [Bind-WAM: Binding-Uncertainty-Guided Selective Future Imagination in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15368)
*Chenhao Qiu,Shuyi Chen,Songen Gu,Shufeng Nan,Tianyu Wang,Siyu Lin,Yifan Li,Bingwen Zhu,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bind-WAM引入语言-物体绑定不确定性作为信号，判断机器人何时需要想象未来，仅在不稳定时调用联合视觉-动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Model每步重规划都生成未来视觉代价高且常无必要，需决定"何时想象未来"的触发准则。

Method: 离线流程用完整示范与动作上下文标注任务相关物体、检测指代失配/不可见/身份漂移，得到伪标签训练轻量router，从当前观测与语言指令预测绑定不确定性；推理时共享WAM策略依据不确定性及其跨步变化选择性触发未来想象。

Result: 仿真与真机实验表明绑定不确定性可作为强且可解释的选择性未来想象信号。

Conclusion: 选择性、不确定性驱动的想象调度是降低WAM计算开销的可行方向，摘要未给出定量增益与代码链接。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) can improve robot learning by jointly predicting future visual states and actions, but generating a visual future at every replanning step is expensive and sometimes unnecessary. This raises a critical question: when should a robot policy imagine the future? In this paper, we propose Bind-WAM, a binding-uncertainty-guided WAM for selective future imagination. Bind-WAM introduces language-object binding uncertainty, selectively invoking joint future-action generation when the current observation does not support stable task-relevant action generation. An offline labeling pipeline uses complete demonstrations and action context to identify task-relevant objects and detect reference mismatch, invisibility, and identity drift. The resulting pseudo-labels supervise a lightweight router that predicts binding uncertainty from the current observation and language instruction. At inference, a shared WAM policy invokes selective future imagination according to the predicted uncertainty and its change between consecutive replanning steps. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Bind-WAM can establish binding uncertainty as a strong, interpretable signal for selective future imagination.

</details>


### [204] [Low-Cost Mobile Bimanual Manipulation for Long-Horizon Toilet Cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15381)
*Sven Schreiber,Bernard Burdiek,Lasse Huber-Saffer,Yung-Hui Li,Donglin Chen,Shang-Ching Liu,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文搭建低成本可复现的移动双臂平台（两台SO-101 + TurtleBot 4 + LeRobot-ROS 2栈），以长程"清洁马桶"任务为案例，采集250条真实演示，在同一具身与任务条件下对比评测π0、π0.5、SmolVLA、GR00T及SARM进度信号/记忆增强变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作上展现出潜力，但其在低成本、可及的移动双臂平台上的表现研究不足；缺乏对长程移动双臂任务中主流VLA模型的统一、可复现横向评测。

Method: 硬件：两台LeRobot SO-101机械臂+TurtleBot 4移动底盘；软件：LeRobot-ROS 2栈。任务：长程马桶清洁。数据：250条真实世界遥操作演示。评测对象：π0、π0.5、SmolVLA、GR00T；并构建基于SARM的任务进度信号变体与短时MEM观测历史变体，做消融对比。

Result: π0表现最佳：严格成功率60%、宽松成功率70%，在有限人工干预下提升至90%。SARM进度信号与记忆增强变体均未带来相对基线的性能提升。

Conclusion: 证明了在可及硬件上实现长程移动双臂操作的可行性，同时指出可靠VLA部署仍存在挑战；简单堆叠进度/记忆信号并非普适增益。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising capabilities for robot manipulation, but their performance on accessible mobile bimanual platforms remains underexplored. We present a low-cost and reproducible mobile bimanual platform integrating two LeRobot SO-101 manipulators with a TurtleBot 4 and a LeRobot-ROS 2 software stack. Using long-horizon toilet cleaning as a case study, we collect 250 real-world demonstrations and evaluate $\pi_0$, $\pi_{0.5}$, SmolVLA, and GR00T under the same embodiment and task conditions. We further evaluate variants using SARM-derived task-progress signals and short-term MEM observation history. Among the evaluated models, $\pi_0$ performs best, achieving 60% strict and 70% relaxed autonomous success, increasing to 90% with limited human intervention. In contrast, the evaluated progress- and memory-augmented variants do not improve their corresponding baselines. Our results demonstrate the feasibility of long-horizon mobile bimanual manipulation on accessible hardware while highlighting remaining challenges in reliable VLA deployment. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [205] [Actuation and Control of Passive Tendon-Driven Soft Extensions Through Rigid Manipulator Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15386)
*Ignacio Cuiral-Zueco,Gonzalo López-Nicolás,Youcef Mezouar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过机械臂关节运动被动驱动腱驱动软体延伸段，分离刚柔运动建模，并用带控制屏障函数的QP在规定末端执行器约束下调节软体末端与形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机械臂行程与精度大，但在狭窄空间中易碰撞破坏结构；软体机器人接触柔顺但可达范围有限。刚柔结合可兼顾二者，然而常需专用驱动与控制硬件，成本与集成复杂度高。作者希望复用现有机械臂自身运动实现软体段驱动。

Method: 将被动腱驱动软体延伸段安装在机械臂末端，腱锚定于上游连杆，机械臂运动导致外部腱跨距变化，进而改变张力并使软体段变形。差分模型将刚性输运与软体变形解耦：解析臂运动学+腱运动学，配合局部辨识的跨距—变形映射。推导在EE约束下腱可传递的独立变形方向数局部上界，以及末端误差指数收敛条件。控制层构造含控制屏障函数的二次规划，施加障碍间隙、支点限制与模型有效性条件。

Result: 仿真与真实实验在自由运动、位置约束运动和支点约束（窄孔洞检查）EE运动下均实现末端与形状调节。

Conclusion: 证明可利用机械臂关节运动被动驱动软体延伸段，并给出可分析收敛性与可控维度的轻量控制框架，无需专用驱动硬件。

Abstract: Industrial manipulators offer reach and precision but can damage nearby structures in confined spaces. Soft robots provide compliant contact but typically have limited reach. Rigid-soft systems combine their advantages, although they often require dedicated actuation and control hardware. We propose actuating a passive tendon-driven soft extension through manipulator joint motion. As the robot moves, the exterior spans of tendons anchored to upstream links change, altering tendon tension and deforming the extension. Inspection through narrow apertures, for example, constrains the motion of the end effector (EE). We develop a controller that regulates the soft tip while preserving such EE constraints. A differential model separates rigid transport from soft deformation, combining analytic arm and tendon kinematics with a locally identified span-to-deformation map. We also derive a local upper bound on the number of independent deformation directions transmitted by the tendons under the EE constraint, and conditions for the exponential convergence of the tip error. A quadratic program with control barrier functions imposes obstacle clearance, fulcrum limits, and model-validity conditions. Simulations and real experiments show tip and shape regulation under free, position-constrained, and fulcrum-constrained EE motion.

</details>


### [206] [SPARC: RoboCup Standard Platform for Autonomous Rescue Competition with Initial Datasets, Software, and Simulation Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15443)
*Yuezhong Wang,Bichi Zhang,Zhongli Yin,Pingcong Li,Raimund Edlinger,Sören Schwertfeger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboCup SPARC 提出以 Unitree A2-W 四足机器人为统一标准平台的自主救援竞赛，发布首个公开数据集、开源自主导航软件栈与仿真资产，降低自主救援机器人研究门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主救援机器人研究受限于硬件自研与维护的高门槛，缺乏统一硬件平台与基准环境，导致结果难以复现与横向比较。

Method: 构建基于 Unitree A2-W 的标准化竞赛平台与基准测试环境；在 ICRA 2026 Legged Robot Challenges 与 RoboCup Rescue 2026 期间采集首批开放数据集；发布开源自主导航软件栈及 RoboCup Rescue 测试环境的初始仿真资产。

Result: 形成"标准平台 + 基准环境 + 开放数据集 + 开源软件栈 + 仿真资产"的资源组合，面向自主导航、感知、操作与具身 AI 研究。

Conclusion: 该工作以平台与资源建设为主，为自主救援机器人提供可复现、可协作的研究基础设施，但未提出新的方法或算法贡献，实际价值有待后续基于该平台的算法研究验证。

Abstract: The RoboCup Standard Platform for Autonomous Rescue Competition (SPARC) is a new competition under development of the RoboCup Rescue Robot League that uses the Unitree A2-W as a common robot platform. SPARC aims to make research on artificial intelligence for autonomous rescue robots accessible to a broader research community, including groups without expertise in developing and maintaining custom robot hardware. By providing a capable standard platform and established benchmark environments, researchers can focus on autonomous navigation, perception, manipulation, and embodied AI for challenging disaster-response scenarios. This paper introduces the SPARC competition, its benchmark environment, and the Unitree A2-W standard platform. Furthermore, we present the first open SPARC datasets collected during the IEEE ICRA 2026 Legged Robot Challenges and RoboCup Rescue 2026, release an open-source software stack for autonomous navigation, and provide initial simulation assets representing RoboCup Rescue test environments. Together, these resources aim to lower the barrier to entry for autonomous rescue robotics and facilitate reproducible research and collaborative development on a common hardware platform.

</details>


### [207] [FloCOS: Predicting Compact Forward Occupancy Sets of Surrounding Vehicles via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15488)
*Chuansheng Dong,Yinxiao Zhan,Zongdian Li,Peng Hou,Jin Wang,Jiayu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FloCOS 用条件流匹配预测前向占用集（时变半空间凸多边形的并集），以覆盖损失与并集面积损失训练，得到紧凑且高覆盖的预测，并可直接嵌入超平面式避碰约束做轨迹优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有周围车辆前向占用预测存在两难：欠近似会低估真实占用、带来碰撞风险；过度保守又会挤压可行空间导致规划失败。需要既紧凑又高覆盖的占用集合预测。

Method: 将前向占用集表示为时变半空间凸多边形并集，由时间基系数参数化；用条件流匹配学习系数的条件分布以驱动各凸分量的时序演化；训练目标含覆盖损失（惩罚漏占）与并集面积损失（惩罚多余面积）；下游轨迹优化中直接以该非凸并集构造超平面碰撞避免约束。

Result: 在 Waymo Open Motion Dataset 上，FloCOS 生成紧凑且高覆盖的前向占用集，交互完成率高于对比基线；实车实验验证其在交互场景中可支撑无碰撞规划。

Conclusion: 以生成式流匹配直接输出可微、可嵌入优化约束的紧凑占用集合，是连接预测与规划的有效思路；但缺乏与闭环安全指标的深入对比与规模化的真车验证。

Abstract: Existing methods for predicting surrounding vehicles' forward occupancy may under-approximate the ground-truth occupancy, exposing the ego vehicle to collision risk, or produce overly conservative regions that unnecessarily restrict feasible space and cause planning failures. To address these issues, we propose FloCOS, a conditional flow-matching framework that predicts compact forward occupancy sets with high occupancy coverage. FloCOS represents the forward occupancy set as a time-varying union of half-space convex polygons parameterized by temporal basis coefficients. Conditional flow matching captures the conditional distribution of these coefficients to govern the temporal evolution of the convex components, trained under coverage and union-area losses that penalize missed occupancy and excess area. For downstream trajectory optimization, the predicted nonconvex union is directly incorporated into hyperplane-based collision-avoidance constraints. Evaluations on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset show that FloCOS generates compact forward occupancy sets with high coverage and achieves a higher interaction completion rate than evaluated baselines. Real-world vehicle experiments further confirm its ability to support collision-free planning in interactive scenarios.

</details>


### [208] [ActionCodec2: A Streaming Action Tokenizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15490)
*Zibin Dong,Yicheng Liu,Shiduo Zhang,Tianyuan Yuan,Baijun Ye,Jinqiu Wang,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActionCodec2: a non-neural streaming action tokenizer that releases actions incrementally within a prescribed error tolerance, using residual-aware quantization plus geometric covering bounds and Set-BPE lossless symbol merging.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing action tokenizers for autoregressive VLA models compress motion into chunks that must be fully decoded before execution, forcing open-loop control and preventing state feedback within a chunk; independently quantized increments also accumulate reconstruction error.

Method: Encoder quantizes each step relative to the decoder's reconstructed state (residual correction instead of error accumulation). Minimal primitive set formulated as geometric covering problem: exact minimum for translation, constant-factor-tight bounds for rotation. Set-BPE then losslessly merges co-occurring primitive symbols across coordinates and adjacent steps, order-free. Universal version trained/fitted on 20k hours across 24 embodiments.

Result: Averages 8.8 tokens/s for 15 Hz end-effector motion; with the same VLA backbone beats compared tokenizers on three simulation benchmarks and zero-shot real-robot evaluation; streaming form enables in-chunk state feedback and gains over open-loop under randomized dynamics, disturbances, moving objects; tokens serve as direct control interface for VLM agents whose composed macros reduce control decisions and thinking tokens and transfer to a held-out task.

Conclusion: A principled, tolerance-bounded streaming action representation improves VLA tokenization efficiency, closed-loop execution, and agent-level skill reuse; a strong candidate replacement for chunk-decode tokenizers.

Abstract: Most action tokenizers for autoregressive vision language action (VLA) models compress motion into chunks that must be decoded in full before their actions become executable. We introduce ActionCodec2, a non-neural streaming tokenizer that releases actions incrementally while keeping trajectory reconstruction error within a prescribed tolerance. Its encoder quantizes each step relative to the decoder's reconstructed state, so the selected motion primitive corrects residual error rather than allowing errors from independently quantized increments to accumulate. Finding the smallest such primitive set is a geometric covering problem; we derive the exact minimum for translation and constant-factor-tight bounds for rotation. Set-BPE then losslessly merges co-occurring primitive symbols across coordinates and neighboring steps, without a fixed coordinate ordering. A universal version fitted on 20k hours of data from 24 embodiments averages 8.8 tokens per second for 15 Hz end-effector motion on this corpus. With the same VLA backbone, ActionCodec2 outperforms the compared tokenizers on three simulation benchmarks and in zero-shot real-robot evaluation. Its streaming representation allows policies to incorporate state feedback within an action chunk, substantially improving performance over open-loop execution under randomized dynamics, external disturbances, and moving objects. The same tokens also can serve as a direct control interface for VLM agents, which compose successful action sequences into reusable macros. These agent-built macros reduce control decisions and thinking-token usage across attempts and transfer to a held-out task. More results are available at this https URL

</details>


### [209] [What Makes a VLA Reactive? A Study of Execution Strategies for Dynamic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15491)
*Zhuoyue Li,Zhendong Han,Pankhuri Vanjani,Wenting Li,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 以 pi-0.5 (π0.5) 预训练 VLA 为对象，系统研究"反应性"来自何处：扫描预测/执行时域、同步 vs 异步推理、加速与轨迹连续性方法、运动线索与潜空间 RL 适应，结论是反应性由策略与执行管线共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型预测动作 chunk，但当物体运动时 chunk 在执行前就过期（stale），导致动态场景下反应迟钝。现有工作对这一问题的来源（策略本身还是执行管线）缺乏系统拆解。

Method: 在两个动态仿真基准和三个真实机器人任务上，对 π0.5 做控制变量式消融：改变预测时域与执行时域；对比同步与异步推理；测试"更早交付动作"与"保持相邻 chunk 连续"两类方法；并用运动线索微调与潜空间强化学习做策略适配。

Result: 最优预测时域约 1 秒；最优执行时域随 checkpoint 变化。短时域在异步执行下收益更大；保持轨迹连续性的方法优于仅降低延迟的方法。策略适配可修复特定失效模式，但在传送带任务上，仅调整执行时域即可获得同等收益。

Conclusion: 反应性同时取决于执行管线与策略本身，评估 VLA 动态性能时应将二者联合考察；单纯做低延迟不等价于提升反应性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions, and when objects move, those chunks go stale before they are executed. We study what makes a pretrained VLA reactive, using \pihalf{} on two dynamic simulation benchmarks and three real-robot tasks. We vary the prediction and execution horizons, compare synchronous and asynchronous inference, test methods that deliver actions sooner or keep successive chunks continuous, and adapt the policy with motion cues and latent-space reinforcement learning. The best prediction horizon is about one second, and the best execution horizon depends on the checkpoint. Short horizons help more with asynchronous execution, and methods that keep the trajectory continuous outperform those that only reduce latency. Adapting the policy repairs targeted failures, but on the conveyor task changing the execution horizon gained as much. Reactivity depends on the execution pipeline as much as on the policy, so both should be evaluated together.

</details>


### [210] [Reading the Table: Sim-to-Real Game State Detection for the Chef's Hat Card Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15524)
*Connor Gäde,Matthias Kerzel,Hauke Bünning,Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Mariana Barros,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出全自动合成数据采集流水线（已知3D坐标重投影自动标注 + 域随机化），构建25413样本数据集微调检测与分割模型，组合推断桌面游戏状态；在真实NICO人形机器人上卡牌-区域指派F1达0.906，实现自主玩Chef's Hat。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人机交互桌面游戏时，机器人需精确识别高度相似的物体（如不同卡面），但真实世界标注专用训练数据成本高昂。

Method: 利用已知3D坐标重投影实现自动化标签生成，结合域随机化缩小仿真-现实差距；采集25413样本数据，微调目标检测与分割模型，将检测到的卡牌指派到游戏相关区域并推断游戏状态。

Result: 在真实NICO人形机器人上，卡牌-区域指派F1-score为0.906，成功构成自主对战的感知基础。

Conclusion: 全自动合成数据流水线可有效替代昂贵人工标注，为机器人感知桌面棋牌类游戏状态提供可行方案，但方法偏系统集成、感知为物体检测级，缺乏方法层面新意。

Abstract: Tabletop games offer a compelling scenario to study the interaction between artificial and human agents, combining social interaction with game state tracking and physical manipulation. To assess the game state, a robot needs to precisely detect closely related game objects, such as different card designs, requiring dedicated training data that is costly to annotate in the real world. Instead, we develop a fully automated synthetic data collection pipeline, using re-projection of known 3D coordinates to generate labels automatically and domain randomization to bridge the reality gap. This approach is used to collect a dataset of 25413 samples to finetune an object detection and a segmentation model, which are combined to assign detected cards to game-relevant zones and infer the game state. When evaluated on a real NICO humanoid robot, our pipeline achieves an F1-score of 0.906 for card-to-zone assignment, thus providing the perceptual foundation for a robot that can autonomously play Chef's Hat against human players.

</details>


### [211] [Generalized Inverse Kinematics via Conditional Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15565)
*Kaitlyn Zhang,Tomás Lozano-Pérez,Leslie Pack Kaelbling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenGenIK：生成式逆运动学框架，统一支持移动底盘、多末端执行器与多目标、场景感知与行为偏好，学习可行的解分布而非单一解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IK把每次查询当作独立的位姿-构型映射，而规划器实际需要的是一组满足未来可达性、避碰、稳定性等偏好的可行解分布。

Method: 生成模型（生成式IK），在公共接口下融合移动基座、多末端执行器/多目标、场景观测与行为偏好条件，输出条件分布采样。

Result: 在双臂举升、翻起等任务上，求解率与运行时间均优于现有方法；可灵活控制采样分布，并在部分场景观测下保持有效。

Conclusion: 将IK重构为条件生成问题，可同时提升求解质量与效率，并支持偏好与场景约束下的分布级控制。

Abstract: Inverse kinematics (IK) is a foundational primitive in robotics, mapping the task-space objectives expressed by higher-level planners to robot configurations that realize them. Traditional IK methods generally treat each query as an isolated pose-to-configuration problem, but planners often need a distribution over valid realizations that accounts for future reachability and preferences such as collision avoidance and stability. We introduce GenGenIK, a generative modeling approach that supports mobile bases, multiple end-effectors and targets, scene awareness, and preferences over robot behavior within a common interface. Experiments show that GenGenIK outperforms existing methods in both solve rate and runtime across tasks such as bimanual lift and flip-up, while allowing flexible control over the sampled distribution and remaining effective under partial scene observations. Website: this https URL .

</details>


### [212] [EMS-Nav: Embodied Multi-Scale Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15589)
*Zenghui Yang,Xinan Wang,Danyang Li,Zhe Xu,Zheng Yang,Jingao Xu,Qiang Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMS-Nav 以"区域"为基本单元组织感知、空间理解与导航，构建从局部区域到全局环境的层级地图，支持机载在线多尺度语言导航，首次尝试成功率较最强基线提升 9.39 个百分点，并降低建图开销与语言模型输入规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业巡检机器人需在不同空间尺度（物体、房间、区域、特定实例）上定位目标，现有开放词汇建图与多尺度导航方法依赖丰富的环境表示，计算与存储开销大，在无稳定无线覆盖、只能依靠机载算力的场景难以部署。

Method: 受生物启发，将物体及其关系聚合成具有功能语义的局部空间（region），并以局部区域为环境理解与计算的基本单位，自局部区域向更大环境构建层级地图；在此组织下实现高效建图，并支持不同尺度语言目标的解析与执行，全流程在线、端侧运行。

Result: 在物体、房间、区域和特定实例四类目标的多尺度导航实验中成功率均为最高，优于现有开放词汇建图与导航 SOTA；总体首次尝试成功率提升 9.39 个百分点，同时降低建图开销和语言模型输入长度；真实室内环境本地部署验证了端侧具身导航可行性。

Conclusion: 以"区域"作为感知、空间理解与语言引导动作的统一组织基础，可在资源受限机器人上兼顾多尺度理解与计算效率。

Abstract: Industrial inspection robots must reach work areas and specific equipment. These destinations span spatial scales from regions to objects, so robots must relate each target to its surroundings to interpret the task and determine where to go. Existing methods support multi-scale navigation through rich environment representations, but at substantial computational and storage cost. These costs limit deployment where wireless coverage is incomplete and robots must rely on onboard computation to keep navigating. We introduce EMS-Nav, a biologically inspired multi-scale navigation framework for online, on-device operation. Its core innovation is to organize perception, spatial understanding, and navigation around regions, making local spaces the basic units of both environmental understanding and computation. EMS-Nav groups objects and their relationships into functionally meaningful spaces and builds a hierarchy from local areas to the broader environment. This organization supports efficient map construction alongside the interpretation and execution of language goals at different scales. In multi-scale navigation experiments, EMS-Nav achieves the highest success rates across all four target categories: objects, rooms, regions, and specific instances. It outperforms state-of-the-art open-vocabulary mapping and navigation methods, improving overall first-attempt success by 9.39 percentage points over the strongest baseline while reducing mapping overhead and language-model input size. Local deployment in real indoor environments further demonstrates its applicability to on-device embodied navigation. By establishing regions as the organizing basis for perception, spatial understanding, and language-guided action, EMS-Nav provides a system that combines multi-scale understanding with computational efficiency to support diverse language goals on resource-constrained robots.

</details>


### [213] [Helping Hand: A Continuum Robotic Grasper for Traction in Single-Channel Endoscopic Submucosal Dissection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15638)
*Bendavid Maimon,Thompson Wong,Aedan Mangan,Sukjun Kim,Michael C. Yip,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双段连续体机器人抓持器 Helping Hand，与电刀共用一条 3.7 mm 内镜工作通道，实现牵引-切割一体化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠 ESD 技术难度高、医生采纳率低，主要瓶颈是缺乏反向牵引（countertraction），且现有器械无法在同一细工作通道内同时完成抓持与切割。

Method: 连续体机器人设计：在 Helping Hand 基座开槽，使电外科刀可从同一腔道伸出，实现单腔内的牵引与切割；随后评估工作空间及不同初始构型下的牵引力，并在离体鸡腿组织上完成隔离与切除的完整流程演示。

Result: 给出工作空间与多构型牵引力表征，并在离体鸡腿实验中完成组织分离与切除的端到端流程验证。

Conclusion: 单通道牵引-切割一体化设计在可行性上得到初步验证，或可改善 ESD 操作；但仅为离体初步验证，缺乏定量对比与在体数据。

Abstract: Endoscopic submucosal dissection (ESD) is a minimally invasive approach for removing large polyps from the colon and offers many benefits compared to other surgical approaches. However, ESD is technically very challenging to perform, leading to limited adoption by physicians. We propose the Helping Hand, a two-segment continuum robotic grasper that, together with an electrosurgical knife, enables tissue traction and cutting within a single 3.7 mm working channel. We achieve this functionality by creating a slot at the base of the Helping Hand that lets the electrosurgical knife extend out, enabling countertraction and cutting through one instrument lumen. We evaluate the Helping Hand's workspace and its ability to provide traction forces from different starting configurations. Finally, we demonstrate the full workflow by isolating and removing tissue in an ex vivo chicken thigh, illustrating the potential for the Helping Hand to help improve ESD procedures.

</details>


### [214] [Single-View RGB-D Force--Deformation Estimation for Robotic Manipulation of Elasto-Plastic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15650)
*Dezhi Sun,Marijn van Hilten,Kenan Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用单目眼在手RGB-D相机与FR3机械臂内禀力估计，建立法向压痕下力—位移体积的关系，验证感知累积变形定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久塑性变形使物理交互与空间分布的形状变化耦合，变形感知的操作缺乏可用的在线力—变形测量基础。

Method: 7-DoF Franka Emika FR3执行法向压痕，Intel RealSense L515采集接触前后表面；点云配准定位变形区域并估计位移体积；在9个力级共27次测量上做力—体积标定；用4个力级52对样本训练紧凑MLP并留出测试；另与二次模型对比；并做每步4 N的8步累积变形实验。

Result: MLP留出RMSE=1.857 N、R²=0.440；在16 N处二次模型误差更低，在19与23.5 N处MLP更优；8步实验中自第二次压痕起可稳定定位累积变形。

Conclusion: 结果给出基于内禀力估计与单视角几何的实用测量基准，为变形感知操作提供数据支撑，但拟合精度有限、泛化性尚待验证。

Abstract: Persistent deformation complicates elasto-plastic manipulation by coupling physical interaction with spatially distributed shape changes. We investigate the force--deformation relationship using a standard robot, a single eye-in-hand RGB-D camera, and intrinsic force estimates. A 7-DoF Franka Emika FR3 performs normal indentations while an Intel RealSense L515 captures pre- and post-contact surfaces. Point-cloud processing localizes deformation and estimates displaced volume. Force--volume characterization includes 27 measurements over nine force levels. A compact multilayer perceptron (MLP), trained on 52 force--volume pairs at four levels, achieves a held-out RMSE of 1.857 N and $R^2=0.440$. A separate comparison shows lower error for a quadratic model at 16 N and for the MLP at 19 and 23.5 N. An eight-step experiment at 4 N per interaction demonstrates cumulative deformation localization from the second indentation onward. These results establish a practical measurement basis for deformation-aware manipulation using intrinsic force estimates and single-view geometry.

</details>


### [215] [Recover, Don't Just Continue: Learning from Feedback for Physical Failure Understanding and Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15651)
*Miao Pan,Kaixiang Yao,Xiaohe Zhou,Xu Wang,Qinfeng Li,Wenqi Zhang,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Embodied-Corrector: 用故障注入构建Judge-Cause-Sub-Fine监督数据，先评测后SFT，再用"自生成前缀 vs 准确前缀"的对比前向OPD把规划分布内化到自生成前缀下，实现失败归因与纠错重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠指令规划的MLLM在具身执行中因本体与环境约束导致动作偏移；模型需具备三类能力：因果故障定位、能力约束下的自适应、经验驱动的决策修订，而现有具身基础模型普遍缺失。

Method: 在成功轨迹的子任务边界注入扰动、重放受影响子任务，仅保留"公共证据可观测且当前状态可恢复"的失败；将动作-后果-失败原因-恢复对齐为 Judge-Cause-Sub-Fine 四段监督；evaluation-first SFT 先学 Judge/Cause 再以正确前缀条件学 Sub/Fine；针对部署时自生成前缀导致的误差传播，用 OPD（对比前向令牌概率蒸馏）将准确前缀下的规划分布内化，部署时移除该分支。

Result: 失败分析与纠错规划显著增强：冻结VLA时复合任务成功率+6.1个百分点；固定GPT-5.5执行时操作与导航任务+13.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 将失败归因显式建模为可监督中间表征，并配合离线前缀对齐，可在不改动底层策略/执行器的情况下以插件形式提升具身任务恢复能力；但证据来自benchmark级评测，缺乏真机部署与代码开源信息。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models are regarded as the cognitive foundation of embodied intelligence, but planning from instructions alone cannot make them reliable. During execution, actions deviate from intended effects, constrained by the embodiment and environment. An embodied brain must understand why execution deviates, identify what can be reliably realized, and revise decisions using interaction experience. These correspond to causal failure localization, capability-constrained adaptation, and experience-driven revision. Current embodied foundation models largely lack these capabilities. To address this, we introduce Embodied-Corrector. We construct failure-recovery data by injecting perturbations at subtask boundaries of successful trajectories, replaying affected subtasks, and keeping failures that are observable from public evidence and recoverable from the current state. We align actions, consequences, failure causes, and recoveries into Judge-Cause-Sub-Fine supervision. Training uses evaluation-first SFT: the model learns Judge and Cause first, then Sub and Fine conditioned on accurate prefixes. Since deployment uses self-generated prefixes, evaluation errors propagate into recovery planning, and response-level rewards are too coarse to locate affected planning tokens. We therefore use OPD to internalize the planning distribution under accurate prefixes into that under self-generated ones: the model generates plans from self-generated prefixes, while a contrastive forward pass with accurate prefixes compares token probabilities to reinforce or suppress planning tokens. At deployment, this branch is removed. Experiments show stronger failure analysis and corrective planning: with a frozen VLA, composite task success improves by 6.1 percentage points; with fixed GPT-5.5 execution, manipulation and navigation tasks improve by 13.9 percentage points.

</details>


### [216] [Embedded Sim-to-Real Visual Servoing: Learned Reaching and Force-Guided Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15658)
*Dezhi Sun,Duy Khang Banh,Kenan Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在Jetson AGX Orin上集成YOLO11-seg+深度反投影+手眼标定实现视觉目标定位，对比PPO学习到达与力引导笛卡尔放置两种策略，真机成功率70%~74%，感知延迟34.55 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式感知-控制一体系统需在算力受限下同时完成视觉定位与可靠接触处理，论文想验证“学习式自由空间到达+显式接触修正”的组合是否能超越纯学习方法。

Method: 共享架构：RealSense D435i采集 + YOLO11-seg分割 + 深度反投影 + 手眼标定得到机器人坐标系下的度量目标；Franka Research 3上采用笛卡尔阻抗控制。对比(1)从仿真迁移的紧凑PPO策略（物体区域与孔洞区域到达），(2)力引导笛卡尔放置，约2 N法向接触力配合螺旋搜索。TensorRT加速。

Result: PPO策略在仿真中各1000回合成功率87.2%和83.1%；真机每策略50次试验，学习式混合流水线完成率70.0%，力引导解析流水线74.0%；TensorRT下平均感知延迟34.55 ms。

Conclusion: 结果支持采用嵌入式架构：学习策略负责无接触自由空间到达，解析力控负责接触阶段的修正；纯学习策略在接触丰富阶段仍落后于显式力引导方法，接触处理应显式建模。

Abstract: Embedded perception-to-control systems must combine visual targeting with reliable contact handling under tight computational constraints. We present a shared architecture on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin controlling a Franka Research 3 through Cartesian impedance control. RealSense D435i observations, YOLO11-seg segmentation, depth back-projection, and hand--eye calibration provide metric robot-frame targets. We compare two strategies: compact proximal policy optimization (PPO) policies transferred from simulation for object- and hole-region reaching, and force-guided Cartesian placement using approximately 2 N normal contact and spiral search. The PPO policies achieved 87.2% and 83.1% success over 1000 simulation episodes each. Across 50 real-robot trials per strategy, the learned hybrid and force-guided analytical pipelines achieved 70.0% and 74.0% completion, respectively. Mean perception latency with TensorRT was 34.55 ms. These results support an embedded architecture combining learned free-space reaching with explicit contact correction.

</details>


### [217] [GraSLA: A Lightweight Spatial-Language-Action Model with Grasp Hypotheses as Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15659)
*Jibao Yuan,Zixiang Zhao,Wensong Xu,Siqi Lu,Shaofei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraSLA 将 6-DoF 抓取假设作为几何先验，结合空间与语言基础模型，通过双向交叉注意力预测连续动作，LIBERO 平均成功率 99.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续动作模仿学习策略受示范多样性不足限制；6-DoF 抓取检测能提供多样抓取假设但开环执行缺乏动态适应性，故希望融合抓取假设的多样性与连续策略的响应性。

Method: 轻量级空间-语言-动作模型：用空间基础模型编码场景几何、语言基础模型编码任务语义，再与抓取假设经双向交叉注意力融合，输出连续动作。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上平均成功率达 99.30%。

Conclusion: 抓取假设作为几何先验可提升语言条件操作策略的行为多样性与有效性；但仅给出仿真基准结果，缺少真机验证与多抓取假设选择的消融分析。

Abstract: This paper focuses on improving the behavioral diversity and effectiveness of language-conditioned manipulation policies trained through imitation learning. Recently, continuous-action policies have become mainstream in robotic manipulation, but the lack of diverse demonstrations tends to restrict their behavioral diversity. For grasping, a fundamental subtask of manipulation, 6-DoF grasp detection provides diverse grasp hypotheses, but open-loop execution constrains responsiveness in dynamic environments. This motivates us to combine the diversity of grasp hypotheses with the adaptability of continuous-action policies. To this end, we propose GraSLA, a lightweight and effective spatial-language-action model with grasp hypotheses as geometric priors. In particular, we employ spatial and language foundation models to encode scene geometry and task semantics, and integrate them with grasp hypotheses through bidirectional cross-attention for continuous action prediction. On LIBERO, GraSLA achieves an average success rate of 99.30%.

</details>


### [218] [What World Model Does Active Object Search Need? A Study under Imperfect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15689)
*Kyeongryeol Go*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 BAMOS（贝叶斯主动漏检感知物体搜索），在检测不完美条件下研究世界模型需要哪些表示来支撑主动搜索决策，即目标信念、占据信念与检测器误差如何影响"去哪看、何时停"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动物体搜索中检测器不完美使两类决策都变得困难：未检测可能是遮挡、目标在视野外或漏检，检测也可能是误检。现有工作缺乏对"目标信念 vs 占据信念 vs 检测器误差建模"各自作用的系统性消融分析，因此需要回答世界模型应维护何种表示。

Method: 构建 BAMOS 框架，显式分离并维护目标信念（target belief）与占据信念（occupancy belief），并结合检测器误检/漏检模型进行贝叶斯更新以驱动搜索决策（下一步观察位置与终止判断）。在 Isaac Sim 与 HSSD 房屋场景中做全面消融，跨多种检测器配置考察各表示及其更新机制的作用。

Result: 消融显示目标信念及其更新机制对搜索性能至关重要，且其贡献随检测误差（尤其漏检）增多而上升；相比之下占据信念的必要性相对次要。这说明在不确定检测观测下维护并更新目标信念是可靠主动搜索的关键。

Conclusion: 为实现不完美检测下的可靠主动搜索，具身智能体的世界模型应显式建模并持续更新目标信念，并配合对检测器误差的显式建模；这为世界模型设计提供了实证指导。但当前验证仅在仿真中进行，缺少真机部署。

Abstract: In active object search, a robot must decide where to look and when to stop. Imperfect detection complicates both decisions: a non-detection may indicate occlusion, an out-of-view target, or a false negative, while a detection may be a false positive. This raises the question of which representations a world model needs to support reliable search under imperfect detection. We introduce Bayesian Active Miss-aware Object Search (BAMOS) to investigate how target belief, occupancy belief, and detector errors affect search decisions. Through comprehensive ablations in Isaac Sim and HSSD houses, we examine the roles of these representations and their update mechanisms across different detector configurations. Notably, target belief and its update mechanisms are critical to search performance, with their contribution generally increasing as detection errors become more prevalent. These findings highlight the importance of maintaining and updating target beliefs to support reliable active search under uncertain detector observations, providing insights into the design of world models for embodied agents. Code is available in this https URL .

</details>


### [219] [Robot-Induced Bias in Movement Smoothness Metrics for Rehabilitation: Quantification and Correction in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15743)
*David Regev,Alex Scherbakov,Eran Beeri Bamani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用闭环人-机器人仿真量化机器人机械特性对康复运动学指标（LDLJ、SPARC 等）的偏倚，并用梯度提升树结合辨识出的机器人动力学进行校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 康复机器人本身的动力学（摩擦、惯量）会污染基于运动学的恢复度评估，导致不同设备间的结果不可比；需量化偏倚来源并给出校正方法。

Method: 构建校准到已发表健康/卒中运动学数据的闭环人-机器人仿真；固定运动控制参数与运动计划做配对实验，以低阻抗参考为基准量化七项指标的偏倚并用外部重复性基准归一化；Sobol 敏感性分析归因；用梯度提升树结合机器人动力学与观测指标做校正，并在留出参数域与替换控制器下测试泛化。

Result: 设备参数子集内 LDLJ 偏倚中位数 0.93（卒中）/1.79（健康）基准单位；库仑摩擦与表观质量为主要贡献因素，偏倚在 11 种鲁棒性变体中持续存在且随运动碎片化减少而上升。校正后 20% 留出集上 LDLJ 归一化绝对误差中位数 0.77→0.10、SPARC 0.24→0.08；四个分别留出参数域残差 ≤0.15；换控制器不重训 LDLJ 误差 1.60→0.17，但对其他指标迁移不一。

Conclusion: 机器人机械特性可系统性扭曲平滑度类康复指标，且该偏倚可被建模与校正；结果为更可比的机器人评估提供依据，但仅限仿真，需患者数据验证。

Abstract: Robot mechanics can bias kinematic measures of recovery and limit comparisons across rehabilitation devices. We developed a closed-loop human-robot simulation calibrated to published healthy and stroke group kinematics. Paired runs held motor-control parameters and movement plans fixed to quantify bias in seven metrics relative to a low-impedance reference, normalized using external repeatability benchmarks. Within a subset informed by published device specifications, median log dimensionless jerk (LDLJ) bias was 0.93 and 1.79 benchmark units for stroke and healthy profiles, respectively. Sobol analysis identified Coulomb friction and apparent mass as leading contributors; LDLJ bias persisted across 11 robustness variants. LDLJ bias increased as simulated movements became less fragmented. If recovery similarly reduces fragmentation, this effect could partly mask improvement in measured smoothness. Correction combining identified robot dynamics and observed metrics with gradient-boosted trees reduced median normalized absolute error from 0.77 to 0.10 for LDLJ and from 0.24 to 0.08 for spectral arc length (SPARC) in the device-informed subset of a random 20% hold-out. Median smoothness residuals remained at or below 0.15 benchmark units across four separately held-out parameter regions. Under an alternative controller, LDLJ error decreased from 1.60 to 0.17 without retraining, although transfer varied across metrics. These simulation results support mechanical characterization and movement-dependent correction toward more comparable robotic assessments, motivating validation in patients.

</details>


### [220] [Prediction-Error-Tightened MPC for Socially Compliant Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15781)
*Alessandro Febbraro,Federico Rollo,Marcello Farina,Luca Bascetta,Navvab Kashiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 扩展MPC框架，将障碍/行人检测、Koopman运动预测与在线预测误差估计直接嵌入避障约束，并用语义聚合减少人群场景约束数；仿真中Markov残差收紧将物理接触率从10.0%降至1.33%（平均多0.68 s行程时间），行人感知处理使足迹/个人空间重叠占时从22.84%降至2.11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拥挤环境中的移动机器人导航需区分行人与一般障碍、预测其未来运动并处理行人/机器人轨迹的不确定性，同时保持行人的心理舒适性；人群场景下逐障碍约束导致计算复杂度高。

Method: 在先前MPC框架上集成在线障碍与行人检测、基于Koopman算子的运动预测、在线预测误差估计（Markov残差收紧），并以语义感知的邻近障碍聚合降低碰撞约束数量。

Result: 300种子配对闭环仿真中，含匀速通用障碍物时接触次数占比由10.0%降至1.33%，行程时间代价均值0.68 s；语义消融中离线足迹/个人空间重叠占比2.11%（行人感知）vs 22.84%（通用障碍处理）。

Conclusion: 语义感知聚合与在线误差界收紧可在不显著增加行程时间的情况下明显提升安全性与计算效率，但验证仅限仿真，缺少实机与真实人群实验。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of mobile robots in crowded environments, while preserving pedestrians' psychological comfort, requires distinguishing pedestrians from other obstacles, predicting their future motion, and accounting for uncertainty in the predicted trajectories of both pedestrians and the robot. This work extends a previous MPC framework by integrating obstacle and pedestrian detection, Koopman-based motion prediction, and online prediction-error estimation directly within the collision-avoidance mechanism. To reduce collision-avoidance complexity in crowded scenarios, nearby obstacles are aggregated, thereby reducing the number of collision-avoidance constraints. Simulation studies evaluate the prediction-error margins and the computational benefits of semantics-aware aggregation. In a paired 300-seed closed-loop comparison with a moving generic obstacle, Markov residual tightening reduces the fraction of runs with physical contact from $10.0\%$ to $1.33\%$, at a mean travel-time cost of $0.68$ s. In a separate crowd-navigation semantic ablation, the offline robot-footprint/personal-space overlap occupies $2.11\%$ of observed time with pedestrian-aware handling versus $22.84\%$ with generic-obstacle handling.

</details>


### [221] [RUMI: A Robotiq-Matched Universal Manipulation Interface for Robot Learning with Augmented Reality and Haptic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15802)
*Kevin Avdiu,Seongjin Bien,Yannik Blei,Philine Meister,Johannes Hechtl,Philipp Schmitt,Kai Wurm,Georg von Wichert,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RUMI：与Robotiq 2F-85几何匹配的手持式通用操作接口，配可换指尖力传感器，结合VR头显内跟踪实现AR运动可行性反馈与力反馈遥操作，用于采集力感知演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模且带力信息的操作数据集稀缺；手持式接口可将交互力经操作者手臂直接传递，但现有设计多针对简单线性夹爪，与常用机器人夹爪的非线性机构不匹配，导致采集的演示与目标平台运动学/力学不一致。

Method: 硬件上按Robotiq 2F-85几何复刻手持夹爪并集成可替换指尖传感器（接触前为自然输入，接触后为力测量）；将跟踪系统嵌入VR头显，实时AR可视化目标机器人关节可行性与位姿初始化；并设计触觉反馈通道，支持仿真与真实机器人的力感知遥操作。

Result: 用户研究表明，开启实时机器人可视化与位姿初始化后，含关节限位违规的回合数显著减少（3/50 vs 16/50）；利用两种指尖模态采集演示并训练模仿策略；在仿真中验证了该触觉接口采集力感知遥操作数据集的能力。

Conclusion: 硬件与目标夹爪匹配的力感知手持接口可提升演示数据质量并支持力反馈遥操作与力感知数据采集，为大规模力感知操作数据集提供可行路径。

Abstract: Many manipulation tasks require carefully controlled contact forces for successful execution, yet large-scale, force-aware datasets remain scarce. Handheld interfaces can help by transmitting interaction forces directly through the operator's arm, but most existing designs are tailored to simple linear grippers and do not match the nonlinear mechanics of widely used robotic grippers. We present RUMI, a handheld universal manipulation interface matched to the Robotiq 2F-85 with exchangeable fingertip sensors that preserves the target gripper geometry while enabling natural input before contact and force measurement after contact. By integrating the tracking system into a virtual reality headset, our method provides real-time augmented reality feedback about the kinematic feasibility of motion on the target robot platform. We further introduce a haptic feedback mechanism that supports force-aware teleoperation for simulated and real robots. A user study shows fewer episodes containing joint-limit violations with live robot visualization and pose initialization (3/50) than without augmented reality (16/50). We additionally train imitation policies from demonstrations, collected using two different fingertip modalities enabled by the gripper-matched hardware, and assess the haptic interface's capability for collecting force-aware teleoperation datasets in simulation.

</details>


### [222] [Runtime Safety and Emergency Stops on a Full-Size Humanoid: Deployment Lessons and a Case for Graduated Force Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15928)
*Max Conway,Yutong Zhang,Allen Devaraj,William Xie,Alessandro Roncone,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开源 Unitree H1-2 人形机器人自主栈的安全机制：所有关节指令经独立安全层，执行位置/速度/力矩限幅，并在状态估计陈旧或急停触发/断连时停机；实测显示被推倒时MPC下肢控制器无法恢复平衡，只能急停，而急停即失去平衡控制导致摔倒，且操作型限幅在正常行走中即被超出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全尺寸人形机器人将被用于拆解动力电池等危险工业作业，需回答"关节限幅能否保证安全"这一部署层面的问题，并从实际部署中提炼安全设计教训。

Method: 工程与实验：构建与控制器进程隔离的安全层，对关节位置、速度、力矩实施限幅，并基于状态估计时效性与硬线急停状态触发停机；对站立机器人施加推力扰动进行平衡/恢复试验，观察限幅与停机在正常行走与失稳场景中的实际表现。

Result: MPC 下肢控制器在推力扰动下未能恢复平衡，所有试验均需人工急停；急停会移除平衡控制而导致机器人摔倒；安全层限幅触发同样的停机，且为操作任务设定的限幅在正常行走时即被突破；靠近人员或电池包时，摔倒的危害可能大于触发停机的原因。

Conclusion: 关节限幅只约束"能施加多大力"，不约束"何时施加合适"；作者主张分阶段授予机器人施力权限，并由明确的个人或组织对其使用承担责任，同时呼吁人形平台安全与失效安全（fail-safe）策略需协同设计。

Abstract: Full-size humanoid robots are being considered for hazardous industrial work such as disassembling electric-vehicle batteries. We describe the safety mechanisms of an open-source autonomy stack for the Unitree H1-2 humanoid and what deploying them taught us. All joint commands pass through a safety layer that runs separately from the control software. It enforces joint position, velocity, and torque limits, and it stops the robot when a state estimate becomes stale or when the hard-wired emergency stop (e-stop) is pressed or disconnected. We pushed the standing robot until it lost balance. With a model-predictive lower-body controller it never recovered, and the operator had to press the e-stop in every trial. Pressing it removes balance control, and the robot falls. The safety layer's own limits trigger the same stop, and limits chosen for manipulation were exceeded during normal walking. Near an operator or a battery pack, the fall can be more dangerous than the cause of the stop. Joint limits bound how much force a robot can apply, but not when applying it is appropriate. Drawing on research on responsibility and on child and adolescent development, we therefore make a case for granting robots force authority in stages, with a person or organization explicitly responsible for its use.

</details>


### [223] [MOSAIC: Modular Obstacle and Spatial-Aware Framework for Manipulation In Cluttered Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15937)
*Richard Shi,Mohammad Mahdavian,Mehrdad Momeni Zadeh,Mohammed Elmahgiubi,Amir Rasouli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOSAIC：为预训练VLA策略注入角色特定语义掩码、深度几何token与障碍感知注意力监督的模块化空间/障碍感知框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖2D视觉与语言指令，场景几何与障碍关系需隐式推断，在杂乱环境中微小空间误差即导致碰撞或误接触。

Method: 以预训练VLA为骨干不变，附加辅助语义接地任务预测目标物/目标区域/周边障碍的角色特定掩码；结合3D重建深度与目标-目标区域掩码构建角色特定几何token注入action expert；并提出障碍感知注意力监督，将执行中机器人附近障碍的注意力特征注入后层action expert。

Result: 在RoboPRO基准（含视觉扰动测试）与真实机器人操作任务上验证；拥挤场景成功率+5.1%，视觉扰动下+9.7%，最难环境提升最高达25.0%。

Conclusion: 显式语义、几何与障碍线索可在不改变预训练VLA架构的前提下有效提升杂乱场景与视觉扰动鲁棒性，具备与现有VLA策略即插即用的模块化价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong performance in robotic manipulation, yet most rely primarily on 2D visual observations and language instructions, leaving scene geometry and obstacle relationships to be inferred implicitly. This limitation is particularly problematic in cluttered environments, where small spatial errors can lead to unintended contacts or collisions. We propose MOSAIC, a modular spatial- and obstacle-aware framework that can be integrated with pretrained VLA policies to improve object-level scene understanding and action generation. Our method augments the VLA backbone with an auxiliary semantic grounding task that predicts role-specific masks for the target object, destination region, and surrounding obstacles. We further combine the target and destination masks with depth estimated from 3D scene reconstruction to construct role-specific geometric tokens that are injected directly into the action expert. In addition, we propose obstacle-aware attention supervision that emphasizes obstacles near the robot body during task execution. The resulting attention features are subsequently injected into later action-expert layers to further guide action generation. Together, these components provide explicit semantic, geometric, and obstacle-aware cues while preserving the underlying pretrained VLA architecture. We evaluate our framework on pretrained VLA model using the RoboPRO benchmark, including visual perturbation tests, and further validate it on real-world manipulation tasks. MOSAIC improves the success rate by 5.1% in cluttered scenes, and by 9.7% under visual perturbations, with gains of up to 25.0% in the most challenging environment. We will release our code and dataset upon acceptance at the following Github page: this https URL

</details>


### [224] [COMBS: Concealed Object Multi-modal Bayesian Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15964)
*Taylor Bergeron,Kevin Leahy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CA-MOMDP与层级转移系统(HTS)建模"隐藏物体搜索"，用COMBS-score融合语义网络、LLM与VLM初始化位置信念，并以贝叶斯更新保证闭合容器不被排除，理论上给出有界搜索与不漏检保证，五个仿真配置中达成92-100%的ID/OOD成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中寻找被隐藏/遮蔽的目标物体，单一模型（仅LLM或仅VLM）先验不可靠，且现有方法缺乏对"谁能隐藏物体"这一不确定性的形式化建模与终止判据（判断目标不存在）。

Method: 1) CA-MOMDP：贝叶斯自适应设定，不确定性同时存在于物体位置与可遮蔽节点集合上，先验为有界本体先验，仅通过交互消解；2) HTS：将环境抽象为房间与物体节点的层级转移系统以供规划；3) COMBS-score：人类语义网络、LLM、VLM 多模态融合初始化信念；4) 贝叶斯规则维护信念，禁止"看一眼"排除闭合容器；5) 提供精确感知下有界搜索、噪声感知下不漏检的形式化证明；并进行逐组件消融。

Result: 五个仿真配置（gridworld 到 Matterport3D+真实检测器）中，ID与OOD目标检索率均为92-100%；速度不劣于基线；大场景下显著优于逐步LLM策略且成本更低；OOD摆放恢复优于TidyBot排序；与仅LLM先验精度相当但每次决策延迟大幅降低；是唯一可在给定误差水平下声明目标缺失的方法；逐步LLM策略的终止动作会放弃12-22%实际存在的目标，COMBS仅1.6-3.2%。

Conclusion: 将混合可观测性MDP、层级抽象与多模态先验融合相结合，可在保持形式化保证的同时高效解决隐藏目标搜索与"缺失判定"问题，且各组件均必要。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of finding a concealed object in an environment by unifying formalisms for partially observable environments with multi-modal priors drawn from a mix of ontologies, semantic networks, LLMs, and VLMs. We propose a concealment-adaptive mixed-observability MDP (CA-MOMDP), a Bayes-adaptive setting where uncertainty is over both object locations and which nodes can conceal them, held as a hypothesis with a bounded ontology prior and resolved only by interaction. We define a hierarchical transition system (HTS) to abstract the environment into room and object nodes that can be planned over via our CA-MOMDP. We further propose a COMBS-score: a multi-modal fusion of a human informed semantic network, LLM, and VLM, to initialize the belief over object locations, which grounds our abstractions and reduces reliance on a single model. Belief is maintained via Bayes' rule, which never lets a look rule out a closed container. We prove a present target is found in a bounded number of searches under exact perception and that its location is never ruled out given missed detection under noisy perception. We ablate each component to demonstrate their significance. Across five simulator configurations, from a gridworld to Matterport3D with a real detector, COMBS finds 92-100% of in-distribution and 92-100% of out-of-distribution targets, matches or beats the baselines in processing speed, significantly outperforms a per-step LLM policy at lower cost on the larger scenes, recovers more out-of-distribution placements than a TidyBot ranking, and matches an LLM-only prior at a fraction of its per-decision latency. COMBS is the only standalone method that can declare a target absent at a stated error level, and the per-step LLM policy offered a termination action abandons 12-22% of targets that were present, against 1.6-3.2% for COMBS on the same episodes.

</details>


### [225] [Video-Action Models for Data-Efficient Industrial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15974)
*Eugenio Chisari,Stefanos Charalambous,Oliver Sanchez,Thomas Umbach,Michael Gentner,Fabio Quattrini,Norica Bacuieti,Sabin Grube Doiz,Valentin Bertle,Sigmund Hennum Hoeg,Alain Ryser,German Rodriguez,Mario Arduini,Dang Nguyen,Florian Aumann,Felix Hegg,Manuel Meier,Philipp Wand,Julian Lotzer,Alessio Desogus,Benedek Forrai,Michael Bloesch,Stephan-Daniel Gravert,Stefan Weirich,Patrick Lanners,Elvis Nava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLUX-mimic：基于大规模网页+工业数据预训练的骨干，联合视频-动作训练得到视频-动作模型，在有限动作数据下实现汽车制造工业操作，变形件装配任务仅需2小时任务示教即达77.5%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业操作（刚性/变形/柔性件对位与操控）中，专用硬件、零件与夹具限制了示教采集与评测规模，预训练视觉运动策略在此场景的收益尚未被充分探索；需厘清任务特定数据需求以评估其可扩展性，因此研究"视频预训练能否在有限动作数据下支撑可靠工业操作"。

Method: 提出 FLUX-mimic 视频-动作模型：骨干在大规模网页与工业任务数据上预训练，外接一个动作专家（action expert）用于生成机器人轨迹；采用视频-动作联合训练（joint video-action training）优化策略。在四个汽车制造基准任务与两种双臂机器人平台上与基线对比，含夹爪式与灵巧手平台。

Result: 在四个汽车制造基准任务、两种双臂平台上，灵巧手平台上优于全部对比基线，夹爪平台上平均成功率与 pi_{0.5} 持平；变形零件kitting任务仅用约2小时任务特定动作标注示教即达77.5%成功率，体现出较好的样本效率。

Conclusion: 视频预训练可为工业操作策略提供有效的先验，联合视频-动作训练在动作数据稀缺条件下实现样本高效学习，具备跨刚/柔/变形件与多平台的迁移潜力，为工厂场景规模化部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: Industrial manipulation requires precise alignment and controlled handling of rigid, deformable, and flexible components. Yet, the benefits of pretrained visuomotor policies remain underexplored in settings where limited access to specialized hardware, components, and fixtures constrains demonstration collection and evaluation. Understanding task-specific data requirements is important for assessing their scalability across diverse processes on the factory floor. We investigate whether video pretraining can support reliable industrial manipulation with limited action data. We propose FLUX-mimic, a video-action model, motivated by the potential of video pretraining to capture motion and interaction patterns that enable sample-efficient training. FLUX-mimic is built on a backbone pretrained on large-scale web and industrial task data, and an action expert to generate robot trajectories. We train our policy by joint video-action training. Across four automotive manufacturing benchmark tasks and two bimanual robot platforms, FLUX-mimic outperforms all considered baselines on the dexterous robot platform and matches pi_{0.5}'s average success rate on the gripper-based robot platform. On a deformable part kitting task, FLUX-mimic reaches 77.5% success with as little as two hours of task-specific action-labeled demonstrations.

</details>


### [226] [DexRemo: A Force-Sensing Hand Platform and Task Suite for Contact-Rich Removal Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15981)
*Liman Wang,Hanyang Zhong,Sun Zhaole,Wenting Cao,Babar Jamil,Andy Tyrrell,Shan Luo,Jihong Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DexRemo三指力控手与九场景真实机器人"移除操作"任务套件，用统一腕部坐标系表达指尖力，基于ACT多模态闭环策略完成粘附/嵌入/清洁/遮挡取物等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多操作任务需分离粘附、嵌入、污染或机械受限的物体，其进展依赖视觉与运动学仅能部分观测的接触状态演化，缺乏统一硬件平台与任务基准。

Method: 设计三指高力输出机器人手，模块化指尖与三轴指尖力传感；构建9个真实机器人场景（粘附剥离、表面清洁与自清洁、遮挡条件下取物）；将指尖力统一投影到以腕为中心的共享坐标系以便多指融合；以ACT为骨干融合多视角视觉、本体感知、指尖位姿与力观测，从示教学习闭环动作块预测；在6个核心任务上做指尖输入与力坐标系消融，另设3个扩展场景做泛化测试。

Result: 学习策略在移除类任务上取得稳定表现；消融显示腕坐标系力（尤其结合指尖位姿）在稳定性与泛化上优于局部坐标系力输入和无基线。

Conclusion: 共享腕坐标系的力表征是提升多指力感知融合与移除操作泛化的关键，硬件+任务套件+坐标系设计共同构成可复用的移除操作研究基线。消融规模有限、任务数较少，跨硬件迁移仍待验证。

Abstract: Many manipulation tasks require separating adhered, embedded, contaminated, or mechanically constrained targets. We study this setting as removal manipulation, where progress depends on evolving contact states that are only partially observable from vision and kinematics. We introduce DexRemo, a three-finger robotic hand with high-force actuation, modular fingertips, and three-axis fingertip force sensing, together with a nine-scenario real-robot task suite spanning adhesive detachment, surface cleaning and self-cleaning, and occluded retrieval. To make multi-finger force observations easier to fuse, we express fingertip forces in a shared wrist-centered frame, giving force components consistent geometric meaning across fingers and time. Using an Action Chunking Transformer backbone, policies fuse multi-view vision, proprioception, fingertip pose, and force observations to predict closed-loop action chunks from demonstrations. Evaluation uses six core benchmark tasks for controlled fingertip-input and force-frame ablations, plus three extension scenarios for additional held-out coverage. Across these evaluations, the learned policies achieve consistent removal performance, and ablations show that wrist-frame forces, especially with fingertip pose, improve stability and generalization over local-frame force inputs and no-force baselines. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [227] [JustRUA: A Controlled Study of GPT-6 Robot-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16009)
*Zefan Wang,Yufan Dang,Xuanwei Shi,Mingqi Gao,Cheng Yin,SikYuen Tam,Hanyu Liu,Yasheng Wang,Yaxi Lu,Songyang Zhang,Xin Cong,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 系统性拆解机器人操作智能体的多类设计选择（harness 结构、动作接口、经验复用、场景几何），提出精简 harness 的 JustRUA：保留完整回合历史并对每段动作提前终止；无需学习策略或自改进循环，在 RoboDojo 仿真基准上将分数从 29.7 提升到 50.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期基于 VLM/大模型的机器人操作智能体在 RoboDojo 等基准上分数快速上涨，但各系统在 harness 与测试子集上不一致，且常同时改动多个设计，难以归因哪一项带来增益，因此需要受控的、单框架内的设计拆解与归因。

Method: 在同一框架内逐项消融四类设计：（1）模型外围 harness（是否保留完整 episodic 历史、是否对每段 motion 提前终止）；（2）动作接口；（3）经验复用；（4）提供给模型的场景几何（RGB 深度预测 vs 真值深度）。最终从官方 L3 harness 出发，仅保留最关键组件构成 JustRUA。

Result: 精简 harness（完整历史 + 动作早停）收益显著；经验复用会改变模型行为但并非"免费午餐"；场景几何贡献最大，即使深度仅由 RGB 预测也有明显帮助。JustRUA 在无学习策略、无自改进循环、无任务特定调优的情况下，将 RoboDojo 仿真分数由 29.7 提升至 50.3。

Conclusion: 机器人操作智能体的性能提升很大程度来自 harness 与观测接口等"外部设计"而非新策略学习，简化设计可显著提升可复现性与效果；但证据仅限仿真基准与单类动作接口，缺少真实机器人验证与跨基准泛化验证。

Abstract: GPT-6 Astra can drive a robot arm directly through a fixed harness, and scores of such robot-use agents on RoboDojo have risen quickly since. Reported systems differ in harness and test subset and often change several designs at once, so it is hard to tell which choices produce the gains. We take these designs apart and test them in one framework, along the harness around the model, the action interface, the reuse of experience and the scene geometry given to the model. A lean harness that keeps the full episode history and stops each motion early goes a long way. Reusing experience changes how the model acts but is not a free lunch, and scene geometry helps most, even when depth is predicted from RGB alone. JustRUA starts from the official L3 harness and keeps only what matters most. Even with no learned policy, self-improvement loop or task-specific tuning, this simple harness raises the score on the RoboDojo simulation benchmark substantially, from 29.7 to 50.3.

</details>


### [228] [PERA: Plug-in Experts for Robotic Actions in Continual Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16188)
*Shangchao Hu,Yadan Sun,Xibo Yu,Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PERA freezes a pretrained mixture-of-experts motion policy and trains a small task expert + routing score, adding new humanoid skills at ~1.03% extra parameters while preserving all 126 original motions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whole-body motion imitation policies for humanoids are hard to extend: full fine-tuning for a new skill degrades existing behaviors, while per-skill separate policies inflate training and maintenance cost. The paper seeks incremental skill acquisition that reuses an existing motion capability without catastrophic forgetting.

Method: Build on a pretrained MoE policy (base actor). Freeze all base parameters; add a new task expert (a small module, ~1.03% of base actor params) plus a learned routing score that gates it. Train the expert and router with either task rewards (RL) or teacher-action supervision. Because routing can be disabled, the original computation path is recoverable exactly.

Result: In Unitree G1 simulation, PERA completes all ten new tasks on top of the pretrained policy. Disabling the expert restores the base path, which still fully executes all 126 original motions. Enabling the expert also improves some dance motions unseen in expert training. Transfer demonstrated with HoloMotion as an alternative base policy.

Conclusion: MoE-structured incremental expansion is an effective, low-cost route to continual skill acquisition for humanoid motion imitation, giving non-forgetting by construction and some cross-skill positive transfer; evidence is simulation-only with no real-robot validation.

Abstract: Motion imitation enables humanoid robots to acquire whole-body skills such as walking and dancing. However, fine-tuning an entire policy for a new skill can affect existing behaviors, while training a separate policy for each skill increases training and maintenance costs. We present PERA, a method for learning new skills by reusing existing motion capabilities. PERA freezes a pretrained mixture-of-experts policy and trains an additional task expert and its routing score using task rewards or teacher-action supervision. In Unitree G1 simulation, each added task module contains approximately 1.03% of the parameters of the base actor. Built on our pretrained policy, PERA successfully completes all ten new tasks. Disabling the expert restores the original computation path of the base policy, which still fully executes all 126 original motions. Enabling the expert also improves the execution of some dance motions not used to train that expert, indicating that its benefits extend beyond its training motions. Experiments with HoloMotion further demonstrate the applicability of PERA to different base policies.

</details>


### [229] [QVS-LIVO RiskMap: Health- and Information-Aware View Selection for Multi-Camera LIVO and Off-Road Risk Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16205)
*Zihan Pan,Jinghan Wang,Hang Li,Feng Cheng,Tianchen Liu,Gaoliang Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QVS-LIVO RiskMap：将视觉状态估计与语义建图解耦的多相机激光-视觉里程计，通过只读预跟踪+通道健康记忆与切换迟滞，从多视角中选至多一个用于光度校正，后端融合全视角语义构成本地BEV与全局2.5D风险地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野环境下多相机观测可靠性差异大，退化几何约束下的视觉互补与鲁棒性难以兼顾；同时语义建图需要多视角覆盖，与状态估计的最优视角选择存在冲突。

Method: 前端在共享的激光-惯性后验上做只读预跟踪，评估观测健康度与几何互补性，用通道健康记忆与切换迟滞选择至多一个可用视角做直接光度校正；无合格视角或校正未通过验收时保留后验。后端在共享世界栅格中融合全部视角语义观测，以车体尺度几何约束风险校正，并做时空证据累积确认。

Result: 两条野外序列水平RMSE为0.910 m与1.664 m，长路线退化序列相对FAST-LIO2误差降低43.3%；12种受控相机故障（丢帧、黑帧、60 ms时间戳偏移）下均保持完整轨迹，误差1.54–2.23 m；Jetson AGX Orin上本地/全局地图分别9.94 Hz与2.00 Hz，本地建图中位延迟32.4 ms。

Conclusion: 解耦式选择性视觉更新可在多相机故障与越野退化条件下实现实时鲁棒里程计与多视角语义风险建图，具备嵌入式部署可行性。

Abstract: Multi-camera observations in off-road environments vary significantly in reliability, posing challenges when complementing degraded geometric constraints. Motivated by this, we propose Quality-aware View Selection Lidar-Vision Odometry Risk Mapping (QVS-LIVO RiskMap), which decouples visual processing for state estimation from that for semantic mapping. The front end evaluates camera observations through read-only pretracking on a shared lidar-inertial posterior, combining observation health with geometric complementarity. Channel health memory and switching hysteresis govern selection of at most one eligible view for direct photometric correction. The posterior is retained when no view is eligible or the correction fails acceptance. The mapping back end integrates semantic observations from all available views in a shared world grid. Vehicle-scale geometry constrains semantic risk corrections, while spatial and temporal evidence supports risk confirmation. The system also produces a local bird's-eye-view (BEV) risk map and a global 2.5D semantic-terrain risk map with obstacle instance voxels. On two field sequences, QVS-LIVO achieved horizontal root mean square errors of 0.910 m and 1.664 m. It reduced error by 43.3% relative to FAST-LIO2 on the degraded long-route sequence. Across 12 controlled camera-fault configurations involving dropout, black frames and 60 ms timestamp shifts, it maintained complete trajectories with errors of 1.54-2.23 m. When deployed onboard an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, the system generates local and global maps at 9.94 Hz and 2.00 Hz, respectively, with a median local mapping latency of 32.4 ms. These runtime results demonstrate the real-time feasibility of selective visual state updates alongside multi-view semantic mapping under challenging off-road conditions. The code and related resources are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [230] [Visibility-Aware Task and Motion Planning with Task-Centric Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16213)
*Sahit Chintalapudi,Xiaoyan Wang,Aryan Zoroufi,Aditya Agarwal,Nancy Kanwisher,Tomás Lozano-Pérez,Leslie Pack Kaelbling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VAToM（Visibility-Aware Task and Motion Planning），将确定性TAMP求解器扩展到部分可观测的物体重排任务：在原本假设全可观测的计划骨架上交织感知动作，并用持久化物体记忆将观测融合为物体网格假设与占据栅格，实现主动感知以应对严重遮挡与部分可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作在不确定性下依然困难；现有确定性TAMP求解器在长时程上高效且具备组合泛化，但依赖全可观测假设，而真实桌面重排场景存在严重遮挡与部分可见，需要引入主动感知与跨时间记忆。

Method: 在确定性TAMP骨架中插入感知动作以解决可见性/不可观测性；引入基于物体的持久记忆模块，将多帧观测融合为每个物体的网格假设（mesh hypotheses）及环境占据地图，从而在重规划时利用累积不确定性估计。

Result: 在真实桌面重排任务（含严重遮挡与部分可见）上验证，性能优于不具备主动感知或缺少持久记忆的基线方法。

Conclusion: 将主动感知与持久物体级记忆嵌入确定性TAMP，是处理部分可观测长时程操作的有效途径，可保留原求解器的效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation under uncertainty remains a challenging problem in robotics. We present \ourmethod{}, an algorithm for Visibility-Aware Task and Motion Planning. \ourmethod{} extends existing deterministic task and motion planning solvers to partially observable object rearrangement tasks, while still being able to take advantage of the efficiency and compositional generalization over long horizons of the base solver. This extension interleaves sensing actions into a plan skeleton that assumed full observability, and uses a novel persistent object-based memory to fuse observations into object mesh hypotheses and an observed occupancy map. We evaluate \ourmethod{} on real-world tabletop rearrangement tasks which feature heavy occlusion and partial visibility and demonstrate that it outperforms baselines that do not perform active perception or have persistent memory.

</details>


### [231] [A Coupled Control Barrier Function Approach to Deadlock-Free Multi-Robot Conflict Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16218)
*Gary Rostomyan,Ketan Savla,Petros A. Ioannou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种带输入约束的多机器人冲突消解的QP反馈控制器：用耦合CBF保证安全，用"让行矩阵"编码机器人间相互影响，可设计让行偏好且从构造上保证无死锁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人交互场景中，单个机器人的决策会改变他人的行为，安全与死锁需同时处理；现有方法多采用固定或硬优先级，缺乏可调让行偏好且难以从原理上排除死锁。

Method: QP形式反馈控制器 + 耦合Control Barrier Function刻画多机联合安全约束 + 让行矩阵（yielding matrix）建模机器人间的相互让步关系；给出控制器局部Lipschitz的充分条件；分析CBF符号不定松弛项的最优值，证明其存在有限一致上界，并通过扩大CBF安全裕度补偿，得到包含于期望安全集的前向不变集；指出由有限最大减速度导致、无法靠增大松弛惩罚消除的不可约松弛分量；推导让行矩阵与标称控制器的充分条件以排除死锁状态。

Result: 理论上证明了松弛项有界性与前向不变性、控制器局部Lipschitz性、无死锁的充分条件，并刻画了不可约松弛这一本质限制；仿真展示了随让行偏好自适应协调及对机器人失效的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为带约束的多机器人冲突消解提供了可解释、可证明安全的CBF-QP框架，兼顾让行偏好灵活性与无死锁保证，理论分析较扎实，但验证仅停留于仿真，缺少真实多机器人平台实验与代码/数据开放信息。

Abstract: Many robotic applications involve scenarios where multiple robots interact and the decisions of one robot influence the behavior of others. This paper presents a quadratic programming (QP)-based feedback controller for multi-robot conflict resolution with input constraints. Safety is enforced using coupled Control Barrier Function (CBF), while a yielding matrix encodes how robots influence each other. Unlike existing methods with fixed or hard priorities, the proposed framework enables yielding preferences while guaranteeing deadlock-free operation by design. Sufficient conditions are established for the controller to be locally Lipschitz. The optimal CBF sign- indefinite slack is shown to admit a finite uniform bound, which is compensated through an enlarged CBF safety margin to obtain a forward invariant set contained within the desired safe set. The analysis also identifies an irreducible slack component due to finite maximum deceleration that cannot be eliminated by increasing the slack penalty. Finally, sufficient conditions on the yielding matrix and nominal controller are derived to exclude deadlock states. Simulations demonstrate adaptive coordination across yielding preferences and robustness to robot failures.

</details>


### [232] [BWM-Forge: Agentic World Programming for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16221)
*BWM Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BWM-Forge：代码驱动的机器人视频生成框架，用编码智能体构建可编辑 Blender 场景程序（物体/机器人运动/相机），作为代理视频为生成模型提供结构与运动参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端机器人视频生成难以显式控制场景几何、机器人动作与视角，导致定向编辑和跨任务复用困难。

Method: 多智能体编排（输入处理、场景构建、审查、渲染）；支持初始图像、带运动数据图像或人类演示视频输入；生成可执行的 Blender 场景程序作为结构-运动代理，再由视频生成模型补足视觉细节；自动评估+用户反馈迭代优化，并配经验库存取可复用建模/渲染规则。

Result: 15 个任务、5 类机器人上与 LingBot-World Base Cam 相比，在美学质量、成像质量、主体/背景一致性、运动平滑度、RoboJudge-9B 物理合理性与指令对齐上更优；基线仅在动态程度上更高。定性案例展示视角控制、场景变化、反馈驱动编辑与经验复用。

Conclusion: 可编辑、可执行的场景程序（程序化世界模型式代理）是获得可控机器人视频的可行路径，并支持多本体与共享场景的多视角。

Abstract: Generating diverse robot videos requires control over scene geometry, robot motion, and viewpoints. End-to-end video generation offers limited direct access to these variables, making targeted edits and reuse across tasks difficult. We present BWM-Forge, a code-driven framework for controllable video generation in robotics. Given robot assets and a task description, the framework accepts an initial image, an image with motion data, or a human demonstration video. A coding agent constructs editable Blender scene programs that specify objects, robot motion, and cameras. The resulting proxy videos provide structural and motion references for a video generation model, which adds visual detail. A multi-agent harness coordinates input processing, scene construction, review, and rendering. Automated evaluation and user feedback support iterative refinement, while an experience repository stores and retrieves reusable modeling and rendering rules. This design supports multiple robot embodiments, synchronized viewpoints, and appearance variation from a shared scene program. We evaluate the framework on 15 tasks covering five robot types. Compared with LingBot-World Base Cam on these tasks, BWM-Forge achieves higher aesthetic quality, imaging quality, subject and background consistency, motion smoothness, and RoboJudge-9B physical adherence and instruction alignment scores. The baseline scores higher in dynamic degree. Qualitative cases illustrate viewpoint control, scene variation, feedback-driven edits, and experience reuse across scenes. These results demonstrate a practical approach to generating controllable robot videos through editable, executable scene representations.

</details>


### [233] [What Can a VLA Learn from the Agent That Calls It? Task-Level Experience Distillation for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16246)
*Jinze Tao,Yuxiao Han,Kangkang Dong,Yancong Wei,Xiaojun Zhu,Bokui Chen,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H2E任务级经验蒸馏：将LLM规划器驱动的导航agent会话转为VLA训练数据，通过屏蔽离目标动作损失、1m内标注停靠、LoRA微调，使8B VLA在REVERIE-CE val-unseen成功率从35.0%升至44.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航agent每回合调用LLM规划器+子指令/tool驱动动作，推理成本高、延迟大；且agent会话中掺杂规划器驱动运动、绕路等噪声，不能直接当作VLA示范。作者选择蒸馏规划器经验到VLA而非蒸馏规划器本身，使VLA测试时可独立导航并学到基础模型无法到达的目标。

Method: H2E将每条运动轨迹在原始任务指令下重写为VLA动作样本，以实际到达位姿为监督目标；对远离目标（偏离进程）的步屏蔽损失；将距目标1m内所有访问状态标为"停"；最后用LoRA对VLA进行适配微调。

Result: 在REVERIE-CE全部3,189个val-unseen回合上，8B VLA成功率由35.0%提升至44.9%（+9.9点）；VLA学会在目标处停靠，并能找到基座模型从未到达的目标。

Conclusion: 将agent成功经验以任务级蒸馏方式注入VLA，是一种无需测试时LLM、可离线部署的低成本范式；关键是会话数据的噪声过滤（离目标步屏蔽）与停靠标签化。

Abstract: Navigation agents use a large language model (LLM) planner to call a vision-language-action (VLA) model. Each episode needs the LLM, adding cost and delay. We distill successful agent experience into the VLA instead of distilling the planner. The VLA then navigates alone at test time. Agent sessions are not VLA demonstrations. They include motions driven by planner tools and sub-instructions, along with detours. We present H2E, a task-level experience distillation method that converts these sessions into VLA training data. Every motion becomes a VLA action under the original task instruction, with the resulting poses as targets. H2E masks the loss on steps that move away from the goal. It labels every visited state within 1 m of the goal as a stop. We then update the VLA with low-rank adaptation. The VLA learns to stop at the goal and find goals the base model never reaches. On all 3,189 REVERIE-CE val-unseen episodes, H2E raises the success rate of an 8B VLA from 35.0% to 44.9%.

</details>


### [234] [Direct-Ink-Writing of Soft Thin Film Force-Sensitive Resistors with Velostat and Printed-Carbon Sensing Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16250)
*Mostafa Mo. Massoud,Paulo H.T.F Alves,Timothy Kennedy,Jacqueline Libby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于Voltera NOVA直写打印银电极与多层Velostat的柔性力传感器，系统表征层数、基材、迟滞、漂移与万次循环稳定性，并探索全打印碳电极架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性电阻式力传感器在可穿戴、康复与人机接口中应用广泛，但其性能高度依赖敏感材料架构与制造工艺，需要可参数化、可复现的打印流程与量化表征。

Method: 采用参数化直写（DIW）工作流，在PET与TPU基板上打印银电极并叠层Velostat敏感层（1/2/4/6层），另做全打印碳基架构探索；测试力-电压响应线性度、面积迟滞、5分钟电压漂移及10,000次循环稳定性。

Result: 力-电压响应强非线性、低力区灵敏度最高；直线拟合R²仅0.475–0.641，迟滞3.90%–7.47%，漂移1.9%–7.3%；层数与指标无单调关系；PET6/TPU6首末100次循环峰值输出变化<0.05 V；打印碳器件≤6层在约0.5 N以下饱和或失效，8层仅在约2 N以下有响应。

Conclusion: 验证了印刷电极+Velostat工艺路线的可行性，但打印敏感材料本身是性能瓶颈，为后续材料开发指明方向。

Abstract: Flexible resistive force sensors are useful for wearable, rehabilitation, and human--machine interfaces, but their performance depends on sensing-material architecture and fabrication. This work presents a parameterized Voltera NOVA-based direct-ink-writing workflow using printed silver electrodes with multilayer Velostat sensing elements on PET and TPU substrates, together with an exploratory fully printed carbon architecture. Velostat sensors containing 1, 2, 4, and 6 sensing layers exhibited strongly nonlinear force--voltage responses, with the greatest sensitivity at low forces. Best-fit straight-line $R^2$ values ranged from 0.475 to 0.641, area-based hysteresis from 3.90\% to 7.47\%, and 5-min voltage drift from 1.9\% to 7.3\%, with no consistent monotonic relationship between layer count and these metrics. Selected PET6 and TPU6 devices showed less than 0.05~V change in peak output between the first and final 100 cycles of a 10,000-cycle test. Printed-carbon devices demonstrated the feasibility of a more fully printed architecture, but configurations with six or fewer layers saturated below approximately 0.5~N or were nonfunctional, while the eight-layer device responded primarily below approximately 2~N. These results establish the feasibility of the printed-electrode Velostat workflow while identifying printed sensing materials as a key area for further development.

</details>


### [235] [Robot Navigation and Obstacle Avoidance from Randomly Positioned External Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16282)
*Raul Steinmetz,Jair Augusto Bottega,Ayanori Yorozu,Akihisa Ohya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VINE用监督学习从固定外部相机观测估计视角不变的障碍地图，解决了外部视角与自我中心动作/目标之间的视角错配，使PPO或A*可用于室内机器人导航，并在评估期相机位姿随机化下超过机载相机PPO的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础设施式固定外部相机（exocentric）可为室内机器人导航提供全局视野，便于避开遮挡与盲区，成本低于机载传感器。但标准端到端PPO在机载（egocentric）相机上有效，在外部视角下却学不起来：外部观测与自我中心的动作、目标表达之间存在视角错配，仅靠奖励信号无法弥合。

Method: 提出VINE框架：先用监督学习训练障碍地图估计网络，输入为外部图像、相机位姿、机器人位姿和房间尺寸，输出视角不变的障碍地图；该地图再分别接入PPO策略或A*规划器。设计上不依赖视角特定的调参，容忍位姿噪声（各轴30cm标准差、约20°旋转误差）与相机内参变化，并可扩展到双相机部分覆盖场景。

Result: 地图估计优于透视映射（perspective mapping）基线；在相机位置与朝向大范围随机化、位姿仅为近似值、内参变化的条件下仍稳定工作；双相机部分覆盖场景同样可扩展。评估时相机位置随机化的情况下，导航成功率仍高于搭载机载相机的PPO基线（仿真环境）。

Conclusion: 将外部视角感知与自我中心决策解耦——用监督学习得到视角不变的中间表征（障碍地图），可绕开端到端RL难以跨越的视角错配问题；该方法为基础设施辅助的室内机器人导航提供了可用的模块化方案，但证据目前限于仿真环境，缺少真机验证。

Abstract: We study robot navigation in indoor environments from fixed exocentric cameras (infrastructure-mounted, external to the robot). We show that standard Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), which succeeds with onboard cameras, fails to learn this navigation end-to-end in our setup. This failure results from the perspective mismatch between exocentric observations and egocentric action and goal formulations, which is too complex to resolve from the reward signal alone. To address this, we propose Viewpoint-Invariant Navigation from Exocentric Cameras (VINE). VINE uses an obstacle map estimation network, trained with supervised learning, that takes exocentric images, camera pose, robot pose, and room dimensions as input and produces a viewpoint-invariant obstacle map. This map can be used either by a PPO policy or by a planner such as A*. VINE's map estimation performs better than perspective mapping approaches, handles cameras placed across a wide range of positions and orientations without view-specific tuning, requires only approximate camera pose estimates (noise with a standard deviation of 30cm per axis in x, y, and z and about 20° of rotation error on average), is robust to intrinsic variation, and extends to two-camera setups with partial coverage. Despite operating from cameras randomized in position during evaluation, VINE surpasses the navigation success rate of PPO with an onboard camera in simulated environments.

</details>


### [236] [Hibikino-Musashi@Home 2026 Team Description Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16297)
*Soma Fumoto,Ryohei Kobayashi,Taiki Watanabe,Yuga Yano,Akinobu Mizutani,Naoki Yamaguchi,Ryo Terashima,Ryusei Sakakibara,Kazuma Futami,Keita Okumura,Kosei Isomoto,Koshun Arimura,Yuta Ohno,Tomoaki Fujino,Sojiro Shimada,Yuichiro Tanaka,Takeshi Nishida,Hakaru Tamukoh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboCup@Home 2026开放平台联赛的系统级技术综述：集成学习型免训练视觉识别、基于RoomPlan的半自动语义地图构建、Whisper+LLM任务规划、储池计算+FPGA动作识别，并开源环境/数据/导航工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为服务机器人在动态家庭环境中打破运行时间与算力约束，提升可复现性、复用性与社区持续进展，构建可落地的整机系统栈。

Method: 系统集成方案：(1) 学习型training-free视觉模型实现已知/未知物体识别；(2) 基于RoomPlan的半自动家具几何与标签注册，生成语义地图；(3) Whisper语音识别 + LLM规划器生成原语技能序列并执行；(4) 储池计算做轻量时序建模用于动作识别，FPGA实现低功耗实时推理；并开源任务环境、数据集工具与导航框架。

Result: 摘要层面给出定性结论：已知/未知物体识别更鲁棒、语义地图构建半自动化、FPGA储池计算实现低功耗实时动作识别并解除运行时约束；未提供定量指标或实机评测数据。

Conclusion: 属系统集成与工程综述类工作，价值主要在开源基础设施与竞赛级集成范式；缺乏新方法贡献与量化验证，短期阅读优先级中等偏低。

Abstract: This paper presents an overview of the integrated technologies developed for the Open Platform League at RoboCup@Home 2026 in Incheon. A vision system integrated with learning-based, training-free models enables robust object recognition for both known and unknown objects. We introduce a RoomPlan-based method that semi-automatically registers furniture geometry and labels to generate semantic maps. Additionally, a large language model-based planner interprets the instructions recognized by Whisper and generates primitive skill sequences for execution. A reservoir-computing framework serves as a lightweight temporal-processing model for action recognition. Its field-programmable gate array-based implementation enables low-power, real-time operation. This is key to removing operational time constraints for service robots. Open-source releases, including task environments, dataset tools, and navigation frameworks, enhance reproducibility, reuse, and continuous progress within the RoboCup@Home community.

</details>
