### [237] [Alloy: A Multi-Material Physics Engine for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16312)
*Pranay Gupta,Deepanshu Rohilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Alloy：开源多材料物理引擎，在一个场景/一次控制步内耦合刚体、布料、线缆、液体、颗粒与软体求解器，并在单GPU上并行数万个世界，同时面向编码智能体设计（MCP工具化、内嵌文档）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规模化训练机器人所需仿真器只擅长刚体；引入其他材料通常意味着另建一套引擎与手写粘合层，导致多材料场景难以统一构建、耦合与并行化。

Method: 统一对象描述一次；按材料分配专用求解器；在每个控制步内做求解器间耦合；基于 Newton、MuJoCo Warp 与 Warp 实现GPU/CPU多世界并行；为每个世界输出健康度评分以区分物理失败与策略失败；通过MCP暴露引擎为工具、内置文档、错误信息直接给出修复方式。

Result: 冷启动测试中，从未接触过Alloy的智能体从包索引安装预发布版本，并在单GPU上训练出行走、线缆操作与倾倒策略；另报告吞吐随世界数的扩展性、深度相机吞吐、与闭式解参考的一致性，以及上千世界的可靠性测试。

Conclusion: Alloy提供了一个多材料统一、可耦合、可大规模并行且对智能体友好的物理仿真底座，为多材料机器人学习与自动化仿真流程搭建提供了可复用基础设施。

Abstract: Robots handle much more than rigid objects: towels, cables, water, sand. The simulators used to train robots at scale are strongest on rigid bodies, and adding another material usually means a second engine and hand-written glue between the two. We present Alloy, an open-source physics engine for robot learning in which rigid robots, cloth, cables, liquids, granular material and soft bodies share one scene. Each object is described once; Alloy assigns it to a solver suited to its material, couples the solvers inside every control step, and runs the scene in many parallel worlds on one GPU, or on a CPU. Every world carries a health grade, to help tell a failure of the physics from a failure of the policy. Alloy is built on Newton, MuJoCo Warp and Warp, and it is written to be driven by coding agents: its documentation ships inside the package, every error names its fix, and the engine is exposed as tools over the Model Context Protocol. In a cold test, agents that had never seen Alloy installed a pre-release from the package index and trained policies for walking, cable handling and pouring on a single GPU. We also report how throughput grows with the number of worlds, depth-camera throughput, agreement with closed-form references, and a reliability test over a thousand worlds.

</details>


### [238] [Memory-Efficient Parallel Policy Learning for Collapse-Reactive Retreat via Hybrid Terrain Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16337)
*Yuki Kadokawa,Ritsuki Matsunaga,Yuqi Yang,Edgar Anarossi,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合地形建模的并行强化学习框架，用刚体仿真非可塌陷地形+虚拟接触力建模可塌陷区域，并以状态复用接触模型实现独立并行经验，训练本体感知的"塌陷触发后撤"策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后与不稳定斜坡场景中，机器人接触可能引发未勘察地形塌陷，需在失去支撑时立即后撤。大规模并行RL需要相互独立的状态-动作经验，但共享刚体可塌陷地形下，一个机器人的塌陷会间接改变他人的接触（间接干扰）；为每个环境复制地形对象内存开销过大，限制并行规模。

Method: 关键洞察：策略学习不必精确建模刚体塌陷过程，塌陷引起的接触响应即可胜任。方法为混合地形建模：非可塌陷区域用刚体仿真，可塌陷区域用虚拟接触力替代；提出状态多路复用接触模型(State-Multiplexed Contact Model)，共享地形几何与接触响应律，同时维护各环境独立的塌陷状态，从而在不为可塌陷区域创建地形对象的前提下获得独立经验。最终训练单一本体感知策略，兼顾正常行走与塌陷反应式后撤。

Result: 在同等GPU显存限制下，并行环境数达精细分辨率刚体块地形方案的100倍以上；可扩展性与样本效率提升使策略收敛速度加快50倍以上，且后撤性能相当。sim-to-sim与实际机器人实验验证了多种塌陷条件下的后撤行为。

Conclusion: 以"接触响应替代刚体塌陷"的混合地形建模+状态复用接触模型，可在内存受限条件下实现大规模并行RL采样，高效获得融合正常运动与塌陷反应式后撤的单一本体感知策略，并具备实物迁移能力。

Abstract: At disaster sites and on unstable slopes, robot contact can collapse uninspected terrain, requiring retreat upon support loss. Massively parallel reinforcement learning of collapse-reactive retreat requires independent state--action experiences. However, on shared rigid-body collapsible terrain, one robot's collapse alters others' contacts without direct interaction. We call this indirect interference. Duplicating terrain objects prevents this but is memory-intensive, limiting parallel sampling. Our key insight is that policy learning does not necessarily require rigid-body collapse modeling, which is feasible but computationally expensive; collapse-induced contact responses can suffice. We therefore propose a memory-efficient parallel policy-learning framework for collapse-reactive retreat via hybrid terrain modeling. Hybrid terrain modeling combines rigid-body simulation of non-collapsible terrain with virtual contact forces for collapsible regions. A State-Multiplexed Contact Model shares terrain geometry and a contact-response law while maintaining environment-specific collapse states, enabling independent experiences without terrain objects for collapsible regions. The framework trains a proprioceptive policy for both normal locomotion and collapse-reactive retreat. It supports over 100 times as many parallel environments as fine-resolution rigid-block terrain within the same GPU memory limit. This scalability and improved sample efficiency enable over 50 times faster policy convergence with comparable retreat performance. Sim-to-sim and real-robot experiments demonstrate retreat under multiple collapse conditions. A supplementary video is available at: this https URL

</details>


### [239] [MotionSkill: From Bipedal Motion Video to Control across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16346)
*Philip Niu,Xiaofeng Han,Sylvie Pan,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionSkill recovers a timed gait reference once from a monocular video and retargets it to ten simulated bipeds via landmark-aligned inverse kinematics, then learns per-robot residual policies in MuJoCo to correct joint commands during contact.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hand-specifying the same gait for each biped is laborious; video provides timing but must be re-executed by each robot's own limbs and actuators.

Method: Single monocular video → one timed reference; align root motion and body landmarks in a common frame with per-timestamp landmark validity; mapper preserves limb directions, rescales to target segment lengths, solves IK under joint limits and closure constraints; per-biped residual RL policy on the mapped reference from a four-second walking clip.

Result: Ten bipeds trained from one clip; 98% trial completion from held-out initial states on the original video, 26% with upper body occluded for one second (simulation only).

Conclusion: A reusable video-to-multi-robot gait pipeline with occlusion-aware landmark tracking plus residual correction; robustness degrades sharply under occlusion, and evaluation remains simulation-only.

Abstract: Reusing a demonstrated gait can reduce the work of specifying the same motion for each biped. A video captures the timing of a step, but each robot must execute it with its own limbs and actuators. MotionSkill recovers a timed reference once from monocular video and adapts it to each target in simulation. It aligns root motion and body landmarks in a common frame and records which landmarks are available at each instant. At each source timestamp, the mapper preserves limb directions, uses the target segment lengths, and solves inverse kinematics under joint limits and closure constraints. In MuJoCo, we train a separate residual policy for each of ten bipeds on its mapped reference from one four-second walking clip. Each policy uses the measured robot state to correct joint commands during contact. From initial states held out during training, MotionSkill completed 98% of trials on the original video and 26% with the upper body occluded for one second.

</details>


### [240] [ManiLadder: Benchmarking Robot Manipulation Through a Categorized and Multi-Difficulty Level Task Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16374)
*Chongkai Gao,Jieao Shi,Zhaoyu Mu,Yudi Lin,Xuanwei Liu,Wei Ken Ng,Baiye Cheng,Anqi Chen,Kerun Liu,Zhuoran Li,Xuchuan Huang,Haoyang Li,Shengjia Zhu,Nga Teng Chan,Yuxuan Zhang,Xingyu Chen,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiLadder：112 个任务、4 个难度等级的机器人操作仿真基准，通过固定数据量+统一 flow-matching 模仿学习策略标定难度，并提供迁移、数据缩放与真机验证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准多集中于简单抓放任务，缺乏沿"任务难度"轴系统衡量机器人学习进展的框架。作者希望构建一个难度可标定、可比较的"阶梯式"基准。

Method: 构建 112 个任务，按 4 个难度等级分层、每级 8 类，覆盖刚体/可变形物体、单臂/双臂、夹爪/灵巧手。为标定难度，所有任务固定相同数据量，采用统一 flow-matching 模仿学习策略与相同训练评估协议，以其成功率作为经验难度信号；任务迭代精修至落入目标难度区间，并在不同随机种子、策略和 agentic 学习系统上交叉验证。此外分析任务属性、策略性能与训练设置对比、任务迁移与数据缩放规律，并进行真机实验。

Result: 任务难度经多随机种子、多策略、多 agentic 系统验证可复现；真机实验表明所设计任务在仿真之外仍保持难度排序；迁移与数据缩放行为得到量化分析。

Conclusion: ManiLadder 提供了一个经过难度标定的阶梯式基准，可用于测量、比较和推进机器人操作能力，并为后续沿难度轴的方法研究提供统一评测平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in robotic manipulation have enabled increasingly complex and dexterous behaviors, yet existing benchmarks still focus on simple pick-and-place tasks and lack a systematic framework for measuring progress in robot learning along the axis of task difficulty. We present ManiLadder, a large-scale and organized simulation benchmark for robotic manipulation. ManiLadder contains 112 carefully designed tasks stratified into 4 difficulty levels, with 8 categories in each level, spanning rigid-body and deformable-object manipulation, single-arm and dual-arm settings, and gripper and dexterous-hand end-effectors. To calibrate difficulty, we fix the data amount the same for every task and use a unified flow- matching imitation learning policy under the same training and evaluation protocols, then use its evaluation success rate as an operational empirical difficulty signal. All tasks are iteratively refined until they fall into target difficulty ranges, which are then verified across different random seeds, policies, and agentic learning systems. We further analyze the task properties, compare different policy performances and training settings, analyze task transfer and data scaling behaviors, and conduct real-world experiments showing that the designed tasks preserve their difficulty ordering beyond simulation. ManiLad- der provides a calibrated ladder for measuring, comparing, and advancing robotic manipulation capabilities. More results are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [241] [Remember What Changes the Odds: Critic-Gated Event Memory for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16386)
*Xuanzhuo Liu,Chuankang Li,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CGEM: 用"评论家门控"的事件记忆,仅保留能显著改变成功率预测的事件,分为进度记录与失败记录并在子任务完成后遗忘失败,解决长程操作中的决策别名问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程操作中同一观测在不同任务进度/历史下需采取不同动作(决策别名),策略需具备执行历史记忆;但处理完整观测历史代价高,现有基于感知、语义或运动学线索的压缩事件记忆可能漏掉对任务成败关键的事件。

Method: 提出"结果锚定"原则:只记忆使最终成功率预测发生显著变化的事件。据此构建 CGEM——critic 门控事件记忆:由 critic 估计事件引起的成功率变化,变化大者入选;正变化编码为进度记录,负变化编码为失败记录,失败记录在对应子任务完成后丢弃。

Result: 在可精确计算成功率的十子任务诊断环境中,CGEM 成功率 88.0%;对比 36 步观测窗口与运动学事件记忆均为 0.0%;移除失败记录后成功率降至 1.7%。

Conclusion: 以任务成败概率变化而非感知/语义/运动学显著性作为记忆选择准则,能有效保留决策关键信息;失败记忆对长程任务成功至关重要。结论基于简化诊断环境,尚需真实机器人长程任务验证。

Abstract: In long-horizon manipulation, a robot may need to take different actions under identical observations, depending on task progress and the outcomes of earlier attempts. We refer to this ambiguity as decision aliasing. Resolving it requires the policy to maintain memory of its execution history. To reduce the cost of processing full observation histories, recent methods build compact event memories using perceptual, semantic, or kinematic cues. However, these criteria may miss events that matter for task success. We instead propose an outcome-grounded principle: remember events that substantially change the predicted probability of eventual success. Following this principle, we introduce CGEM, a critic-gated event memory that uses a critic to select events with large estimated changes in success probability. Positive and negative changes are encoded as progress and failure records, respectively. Failure records are discarded once the corresponding subtask is completed. In a ten-subtask diagnostic environment with exactly computed success probabilities, CGEM achieves 88.0% success, compared with 0.0% for both a 36-step observation window and a kinematic event memory. Removing failure records reduces success to 1.7%.

</details>


### [242] [I See What You Want: Decoding Hierarchical Operator Intent from Visual Teleoperation History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16419)
*Xiangyi Jia,Kaixian Qu,Shengzhi Wang,Chenyu Yang,Robert Katzschmann,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化意图预测任务（同时推断原子动作与长期任务），构建VIAMO遥操作数据集（11个机器人来源、6,600条episode），并微调VLM得到Vision-Intention Model（VIM）；VIM的意图预测可驱动VLA策略在任务中途接替人类操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM已广泛用于机器人场景理解与任务规划，但能否在无显式指令的情况下从遥操作历史中解码操作者意图尚不清楚。若能预测意图，可实现人机无缝交接、减少遥操作负担，并为VLA提供高层监督。

Method: 将问题形式化为层次化意图预测：输入短时视觉遥操作历史，联合输出当前原子动作（action）与所服务的总体任务（mission）。构建VIAMO数据集（6,600 episodes、11个机器人来源、13,653条时间对齐动作标注、9,702条mission标注，基于Sentence-BERT相似度评价）。在GPT-4o、GPT-5.6 Luna等闭源大模型基线上，对VLM进行有监督微调得到VIM，并做去除训练标签的泛化测试。

Result: 闭源模型表现较弱（GPT-4o：动作0.563/任务0.470；GPT-5.6 Luna：0.581/0.554）；VIM提升至0.825/0.821，在35.4%训练中缺失标签的测试动作上仍达0.726/0.717。下游实验中，VIM预测的mission使VLA策略可在VIM微调未见过的真实环境中接管任务，相比GPT-4o减少34.6%、相比GPT-5.6 Luna减少39.8%的人工遥操作时间，意图预测延迟平均降低约70%。

Conclusion: 微调后的VLM能有效从遥操作视觉历史中层次化解码人类意图，并可作为下游VLA自主接管的触发与条件信号，在真实环境中显著降低人工遥操作成本与推理延迟；VIAMO为意图理解研究提供了跨机器人平台的数据基础。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have become a powerful foundation for robotic scene understanding, task planning, and language-conditioned control. A natural question follows: can these models also decode what a human operator intends while teleoperating a robot, without any explicit instruction? We formulate this as hierarchical intent prediction, jointly inferring the ongoing atomic action and the broader mission it serves from a short visual teleoperation history. To study this, we introduce VI AMO (Vision Intention Action and Mission Ongoing), a dataset of 6,600 episodes from eleven robot sources with 13,653 temporally aligned action annotations and 9,702 mission annotations. Large proprietary models struggle with this problem: on held-out episodes, GPT-4o reaches Sentence-BERT similarities of only 0.563 and 0.470 for action and mission, and GPT-5.6 Luna achieves only 0.581 and 0.554. Fine-tuning a VLM on VI AMO yields our Vision-Intention Model (VIM), which raises these scores to 0.825 and 0.821 and still reaches 0.726 and 0.717 on the 35.4% of test actions with both labels absent from training. Finally, as a downstream application, VIM's predicted mission enables a vision-language-action (VLA) policy to take over from the operator mid-task in a real-world environment unseen during VIM fine-tuning, reducing human teleoperation time by 34.6% relative to GPT-4o and 39.8% relative to GPT-5.6 Luna, while cutting intent-prediction latency by roughly 70% on average.

</details>


### [243] [BlinkMimic: Training a Humanoid Motion Tracker in Minutes on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16422)
*Fuyi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BlinkMimic：基于世界模型反传的全身运动追踪策略快速训练配方，单卡 RTX 4090 上 3.4 分钟训完 Unitree G1 的 40 条 LAFAN1 动作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身运动跟踪策略通常需要数小时 GPU 训练才能覆盖一个动作库；SuperMimic 与 InstantTrack 已证明"通过可微世界模型反传 + Muon 优化 + 残差策略 + 部署平均策略"可大幅加速，但仍有进一步压缩训练时间的空间。

Method: 在 SuperMimic/InstantTrack 基础上提出三处新组件：(1) 训练进展后衰减的步长；(2) 依据部署条件下部署策略的失败触发定向 reset；(3) 对整个迭代过程做 CUDA Graph 捕获。并将总训练时间分解为"单次迭代成本 / 拟合动作所需迭代数 / 消除残余失败所需迭代数"三项，逐组件归因，并用三随机种子 leave-one-out 消融验证。

Result: 在仿真 Unitree G1 上，三随机种子均值下，单张 RTX 4090、3.4 分钟训练出覆盖全部 40 条 LAFAN1 片段的单一策略（判据为连续三个 checkpoint 归一化回报≥0.82 且无失败片段）；消融显示各新组件分别作用于不同的时间项，并通过梯度、更新规则与失败案例分析解释其机制。

Conclusion: 将训练加速问题显式分解为乘法式时间结构（迭代成本×迭代次数）是有效且可解释的工程范式；该方法面向人形机器人全身跟踪，验证限于仿真，代码与逐种子结果开源。

Abstract: Whole-body motion tracking lets a humanoid robot follow reference motions, but training a tracking policy for a library of motions has usually taken hours of GPU time. SuperMimic showed that the policy can instead be trained by backpropagating through a world model learned from parallel simulation, and InstantTrack added Muon updates, a residual policy around the reference and an averaged deployed policy. We build on both and present BlinkMimic, a recipe that makes this training faster. On a simulated Unitree G1, BlinkMimic trains one policy for all 40 LAFAN1 clips in 3.4 minutes on a single RTX 4090, measured until three consecutive checkpoints reach a normalized return of at least 0.82 with no failed clip (mean of three seeds). We split training time into the cost of an iteration, the iterations needed to fit the motions, and the iterations needed to remove the last failures, and assign each component, inherited or new, to one of these terms. The new components are a step size that decreases once training has progressed, resets directed by failures of the deployed policy under deployment conditions, and GPU graph capture of the whole iteration. A leave-one-out ablation with three seeds per component shows which term each component affects, and further measurements of the gradient, the update rule and the failure cases explain these effects. Code and per-seed results are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [244] [VOKE: Visual Object Kinematics Estimator for Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16423)
*Shengjia Zhu,Wenqiang Xu,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VOKE：以运动学感知的视觉框架，在现成位姿估计器输出上附加可插拔运动学模块（PKM）预测物体线/角速度与加速度，并用运动学引导位姿精化模块（KPRM）以二阶运动先验作参考位姿修正逐帧位姿；在DexYCB、OakInk上跨三种backbone提升位姿精度并显著降低速度/加速度误差，下游以运动学增强强化学习（KARL）训练策略并零微调迁移到真机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作系统的视觉感知输出多为静态掩码、几何或6D位姿，物体动态需由下游控制器从含噪视觉观测中隐式推断，导致控制质量受限。作者希望显式恢复完整物体运动学状态（位姿+线/角速度+线/角加速度），并打通感知到控制的通路。

Method: VOKE框架包含两个感知模块：(1) 可插拔运动学模块PKM，接在标准位姿估计器输出之后，从时序视觉特征回归物体线速度、角速度与加速度；(2) 运动学引导位姿精化模块KPRM，将该运动向量转换为二阶运动先验参考位姿，用以精化逐帧位姿估计。下游用KARL（运动学增强强化学习）将运动学状态输入策略，与位姿历史基线对比。

Result: 在DexYCB和OakInk数据集上，VOKE在三种不同backbone下均一致提升物体位姿估计精度，并相较"位姿差分"方法大幅降低速度与加速度估计误差；仿真中训练的运动学增强策略可零微调直接迁移至真实机器人。

Conclusion: 显式建模物体运动学可有效增强视觉感知并改善下游控制，VOKE以即插即用方式兼容现有位姿估计器，并验证了从感知到真实机器人控制的完整链路，但真机部分仅为应用性验证，缺少定量实机操作性能与速度指标。

Abstract: Vision-based dexterous in-hand manipulation requires knowing not only where an object is, but also how it moves. However, most robotic manipulation systems rely on off-the-shelf visual perception models whose outputs are limited to static object masks, geometry, or 6D poses, leaving downstream controllers to infer object dynamics implicitly from noisy visual observations. We present VOKE, a kinematics-aware perception framework that augments existing object and hand-object pose estimators to recover a complete object kinematic state comprising pose, linear and angular velocity, and linear and angular acceleration. VOKE contains two core perception modules and a downstream application study. First, a Pluggable Kinematics Module (PKM) attaches to the output of a standard pose estimator and predicts the object's linear and angular velocity and acceleration from temporal visual features. Second, a Kinematics-guided Pose Refinement Module (KPRM) converts this motion vector into a second-order, motion-prior reference pose and refines the per-frame pose estimates. We evaluate VOKE on DexYCB and OakInk, where it consistently improves object pose estimation across three different backbones and substantially reduces velocity and acceleration estimation error compared with pose differencing. As a downstream application, we use Kinematics-Augmented Reinforcement Learning (KARL) to compare a kinematics-augmented policy with a pose-history baseline. After training the policies in simulation, we transfer the kinematics-augmented policy to the physical robot without policy fine-tuning, demonstrating the complete perception-to-control pathway in the target domain.

</details>


### [245] [HALO: Human-Aligned Data Collection for WhoLe-body MObile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16439)
*Shuai Yang,Fangyi Xu,Yishu Shen,Kecheng Zheng,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALO 是一个面向全身移动操作的人类对齐数据采集系统：操作者通过动觉示教直接控制机器人双手，并推动低阻力平台实现自然移动，再通过部署对齐的处理（标定与时间对齐平台运动、掩码并修复示教者手部）弥合示教与部署差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动操作示教接口在直接控制、感知-动作对齐与操作者自然移动之间存在权衡，难以高效采集双臂+移动的全身演示数据。

Method: 操作者动觉示教机器人末端执行器，同时推动低阻力平台（不含实际移动底盘）；配套部署对齐的示教处理流程：标定并将平台运动与真实底盘响应做时间对齐，对观测图像中的操作者手部进行掩码与修复（inpainting）。

Result: 相较 Mobile ALOHA 与 VR 遥操作，示教采集速度最高快 2.9 倍，成功率相当或更高；训练策略可完成扎带紧固等精细任务，并在需反复协调底盘与手臂的六阶段长程任务上达到 73.3% 成功率（有真实机器人验证）。

Conclusion: 通过保持人-机器人紧耦合的物理示教接口并对齐部署设置差异，HALO 在保持数据质量的同时显著提升全身移动操作示教效率，验证了"人机对齐采集 + 后处理对齐"范式的有效性。

Abstract: Learning whole-body mobile manipulation requires demonstrations that coordinate bimanual actions with locomotion across extended workspaces. However, existing interfaces trade off direct robot control, perception and action alignment, and natural operator mobility, making such demonstrations difficult to collect. We introduce HALO, a human-aligned data collection system for whole-body mobile manipulation. The system aligns the operator's perception and action space with the robot while preserving direct physical feedback and natural locomotion. Operators guide the robot end-effectors through kinesthetic teaching and move a low-resistance platform without the deployed mobile base while performing bimanual manipulation. This interface enables more natural whole-body demonstrations, but the close human-robot coupling introduces discrepancies between the demonstration and deployment settings. We address these discrepancies with deployment-aligned demonstration processing, which calibrates and temporally aligns platform motion with the deployed base response and masks and inpaints demonstrator hands in recorded observations. Compared with Mobile ALOHA and VR teleoperation, HALO enables up to 2.9x faster demonstration collection while maintaining comparable or higher success rates. Policies trained with HALO perform precise manipulation tasks, including zip-tie fastening, and achieve a 73.3% success rate on a six-stage long-horizon mobile manipulation task involving repeated coordinated base and arm motion. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [246] [RGB-D SLAM on the Edge: Benchmarking and Profiling RTAB-Map and ORB-SLAM3 on the Raspberry Pi 5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16472)
*Himanshu Patil,Jaideep Joshi,Rohit Saluja,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在Raspberry Pi 5上基准测试RTAB-Map与ORB-SLAM3，覆盖6条TUM RGB-D序列×3种分辨率，记录跟踪覆盖率、轨迹误差、单帧耗时、内存、CPU与温度，并做阶段级与函数级剖析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无GPS的微型飞行器常在ARM边缘板卡上跑SLAM，但标准RGB-D SLAM在这类硬件上的公开实测数据极少，缺乏可参照的性能与可靠性基线。

Method: 在Pi 5上对两个系统做系统化benchmark：3种图像分辨率×6条TUM RGB-D序列；逐次记录tracking coverage、ATE类轨迹误差、每帧时间、内存、CPU负载、温度；对两系统做stage级与function级profiling；并将RTAB-Map配置为逐帧处理（后端每帧运行，而非默认1Hz）。

Result: 全配置均可跟踪的序列上，RTAB-Map前端38-74 ms/帧、全流程150-462 ms，ORB-SLAM3为13-45 ms与26-62 ms；RTAB-Map主要开销来自不断增长的位姿图优化（工作内存默认无界），ORB-SLAM3的局部BA约等于其跟踪开销但并行执行。四分之一分辨率下两者在多数序列丢失跟踪；丢跟踪的运行更快结束且保留位姿误差更低，故需与覆盖率联读。计时可重复性1-5%，但轨迹误差同配置间波动达27%，系统间精度差异不具结论性；未出现热降频。

Conclusion: 给出了ARM边缘板上RGB-D SLAM的可复现实测画像与瓶颈定位（无界工作内存的位姿图优化 vs 并行局部BA），并强调覆盖率、时延与精度必须联合报告；两项系统间的精度比较因同配置方差过大而无定论。

Abstract: Micro aerial vehicles that operate without GPS often run SLAM on ARM-based edge boards, yet there is little published data on how standard RGB-D SLAM systems behave on such hardware. We benchmark RTAB-Map and ORB-SLAM3 on the Raspberry Pi 5 using six TUM RGB-D sequences at three image resolutions, recording tracking coverage, trajectory error, time per frame, memory, CPU load and temperature for every run, and we profile both systems at the stage and function level. RTAB-Map was configured to process and keep every frame, so its back end runs on every frame rather than at its default rate of 1 Hz. On the sequences that every configuration tracked, its front end takes 38-74 ms per frame and its full pipeline 150-462 ms, compared with 13-45 ms and 26-62 ms for ORB-SLAM3. RTAB-Map's main cost is optimizing a pose graph that keeps growing, because its working memory is unbounded by default; ORB-SLAM3's local bundle adjustment costs about as much as its tracking but runs in a parallel thread. At quarter resolution, both systems lose tracking on most sequences, and runs that lose tracking finish sooner and report lower error on the poses they keep, so time and accuracy must be read alongside coverage. Timing is repeatable to within 1-5%, but trajectory error varies by up to 27% between identical runs, so the accuracy differences between the two systems are inconclusive. No thermal throttling occurred.

</details>


### [247] [Robots That Talk Too Much and Don't Listen Enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16473)
*Graham Wilcock,Kristiina Jokinen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 复盘社交机器人语音识别与轮流发言（turn-taking）方法的演进，主张用多模态双向流式处理来降低轮次失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音识别进步后，轮流发言失败与其他误解仍是自然口语交互的主要障碍，需要重新审视旧方法并明确当前可行路径。

Method: 综述性短论文（position/short paper），回顾早期用于提升社交机器人语音识别与轮次切换的技术，对比当下可用手段，提出多模态双向流式（multimodal bidirectional streaming）作为核心方案。

Result: 以论述与经验回顾为主，未给出新的量化实验结果或系统评测数据。

Conclusion: 减少轮次失败需要同时利用多模态线索并支持双向流式建模，仅靠改进 ASR 不足以支撑自然人机对话。

Abstract: Until recently, the biggest obstacles to natural spoken interaction with social robots were speech recognition errors and turn-taking failures. Advances in speech recognition have enabled much better spoken interaction, but turn-taking failures and other misunderstandings continue to be far too prevalent. This short paper looks back at earlier methods for improving speech recognition and turn-taking in interaction with social robots, and then looks at what can be done today, arguing that multimodal bidirectional streaming is needed to reduce the frequency of turn-taking failures.

</details>


### [248] [Input-output characterization of robotic grasping: Connecting design parameters to grasp behavior through the admittance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16507)
*Julien Vanderheyden,Guillaume Drion,Pierre Sacré,Fulvio Forni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用小信号分析下的"导纳"传递矩阵刻画抓取动力学，恢复零频刚度/稳定性并捕捉固有频率与峰值增益，在平面二指PD控制夹爪上验证同位置同接触力抓取刚度可差两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低阻抗夹爪使抓取动力学变得重要，但现有抓取指标是静态的，只描述物体对恒定载荷的抗力，无法反映对时变载荷的响应。

Method: 基于小信号分析，将抓取在平衡点附近线性化为导纳传递矩阵（载荷扰动到物体位移的映射）；在平面二指夹爪+PD控制下计算该矩阵，并与非线性仿真对比验证。

Result: 同一物体、同一位置、同一接触力的两个抓取刚度可相差两个数量级；刚度相同的抓取也可表现出显著不同的共振特性；非线性仿真证实小信号分析能有效刻画抓取动态行为。

Conclusion: 导纳指标补充了静态抓取指标的不足，可作为低阻抗抓取的动力学评价与设计工具。

Abstract: Low-impedance grippers make the dynamics of a grasp matter. Current grasp metrics, however, are static: they describe how a held object resists constant loads, not how it responds to time-varying ones. To quantify this, we rely on small-signal analysis to characterize a grasp by its admittance, the transfer matrix relating load perturbations to object displacements around an equilibrium. This transfer matrix recovers the stiffness and stability of the grasp at zero frequency and captures its natural frequencies and peak gain under time-varying loads. We apply this characterization to a planar two-finger gripper under proportional-derivative control. We show that two grasps of the same object in the same position and with the same contact forces can differ in stiffness by two orders of magnitude. Likewise, two grasps that share the same stiffness can exhibit very different resonances. Nonlinear simulations confirm the effectiveness of our small-signal analysis in capturing the dynamic behavior of grasping.

</details>


### [249] [STVLA: Spatial-aware and Temporal-memory forVision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16589)
*Zhuo Han,Yeqiang Wang,Xinran Wang,Hongwei Han,Yi Xiong,Zunian Wan,Jianbo Mao,Huan Peng,Yongcheng Liu,Wei Shan,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STVLA 将预训练视觉几何基础模型的特征引入时序记忆，与外观特征共同构成记忆库，使 VLA 策略在动作决策时可回看过去的场景几何结构；并用带理论依据的打分规则在"当前观测相关性"与"记忆互补性"之间权衡来选取历史片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略多依赖当前观测，缺乏显式空间结构与任务历史的整合：纯外观记忆对遮挡与视角变化敏感，而仅用当前场景的空间表征又无法保留执行过程中场景如何演化。

Method: 以预训练视觉几何基础模型（如深度/点图类几何特征）提取空间表示，并与视觉外观特征一并写入时序记忆；设计数学上可解释的打分函数，综合"与当前观测的相关性"和"记忆提供的互补证据"来检索信息量最大的历史帧，作为动作决策上下文输入策略。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Mem、MIKASA-Robo、SimplerEnv Bridge 四个基准上均取得对比方法中最高的平均成功率；真机积木堆叠、物品整理、手机包装任务分别较 π0.5 提升 58.33、30.00、35.00 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将场景几何显式融入时序记忆，对同时需要空间精度与任务历史的操作任务有效，且能显著超越强基线 π0.5，具备真机可迁移性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires both spatial scene understanding and task history. However, many vision-language-action (VLA) policies rely primarily on current observations, without explicitly integrating spatial structure and task history. Appearance-based memories can be sensitive to occlusion and viewpoint changes, while spatial representations of the current scene alone do not preserve how the scene has changed during task execution. We introduce STVLA, a framework that incorporates features from a pretrained visual geometry foundation model into temporal memory alongside visual representations. This allows the policy to recall past scene structure as context for current action decisions. To select informative history, STVLA uses a mathematically grounded scoring rule that balances relevance to the current observation with complementary evidence from memory. Across evaluations on LIBERO, LIBERO-Mem, MIKASA-Robo, and SimplerEnv Bridge, STVLA achieves the highest average success rate among the compared methods on each benchmark. On real-world block stacking, object organization, and phone packaging, it improves success over $\pi_{0.5}$ by $58.33$, $30.00$, and $35.00$ percentage points, respectively. These results support integrating scene geometry into temporal memory to improve manipulation requiring both spatial precision and task history. Project website: \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }

</details>


### [250] [Reins: Frontier Multimodal Models Driving Real Robot Arms on Long-Horizon Tasks, Zero-Shot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16632)
*Pengfei Ye,Zhiyang Dou,Yuxiang Ma,Haonan Chen,Heng Yang,Edward H. Adelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Reins是一个"harness"框架：让现成前沿大模型通过3D感知工具+带接触/夹持反馈的原子动作，驱动双臂机器人完成长时序、富接触操作；动作链带前置条件、首个失败即停，另设"narrator"实例持续看摄像头报告变化；并从图像构建可仿真数字孪生用于持续策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型虽能规划与调用工具，但难以驱动真实机器人执行长时序、富接触操作，尤其机械臂运动/模型思考期间处于"盲态"，缺乏闭环感知与状态反馈。

Method: Reins harness：off-the-shelf 模型 + 3D 感知工具 + 原子动作（返回接触与夹持信号）；单次调用内串联带守卫条件的动作序列，首条件失败即中止；第二个模型实例作为 narrator 持续监控相机、逐结果报告场景变化；由图像重建场景仿真孪生以支持持续策略学习；全程自然语言提示、无任务专用代码。

Result: GPT-6.1 Sol 在五个任务的真实 episodes 上成功率 88%；无 harness 时所有 shell game 全部失败且耗时约 8 倍；三种前沿模型表现相当，但在"执行期间场景变化"的跟踪上存在差异。

Conclusion: 结构化交互封装（条件-动作链+持续叙述监控+仿真孪生）比单纯更强的模型更关键，可将通用前沿模型零样本转化为可用的双臂操作者；同时暴露了思考期间盲视下的状态跟踪是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Frontier models such as GPT-6.1 Sol, GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5.5 can now plan and call tools, yet they still struggle to drive real robots through long-horizon, contact-rich manipulation, blind while the arm moves and while they think. We present Reins, a harness in which an off-the-shelf model operates a dual-arm robot with 3D perception tools and atomic actions that report contact and grip. The model chains actions in one call, each with a condition that must hold, stopping at the first that fails; a second instance, the narrator, watches the cameras continuously and reports what changed with every result. From images, Reins also builds a simulation-ready twin of the scene for continual policy learning. With natural-language prompts and no task-specific code, GPT-6.1 Sol succeeds in 88% of real episodes on five tasks; without the harness, it fails every shell game, taking 8 times as long. Three frontier models perform alike but differ in tracking a scene that changes while the actor thinks. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [251] [Rethinking Actions: Continuity-Aware Priors for World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16645)
*Shaobo Yang,Zichen Ai,Ziyang Ma,Qian Yang,Bin Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LG-DKGP：用局部性门控的深度核高斯过程先验，从专家动作的时间结构学习动作延续提议，并冻结世界模型评估进展，在五个仿真任务上取得 87.07% 宏观成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型规划常以独立或通用平滑的方式采样动作提议，忽略了专家演示中显著的时间连续性结构（有序 vs 打乱分析已验证该依赖存在）。作者希望用演示动作的时间结构（而非通用平滑正则）来塑造动作提议分布，从而提升规划效率与成功率。

Method: 1) 提案分布建模：LG-DKGP 结合近期执行动作历史与更长程历史，用神经网络预测"延续动作"均值，再在其上学习时间高斯过程残差；2) 局部性门控：根据历史相关性调制核的局部范围；3) 训练流程为两阶段：先拟合神经均值，再联合优化先验与 GP 残差（以专家未来动作为监督）；4) 规划时用训练统计导出的探索尺度在修正均值周围初始化搜索，并由冻结世界模型评估目标进展；5) 通过组件消融与"相对于专家的动作结构"对比进行分析。

Result: 五个仿真任务（原生目标与片段级目标达成）上宏平均成功率 87.07%，对比 INTACT 71.03%、PRISM 61.66%、LeWM 58.66%；组件干预与专家相对动作结构对比支持"演示支持的延续"有效，并揭示视觉条件、历史长度与规划视界存在任务依赖效应。

Conclusion: 将专家演示的时间结构显式编码进动作先验（神经均值 + GP 残差），可显著提升冻结世界模型下的视觉规划成功率，且增益来源可归因于延续提议的合理性而非通用平滑；效果依赖任务，需按任务调节视觉条件、历史长度与规划视界。

Abstract: Actions in expert demonstrations exhibit temporal structure. An ordered-versus-shuffled analysis across five environments confirms this dependence. We ask whether action proposals that better reflect expert continuity can improve visual world-model planning. Rather than imposing generic smoothness, we learn continuations from the temporal structure of demonstrated actions. Our locality-gated deep-kernel Gaussian-process prior, LG-DKGP, combines recent and longer-range executed action history, predicts a neural continuation, and learns a temporal Gaussian-process (GP) residual. A training-derived exploration scale initializes search around the corrected mean; a frozen world model evaluates progress toward the goal. Training first fits the neural mean, then jointly optimizes the prior with the GP residual using expert future actions. The resulting planner achieves 87.07\% macro success across five simulated tasks spanning native and fragment-level goal attainment, compared with 71.03\% for INTACT, 61.66\% for PRISM, and 58.66\% for LeWM. Component interventions and expert-relative action-structure comparisons support demonstration-informed continuations and reveal task-dependent effects of visual conditioning, history length, and planning horizon.

</details>


### [252] [NSDL: Neuro-Symbolic Division of Labor for Multi-Robot Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16671)
*Xincheng Tan,Ke Wang,Yining Di,Minfeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NSDL 提出"神经-符号分工"范式：小规模视觉语言模型只负责从场景图中判断尚需完成的目标与任务分解（输出 plan sketch），确定性求解器负责展开前置动作、机器人交接与时序，二者通过工具调用与 solver-in-the-loop 训练结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人团队从单张场景图和自然语言指令规划时，包含两类异质决策：需从视觉判断的（目标是否已达成、任务如何拆分、哪个机器人做哪步）与可由此推导计算的（前置条件、交接、时序）。端到端大模型把两类混在一起，导致结构性的顺序与调度错误。

Method: 训练一个小型 VLM 作为"判断器"，将判断写成 plan sketch（残留目标列表 + 有序步骤及各步骤指派的机器人）；模型调用两个工具——检测器（提供证据）与符号求解器；求解器把 sketch 扩展为可执行的联合计划；训练时采用 solver-in-the-loop。另构建 LOCUS-Bench，将两类难度分开评分，并在 VIKI-L2 上评测。

Result: 在 VIKI-L2 与 LOCUS-Bench 上，NSDL 优于端到端规划器与"先形式化再规划"基线，跨 3B–8B 三种骨干均成立；7B 模型在 VIKI-L2 分布外准确率达 97.0%。引入求解器生成结构后，顺序与调度错误几近消失，剩余失败主要为状态误判，漏掉的目标多为模型未列出的目标。

Conclusion: 将"需判断"与"可计算"的决策分离并由各自适配的组件承担，能显著提升异构多机器人任务规划的可靠性；瓶颈从结构推理转移到视觉状态判定与目标召回。代码、权重与基准已开源。

Abstract: Planning for a heterogeneous robot team from a scene image and an instruction involves two kinds of decisions. Some must be judged from the scene: which goals are already met, how the task splits, and which robot does what. Others follow from these and can be computed: prerequisite actions, handoffs between robots, and timing. We argue that each kind should go to the component suited to it: a small vision-language model learns the first, and a deterministic solver computes the second. In NSDL (Neuro-Symbolic Division of Labor), the trained model writes its judgments as a plan sketch, which lists the goals that still need action and the intended steps in order, each with a robot. The solver expands the sketch into an executable joint plan. The model calls two tools, a detector for evidence and the solver, and is trained with the solver in the loop. We evaluate on the public VIKI-L2 benchmark and on LOCUS-Bench, a new benchmark that grades the two kinds of difficulty separately. NSDL outperforms end-to-end planners and formalize-then-plan baselines on both benchmarks and with three backbones from 3B to 8B; the 7B model reaches 97.0% out-of-distribution accuracy on VIKI-L2. Ordering and scheduling errors nearly disappear once the solver writes the structure; the remaining failures are misjudged states, and most goals the model misses are ones it never lists. Code, model weights, and LOCUS-Bench are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [253] [Safe and Efficient Navigation Considering Texting Pedestrians via Attention-Aware Cost-map Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16725)
*Shunya Tadano,Yusuke Tamura,Ankit A. Ravankar,Yasuhisa Hirata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出注意力感知导航框架：用3D骨架特征+随机森林实时判别行人是否分心，并动态放大分心行人的代价地图安全裕度，实现主动避让；12人252试次用户研究显示平滑性与可预测性显著提升且无额外耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航方法将所有行人视为同质对象，忽略其注意力状态，导致对"低头玩手机"行人无法可靠预判让行行为，存在安全风险。

Method: 基于3D骨架特征与随机森林分类器实时判定行人attentive/distracted；对分心个体动态扩大代价地图安全边距，驱动更早、更保守的绕行规划；与LiDAR-only基线及纯轨迹预测方法在组内被试研究中对比。

Result: 252次试验：相比两个基线，感知平滑度(p=0.003)与可预测性(p=0.034)显著提升，行驶时间无增加；单独轨迹预测若不结合注意力状态代价调整，则不改善主观导航质量。

Conclusion: "判断前方是谁"比单纯轨迹平滑度更关键；注意力状态感知是社交导航中有效且低成本的先验。局限在于样本规模小(12人)、分类器为二值、未报告真实开放环境长期部署与消融细节。

Abstract: Smartphone-distracted pedestrians deviate from social norms unpredictably and cannot be relied upon to yield to approaching robots -- a safety challenge that existing navigation methods are poorly equipped to handle, since they treat all pedestrians uniformly regardless of attention state. We propose an attention-aware navigation framework that classifies each pedestrian as attentive or distracted in real-time using 3D skeletal features and a Random Forest classifier, then dynamically widens cost-map safety margins around distracted individuals to drive proactive avoidance. A within-subjects user study with 12 participants across 252 trials demonstrates significant improvements in perceived smoothness (p = 0.003) and predictability (p = 0.034) over both a LiDAR-only baseline and a trajectory-prediction-only method, without any increase in travel time. Notably, trajectory prediction alone does not improve subjective navigation quality unless coupled with attention-state-aware cost adjustment -- confirming that appropriately responding to who is in front of the robot can matter more than trajectory smoothness alone.

</details>


### [254] [EgoX: Egocentric Cross-Embodiment Manipulation with Embodiment Dreaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16728)
*Yaru Niu,Binghong Chen,Changwei Yao,Shangtao Li,Zhenlong Fang,Revanth Krishna Senthilkumaran,Shuai Zhou,Yuemin Mao,Kalie Ching,Po-Ju Chao,Yudong Luo,Peiqi Yu,Heng Zhang,Hao Zhang,Bingqing Chen,Chen Qiu,H. Eric Tseng,Ye Yuan,Umar Iqbal,Changliu Liu,Jonathan Francis,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoX：以语义对应的观测/动作模态组织人类、真机、仿真异构示范，预训练模块化跨具身Transformer MXT+（每模态专用tokenizer、每动作模态专用专家、共享Transformer主干、仅训练期的"具身 dreaming"预测未来视觉/本体潜表征），再在目标具身上微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用操作策略可利用人类、真机与仿真智能体的互补经验，但形态、传感与控制接口的差异使联合示范难以有效学习。

Method: 基于语义对应的观测与动作模态组织异构示范；MXT+ 为各观测模态配置专用 tokenizer、各动作模态配置专用专家，Transformer 主干跨具身共享；引入训练期具身 dreaming 目标，预测未来视觉与本体状态的潜表征以促使共享主干建模各具身动态；随后在目标具身示范上微调。

Result: 在覆盖5个任务、6种具身（5台真机+1个仿真人形）的13个任务-具身组合上，跨具身预训练使 MXT+ 平均归一化任务进度从0.63提升至0.80；分布外条件下从0.53提升至0.77。消融显示更广预训练混合、模块化接口与具身 dreaming 均有收益，dreaming 使不同具身的潜空间几何更接近。

Conclusion: 通过模态级模块化接口与具身 dreaming 潜空间预测，可有效融合人类/真机/仿真异构示范，显著提升目标具身策略的分布内与分布外表现。

Abstract: Generalist manipulation policies can benefit from the complementary experience of humans, real robots, and simulated agents. However, differences in morphology, sensing, and control interfaces make it difficult to learn effectively from their combined demonstrations. We introduce EgoX, an egocentric cross-embodiment learning framework that organizes heterogeneous demonstrations through semantically corresponding observation and action modalities. EgoX pretrains a Modularized Cross-Embodiment Transformer with Embodiment Dreaming (MXT+) on this mixture and fine-tunes it on target-embodiment demonstrations. Within each embodiment, MXT+ assigns a dedicated tokenizer to each observation modality and a dedicated expert to each action modality, with a Transformer trunk shared across embodiments. A training-only embodiment-dreaming objective predicts latent representations of future visual and proprioceptive states, encouraging the shared trunk to model each embodiment's dynamics. Across 13 task-embodiment pairs spanning five tasks and six robot embodiments, including five physical robots and one simulated humanoid, cross-embodiment pretraining raises MXT+'s average normalized task progress from 0.63 to 0.80 over training from scratch. The gains are particularly pronounced under out-of-distribution conditions, where average task progress increases from 0.53 to 0.77. Ablations demonstrate the benefits of broader pretraining mixtures, modular interfaces, and embodiment dreaming, with dreaming producing more similar latent geometry across embodiments. Project page: this http URL .

</details>


### [255] [AgriSense: Vision-Language Decision Support and Robotic Intervention in a Virtual Greenhouse Copy title](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16752)
*Lyes Saad Saoud,Oualid Doukhi,Reza Ghorbani,Moussa Ayyash*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgriSense是一个面向虚拟温室的集成框架，结合Isaac Sim、四足移动操作臂、RGB/LiDAR-惯性感知、环境调控与监督式视觉-语言决策支持，并通过消融揭示多模态对区域决策无增量贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主温室管理需要机器人系统同时具备感知、导航、环境调控与植株级干预能力，而现有工作缺乏统一集成与可评估的决策支持框架，且需明确各模态与环境输入的贡献边界。

Method: 构建Isaac Sim虚拟温室与四足移动操作臂平台，采用RGB与LiDAR-惯性感知、环境控制模块；使用冻结的DINOv2与BERT编码器加结构化环境输入，联合预测12项环境参考值、目标区域与干预标志；进行checkpoint回放、五种子源分组测试集上的模态消融、离线Point-LIO轨迹评估、40个虚拟臂目标几何验收及6个长时序气候场景与故障协议测试。

Result: checkpoint回放复现41/43区域决策；全模型、去图像、去语言三配置各达43/44正确区域决策，多模态无增量证据；全模型MAE为温度0.249°C、湿度2.030个百分点、土壤湿度0.997个百分点；仅传感器输入与全模型区域精度相同，仅语言输入为68.64%；真实图像基准显示分布偏移限制；Point-LIO轨迹刚性对齐误差5.22–17.32 mm；40个虚拟臂目标中32个通过几何验收、8个在边界被拒。

Conclusion: 本文贡献为一个经评估的虚拟温室决策支持与机器人干预框架；导航与集成处理依赖仿真器状态输入，物理温室性能与农艺干预效果尚未验证，模态增益缺乏证据。

Abstract: Autonomous greenhouse management increasingly requires robotic systems that integrate perception, navigation, environmental regulation, and plant-level intervention. This paper presents AgriSense, a framework combining an Isaac Sim virtual greenhouse, a quadruped mobile manipulator, RGB and LiDAR-inertial sensing, environmental control, and supervised vision-language decision support. Frozen DINOv2 and BERT encoders, together with structured environmental inputs, support joint prediction of twelve environmental references, a target zone, and an intervention flag. Checkpoint replay reproduces 41/43 zone decisions. Across five seeds on a source-grouped test set, the full, image-removed, and language-removed configurations each achieve 43/44 correct zone decisions, providing no evidence of incremental multimodal zone-accuracy improvement. The full model achieves mean absolute errors of 0.249 degrees C for temperature, 2.030 percentage points for humidity, and 0.997 percentage points for soil moisture. Sensor-only inputs match full-model zone accuracy, while language-only inputs achieve 68.64%. External real-image benchmarks reveal distribution-shift limitations. Five offline Point-LIO routes yield rigidly aligned trajectory errors of 5.22-17.32 mm. Of 40 virtual-arm targets, 32 satisfy geometric acceptance criteria and eight are rejected at boundaries. Six long-horizon climate cases and fault protocols characterize integration limits. Navigation and integrated treatment rely on simulator-state inputs; physical greenhouse performance and agronomic treatment efficacy remain unvalidated. The contribution is an evaluated decision-support and robotic-intervention framework for a virtual greenhouse.

</details>


### [256] [ACE-CaP-0: Real-World Code-as-Policies in the Era of Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16872)
*ACE-CaP Team,Changxu Wei,Yunpeng Qing,Guozheng Ma,Bowen Liu,Zexin He,Yilun Kong,Haoming Gu,Jingyuan Liu,Tianyu Zhu,Jianfei Wang,Junqiang Kou,Huinan Mo,Hao Lv,Pengbin Li,Yuesong Wang,Bo Li,Huan Zhou,Shunyu Liu,Chunxiao Liu,Bixuan Jin,Shaopeng Yin,Fei Wang,Zhi Hou,Jiaxing Huang,Changqing Zou,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACE-CaP-0：以“按需示范+按需仿真”为经验来源的智能体式Code-as-Policies配方，冻结代码策略在8个真实操作任务上达92.1%成功率，单任务平均执行0.97–2.27分钟，运行期无需人类或编码智能体介入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型驱动的智能体机器人控制需在运行时反复调用模型，显著拉长任务完成时间。Code as Policies 可将推理投入固化为可复用程序，但如何低成本地构建可靠代码策略、并减少真实世界试错代价仍是核心难题。

Method: 研究人类指导作为可行动作先验、仿真作为候选策略探索与测试的辅助环境两种经验来源；结论是“按需机器人示范”优于持续仿真，“仿真按需选择性使用”用于真实策略精炼。据此提出 ACE-CaP-0 配方：将策略修订与经验获取耦合，通过 Demonstrations on Request 与 Simulation on Demand 迭代改进代码策略，最终冻结程序，运行期仅依赖实时感知与控制。

Result: 8个真实操作任务（对齐、多物体操作、双臂协调），冷冻代码策略成功率92.1%（221/240），平均执行时间0.97–2.27分钟，运行期无人类或编码智能体在环。

Conclusion: 按需示范+按需仿真的智能体式代码策略能将以推理形式投入的经验转化为可靠、可复用、执行高效的物理技能，展示了CaP在机器人技能固化方面的潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in frontier models have pushed the boundaries of agentic robot control, enabling agents to perform complex robotic tasks through reasoning and interaction. However, repeated calls to these models during execution can substantially increase task completion time. Code as Policies (CaP) offers a route for coding agents to acquire physical skills through experience and retain them in executable programs, allowing the reasoning invested in development to be reused across runs. Developing reliable code policies while limiting the cost of physical experimentation remains a central challenge. We investigate human guidance as a source of priors for feasible actions and simulation as an auxiliary environment for exploring and testing candidate policies. Our findings favor robot demonstrations available on request and simulation used selectively during real-world policy refinement. These findings inform ACE-CaP-0, an agentic recipe that couples policy revision with experience acquisition through Demonstrations on Request and Simulation on Demand. Across eight real-world manipulation tasks spanning alignment, multi-object handling, and bimanual coordination, the resulting frozen programs achieve 92.1\% success (221/240 trials). Mean execution times across tasks range from 0.97 to 2.27 minutes. The programs execute with runtime perception and control, without a human or coding agent in the loop. These results highlight CaP's potential to turn experience into reliable, reusable physical skills.

</details>


### [257] [Informative Path Planning with Gaussian Processes: Error Bounds, Algorithms, and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16877)
*Kalvik Jakkala,Jennifer Wakulicz,Donald G. Dansereau,Srinivas Akella,Lantao Liu,Jason M. O'Kane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文对GP-based Informative Path Planning (IPP) 的误差界与算法进行严谨理论分析：论证预测方差是理论上最优的优化目标，并证明GreedyCover的近似保证达到该问题的近似硬度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IPP方法依赖启发式运行约束，且缺乏对最坏情况估计误差与近似质量的同时理论保证；作者不做新启发式方法，而是厘清既有误差界与算法的数学基础。

Method: 比较概率性与确定性重构误差界，分析预测方差作为GP-IPP目标的理论合理性；对GreedyCover（优化预测方差的贪心方法）进行近似保证分析，并与问题近似硬度极限对照；辅以在多个信息论目标与优化方法上的基准对比实验。

Result: 理论上建立了预测方差为最优目标、GreedyCover近似保证匹配问题硬度下界的结论；实验中在估计精度与计算效率上优于主流信息论目标与优化方法。

Conclusion: 为有界不确定性约束下的离散离线IPP提供了严谨且近最优的理论基础，并为连续空间与在线形式化问题提供原则性指导。属于理论/方法论贡献，缺少机器人实机或大规模部署证据。

Abstract: Accurate environmental monitoring relies on Informative Path Planning (IPP) to deploy autonomous systems that maximize information gain. While Gaussian Processes (GPs) provide a robust mathematical foundation for modeling the environment, contemporary IPP approaches remain hindered by heuristic operational constraints and the lack of simultaneous theoretical guarantees for worst-case estimation error and approximation quality. Rather than introducing yet another heuristic, this paper presents a rigorous theoretical analysis of existing error bounds and algorithms to further develop the mathematical foundation for IPP. By evaluating probabilistic and deterministic bounds on reconstruction error, we establish that predictive variance is the theoretically ideal optimization objective for GP-based IPP. Furthermore, we analyze GreedyCover, a recent approach for optimizing predictive variance, and demonstrate that its near-optimal approximation guarantee matches the fundamental hardness-of-approximation limit for the problem. Comprehensive benchmarking confirms that this approach delivers superior estimation accuracy and computational efficiency compared to prominent information-theoretic objectives and optimization methods used in IPP. Finally, this theoretical analysis establishes a rigorous, near-optimal foundation for discrete offline IPP under bounded uncertainty constraints, offering clear and principled guidance for future work in continuous-space and online formulations.

</details>


### [258] [CT-SGAL-LIO: Causal Continuous Time Refinement of Discrete LiDAR Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16918)
*Nam Tran,Thu Tran,Toan Nguyen,William J Beksi,Tuan Dang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CT-SGAL-LIO 是一个即插即用的连续时间轨迹精化模块，在离散 SGal(3) LIO 估计器的后验位姿流上拟合三次曲线并做固定小滞后的插值，从而平滑位姿振荡而不改变原估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LIO 方法全局精度高，但位姿流在传播与校正间交替，短时窗内出现振荡；已有改进要么改滤波器加快更新，要么从原始 LiDAR/惯性数据做连续时间轨迹估计（需额外优化、延迟更新、快速旋转/加速时鲁棒性差）。

Method: 不改动原 SGal(3) 估计器，直接在其投影后的后验位姿上做连续时间精化：在有限窗口的近期位姿上拟合三次轨迹（cubic spline/多项式），并在小的固定时滞处求值，使查询时刻落在已观测区间内，从而以插值而非外推获得精化位姿，同时保持因果性。

Result: 在 GRAND-TOUR、MCD、GEODE 三个公开基准共 17 个序列上提升相对轨迹精度并基本保持全局精度：GRAND-TOUR 上 RTE 最多提升 70%，GEODE 全部 7 个序列提升 9.9–51.1%；19.5 分钟的 R-Campus 实跑中后验跳变减少 85.4%，而首尾位姿差仅变化 4.7 mm。

Conclusion: 轻量、因果、模块化的后处理式连续时间精化能在不牺牲全局精度的前提下显著改善 LIO 位姿流的短时平滑性与相对精度，代码与结果将开源。

Abstract: State-of-the-art LiDAR-inertial odometry methods, including those on richer state manifolds, achieve strong global accuracy, yet their pose stream alternates between propagation and correction and can oscillate over short horizons even when globally accurate. Most existing methods follow these approaches: they either modify the filter to enable faster updates or estimate a continuous-time trajectory from raw LiDAR and inertial data. The latter involves extra optimization, delaying state updates and becoming less robust during rapid rotations or accelerations. We propose CT-SGAL-LIO, a continuous-time refinement module that smooths the posterior pose stream of a discrete SGal(3) estimator without modifying the estimator itself. The key novelty is to perform continuous-time trajectory refinement directly on the projected SGal(3) posterior: we fit a cubic trajectory over a bounded window of recent poses and evaluate it at a small fixed lag. This places the query time within the observed interval, so the refined pose is obtained by interpolation rather than extrapolation, while preserving causal operation. CT-SGAL-LIO improves relative trajectory accuracy on 17 sequences from three public benchmarks. Across 17 sequences from GRAND-TOUR, MCD, and GEODE, CT-SGAL-LIO improves short-term motion accuracy while largely preserving global accuracy. RTE improves by up to 70% on GRAND-TOUR and on all seven GEODE sequences, with 9.9-51.1% reductions. A 19.5-min R-Campus run also reduces posterior jumps by 85.4% with only a 4.7 mm change in the end-to-start gap. We plan to release our source code and results to support community validation and reproducibility.

</details>


### [259] [Maximizing LLM Utility While Managing LLM Fallibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17020)
*Ekta U. Samani,Christopher G. Atkeson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 综述：将LLM视为不可靠证据源，用决策论、估计与在线学习的经典方法动态调节系统对LLM的依赖度，以在最大化其效用同时抑制其错误影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能为自主系统提供规划、预测与判断，但输出有时错误；系统需要在利用LLM能力与管理其不可靠性之间取得平衡。

Method: 借鉴由不完美部件（噪声传感器、故障软件）构成的传统系统的鲁棒设计思路：依据LLM在系统中的角色与贡献类型，估计其可靠性（以性能证据累积为依据），再用决策论、估计理论与在线学习的方法把可靠性估计转化为对LLM依赖方式的调整决策；对罕见但严重的错误，引入独立验证机制加以阻断。

Result: 依赖度自适应可降低LLM错误对系统的影响频率；独立验证机制可防止关键错误被执行；无验证机制时，基于可靠性估计的依赖自适应仍能将风险控制在有界范围内。

Conclusion: 现有自适应依赖研究仍处早期且数量有限，但综述中的比较表明自适应依赖能提升系统性能；该框架为LLM集成系统提供了可落地的可靠性管理范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can supply autonomous systems with plans, predictions, and judgments, but these contributions are sometimes wrong. This review asks how a system can maximize an LLM's utility while managing its fallibility. Our answer is to adapt the system's reliance on the LLM as evidence about the LLM's performance accumulates. Systems built from imperfect components, such as noisy sensors and faulty software, have long worked in this way, and the classical methods they use can be applied to LLM-based systems as well. A system can change its reliance on an LLM in several ways, depending on the LLM's role and on the other components in the system. Each change needs an estimate of the LLM's reliability, and the LLM's role and the kind of contribution it makes determine what to estimate. Classical methods from decision theory, estimation, and online learning then turn these estimates into reliance decisions. These decisions can reduce how often LLM errors affect the system. However, some errors matter even when they are rare. An independently verified mechanism can prevent such errors from being executed. Without one, adapting reliance using reliability estimates can still keep their risk within a bound. Studies on adapting reliance on LLMs are emerging but limited, and the reviewed comparisons suggest that adapting reliance can improve system performance.

</details>


### [260] [SALA: Semantically Aligned Latent Actions Enable Dual-Axis Scaling of Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17110)
*Jiachun Jin,Zetong Zhou,Haonan Wang,Xiao Yang,Jun Zhu,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SALA 通过语言描述计算人类视频与机器人片段的语义相似度，构建软邻域结构来监督跨本体潜在动作对应，得到共享潜在动作空间，从而在无配对数据下用人类视频大规模预训练具身基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在动作模型（LAM）仅靠视频重建学习潜动作，表征易按视觉外观而非底层动作聚类，导致人类动作难以对齐到机器人对应动作；同时人类视频与机器人数据缺乏配对，跨本体监督信号缺失。

Method: 无需配对数据：用非配对人类片段与机器人片段的语言描述估计语义相似度，转化为软邻域结构，引导人类潜动作向语义相关的机器人潜动作靠拢；同时以机器人动作重建将潜空间锚定在控制上，形成共享潜动作空间作为统一监督信号训练具身基础模型。

Result: 潜动作监督支持双轴扩展：预训练阶段，增加通用人类视频量可持续降低下游任务的机器人动作预测误差；后训练阶段，增加任务相关人类视频量显著提升真实机器人任务成功率。

Conclusion: 语义对齐的潜在动作空间为人类与机器人视频提供了统一监督，验证了跨本体人类视频在预训练与后训练两阶段的规模化价值，是一种低成本扩充机器人经验的有效途径。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models hinge on diverse robot interaction data, which are costly to collect. Egocentric human videos offer a complementary source of experience, and latent action models (LAMs) provide a popular means of leveraging these videos for policy supervision. Compared with world-action models (WAMs), LAMs can offer greater training efficiency and potentially lower inference costs by avoiding explicit pixel prediction during policy learning and execution. However, most existing LAMs learn latent actions solely through video reconstruction, which can cause their representations to cluster by visual appearance rather than by the underlying actions. As a result, they struggle to align human actions with their robot counterparts. To bridge the gap, we introduce SALA, which emphasizes establishing the semantic correspondence for latent actions. Without requiring paired data, we use the language descriptions of unpaired human and robot clips to estimate semantic similarities and supervise cross-embodiment correspondence. Specifically, we translate these similarities into soft neighborhood structures that guide human latent actions toward semantically related robot latents, while robot action reconstruction grounds the latent space in control. The resulting shared latent action space provides a unified supervision signal for training an embodied foundation model on both human and robot videos. Notably, latent action supervision enables scaling along two axes: (1) pre-training on increasing amounts of general human video consistently reduces robot action prediction error on downstream tasks, and (2) post-training on increasing amounts of task-specific human video substantially improves real-world robot task success rates.

</details>


### [261] [FORGE: Force-Aware Multi-Granularity Control for Efficient Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17142)
*Liangjun Xing,Ke Fan,Kailin Li,Bin Wang,Xiaojie Niu,Qingwei Ben,Jiafei Cao,Wei Yao,Yebin Liu,Jingbo Wang,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FORGE：面向人形机器人全身loco-manipulation的力感知多粒度遥操作控制系统，仅需稀疏双腕相对骨盆位姿与粗粒度指令即可部署，并通过动量观测器+学习式校正实现无腕部力/力矩传感器的低频腕等效外力估计与准静态力自适应闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在频繁接触下的loco-manipulation遥操作演示采集困难：控制器需同时满足精度、稳定性与接触感知。现有全身遥操方案依赖完整人体运动轨迹或关节角参考，部署门槛高，且缺乏力觉反馈通道以支持持续接触任务（如挤压、抓取搬运）。

Method: 提出粗到细的指令空间：速度导航→平面足端落点→腕部位姿跟踪→可选力条件输入。策略训练无需全身人体运动轨迹或关节角参考，部署仅需相对骨盆的稀疏双腕位姿、粗运动指令和可选的期望腕负载。持续接触方面，采用动量观测器加学习式校正（仿真接触力监督训练）；部署时由本体感受与重建的低层手臂PD力矩估计低频腕等效交互力，无需腕部F/T传感器。准静态虚拟目标环利用该估计与期望施加力输入在线调整腕目标；冻结的运动先验结合非对称残差动作稳定操作引起的全身修正。实验评估统一控制稳定性、任务空间跟踪、准静态腕等效力估计、刚性箱持续接触挤压力，以及跨负载条件下扩散策略执行蹲举任务。

Result: 摘要未给出定量指标，但实验覆盖五个维度：统一控制稳定性、任务空间跟踪精度、准静态腕等效外力估计精度、刚性箱持续接触中的挤压力保持，以及下游扩散策略在多种负载条件下执行蹲举-举升任务的表现，构成从控制层到策略层的完整验证链。

Conclusion: FORGE 以低成本传感（无腕部F/T）与稀疏输入实现了人形机器人力感知全身遥操作，并将力条件化接口下沉为策略的通用输入，可支撑接触丰富任务的演示数据采集与下游模仿学习；后续值得关注真机定量指标与跨任务泛化性。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires coordinated locomotion, balance regulation, and manipulation under frequent contacts, making teleoperated demonstration collection difficult: a practical controller must simultaneously provide precision, stability, and contact awareness. We propose FORGE, a force-aware multi-granularity control system with a coarse-to-fine command space spanning velocity navigation, planar foot placement, wrist-pose tracking, and optional force-conditioning inputs. Policy training requires no full-body human motion trajectories or joint-angle references; deployment consumes only sparse bilateral wrist poses relative to the pelvis, coarse motion commands, and optional desired wrist-load inputs. For sustained contact, FORGE combines a momentum observer with a learned correction trained using simulator contact-force supervision. At deployment, it estimates low-frequency, wrist-equivalent interaction forces from proprioception and reconstructed low-level arm PD torques, without wrist force/torque sensors. A quasi-static virtual-target loop uses this estimate and an optional desired applied-force input to adapt the wrist target. A frozen locomotion prior and asymmetric residual actions stabilize manipulation-induced whole-body corrections. Experiments evaluate unified-control stability, task-space tracking, quasi-static wrist-equivalent force estimation, squeezing forces during sustained contact with a rigid box, and downstream diffusion-policy execution of a squat-and-lift task across payload conditions. Website: this https URL .

</details>


### [262] [Inner Product Regularization for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17159)
*Benjamin A. Christie,Tianyu Qiu,Amy H. Choi,David Fridovich-Keil,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出内积正则化 InneR：通过对策略雅可比诱导的状态空间内积进行聚类与对齐，使邻近状态共享一致的扰动零空间，从而在模仿学习中提升对无关状态扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略需在传感器噪声、场景变化与复合误差等状态扰动下仍能外推。现有正则化（如雅可比范数惩罚）独立看待每个状态，仅限制该状态处策略对扰动的敏感度，无法刻画"相同扰动在不同状态间应产生一致响应"这一结构性质。

Method: 核心洞察：局部一致的扰动响应。做法：(1) 利用动作空间内积经策略雅可比诱导出状态空间内积（随机策略下即 Fisher 信息矩阵），其零空间决定哪些状态扰动对策略有一阶影响；(2) 将内积对齐的状态聚类，在每簇内平均内积矩阵；(3) 最小化平均矩阵的最小特征值，迫使邻近状态共享共同零空间。不限制策略表达能力，可即插即用于确定性/随机策略的模仿学习流程。

Result: 仿真实验中，InneR 使策略对无关状态维度及训练-测试分布偏移更鲁棒，而范数惩罚常劣于行为克隆；真实机械臂三项操作任务上，无需额外数据即可将扩散策略平均成功率从 18% 提升至 57%。

Conclusion: 以状态空间内积对齐为零空间一致性约束的正则化，是提升模仿学习策略扰动鲁棒性的有效且通用手段，并已在真实机器人上验证显著增益。

Abstract: Robots that learn from demonstrations should extrapolate their policy despite state perturbations such as sensor noise, scene variation, and compounding errors. One approach to this robustness is regularization. Prior regularizers consider each state on its own and limit how sensitive the policy is to any perturbation at that state. By contrast, we explore how the policy's output shifts when given the same perturbation at different states. Our insight is that, locally, the robot should respond to state perturbations in a consistent manner: e.g., if changing an irrelevant object position does not impact the policy at one state, that same change should not shift the policy output at a nearby state. Applying this insight, we develop inner product regularization (InneR). We show that the inner product on action space induces an inner product on state space through the policy Jacobian (the Fisher information for stochastic policies), and that the null space of this inner product governs which state perturbations have a first-order effect on the policy. By clustering states whose inner products are aligned, averaging the inner products within each cluster, and minimizing the smallest eigenvalues of that average, InneR shapes the learned policy so that nearby states share a common null space. InneR enforces consistent responses to perturbations without limiting policy expressiveness, and can be appended to existing imitation learning pipelines for deterministic or stochastic policies. In simulated experiments, InneR makes learned policies more robust to irrelevant state dimensions and shifts between training and test distributions, whereas norm penalties often perform worse than behavior cloning. On a real robot arm performing three manipulation tasks, InneR raises the average success rate of a diffusion policy from 18% to 57% without additional data.

</details>


### [263] [XBot-Omni: An Omni-Modal Model for Embodied Understanding and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17160)
*Kerui Gu,Xing Fu,Shuoyang Sun,Cheng Peng,Jingru Duan,Huayu Gu,Zhenyu Chen,Jiaxin Huang,Cheng Wen,Rui Zhen,Kairui Chen,Zhihui Peng,Shaoli Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XBot-Omni提出Thinker–Talker–Actor架构，将Omni多模态模型扩展到机器人特有的身体动作与面部表情模态，实现从全模态输入到语音+全身表达的端到端具身交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身交互要求智能体融合多模态感知并同时输出语言与非语言行为（语音、身体运动、面部表情），而现有分阶段（staged）设计存在信息损失与不协调问题，需将Omni范式扩展到机器人模态。

Method: Thinker-Talker-Actor统一架构：Thinker处理文本、图像、音频、视频生成上下文响应；Talker负责语音合成；新增Actor作为协调的流式运动生成模块，复用Thinker/Talker的原生表征，端到端流水线仅引入有限延迟开销，以全模态输入直接条件化多模态生成。

Result: 在七个基准上评估，在通用域与机器人中心视频上均展现强多模态理解能力，并生成高质量、自然且协调的运动；验证了可直接从全模态输入生成具身多模态响应。

Conclusion: XBot-Omni将Omni模型从对话式交互扩展到物理环境中的具身沟通，使机器人既能感知全模态世界，也能通过语音与身体行为表达自身；但报告级工作，缺乏真机闭环执行验证。

Abstract: Embodied interaction requires an agent to integrate multimodal perception with verbal and nonverbal behaviors to effectively communicate its intentions, including speech, body motion, and facial expressions. We present XBot-Omni, a Thinker--Talker--Actor architecture that extends the Omni paradigm to robotic modalities, including body motion and facial expressions, with ``X'' representing the extensibility to robotic-specific modalities. The Thinker processes text, images, audio, and video to formulate contextually appropriate responses, which are then realized by the Talker and Actor through speech and coordinated bodily expressions, respectively. In this report, we innovatively introduce the concept of ``Actor'' as a dedicated component for embodied agents: a coordinated, streaming module that leverages the native representations of the Thinker and Talker within an end-to-end pipeline, introducing only modest latency overhead. This unified architecture enables multimodal generation conditioned on omni-modal inputs directly to prevent information loss in staged designs. We comprehensively evaluate XBot-Omni across seven benchmarks, demonstrating strong multimodal understanding capabilities on both general-domain and robot-centric videos, as well as high-quality, natural, and well-coordinated motion generation. Furthermore, our results validate its capability to generate embodied multimodal responses directly from omni inputs, meeting the diverse requirements of embodied interaction. XBot-Omni enables robots not only to perceive the omni world but also to express themselves through speech and bodily behaviors, extending Omni models from conversational interaction to embodied communication in physical environments.

</details>


### [264] [UAV-Flow++: A Unified Suite for Benchmarking and Learning Language-Guided UAV Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17163)
*Donglin Yang,Xiangyu Wang,Yantong Zhong,Wenhao Zheng,Zihan Ding,Yue Liao,Hongsheng Li,Ke Xu,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出Flow任务与UAV-Flow数据集（3万余条真实飞行轨迹+1万条仿真轨迹），并设计FlowVLA模型，通过混合真实-仿真模仿预训练与Tail-Aware GRPO在线后训练，实现语言指令驱动的短程无人机飞行机动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导无人机导航研究集中于长程路径规划，缺乏对短程、物体相对飞行机动（原始运动指令与物体交互行为）的探索，需同时考察运动意图理解与空间上下文定位能力。

Method: 构建Flow任务与UAV-Flow数据集（30,692条专家真实飞行轨迹，8类运动；10,109条带任务完成判据的仿真轨迹）；提出FlowVLA，在混合真实-仿真示教上做模仿预训练，再用Tail-Aware GRPO（基于组内奖励分布强化高奖励动作序列）进行在线后训练。

Result: 在273个留出仿真episode上，预训练FlowVLA平均成功率达75.58%，超过最强基线6.72个百分点；TGRPO后训练提升至93.16%，样本效率更优；真实室内外无人机实验验证了物体相对指令的稳健执行。

Conclusion: Flow为语言条件飞行机动建立了新基准与学习框架，表明真实-仿真混合预训练结合奖励分布尾部感知的强化后训练可有效提升无人机指令跟随的成功率与效率。

Abstract: Language-guided UAV navigation has largely focused on long-horizon route planning, leaving short-range, object-relative flight maneuvers underexplored. To address this gap, we formulate Flying-on-a-Word (Flow), a task requiring a UAV to convert concise natural-language instruction, egocentric visual observation, and current state dynamics into continuous flight actions. Flow encompasses both primitive motion commands and complex object-interactive behaviors, assessing motion intent comprehension and spatial context grounding. To enable policy learning and closed-loop evaluation, we introduce UAV-Flow, a comprehensive dataset comprising 30,692 expert-piloted real-world trajectories across eight distinct motion categories, alongside 10,109 simulation trajectories in the UAV-Flow-Sim environment equipped with task-specific completion criteria. Furthermore, we develop FlowVLA, which undergoes imitation pretraining on mixed real-sim demonstrations and online post-training via Tail-Aware GRPO (TGRPO). By leveraging group reward distributions, TGRPO effectively reinforces high-reward action sequences. Evaluated on 273 held-out UAV-Flow-Sim episodes, pretrained FlowVLA achieves a mean success rate of 75.58%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 6.72 percentage points, which TGRPO post-training further elevates to 93.16% with superior sample efficiency. Physical UAV experiments demonstrate robust execution of object-relative commands in both indoor and outdoor real-world scenes. Together, Flow establishes a novel benchmark and learning framework for language-conditioned flight maneuvers.

</details>


### [265] [Collaborative Task Allocation for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Systems Through Iterative Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17213)
*David R. Martin,Brooks A. Butler,Scott Nivison,Magnus Egerstedt,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Pramod Khargonekar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出迭代聚类算法，将异构多机器人协同任务分配的全局优化问题分解为可解子问题，并保证效用单调提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多机器人任务分配本身复杂，且机器人间的协同交互产生"涌现能力"，使团队可完成单机无法完成的任务，全局优化因此不可解。

Method: 迭代聚类：交替进行机器人-任务聚类与簇内分配优化；约束机器人始终与其当前分配任务保持同簇，从而保证全局效用单调不减；分析算法收敛性，并刻画簇规模约束下会导致陷入次优解的分配情形。

Result: 仿真中在计算时间与解质量上均稳定优于模拟退火与群体拍卖，在解质量上优于享乐博弈方法。

Conclusion: 迭代聚类为异构多机器人协同任务分配提供了可扩展的近似最优方案，具有单调性保证且仿真表现优于常用基线，但尚未在真实机器人系统上验证。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems face the challenge of efficiently allocating teams of heterogeneous robots to tasks. The task allocation problem is complicated by collaborative interactions between robots where teams of robots develop emergent capabilities that enable them to complete tasks that would be inefficient or impossible for individual robots. To address these challenges, we present an iterative clustering algorithm for collaborative task allocation in heterogeneous multi-robot systems. This approach partitions the computationally intractable global optimization problem into smaller, tractable subproblems by iteratively forming clusters of robots and tasks, then optimizing assignments within each cluster. By ensuring robots remain clustered with their currently assigned tasks, we guarantee monotonic improvement in overall utility with each iteration. We analyze the convergence of the algorithm and characterize how cluster size constraints determine which suboptimal assignments could trap the algorithm. In simulation, iterative clustering consistently outperforms simulated annealing, and a group-based auction in both computation time and solution quality, and outperforms a hedonic game approach in solution quality.

</details>


### [266] [HEIR: Harness Egocentric Intent for Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17238)
*Yichi Zhang,Bo Wang,Zhenghan Zhu,Yutong Han,Jiayin Zou,Gao Kejun,Chang Liu,Shaoxuan Xie,Chi Zhang,Shi Haoyang,Zongxuan Lai,Sen Cui,Guocai Yao,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HEIR：将人类语音、眼动、自我中心视频与机器人观测及遥操作导航-操作轨迹配对的数据资源，用于研究如何利用自我中心意图进行人机交互，支撑意图 grounding、执行规划与任务完成研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人要辅助与协作人类，需同时理解语言/多模态意图线索，并执行导航与操作交织的长时序任务；现有资源缺少把人类意图（语音、注视、自我中心视角）与机器人长时序执行轨迹统一对齐的数据集。

Method: 构建多模态人机交互数据集：每个会话保留连续 episode，每段人类请求拆解为多个执行子任务；配对人类语音、测量眼动、自我中心视频与机器人观测及遥操作导航-操作轨迹，并提供人工标注；在其上做离线实验评估模型如何利用额外的人类中心上下文，并配合真机实验评估执行任务完成度。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果；提供离线实验证明人类中心上下文对意图 grounding、执行规划、任务完成有增益，并有真机实验评估执行完成度，数据与 demo 已公开。

Conclusion: HEIR 为自然家居场景下用户中心的机器人交互研究提供统一的多模态数据集与评测基线，长时序、多子任务、带眼动的意图对齐是其主要特色。

Abstract: For robots to assist, serve, and collaborate with humans, they must not only interpret human requests conveyed through language and multimodal intentional cues, but also execute long-horizon tasks that interleave navigation and manipulation. In this paper, we introduce HEIR, a resource for studying how to Harness Egocentric Intent for Human-Robot Interaction. HEIR pairs human speech, measured gaze, and egocentric video with robot observations and teleoperated navigation-manipulation trajectories, while each session preserves successive episodes, with each request fulfilled through multiple execution subtasks. The captured data, together with human-authored annotations, support the study of intent grounding, execution planning, and task completion within a unified interaction setting. To demonstrate the value of HEIR, we conduct extensive offline experiments to evaluate how models benefit from additional human-centric context across these tasks, together with real-robot experiments that assess execution task completion. HEIR thus provides a valuable resource for studying user-centric robot interaction in naturalistic household environments. Our demo and dataset are available online.

</details>


### [267] [ProtoVis-Nav: Prototypical Visual Imagination for Visual-Spatial Aligned UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17330)
*Guangyuan Sui,Yunzhong Hou,Chi Harold Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProtoVis-Nav 提出"原型视觉想象"框架，让无人机 VLN 模型先预测下一运动目标区域的特征原型线性组合，想象该区域的局部视觉表征，再以此为上下文生成航点动作，提升视觉上下文与输出航点的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视觉语言导航（VLN）中 3D 运动预测的微小误差会直接导致导航失败或碰撞；现有方法无法"推演"3D 环境中的执行结果，难以由语言指令生成准确航点。

Method: 引入可学习的特征原型库，将目标区域视觉特征表示为若干原型的线性组合进行"想象"，得到下一意图位置的局部视觉表征；随后以预测的原型作为上下文条件生成最终动作（航点），使视觉上下文与航点输出相互对齐。

Result: 摘要仅给出定性结论：大量实验验证框架有效性，并相较现有基线取得"实质性提升"（未给出具体指标）。

Conclusion: 以"先想象后执行"的原型视觉表征作为显式中间表示，可缓解 3D 结果不可预知带来的航点预测偏差，为空中 VLN 提供一种轻量可插拔的表征级改进思路。

Abstract: Vision language navigation (VLN) is very challenging for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) since small errors in 3D motion prediction can lead to navigation errors and collisions. Unable to fathom execution outcomes in 3D environments, existing methods often struggle to predict accurate navigation waypoints from language instructions. To solve this, we introduce the Prototypical Visual Imagination framework, shortened as ProtoVis-Nav, which forces the model to imagine an outcome-associated local visual representation for the intended next UAV movement. Specifically, we predict feature prototypes and use their linear combination to estimate the visual feature of the target region. In this manner, the VLN model first imagines the local visual representation of the next intended location, and then uses predicted prototypes as context for generating the final action, promoting alignment between visual context and waypoint outputs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the ProtoVis-Nav framework and show substantial improvements over existing baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [From State of THE ART to Human Prediction Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16464)
*Conghao Wong,Ziqian Zou*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文以交互艺术与人类轨迹预测算法为对照，从感知、交互、时序三个维度分析两领域的结构性共性，指出轨迹预测在指标驱动下逐渐丢失对人类行为与社会交互的考量，并提出艺术启发的预测模型可能揭示新的行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 轨迹预测模型在定量指标上不断刷新 SOTA，但其中对人类行为、未来意图与社会交互的建模却呈现反直觉的弱化趋势，被数字与指标所掩盖。作者希望借交互艺术这一异质视角重建对该领域发展路径的反思。

Method: 概念性与比较性分析：以具体交互艺术作品与代表性轨迹预测算法为案例，沿感知（perception）、交互（interaction）、时序（timing）三个维度进行结构与演化层面的对照解读，而非提出新的量化模型或实验基准。

Result: 发现两领域在结构与发展上存在可对应的共性；并观察到部分受艺术启发的预测算法能从数据中归纳出有趣的人类行为模式，为理解人类活动、技术现状与 AI 算法之间的关系提供新线索。

Conclusion: 文章属于立场性/视角性论文，主张轨迹预测研究应重新纳入对人类直觉、行为意图与社会交互的建模，并提出艺术可作为连接人类直觉与 AI 算法的分析框架；但缺乏可验证的方法与实验证明。

Abstract: Human curiosity and longing for the future have driven human progress from ancient times to the present. With the advancement of AI technology, human prediction algorithms have also drawn increasing attention from researchers. However, as AI technology continues to advance, we observe a counterintuitive phenomenon. Take human trajectory prediction algorithms as an example. Although models' quantitative prediction performance has improved, yielding one state-of-the-art model after another, the consideration of human behaviors, future thoughts, and social interactions themselves presents a gradually diminishing trend in the construction of these prediction algorithms, buried beneath numbers and metrics. In this manuscript, taking interactive art and human trajectory prediction algorithms as examples, we attempt to adopt a different analytical perspective to analyze the structural and developmental commonalities between these two fields. By examining specific interactive artworks and prediction algorithms, we aim to explore how human intuitions and art are associated with AI algorithms through three aspects, including perception, interaction, and timing, thereby providing new insights for the understanding and further research in this domain. We also observe an interesting phenomenon that certain art-inspired prediction algorithms can actually deduce intriguing patterns of human behaviors from the data, offering new potential avenues for understanding the relationship between human activities, state of The Art, and AI algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [Demo: Matter-Based IoMT Telemetry and Security Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16275)
*Muhammad Hassan,Masooda Bashir,Carl Gunter*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 基于Matter标准的IoMT测试床，自定义HealthVitals簇在树莓派5上输出模拟心率/血氧/体温数据，验证了配网、属性读取、丢包通信与流量特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联健康设备生态碎片化，各厂商App与接口割裂，Matter标准虽提供标准化配网与安全通信，但其簇库不含生理指标测量语义。

Method: 在connectedhomeip SDK与Raspberry Pi 5上实现自定义HealthVitals簇，模拟心率和血氧饱和度、体温；通过参考控制器测试配网流程、属性检索、模拟丢包下的通信以及运行流量。

Result: 定制遥测模型可在不改动配网与安全会话机制的前提下，在测试的Matter配置中正常运行。

Conclusion: 提供一个面向健康扩展的可复用测试床平台，为后续本地遥测集成与跨设备读数共享研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Connected health devices increasingly support health monitoring outside clinical settings, making secure and accessible physiological data important for patients and caregivers. However, device-specific applications and interfaces complicate the integration of readings from different devices. The Matter standard provides standardized device commissioning and protected communication, but its cluster library does not represent the health attribute measurement. We present a preliminary demo study of a Matter-based IoMT testbed with a specific HealthVitals cluster. Implemented in the connectedhomeip SDK on a Raspberry~Pi~5, the cluster exposes simulated heart rate, blood oxygen saturation, and body temperature. We examine commissioning, attribute retrieval, communication under simulated packet loss, and operational traffic through a reference controller. The study demonstrates that this custom telemetry model can operate within the tested Matter configuration without modifying commissioning or secure-session mechanisms. The resulting testbed gives researchers and device developers a concrete platform for investigating health-oriented extensions. It also supports exploration of local telemetry integration that could help patients and caregivers access readings across connected devices.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [One Block Is Enough: LoopMR for Accelerated MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14947)
*Yimeng He,Yibin Xie,Yi Zheng,Yifan Gao,Lixia Wang,Yin-Chen Hsu,Yufeng Wang,Meng Lu,Debiao Li,Xiuzhen Huang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: LoopMR 在加速 MRI 重建中循环复用单一 Restormer 块（配合数据一致性与隐图像输入），以约 1/9 PromptMR+ 的参数在脑/膝/心/颈动脉四数据集全加速倍率上显著超越 E2E-VarNet、PromptMR、PromptMR+，并证明共享权重的 Transformer 块可匹敌甚至优于独立权重级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SOTA 展开网络（PromptMR+ 等）为每一级级联学习独立块，参数量随级联数线性增长；而早期共享权重模型多用极小 CNN 块、性能偏弱，导致"权重共享本身是否可行"与"块的选择问题"未被区分。近期 looped transformer 在多个任务上表明循环复用同一块可匹配独立权重模型，作者据此重新审视 MRI 展开网络中的权重共享范式。

Method: 提出 LoopMR：所有迭代复用同一个 Restormer 块作为核心；块输入除当前图像估计外，还包含数据一致性（DC）输入与隐图像（latent image），实现迭代间的信息注入与区分。在 6/12/24 迭代下做共享 vs. 独立权重的受控消融，并对比共享 CNN 块与共享 Transformer 块的差异。

Result: 在 CC359（脑）、fastMRI（膝）、CMRxRecon2023（心）、自建颈动脉四个数据集上，所有加速倍率下 PSNR/SSIM 显著优于 E2E-VarNet、PromptMR、PromptMR+（p&lt;0.01），参数 10.3M vs 92.9M；在未见加速倍率与未见数据集上仍保持领先；共享 Transformer 块在 6/12/24 迭代匹配或超越独立权重版本，而共享 CNN 块表现更差。

Conclusion: 结论是"循环一个精心选择的块"可优于堆叠独立级联，在极低参数预算下实现 SOTA MRI 重建；性能差异主因在于块的选择而非权重共享本身。局限在于验证限于 MRI 重建任务，未涉及其他逆问题或真实临床部署。

Abstract: Deep-learning unrolled networks are the leading approach to accelerated MRI reconstruction. State-of-the-art models such as PromptMR+ learn a distinct block for every cascade, so their size grows with the number of cascades. Sharing one block across iterations avoids adding parameters, but earlier shared-weight models typically used a tiny block and produced worse images than models with distinct weights. Therefore, it remained unclear whether weight sharing itself or the choice of block was the problem. Recent advances in looped transformers, which reuse the same block across layers, show that they can match or outperform their distinct-weight counterparts on several tasks. Motivated by this, we propose LoopMR, which reuses a single Restormer block at every iteration as its core. Besides the current image estimate, the block receives data-consistency inputs and latent images. On four datasets covering the brain (CC359), knee (fastMRI), heart (CMRxRecon2023) and carotid (in-house), LoopMR significantly outperforms E2E-VarNet, PromptMR and PromptMR+ in PSNR and SSIM at every acceleration factor (p < 0.01), with about 1/9 of PromptMR+'s parameters (10.3M versus 92.9M). It also keeps its lead at unseen acceleration factors and on an unseen dataset. With our transformer block, the shared model matches or outperforms its distinct-weight counterpart at 6, 12 and 24 iterations. At a fixed number of iterations, shared transformer blocks match or outperform their distinct-weight versions, while shared CNN blocks perform worse. These results suggest that looping one well-chosen block can outperform stacking distinct cascades in MRI reconstruction, with a fraction of the parameters.

</details>


### [271] [Aggregate Accuracy Hides Diagnosis-Specific Failure in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14975)
*Shreya Ajay Kale,Angad Ripudaman Singh Bajwa*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 审计五个开源医学VLM在平衡的ChestX-ray14问题上，发现HuatuoGPT-Vision与Qwen2.5-VL对气胸召回率为0却能用流畅解剖学语言描述后否认，属输出层抑制而非表征盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VLM普遍以跨发现平均准确率评估，掩盖了对单一诊断的完全失败；作者希望揭示聚合指标无法证明模型"能看见"某征象。

Method: 在6,048条按性别/年龄/发现/标签平衡的ChestX-ray14问题上审计5个模型（MedGemma-4B、Lingshu-7B、HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B、MedVLM-R1）；做提示格式、原分辨率、单标签、同模型内强制提示对照、十倍规模缩放与SIIM-ACR独立数据集复现；并读取logits与隐状态，用线性探针检验内部表征。

Result: 432例气胸阳性中HuatuoGPT与Qwen2.5-VL检出0例（平衡准确率0.500即随机），MedGemma与Lingshu分别为38%和67%；失败跨4种提示格式、原分辨率、单标签情形持续存在，72B版本仅4%；SIIM-ACR上0/432（含最大144例全0）；同模型内结节召回79%而气胸仅6%；线性探针可从隐状态恢复气胸信息，说明表征存在但输出被抑制。

Conclusion: 聚合准确率不足以保证医学VLM可识别某征象，因漏检模型仍能自信流畅地描述该征象；需在平衡样本上报告逐发现召回率与特异度。该结论对具身/机器人视觉-语言策略的评测协议亦有方法论警示。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) are compared mainly by accuracy averaged across findings, a summary that can hide complete failure on a single diagnosis.
