We audit five open medical VLMs (MedGemma-4B, Lingshu-7B, HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and MedVLM-R1) on 6,048 ChestX-ray14 questions balanced by sex, age, finding, and label. On the 432 pneumothorax-positive X-rays, HuatuoGPT-Vision and Qwen2.5-VL detect zero cases while MedGemma and Lingshu detect 38% and 67% of the same images. The two failing models answer "no" to all 864 pneumothorax cases (balanced accuracy 0.500, chance), yet produce fluent, anatomically specific explanations that name the signs of a collapsed lung and then deny them, with no hedge or abstention. This failure is not an artifact of prompting, image resolution, label ambiguity, or model scale. It persists across four prompt formats, at native resolution, and on single-label cases. A within-model control shows the same forced prompt gives HuatuoGPT 79% recall on nodule but only 6% on pneumothorax. It survives a tenfold scale increase (Qwen2.5-VL-72B detects 4%) and replicates on SIIM-ACR, an independent dataset with radiologist-drawn masks, where the failing models detect 0 of 432 confirmed pneumothoraces, including 0 of the 144 largest lesions. Reading the models' logits and internal states shows this is not blindness. The two failing models are highly confident yet no better than chance and a linear probe recovers pneumothorax from their hidden representations even where recall is zero, so the finding is represented internally but suppressed at the output. Aggregate accuracy therefore cannot establish that a medical VLM can see a given finding, because a model that misses one can still describe it convincingly. Reporting per-finding recall and specificity on balanced cases is needed to tell the difference.

</details>


### [272] [Coarse-to-Fine 3D MRI Reconstruction via Resolution-Agnostic Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14979)
*Jiayun Wang,Ram Daftari,Weijie Gan,Valentin Duruisseaux,Ruibo Wang,Armeet Singh Jatyani,Boris Bonev,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: KIR-NO：基于离散-连续卷积的3D神经算子框架，用低分辨率全反向传播训练、零样本迁移到高分辨率推理，实现分辨率无关的加速3D MRI重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全容积3D MRI深度学习重建受GPU显存与算力限制，常退化为逐层2D重建后堆叠，丢失层间结构；而直接在高分辨率训练代价高。作者希望实现"低分辨率低成本训练、高分辨率部署"的coarse-to-fine策略，但常规CNN是分辨率特定（resolution-specific）的，跨分辨率评测性能显著下降，因此需要分辨率无关的网络。

Method: 提出KIR-NO（k-space-to-Image Reconstruction Neural Operator），核心为基于离散-连续卷积的3D神经算子：卷积核在连续基函数中参数化并按任意体素分辨率采样，从而保留卷积的局部归纳偏置同时具备离散化无关性（discretization-agnostic）。框架由k-space神经算子头先细化欠采样的傅里叶测量，再由图像域神经算子级联通过交替数据一致性（data consistency）更新完成重建；训练仅在低分辨率体数据上做完整反向传播，推理时零样本用于高分辨率。

Result: 在3D膝关节与脑部MRI基准（SKM-TEA等）上优于经典方法与CNN基线；在半分辨率、4倍加速下相较某先进3D CNN提升2.2 dB PSNR，8倍与16倍加速下增益依然保持。

Conclusion: 分辨率无关的神经算子使"廉价低分辨率训练+高分辨率部署"的coarse-to-fine 3D MRI重建在多种解剖结构上变得可行，为大规模3D医学影像重建提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Deep-learning-based 3D MRI reconstruction is limited in practice by the GPU memory and compute cost of full-volume training; 3D acquisitions are thus most often reconstructed by 2D models applied slice-by-slice and then stacked, which ignores through-plane (stacking-axis) structure. We reconstruct in full 3D and ask whether a model can be trained at low resolution (fast and memory-feasible) yet deployed at higher resolution. This coarse-to-fine strategy needs reliable cross-resolution performance, but standard networks are resolution-specific and degrade substantially when evaluated outside the training setting. We therefore use resolution-agnostic neural operators (NOs) and propose KIR-NO (k-space-to-Image Reconstruction Neural Operator), a discretization-agnostic framework for accelerated 3D MRI reconstruction. KIR-NO is trained with full backpropagation on low-resolution volumes and applied zero-shot to higher-resolution volumes at inference. Its core building block is a 3D neural operator based on discrete-continuous convolutions: convolutional filters are parameterized in a continuous basis and sampled at arbitrary voxel resolutions while preserving the local inductive bias of convolution. KIR-NO integrates these learned operators with physics-based consistency: a k-space neural-operator head first refines the undersampled Fourier measurements, and image-space neural-operator cascades then refine the reconstruction through interleaved data-consistency updates. Across 3D knee and brain MRI benchmarks, KIR-NO improves over classical and CNN baselines: it improves over a state-of-the-art 3D CNN by 2.2 dB PSNR at half resolution under 4x acceleration on SKM-TEA, with gains persisting at 8x and 16x. These results show that resolution-agnostic operators make coarse-to-fine 3D MRI reconstruction - cheap low-resolution training with high-resolution deployment - practical across anatomies.

</details>


### [273] [Supervision-Aware Evaluation of MedGemma for Reference-Assisted Radiotherapy Contour Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15370)
*Jaden Moon,Caiwen Jiang,Songlin Zhao,Wei Liu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估冻结的MedGemma 1.5在放疗靶区轮廓排序任务上的增益来源，通过读出层对照实验（输出概率 vs. 最终提示状态 vs. 视觉token vs. 结构化特征）区分监督信号、表征访问与输入模态的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像视觉语言模型（VLM）报告的性能提升往往难以归因：究竟来自监督信号、评分构造、可用输入还是表征访问方式不明确，缺乏严谨的对照实验，可能导致对模型能力的误判。

Method: 在单机构数据上评估冻结MedGemma 1.5：12例HaN-Seg留出测试集、30例非测试HaN-Seg患者五折交叉拟合、以及不重训练直接迁移到120例SegRap2023。对比四类读出：yes/no输出概率、最终提示状态、投影视觉token、结构化面板特征；并设置参数数匹配的非线性对照与解剖条件读出对照组。

Result: 留出集上，最终提示状态读出比输出概率读出高+0.3047 pooled AP（95%患者bootstrap区间[+0.2822,+0.3269]），且优于参数匹配非线性对照；交叉拟合与迁移中视觉token读出超过最终状态读出（SegRap等权平均+0.0706 AP，区间[+0.0607,+0.0804]）；结构化面板特征读出与视觉token竞争力相当；解剖条件读出未显示优于原生特征对照。

Conclusion: 医学影像VLM评估必须引入视觉token与结构化特征对照组，否则性能差异可能被误读为模型能力；本文结果限于计算与合成指标，不涉及临床可接受性。

Abstract: Radiotherapy contour ranking can support quality assurance, but vision-language model (VLM) gains may reflect supervision, score construction, available inputs, or representation access. We evaluated frozen MedGemma 1.5 in a single-institution study, a 12-patient held-out HaN-Seg test, five-fold cross-fitting on 30 non-test HaN-Seg patients, and transfer without refitting to 120 SegRap2023 patients. On held-out HaN-Seg, a supervised readout of the final prompt state exceeded a supervised readout of the model's yes/no output probabilities by +0.3047 pooled average precision (AP; 95% patient-bootstrap interval [+0.2822, +0.3269]); the advantage persisted against a parameter-count-matched nonlinear control. A readout of projected visual tokens exceeded the final-state readout in cross-fitting and transfer (+0.0706 AP averaged equally across patients on SegRap; 95% interval [+0.0607, +0.0804]), while a structured panel-feature readout remained competitive with visual tokens. The anatomy-conditioned readout did not demonstrate an advantage over the native-feature comparator. These results motivate visual-token and structured-feature controls in medical-imaging VLM evaluations; outcomes were computational or synthetic, not clinical acceptability.

</details>


### [274] [Physics-based simulation unlocks scaling laws for generalizable MRI reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15758)
*Hong Shang,Zijian Zhan,Sen Jia,Xiang Fan,Jintao Meng,Yang Zhou,Xiang Zhou,Tianyu He,Xinran Chen,Qiaoling Zeng,Keyan Yu,Jianhua Yao,Dong Liang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出 Scalable Inverse Model (SIM) 框架，通过物理仿真数据生成、模型架构与学习范式的可扩展协同设计，实现跨扫描仪/人群/解剖/协议的零样本 MRI 重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习 MRI 重建的临床落地瓶颈在于泛化性：难以泛化到未见扫描仪、人群、解剖部位与采集协议；而获取多样临床数据受成本、隐私与机构偏倚限制。

Method: 以"可扩展性"为中心联合设计三要素：物理仿真的训练数据合成、模型架构、学习范式，用算力扩展替代数据扩展；据称学到可解释、动态自适应的重建基元而非解剖特定先验（注意力图分析佐证）。

Result: 在 14 个公开与自建数据集（7 个国际中心、5 家厂商、0.3–7 T、>10 个解剖区域、>10 种采集协议、下游定量与分子任务、150 例盲法放射科医生研究）上展现稳健零样本泛化，恢复细节并抑制混叠伪影，优于传统深度学习方法。

Conclusion: SIM 提供了可扩展、隐私友好、可普及的通用 MRI 重建基础设施，为医学成像逆问题的可泛化 AI 提供范式蓝图。

Abstract: Deep-learning-based magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) reconstruction holds great promise for accelerating acquisitions, yet its clinical translation remains bottlenecked by poor generalizability across unseen scanners, populations, anatomies, and protocols. Gathering sufficiently diverse clinical datasets to overcome this bottleneck is hindered by high acquisition costs, privacy restrictions, and institutional biases. Here, we introduce Scalable Inverse Model (SIM), a framework that systematically co-designs physics-simulated training data, model architecture, and learning paradigms around scalability--leveraging compute scaling to overcome historical data bottlenecks and achieve robust zero-shot MRI reconstruction. Across 14 public and in-house datasets--spanning seven international centers, five vendors, field strengths of 0.3-7 T, over ten anatomical regions, more than ten acquisition protocols, downstream quantitative and molecular tasks, and a 150-scan blinded radiologist study--SIM demonstrates robust zero-shot generalizability, recovering fine anatomical details while suppressing aliasing artifacts where conventional deep-learning methods fail. Attention-map analyses confirm that SIM learns physically explainable, dynamically adaptive reconstruction primitives rather than anatomy-specific priors. SIM establishes a scalable, privacy-preserving, and democratized infrastructure for universal MRI reconstruction, providing a blueprint for generalizable AI across inverse problems in medical imaging.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [Neural Render Proxies for Interactive and Differentiable Lighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17185)
*Sergio Sancho,Alexander Rath,Marco Manzi,Pascal Chang,Amit H. Bermano,Derek Nowrouzezahrai,Markus Gross,Marios Papas*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出神经渲染代理（NRP）：用一次与光照无关的路径采样pass收集光传输数据，训练场景专用的轻量网络，实现固定相机/材质/静态场景下可交互速率（30-60Hz）的可微重光照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CG动画生产中光照迭代代价极高：微小调整也需用慢速离线渲染器重渲大场景，全局光照采样与复杂着色器求值导致单帧迭代需数分钟至数小时。

Method: 核心思想是将传统渲染解耦为“路径采样”与“发光/辐照计算”。从单一光照无关的渲染中缓存路径样本作为光传输数据；在线快速采样光照条件，训练场景特定的轻量神经网络学习从任意场景位置到任意像素的光传输映射。兼容非可微生产渲染器，推理显存开销小，规模仅随分辨率与光源/参数数量增长，与场景和外观复杂度无关。

Result: 重光照达到约30-60Hz交互帧率，视觉保真度接近ground-truth路径追踪；可微NRP支持基于梯度的逆向工作流，可从图像空间编辑或生成目标快速求解光照参数。

Conclusion: 该工作为离线渲染管线的光照迭代提供了实用的神经代理方案，兼顾交互速度与可微优化能力，但局限于固定相机、固定材质的静态场景，且需场景专用训练。

Abstract: Within the CG animation production pipeline, the challenges artists tackle in Lighting can be immense. Even minor adjustments require re-rendering massive scenes with slow offline renderers; global illumination has to be sampled and complex shaders have to be evaluated, leading to iteration times of minutes to hours per frame. To accelerate this process, we introduce a novel neural render proxy (NRP) that enables differentiable relighting of static scenes with fixed camera and materials at interactive rates. Our main insight is the decoupling of traditional rendering into path sampling and emission computation. From a single, light-agnostic render pass, we collect light transport data in the form of path samples. This enables rapidly sampling lighting conditions on the fly, and training a scene-specific lightweight neural network that learns how light is transported from any scene location to any image pixel. This approach is compatible with non-differentiable production renderers, induces minimal memory requirements during inference, and scales only with resolution and the number of light sources and parameters, but independently of scene and appearance complexity. Our evaluation demonstrates interactive frame rates for relighting (~30-60 Hz) while closely approximating the visual fidelity of ground-truth path tracing. In addition, the differentiable NRP enables fast, gradient-based inverse workflows, allowing artists to efficiently solve for lighting parameters from intuitive image-space edits or generative targets.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [276] [A Class of Bayesian Conjugate Distributions on $SE_n(3)$ for Motion Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16822)
*Shijie Wang,Haichao Gui,Rui Zhong*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出定义在扩展特殊欧氏群 SE_n(3) 上的贝叶斯共轭分布（BCD）类，用于全局运动不确定性表示与精确闭式贝叶斯推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 李群上的局部坐标存在奇异性，且缺乏对常见采样分布的共轭结构，导致难以进行精确闭式贝叶斯推理；需要一种无奇异、可共轭的全局不确定性表示。

Method: 将矩阵高斯分布限制在 SE_n(3) 上构造 BCD；证明其对运动估计中一大类采样分布具有共轭性；推导两类最优估计（MAP 与最小均方误差均值）、矩匹配（最小化 KL 散度）与极大似然估计；在假设密度滤波框架下构建 UAV 位姿-速度贝叶斯估计器，传播与状态提取利用上述最优估计，路标量测以闭式方式融合。

Result: 数值仿真中，BCD 估计器在不同初始不确定度与量测噪声下优于两种经典李群卡尔曼滤波。

Conclusion: BCD 提供了无奇异、具共轭性与最优性保证的全局运动不确定性建模工具，适用于滤波类运动估计任务。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel class of Bayesian Conjugate Distributions (BCDs) on extended special Euclidean groups SE_n(3) for global motion uncertainty representation and estimation. BCDs are constructed by restricting matrix Gaussian distributions on ambient Euclidean space to SE_n(3), avoiding singularity of local coordinates. The unique feature of BCDs is their conjugacy to a broad class of sampling distributions arising in motion estimation, enabling exact closed-form Bayesian inference. Optimal estimation with respect to BCDs is further investigated for implementing motion estimation. Two means are extracted as random state estimates, which maximize probability and minimize mean squared error, respectively. Moreover, within the BCD class, moment matching is shown to minimize the Kullback-Leibler divergence, and maximum likelihood estimation is also derived, which provide analytical methods to approximate underlying distributions with the optimality guarantee. As an application, a Bayesian estimator for pose and velocity estimation of an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) is developed within an assumed density filtering architecture. The propagation and estimate extraction leverage the optimal estimation related to BCDs, and the landmark measurements are exactly fused with the BCD prior in closed form due to the conjugacy. Numerical simulations of a UAV flight indicate the advantages of BCD-based estimators over two classic Kalman filters on Lie groups with various initial uncertainties and measurement noise.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [An Integrated Architecture for Data-Efficient Real-Time Neural Imitation of Nonlinear Time-Optimal Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17005)
*Maarten De Baecke,Flavia Sofia Acerbo,Andrea Giusti,Wilm Decré*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该工作用神经网络模仿长时域、非凸、带硬终端约束的时间最优MPC（TOMPC），提出自知情采样SIFS（用学到的time-to-go只在策略偏离时间最优处查询专家）、完整训练与控制架构（应对数据相关性、次优标签、协变量偏移、约束满足与模型失配），并在Furuta摆上做实机验证：SIFS以约1/3训练时间、不到一半数据达到标准DAgger性能，控制器保持专家吸引域并将控制频率从1.4 Hz提升到1 kHz，实机实现摆起与稳定且轨迹接近专家解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性TOMPC性能优越但计算代价高，难以用于快速动力学系统的实时控制；已有神经网络模仿工作多针对二次代价的仿真场景，且孤立处理神经控制器问题。本文针对计算代价极高、因而训练数据生成受限的长时域非凸硬约束TOMPC，同时解决数据效率与神经控制器的工程挑战。

Method: (1) SIFS（self-informed sampling）：利用学习的time-to-go作为专家查询指标，仅在策略偏离时间最优行为的区域采样专家数据，提升数据效率；(2) 构建统一的训练与控制架构，联合处理数据集相关性、专家标签次优性、协变量偏移、约束满足与模型失配；(3) 在Furuta摆上完成实机部署验证。

Result: SIFS在约1/3训练时间、少于一半训练数据下达到标准数据集聚合（DAgger）的性能；所得神经控制器匹配专家TOMPC的吸引域，控制频率由1.4 Hz提升至约1 kHz（约700倍）；硬件上成功摆起并稳定摆，轨迹紧密贴近专家TOMPC解。

Conclusion: 以time-to-go引导的主动专家采样可显著降低长时域非凸TOMPC的数据生成成本，配合系统性架构设计可缓解模仿学习中的约束与分布偏移问题，实现从Hz级最优控制到kHz级实时神经控制的实际跨越，为快速动力学系统的实时最优控制提供可行范式。

Abstract: Nonlinear time-optimal model predictive control (TOMPC) enables highly performant control of constrained systems, but its computational cost prohibits real-time use on fast dynamical systems. Neural network imitation of the TOMPC control law can reduce online computational costs, yet existing work mostly targets quadratic cost formulations in simulation and addresses the challenges of neural controllers in isolation. This work imitates a long-horizon nonconvex TOMPC with hard terminal constraints, whose high computational cost limits training-data generation. We present (i) \emph{self-informed sampling (SIFS)}, a data-efficient expert-sampling strategy that uses a learned time-to-go to query the expert only where the policy deviates from time-optimal behavior, (ii) a complete training and control architecture that jointly addresses dataset correlation, suboptimal expert labels, covariate shift, constraint satisfaction and model mismatch, and (iii) real-world validation on a Furuta pendulum. SIFS matches standard dataset aggregation performance in roughly one-third of the training time and with less than half the training data. The resulting controller matches the region of attraction of the expert TOMPC while raising the control frequency from 1.4~Hz to 1~kHz. On hardware, the controller swings up and stabilizes the pendulum along trajectories closely matching the expert TOMPC solution.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [Autonomous Mobile Robots on a Computing Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16652)
*Yonghwan Kim,Gil-Tak Kong,Un-Sook Choi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CABOT模型：网格地板本身是元胞自动机（活跃计算介质），而机器人极度弱化（无记忆、无灯、无多重检测、视野1、不可写入地板），以有限状态集大小|Q|作为资源度量，给出直线形成、聚集、探索等任务的紧致状态数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典LCM（Look-Compute-Move）模型中，提升能力需增强机器人（加灯、加内存、加视野），成本按机器人计。作者希望把计算能力“转移到环境中”，让地板承担计算，从而用更弱、更便宜的机器人完成集体任务，并据此提出以环境状态数为核心的资源度量。

Method: 定义CABOT：离散平面每格持有有限状态集Q中的一个状态，按统一局部规则同步演化且能感知格上是否有机器人（地板即元胞自动机）；Look操作除可见机器人位置外还返回可见格状态。机器人为oblivious、silent、lightless、视野1、无多重检测且不能写地板；以|Q|为资源度量，对若干任务给出算法与状态数上下界证明。

Result: 惰性地板（|Q|=1）下这些机器人无法成线、聚集或探索；少量状态即可改变局面：连通初始构型的Line Formation需4状态，field-silent（地板亦收敛到不动点）需5状态；任意（可非连通）构型Gathering需3状态，Line Formation需6状态，并证明二者在任何状态数下都无法做到field-silent。单机器人永久探索任意有限网格需4状态、任意环面需2状态（最优）。另有经仿真验证的规则集：等间距排列、填充地面上绘制的图案、穿越迷宫。

Conclusion: 将计算介质从机器人转移到环境是可行且高效的：极弱机器人借助少量地板状态即可完成经典难题，且资源以环境状态数计量；同时揭示了field-silent可达性的本质不可能性边界。仿真示例显示模型可扩展到图案填充、迷宫导航等任务，预示其适用范围较广，但多数扩展结论尚缺乏形式证明。

Abstract: We introduce CABOT, a computational model for autonomous mobile robots in which the discrete plane the robots operate on is not a passive stage but an active computing medium. In the classical Look-Compute-Move (LCM) model every increase in computational capability is obtained by strengthening the robots themselves, with lights, memory or wider visibility, at a cost paid once per robot. CABOT strengthens the environment instead: each cell of the grid holds a state from a finite set Q, the states evolve synchronously under a uniform local rule that also senses whether a robot stands on the cell, that is, the floor is a cellular automaton, and the Look operation returns the states of the visible cells in addition to the positions of the visible robots. The robots are as weak as we can make them: oblivious, silent, lightless, myopic with visibility range 1, without multiplicity detection and unable to write to the floor. The number of field states |Q|, rather than any robot capability, becomes the measure of the resources required. On an inert floor (|Q| = 1) such robots can neither form a line, nor gather from scattered positions, nor explore a grid. We show that a handful of states changes this. Line Formation from connected configurations is solvable with four states, and field-silently, so that the floor also reaches a fixed point, with five. Gathering from arbitrary, possibly disconnected, configurations is solvable with three states and Line Formation from arbitrary configurations with six; we prove that neither can be solved field-silently with any number of states. A single robot explores every finite grid forever with four states and every torus with two, which is optimal. Further rule sets, validated by simulation only, equalize the spacing of robots on a row, fill a pattern painted on the floor and guide robots through a maze, and indicate the range of the model.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [279] [SPIRE: Task-agnostic embodied continual learning through autonomous situation inference and causal motor-primitive reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14840)
*Yiyang Jia,Mingyang Lyu,Gongce Cao,Feifei Zhao,Yinqian Sun,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIRE：用脉冲循环神经网络从动作-结果证据中在线推断潜在情境并学习情境专属运动actor，成熟actor作为受保护源与目标情境残差组合，实现无标签连续具身任务中的技能自主恢复与因果复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续具身学习要求控制器在无任务标签、无已知任务边界的情况下推断情境变化、保持旧行为并复用运动技能；难点在于判断何时恢复旧行为、哪些技能能支撑新学习。

Method: 循环脉冲网络系统：由动作-结果证据推断潜在情境，在线学习情境专属运动actor；成熟actor冻结为受保护源，与目标情境残差组合驱动循环脉冲运动群体；并以"回溯兼容源actor"干预做因果性检验，与7种无任务循环持续学习与模块化控制基线比较。

Result: 在坐标、三关节、隐力控制任务中保持控制、自主推断情境、保留先验行为，并使未见源组合的习得交互次数减少约40%；回溯干预一致损害早期习得；平均先验学习增益为最强合规无任务基线（逐流配对选取）的2.4倍。

Conclusion: 结果支持连续具身控制中运动技能的自主恢复与因果复用，表明"受保护源+情境残差"组合是一种可用的持续学习机制。

Abstract: Embodied continual learning requires controllers to infer changing situations, retain prior behavior and reuse motor skills without task labels or known boundaries. The challenge is deciding when to recover earlier behavior and which skills can support new learning. We introduce Situation-guided Primitive Integration for Recurrent Embodiment (SPIRE), a recurrent-spiking system that infers latent contexts from action-outcome evidence and learns context-specific motor actors online. Mature actors become protected sources that combine with a target-context residual to drive recurrent-spiking motor populations. Across coordinate, three-joint and hidden-force control, SPIRE maintained control, autonomously inferred situations, retained prior behavior and accelerated acquisition of an unseen source composition, reducing mean interactions to criterion by approximately 40% relative to matched fresh controllers. Rewinding compatible source actors impaired early acquisition in every matched intervention, demonstrating causal reuse. Against seven recurrent continual-learning and modular-control comparators, SPIRE achieved 2.4 times the mean prior-learning advantage of the strongest eligible task-free alternative selected for each paired stream. These results demonstrate autonomous recovery and causal reuse of motor skills for new learning in continuous embodied control.

</details>


### [280] [Poison-then-Hide: Finetuning-Activated Backdoor Attack on Pretrained Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14970)
*Qixuan Jin,Abinitha Gourabathina,Vinith Menon Suriyakumar,Walter Gerych,Marzyeh Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Poison-then-Hide：在预训练编码器中植入休眠后门，在全模型域迁移微调（干净数据）后仍可激活，通过触发器优化、基编码器投毒与定向遗忘隐藏后门，六个数据集三种架构上达到最高97%攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"微调激活型"后门攻击假设域偏移有限或编码器层冻结，在整模型微调下失效；需要一种在预训练-微调全流程（包括域迁移与全参数更新）中仍隐蔽有效的休眠后门。

Method: 三部分组成：(1) 触发器优化；(2) 基编码器投毒（与良性目标任务联合学习，此联合训练被证明是成功关键）；(3) 定向遗忘以掩盖后门痕迹。在下游干净数据微调时触发激活。

Result: 6个数据集、3种架构上实现SOTA攻击成功率（最高约97%）；标准检测与缓解防御无法完全移除后门，良性微调后后门可重新出现。

Conclusion: 全模型微调并不能清除预训练阶段植入的后门；现有防御不足，需针对"投毒-微调再激活"链条设计更强的供应链级防护。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks threaten the integrity of machine learning models by allowing attackers to control model behavior through triggers. Models can be compromised during finetuning by backdoor attacks through either poisoned data or adversarial training objectives. Because existing finetuning-activated attacks assume limited domain shift or frozen encoder layers, they often fail under full-model finetuning. We target a stealthy finetuning-activated attack where a dormant backdoor is implanted in a pretrained encoder, and later activated by finetuning on clean downstream data. We propose Poison-then-Hide, a novel attack that remains effective when the entire model is finetuned in a domain transfer. Our approach consists of three components: trigger optimization, base encoder poisoning, and targeted unlearning to conceal the backdoor. We evaluate our method on six datasets and three model architectures, and achieve state-of-the-art (up to 97%) attack success rates. We find that jointly learning the benign target task alongside the poisoning is critical to the attack's success. We demonstrate standard detection and mitigation defenses cannot fully remove the backdoor, which can reappear after benign finetuning.

</details>


### [281] [AROMA: Adaptive Representation-Oriented Multimodal Actuation for Vision-Language Model Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15041)
*Zhaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AROMA：冻结 VLM 的可解释控制框架，将"在哪干预/用什么几何与增益/如何抵达目标/追求哪个目标/何时行动"拆为五个独立控制子问题；提出幅值归一化因果子空间驱动与迭代最小努力驱动（MEA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性定位本身不产生可用控制：因果敏感方向可能只占极小激活能量，局部线性方向在有限尺度下失效，可达的数值目标未必正确。需要把"定位—驱动—可达性—目标质量—选择性部署"作为不同控制问题分别求解。

Method: 在 Llama-3.2-11B-Vision-Instruct 中定位经因果验证的 cross-attention head，用 4 个维度的因果子空间解释基数敏感性；采用幅值归一化的因果子空间驱动实现有限步长控制；提出 Iterative Minimal-Effort Actuation（MEA）迭代逼近目标；冻结目标与门控后做前瞻路由（prospective routing）与选择性编辑，并与 attribution-steering 类基线对比。

Result: 4 维因果子空间解释 89.82% 的基数敏感性但仅 9.52% 的中位激活能量；幅值归一化后，未接触的过程性数据精确计数准确率 +8.00 pp；MEA 在同等步数预算下达 81.25% oracle 目标（冻结方向仅 15.18%）；目标与门控冻结后，Stage-2 自然队列（N=630）从 34.444% 提升至 38.730%（配对 p=4.63e-7），全 7,992 例 65.741% vs 65.153%，仅编辑 63 个输入。

Conclusion: 将定位、驱动、可达性、目标质量与选择性部署分离为独立控制问题，可在冻结 VLM 上获得可验证、低侵入的定向行为控制；跨 head/模型有复现性，为可解释性走向可用控制提供可操作范式。

Abstract: Mechanistic localization does not by itself yield usable control: causal sensitivity may occupy little activation energy, an accurate local direction may fail at finite scale, and a reachable numeral target may be wrong. We introduce AROMA (Adaptive Representation-Oriented Multimodal Actuation), a frozen-VLM control framework separating where to intervene, which geometry and gain to use, how to reach a target, which target to pursue, and when to act. In Llama-3.2-11B-Vision-Instruct, four dimensions of a causally validated cross-attention head explain 89.82% of cardinality sensitivity but only 9.52% of median activation energy; magnitude-normalized causal subspace actuation converts this mismatch into finite control, improving exact-count accuracy on untouched procedural data by +8.00 pp. Iterative Minimal-Effort Actuation (MEA) reaches 81.25% of oracle targets versus 15.18% for a frozen direction under matched step budgets. With target and gate frozen before evaluation, targeted MEA improves the previous frozen policy from 34.444% to 38.730% on a new natural Stage-2 cohort (N=630; paired p=4.63e-7) and yields 65.741% versus 65.153% over all 7,992 examples while editing only 63 inputs. Prospective routing, replication across heads and models, and comparisons with attribution-steering baselines further characterize each stage, separating localization, actuation, reachability, target quality, and selective deployment as distinct control problems.

</details>


### [282] [Imitating the Imperfect: Offline-to-Online Robust Imitation Learning from Heterogeneous Demonstrators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16020)
*Cheng Pan,Nan Lu,Kai Arulkumaran,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEOS-IL：从异构且次优的离线示范中联合学习状态-动作最优性评分模型与每个示范者的潜在专业水平，再将评分模型嵌入在线策略优化并迭代精化，缓解分布偏移，附带可识别性与条件最优占据测度/策略恢复的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习假设示范者同质或接近最优，而真实数据常来自专业水平未知且异质的多示范者，质量混杂；需要在此实用场景下仍能从无标签次优数据中学习有效策略。

Method: 提出 LEOS-IL 框架：以无标签次优离线示范为输入，(i) 学习状态-动作最优性评分模型，(ii) 估计每位示范者的潜在专业水平（latent expertise），并据评分对数据加权/筛选；随后把评分模型接入在线策略优化目标，令智能体最大化估计的最优性分数，同时用在线 rollout 迭代更新评分模型以校正分布偏移；给出最优性评分可辨识性及条件最优占据测度与策略恢复的充分条件。

Result: 在连续控制基准上，于高度异构、低质量示范情形下稳定优于现有基线（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 将示范者专业度作为潜变量与最优性评分联合建模，并把离线评分与在线 RL 精化闭环结合，是处理混合质量示范的可行路径，并有理论支撑；但仅在仿真连续控制中验证，缺乏真实机器人/高维视觉输入证据。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) typically relies on large-scale offline demonstrations collected from multiple human or algorithmic demonstrators. However, most existing approaches assume these demonstrators are homogeneous or near-optimal experts, a convenient but unrealistic assumption in real-world applications. In practice, demonstrations are often collected from heterogeneous demonstrators with unknown and varying levels of expertise, resulting in highly mixed-quality data. In this work, we study a challenging yet practical setting of imitation learning from such heterogeneous imperfect demonstrators. We propose Latent Expertise and Optimality Scoring based Imitation Learning (LEOS-IL), a principled framework that jointly learns (i) a state-action optimality scoring model and (ii) latent expertise levels for each demonstrator from unlabeled suboptimal offline demonstrations. The learned scoring model is then integrated into an online policy optimization procedure, where the agent is trained to maximize estimated optimality scores while its online rollouts are iteratively leveraged to refine the scoring model, thereby mitigating distributional shift during online adaptation. We further provide theoretical guarantees for optimality score identification and conditional optimal occupancy and policy recovery under explicit sufficient conditions. Experiments on continuous control benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing baselines across highly heterogeneous and low-quality demonstration regimes.

</details>


### [283] [Asymmetric Multimodal Learning with Decomposed JEPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16639)
*Yusuf Shihata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对齐冗余上界（Alignment Redundancy Bound），证明仅靠跨模态对齐最多保留冗余信息原子而丢失模态私有信息与协同信息；提出D-JEPA，将潜变量分解为共享对齐核与私有纤维，恢复对齐丢弃的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态对齐被视为多模态表示的标准目标，但在模态信息不对称时（如VQA中问题只选择性地探测稠密视觉场），对齐无法保留任务所需信息。作者希望从信息论角度量化这一缺陷，并给出补救方案。

Method: 先给出信息分解下的理论界：对齐仅最小化Dirichlet能量时保留≤Red(Z;X1,X2)，湮灭Unq与Syn。据此设计D-JEPA，将latent因子化为对齐的共享核心与模态私有纤维，私有纤维通过上下文掩码预测与非对称跨子空间独立性约束来学习。

Result: 在信息不对称条件下，仅对齐训练在组合推理基准上出现灾难性语义坍缩，仅在二值查询上幸存；D-JEPA避免了坍缩，下游准确率显著优于对齐基线与对齐+重建基线，并将多模态协同差由负转正；信息论审计显示其从冻结骨干中恢复了大部分可用互信息，纤维交换的因果干预证明恢复的信息定位于私有纤维。

Conclusion: 对齐应作为部分目标而非唯一目标；显式分解共享与私有子空间是避免语义坍缩、捕获协同信息的有效途径。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment is the standard objective for building multimodal representations. We show that alignment as a \emph{sole} objective is lossy wherever modalities are informationally asymmetric---where neither can express what the other carries. Visual question answering is an obvious case: a question is a selective relational operator probing a dense visual field whose fine-grained features no query recovers, so what alignment preserves is not what the task requires. We prove an \textbf{Alignment Redundancy Bound}: alignment-only Dirichlet energy minimization retains at most the redundant information atom $\Red(Z; X_1, X_2)$, annihilating private modality-specific structure $\Unq(Z; X_m)$ and cross-modal synergy $\Syn(Z; X_1, X_2)$. We introduce \textbf{Decomposed JEPA (D-JEPA)}, which factorizes latents into an aligned shared core and private modality-specific fibers. The shared core retains most of the structure alignment targets; the private fibers recover what alignment discards, via contextual masked prediction and asymmetric cross-subspace independence. Under information asymmetry, alignment-only training undergoes catastrophic semantic collapse on compositional reasoning benchmarks, surviving only on binary queries. D-JEPA averts this collapse, substantially improving downstream accuracy over both alignment-only and alignment-with-reconstruction baselines, and restoring a positive multimodal synergy gap in place of the baseline's inverted gap. Information-theoretic audits confirm that D-JEPA recovers most of the mutual information available from a frozen backbone, and causal fiber-swap interventions demonstrate that the recovered information is causally localized in the private fibers.

</details>


### [284] [Dataset Distillation via Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16642)
*George Cazenavette,Angelina Quan,Giannis Daras,Antonio Torralba,Vincent Sitzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无分类目标的数据集蒸馏方法，通过在特征空间中将合成样本"漂移"到类条件真实数据分布附近并相互远离，使蒸馏数据直接逼近类分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法大多以分类器性能为目标优化合成样本，导致蒸馏数据偏离真实数据分布、难以解释数据与模型的交互。作者希望蒸馏出可直接近似各类数据分布的样本，并提供基于分布的互补评价指标。

Method: 在预训练特征提取器的隐空间中，对合成样本施加两种"漂移"力：向真实类数据靠拢、样本间相互排斥；不使用分类损失，仅优化分布匹配（latent 空间距离/覆盖），从而生成覆盖真实分布的合成集。

Result: 摘要称漂移数据对真实分布的覆盖显著优于现有方法，同时仍是强力的分类训练样本；方法还可作为模型可视化/解释工具，并已扩展到图像之外的模态。

Conclusion: 提出新的基于分布近似的蒸馏范式及分布度量评价体系，拓展数据集蒸馏前沿，有助于理解数据与模型的交互方式。

Abstract: The task of dataset distillation concerns the learning of a small set of synthetic samples that can serve as an effective replacement for a large real dataset when used for downstream tasks. While many solutions have been proposed since the problem's inception, most of them primarily seek to optimize the distilled data with respect to a classifier. In this work, we propose a new distillation method that learns a set of samples such that they directly approximate the per-class data distribution. Specifically, we "drift" our distilled data in the latent space of a feature extractor such that they move both toward the real data and away from each other without using a classification objective. We find that our drifted data cover the real distribution significantly better than existing methods while still serving as powerful training samples for classification. Furthermore, we showcase our method's use as a visualization tool for better interpreting models and extend it beyond images to other modalities. Our work advances the frontier of dataset distillation, offering a novel synthesis method and proposing distribution-based metrics as a complementary evaluation. More broadly, our findings help address the growing need to understand both data and models by uncovering more about how they interact.

</details>


### [285] [ScrewSense: Mapping the Invisible - Anomaly Detection and Error Classification in Screwdriving Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16789)
*Tobias Lettner,Tatyana Ivanovska,Anwar Al Assadi,Christian Bergler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用VQ-VAE嵌入空间进行拧紧过程异常检测，并比较四种深度模型做多类误差分类，准确率可达100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拧紧系统过程数据激增，准确检测与分类拧紧错误对保证质量、减少返工及支持螺钉连接产品的维护/再利用/回收至关重要；传统窗口法或包络曲线监控能力有限，需探索机器学习替代方案。

Method: 以扭矩、旋转角度等过程参数构成的多变量时间序列为数据语料；异常检测采用向量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）的嵌入空间分析；误差分类评估四种不同的深度模型架构，并额外用真实数据检验鲁棒性。

Result: 异常检测有效；深度模型可区分多类误差（超出传统二分类），准确率最高达100%；真实数据测试显示较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 模型在工业拧紧过程中具备实用潜力，可免除误差识别对专家知识的依赖，显著减少人工投入与分析时间。

Abstract: The growing volume of process data generated by screwdriving systems, combined with advancements in computing power, data storage, and algorithms, is facilitating the application of artificial intelligence in this field. Accurately detecting and classifying screwdriving errors is crucial for ensuring product quality, avoiding costly rework in manufacturing, and supporting sustainability by facilitating the maintenance, reuse, and recycling of screw-connected products. In this context, machine learning approaches offer a promising alternative to traditional monitoring techniques, such as window-based methods or envelope curves. This study examines the use of deep learning techniques for anomaly detection and error classification in screwdriving processes. The data corpus consists of multivariate time series capturing process parameters, such as torque and rotation angle. For anomaly detection, the embedding space of a vector quantized variational autoencoder was analyzed. In terms of error classification, four different model architectures were evaluated. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of anomaly detection and confirm that deep learning models can distinguish between multiple error categories beyond traditional binary classification. This extends the capabilities of conventional monitoring approaches, achieving accuracies of up to 100%. Additionally, real-world data material was examined in order to further demonstrate model robustness. Overall the results indicate strong generalization capability and highlight the practical potential of the developed models for use in industrial screwdriving processes, eliminating the need for expert knowledge in error identification and significantly reducing manual effort and analysis time.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Robo-SWE: An Executable Issue-Resolution Dataset Mined from Robot Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16116)
*Vahid Haratian,Vahid Ansari,Mohammadhossein Malmir,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Robo-SWE：236 个可执行 issue-resolution 任务，源于 28 个机器人仓库（ROS 2、仿真/物理引擎、算法库、机器人学习框架）的已合并 PR，含容器配方、开发者修复与测试，并提供完整 provenance 账本与 3,540 次 agent 尝试记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码 agent 评测集中于通用软件，而机器人软件（ROS 2 构建工具链、基于物理引擎的仿真与学习框架、时序/硬件相关行为）难以用通用基准评测，缺少领域感知的可执行评测集。

Method: 从 28 个公开机器人仓库挖掘并筛选 PR，构建 236 个任务：每任务包含原始 issue 文本、修复前 commit 的容器环境配方、开发者 patch 与开发者测试（修复前失败、修复后通过，多次离线重复验证）；同时记录 1,567 个候选 PR 的筛选阶段与拒绝原因；附带五种 agent 配置的 3,540 次分级尝试与 3,529 条轨迹。

Result: 每主配置三次运行的平均 resolve rate：Claude Sonnet 5.5 45.8%、Claude Opus 5.5 56.5%、GPT-6.1 Sol 最高 54.1%（medium effort 52.5%）、GPT-6 Astra 51.4%、GLM-5.3 46.2%、Kimi K3.1 Pro 43.9%。

Conclusion: 任务以执行结果作为有效性校验，可作为机器人领域软件工程 agent 的领域感知基准并提供公开 leaderboard；其公平性的人工评审验证尚未完成，为后续工作。

Abstract: Coding agents are evaluated mostly on general-purpose software, but domain-specific systems require domain-aware evaluation. Robot software is a particularly challenging case: Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2) packages have their own build tools, simulators and learning frameworks are built on physics engines, and behavior that depends on timing and hardware is hard to test. We present Robo-SWE, a dataset of 236 executable issue-resolution tasks mined from merged pull requests in 28 public robotics repositories in four domains: ROS 2 systems, simulation and physics engines, robotics algorithm libraries, and robot-learning frameworks. Each task contains the original issue text, a container recipe for the repository at the commit before the fix, the developers' fix, and the developers' tests, which fail before the fix and pass after it in repeated offline runs. The dataset also keeps a provenance ledger of all 1,567 candidate pull requests, with the stage each reached and the reason for every rejection, and, as a first use, 3,540 graded attempts by five coding-agent setups with recorded patches and 3,529 available trajectories. Across three runs per primary setup, resolve rates range from 45.8% for Claude Sonnet 5.5 to 56.5% for Claude Opus 5.5; GPT-6.1 Sol reaches 54.1% at maximum effort and 52.5% at medium effort, and GPT-6 Astra 51.4%. GLM-5.3 resolves 46.2%, and Kimi K3.1 Pro 43.9%. The tasks are validated by execution; verification of their fairness by human reviewers is future work. The dataset and public leaderboard are available online.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [287] [Spatiotemporal learning improves quantification of pore-space evolution and fluid displacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17111)
*Zhuangzhuang Ma,Rukuan Chai,Qianqian Ma,Yanghua Wang,Branko Bijeljic,Martin J. Blunt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer的4D（时空）分割框架，利用序列CT图像的时间信息消解相位歧义、区分真实物理变化与分割误差，实现无需逐图调参的孔隙空间演化与流体驱替定量分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次时间分辨micro-CT实验可产生数十至数百幅3D图像，分割是量化物性与图像仿真的前提；传统阈值法在低对比度噪声图像上表现差且需逐图人工调参；3D深度网络逐帧独立分割缺乏时间信息，帧间误差波动易被误判为物理变化。

Method: 构建Transformer架构的4D分割框架，融合空间与时间信息；以序列图像联合推理求解模糊的相态归属，将物理变化与分割误差解耦。在矿物溶解数据集上训练评估，并与忽略时间信息的等价3D模型对比；另在两种流体驱替数据集上验证。

Result: 在矿物溶解图像上，孔隙体积增长与岩石边界的测量精度优于等价3D模型；渗透率增长与参考分割及流动模拟结果一致；优势可在未重训练的另一溶解样品上迁移；在两个流体驱替数据集上减少预测岩石的伪变化与孔隙度波动，并准确识别充填事件。

Conclusion: 时空分割可将数百幅3D图像序列转化为更可靠的孔隙空间演化与流体驱替定量测量，且无需逐图参数调整，支撑时间分辨CT成像分析。

Abstract: Pore-space evolution and fluid displacement influence how rocks transmit and store fluids, affecting geological carbon storage, underground hydrogen storage and groundwater contaminant transport. A single time-resolved micro-CT experiment may produce tens to hundreds of three-dimensional images, and segmentation is required to quantify physical properties and run image-based simulations. Traditional methods such as thresholding perform poorly on noisy, low-contrast images and need manual adjustment per image; 3D deep learning networks segment each image without temporal information, so their errors vary between images and could be mistaken for physical change. Here we introduce a transformer-based 4D segmentation framework combining spatial and temporal information. It uses sequential images to resolve ambiguous phase assignments and separate physical change from segmentation error. The method is tested on images of mineral dissolution: it measures pore-volume growth and rock boundaries more accurately than an equivalent 3D model that ignores temporal information. It also captures the increase in permeability verified against reference segmentations and flow simulations. The advantage transfers to another dissolution sample without retraining. On two other datasets of fluid displacement, it reduces spurious changes in predicted rock and porosity fluctuations and accurately identifies filling events. Spatiotemporal segmentation turns sequences of hundreds of three-dimensional images into more reliable quantitative measurements of pore-space evolution and fluid displacement without per-image parameter adjustment to support the analysis of time-resolved CT imaging.

</details>
